# Supplementary Knowledge — SugarWish Ground Truth

This is the institutional memory an AI assistant **cannot** reconstruct from schemas, repo names, or org charts: how SugarWish (a corporate-gifting company) wires its systems together (SERP, Laravel, WishDesk/SWAC, Retool, Serpy, n8n, and the now-frozen Odoo), who the real people are and what they own, and where the obvious-looking inference is the wrong one. Treat it as ground truth that **overrides** naming conventions, default-branch guesses, and "sensible" assumptions. Jack Kiefer (the user) is SugarWish's Solutions Engineer and sole owner-dev of **SERP** — the in-house ERP that replaced Odoo at the **2026-09-26 cutover**. Most of what follows exists because it tripped up a previous assistant. (Provenance: the original dictionary plus a distillation of 2,196 records in the `discoveries_encrypted` store.)

---

## TL;DR — Read This First

- **SERP replaced Odoo at the 2026-09-26 cutover.** SERP's production DB **`serp_app`** (MySQL, Hetzner serp-db box `5.161.233.240:3306`) is the ERP source of truth for products, BOMs, MOs, POs, quants, SVL, on-hand and raw-material (RM) inventory, and accounting/valuation. **Odoo is FROZEN** read-only history (last `purchase_order` write 2026-09-25 21:12): nothing writes to it, its crons no longer drive orders, and it is stale for anything after 9/26 — there is no mirror, no shadow leg, no rollback feed. The Odoo.sh subscription ends **2026-10-18**; a one-time `pg_dump` restored into a long-term reference DB on AWS/Hetzner is planned but not done (WW-3272, Munyr, backlog) — after that date the `odoo` MCP key may stop working. Investigate discrepancies in `serp_app`, not Odoo. There is no server literally named `serp_prod`. `serp_app` and `serp_test` get daily offsite `mysqldump` backups (03:00 UTC) plus binlog-archived PITR to Hetzner Object Storage bucket `sugarwish-serp-db-backups`, 30-day retention (WW-3273, 2026-09-28; runbook `SERP/serp-db-backup.md` in `sugarwish-infrastructure`).
- **`serp_app` = PRODUCTION SERP; `serp_test` = STAGING SERP** (same Hetzner host, both reached through the `jump.sugarwish.com` bastion). The name "test" is a misnomer: `serp_test` is a real staging DB (serp-dev, `serp-test.sugarwish.com`), not a throwaway/pytest DB, but it is **NOT production**. Both hold the full `serp_*` ERP schema (Odoo history was seeded into them at cutover). Never use a local Docker DB to reproduce live state — it is reseeded from scratch and lacks runtime-created rows. The old `live_darklaunch_db` MCP key was removed; `serp_test` is that same DB.
- There are three SERP deployments: **serp-prod** (`main` branch → `serp_app`), **serp-dev** (`dev` branch → `serp_test`, on the non-prod node), and **serp-staging** (`staging` branch, a browse-only copy that reads prod `serp_app` with NO workers/crons and runs NO migrations, WW-3307).
- `laravel_live` is **NOT** "the SERP database" — it is SugarWish's PRODUCTION Laravel e-commerce DB. When Jack says "live" he means `laravel_live`. It co-hosts 70 `serp_*` base tables (plus the inventory views) that are mostly a near-empty pilot bridge (19 `serp_sale_order` rows, 0 POs) and are NOT live SERP ERP data — **except** `serp_published_stock` / `serp_published_kits` (SERP publishes into these; Laravel reads them) and `serp_mrp_bom` (see the kits bullet). Target state (owner Manish, 9/29 SERP Touchbase): drop every `serp_*` table from `laravel_live` except the two `serp_published_*` tables.
- **Kit / BOM data: which DB depends on the BOM type.** Kits (phantom BOMs) are served from **`laravel_live.serp_mrp_bom`** via SerpBomService's on-order kit explosion — the intended current SoT, not a bug; `serp_app`/`serp_test` hold a different BOM set, so a small kit/BOM count from `serp_app` is the WRONG DB, not absence (the wrong-DB query succeeds and returns real rows). Normal (manufacturing) BOMs live in `serp_app.serp_mrp_bom`. Laravel `kits`/`component_kits` are FROZEN read-only (colored-box packaging); all kit editing goes through `serp_update_kit`.
- **Source of truth is split PER-ENTITY.** `serp_app` owns normal BOMs/MOs/products/POs/quants/SVL; kits come from `laravel_live.serp_mrp_bom`; `receiver_products` is Laravel-owned — Serpy's `laravel` handlers write it through `laravel_routed_pool`, which targets `laravel_live` from prod and `manage` from serp-dev.
- **Inventory:** RM + on-hand + valuation = `serp_app` (`serp_stock_quant`, SVL). Sellable (SA) = the `laravel_live` VIEW **`receiver_product_inventory`** (`sellable_qty` = available + `prelist_qty`, minus unsynced demand = orders with `ec_order.serp_sync IN (0,2)` over a 30-day window, minus `reservation_hold_qty` from `inventory_reservations`; twin `component_product_inventory` for components), fed from `serp_published_stock`. Laravel is the SoT for what's _available to sell_ because it deducts pending orders. ⚠️ The legacy stock columns are soft-deleted on `laravel_live` and the old names throw "Unknown column": `receiver_products.inventory_qty`, `odoo_inventory`, `mi_inventory_qty`, `inventory_updated_at`, `sw_inventory_updated_at`; `components.inventory_quantity`, `inventory_source`, `mi_inventory_qty`; `component_orders.inventory_source` — all renamed `<name>(deleted)`. Never cite them as live. The Odoo→Laravel `SynchronizeOdooInventory` Lambda sync is dead with Odoo frozen (its code — `app/Sidecar/SynchronizeOdooInventory.php`, `OdooInventoryService.php`, `Http/Controllers/Odoo/**` — is still on Laravel `main` as dead code). Manual inventory corrections go through **SERPY** draft→approve. (History: WW-2442, WW-2444 and WW-2810 moved readers to `sellable_qty` ahead of cutover, released 2026-09-09.)
- **Deploy: every push to SERP `main` AUTO-DEPLOYS.** A GitHub webhook makes Jenkins (`ciservice.sugarwish.com`, AWS `18.205.218.252`) SSH into the K3s node `5.161.95.56` (namespace `serp`) and run `bash deploy-k8s.sh main` ~1s later — so a merge to `main` ships; never start a second deploy by hand. The script only PREPARES the release: [1/6] pull → [2/6] build + push images to ECR (`150866789693.dkr.ecr.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/serp-{backend,frontend,edge-auth}`) → [3/6] `migrate:serp-app` against `serp_app` → [4/6] upsert Secrets + rewrite image tags under `SERP/serp-prod/` in `sugarwish-infrastructure` and push → [5/6] health check → [6/6] prune. The rollout itself is done by the **ArgoCD `serp-prod` Application** (UI `https://deploy.sugarwish.com`, on a separate control cluster owned by Munyr — not on the `5.161.95.56` cluster); it does not `kubectl apply` Deployments/Services/Ingress, and manual `kubectl` edits get overwritten by ArgoCD. `serp-prod` watches `SERP/serp-prod` on `main` with automated sync (no selfHeal/prune), so the [4/6] tag push auto-rolls. "Is it live?" = check the ArgoCD sync state / running pod image tag, not just the script's exit code. Branch flow: feature → `dev` → `staging` → `main`; hotfixes to `main` must back-merge to `dev`. The old AWS EC2 `34.203.231.65` (`deploy.sh`, PM2+nginx) is frozen legacy — never deploy to it.
- **`serp_test` and `serp_app` run INDEPENDENT `_migrations` tracks** (versions are timestamp-named `YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS_desc`, e.g. `20260928_192637_restore_unlink_fk_indexes`). Prod's `deploy-k8s.sh` migrates `serp_app` at [3/6], before the ArgoCD rollout; serp-dev's deploy migrates `serp_test`; serp-staging runs none. ⚠️ **A version gap between the two is the NORMAL steady state, NOT a failed or mis-targeted migration** — don't conclude "it didn't work / went to the wrong database" from a change visible in one and absent in the other; establish which DB was _intended_ first. Schema changes are committed migrations applied by the runner — no manual DBA step. Both DBs contain same-named tables, so presence in one says nothing about the other (e.g. the Serpy AI-turn log is now `serpy_turns` — with `serpy_messages` / `serpy_prompt_logs` — in each DB, one table per env with no `_live`/`_dev` suffix; `serp_ai_turns_live_backup` / `serp_ai_turns_dev_backup` are frozen 2026-08-03 backups and there is no `serp_ai_turns_live`).
- **`serp_test` contains ZERO Laravel catalog tables, and no Laravel mirror exists anywhere.** None of `components`, `receiver_products`, `items`, `ec_order`, `buyer_products`, `inventory_reservations`, `kits`, `component_kits`, `odoo_inventory` exist there. `staging_copier_worker.py` was DELETED on 2026-07-30 (SERP PR #707, `94deba129`) with its `STAGING_COPIER_ENABLED` flag — don't cite its line numbers. Any "the cross-DB `serp_*` ↔ Laravel join is fine because both sides are co-resident" reasoning is false; Laravel-owned data is reached through the Laravel pools (`laravel_live_pool` = always live reads; `laravel_routed_pool` = per-environment writes).
- **Orders flow Laravel → SERP through `serp_order_queue_worker.py`** (LIVE): it ingests Laravel `ec_order` into `serp_sale_order` in `serp_app`/`serp_test` (new rows have `odoo_id` NULL) and also rebuilds the published tables Laravel reads — `serp_published_stock` (~4.9K rows, stock reconcile every 600s) and `serp_published_kits` (~635 rows / 174 buyer products, every 3600s, plus on-demand via the `serp_publish_kits_requests` queue), both computed from `serp_*` tables. Production runs on a single dedicated server with the order queue as an independent process. The legacy Laravel↔Odoo link was PULL-based (Odoo crons pulled from Laravel's `/api/odoo/*`; Laravel has no XML-RPC client) — that path is dead.
- **Order sync flag = `laravel_live.ec_order.serp_sync`** — SERP's order worker advances it: `0` = pending, `1` = synced into SERP, `4` = cancelled, `5` = bypass product types (Gift Cards/Vinebox/Bouqs); `2` also exists from the historical backfill and still counts as unsynced demand. Twin `ship_date_serp_synchronized`. `items` has NO `serp_sync`; item-level sync reads the parent order's flag. ⚠️ **The Odoo sync flags are soft-deleted** — `ec_order.oddo_synchronized`, `ship_date_odoo_synchronized`, `component_imported`, the `preselect_orders` Odoo flags and `items.odoo_sync` are renamed `<name>(deleted)`, so the old names throw "Unknown column". (History: the legacy Odoo flag was the intentionally misspelled **`oddo_synchronized`** — double-d, one o — advanced 0→2→1 by Odoo via `/api/odoo` callbacks, with `5` = bypass; value `3` never existed in prod. Its frozen distribution was `1`=3,973,097, `0`=80,917, `5`=18,727, `2`=1 row, and every order since cutover sits at `0`. Jack decided 2026-07-28 on a like-for-like `serp_sync` mirror, backfilled before the flip because an all-zero `serp_sync` would have netted every product negative and disabled the catalog.)
- **Serpy sync:** Serpy ops queue in **`serpy_sync_queue`** (in `serp_app`; was `odoo_sync_queue_live`), drafts in **`serpy_drafts`** (was `serp_draft_operations_live`), worker `workers/serpy_sync_worker.py`. Every op routes to `serp:<entity>` handlers; live `sync_target`s are `serp`, `multi`, `laravel` (last `odoo` target created 2026-09-25). "x/y synced" means applied to SERP; "partial" = a SERP-side validation rejection. The Odoo XML-RPC handler arms (and `/queue-test`) were deleted in SERP commit `90e712f39` (2026-09-26); only the read-only `odoo_xmlrpc_service.py`, the `ODOO_*` config (incl. `ODOO_SYNC_WORKER_ENABLED`), `workers/odoo_parity_reports/` and `serpy/ops/schemas/odoo/` remain (removal planned). Retool's `odoo_sync_queue*` tables are frozen legacy — Retool is not the sync engine. ⚠️ **Failed/falsely-synced SERPY ops are never auto-repicked** — recovery is an explicit re-queue via `/api/admin/sync-queue` or a DB update to re-run those drafts.
- **SERPY PO-receipt idempotency guard is demand-aware** (the nested `_already_received()` in `workers/handlers/serp_orm/purchasing.py`): while an open incoming picking exists it decides "already received?" from each PO line's NET `qty_received` vs `product_qty` (not the cumulative done `qty_done` sum, which false-positived on partial-then-backorder receipts — PO P02256), falling back to the open picking's move-line remaining demand (`qty_done < product_uom_qty`) when there is no PO-line linkage. The older `_origin_has_open_demand` lived in the Odoo XML-RPC purchasing handler, which has been removed. (History: before SERP PR #710, 2026-07-30, `_already_received_result` compared a SKU's cumulative `qty_done` across ALL done pickings to only the new payload qty, so receipts reported "Fully synced" without posting the move — PO **P02123** drafts **#1728/#1748**, 176 × `RM-39-096-A`; 3 POs affected, P02123 / P02065 / P02083, ~348 units.)
- **Darklaunch is RETIRED** — it was a dual-write VALIDATION/reconciliation system writing only to a replica DB, gated on **<1% drift** as the cutover-readiness signal (never a feature-flag library). It was switched off at cutover: the drift-monitor package is deleted, the `darklaunch-drift` / `publish-serp-kits` / `reconcile-published-stock` crons are retired, and `SERP_DARKLAUNCH_ENABLED`, `SERP_SHADOW_WRITES_ENABLED`, `USE_SERP_AS_LIVE` and `ENVIRONMENT_SET` no longer appear in SERP `backend/` (config derives from `DETECTED_ENVIRONMENT`). The local `*_replica` (clean, sparse, pure Laravel, ZERO Odoo data) and `*_darklaunch` (full Odoo-merged dataset) DB names are legacy opposites — never interchange them; local SERP DBs are now `serp_local_staging` / `serp_local_prod`.
- **Cutover mechanics (history):** executed 2026-09-26 by flipping `ENVIRONMENT_SET` `odoo` → `serp` after Odoo crons off → SERP worker paused → ops halted → seed → inventory validated → enable. Odoo history was seeded one-way into SERP's own DBs (not bulk-copied into `laravel_live`) — FULL historical seeding, no 2026-onward cutoff (FIFO costing builds cumulatively from zero; 7-year audit window). ⚠️ Don't infer prod seeding policy from the **local** 90-day cap (`SEED_RECENT_DAYS`/`DEFAULT_RECENT_DAYS = 90`), which only keeps local Docker disks alive. Plan of record: `docs/plans/serp-odoo-cutover.md` (SERP PR #705).
- **Remaining Odoo teardown is target state, not done** (owner Manish, 9/29 SERP Touchbase): remove Laravel inventory transactions, the `(deleted)` columns and related Laravel code (incl. the dead Odoo sidecar/controllers); remove the Odoo inbound/outbound sidecars and SERP's Odoo connection code (Jack is removing SERP's side).
- Join SERP ↔ Odoo on **`odoo_id`**, **NEVER** `id = id`. Durable origin test: `odoo_id IS NULL` = SERP-native (every row created by SERP since cutover, and every darklaunch-worker row since PR #291, 2026-06-25), `IS NOT NULL` = Odoo-seeded. **NOT** any `id >= 1_000_000_000` range (that scheme was reversed the next day).
- `stock_move`/`serp_stock_move` `state` enum is positive: `draft`,`confirmed`,`waiting`,`partially_available`,`assigned`,`done`,`cancel`. **`assigned` = stock RESERVED/ready-to-pick, NOT shipped.** `done` is the only state that moved inventory.
- **PERF FOOTGUN:** never filter `stock_move` state with a NEGATED predicate (`NOT IN ('done','cancel')`) on the ~15.8M-row table — forces a seq scan and times out (Odoo.sh 330s limit). Use the POSITIVE list `state IN ('draft','confirmed','waiting','partially_available','assigned')`.
- `stock_picking.state` is the Odoo picking lifecycle (`assigned` = reserved/ready, `done` = actually shipped), **NOT** a payment/order status.
- **Jason Kiefer ≠ Jack Kiefer ≠ Anna Kifer** — three distinct people. Jason = founder/CEO (CEO since 2026-01-27; Jack's father, `jasonbkiefer` org; major design decisions route through him); Jack = SERP developer (the user, `Jack-Kiefer` org); Anna **Kifer** (one E) = Director of Software Dev & QA (dev PM, QA gate, Prixite liaison, SERP sponsor). The spelling difference is **NOT** a typo.
- **Jack Kiefer (the user) is SERP's sole owner-dev**; infra/Hetzner/Jenkins/ArgoCD = Munyr; post-cutover Laravel-side Odoo/`serp_*` cleanup = Manish. Full people/ownership map: **Who's Who**.
- **Two Madisons:** Madison Parks (SWAC/WishDesk developer) ≠ Madison Meilinger (CS/ops lead). Never merge them.
- SERP is a deliberate from-scratch clone of **Odoo 15's ORM** held to line-by-line parity — divergences are **bugs to fix against Odoo 15 source**, not "best-practice" refactors.
- **`serp_res_users` (ORM `res.users`) in `serp_app` is the ONLY user table** — login, passwords and roles (`serp_res_groups`) all live there, with Odoo/SERP/Laravel user info consolidated into it. The old split (identity/passwords in Retool PG `serp_users`, roles in the ORM, bridged by email) ended at cutover: migration `20260926_120000_consolidate_serp_users_into_res_users` merged the bridge and `20260926_120100` dropped it (SERP commit `feac4d1f4`); Retool `serp_users` is legacy (last update 2026-04-23). The cutover reseed shifted SERP-native user ids by one; `20260928_120000_fix_serp_users_offby1_remap` repaired the references.
- **Serpy approvals are enforced in `config.py`:** `SERPY_APPROVAL_RULES` (PO receiving + PO creation → James Emeric only) and `SERPY_THRESHOLD_APPROVAL_RULES` (inventory adjustments/scraps netting beyond ±500 units → Matthew Patrick, no admin bypass). `serp_approval_thresholds` does not exist in `serp_app` (only a legacy, unreferenced copy in Retool), and PO approval-gate columns (`ready_for_approval_at`, `ops_approved_at`, …) do not exist in `serp_app` — design intent only.
- `ec_order.size` is **MISNAMED** — it holds `buyer_products.id`, NOT a physical size. `sw_fulfill` = in-house vs vendor, NOT a shipment-status flag.
- **The word "orders" means (at least) three different things** (eCard `ec_order`, receiver/preselect orders, merch `component_orders`), and **"reserved"/`move_type`** mean different things in different systems — disambiguate before querying.
- **SWAC IS WishDesk** — the GitHub description "SugarWish Activity Coordinator" is misleading. ⚠️ **The `knowledge` MCP also indexes swirl, whose `reference/glossary.md` CONTRADICTS this** — it lists SWAC and WishDesk as two separate systems and calls SWAC "Next.js" and WishDesk "Laravel". All three are wrong: SWAC is React 18.3 + Express + Vite + Drizzle (no Next.js, no Vue); `swirl/architecture/swac/overview.md` itself says "WishDesk (codebase name: SWAC)". swirl is read-only from the hub, so if a search returns that glossary row, **trust this line instead**.
- **WishDesk is downstream, not a source:** it reads the SugarWish DB (`laravel_live`) and stores only ids + cached snapshots.
- **Retool is a SHARED multi-app PostgreSQL DB, not SERP's database.** Environment separation there is by table-name SUFFIX, not separate DBs (`_live` = prod with the real high-row-count data, `_dev`/base = local). Several finance tables (`quickbooks_dashboard`, `stripe_dashboard`, `mock_*`, …) are stale mock/demo data, not live sources.
- SERPY is an **AI inventory-ops agent** (Slack bot), NOT a typo for SERP and NOT a human; SERPY Slack messages are bot-authored.
- `git stash` is **FORBIDDEN** in all repos. Never `git add -A`. Advisory by default on anything risky/data-related.
- Inventory Days formula (Jason originated): `current inventory / (last-7-day use / 7)`.

### ⚠️ Spelling & Identity Footguns (read first)

- ❌ **AI assumes "Kifer" and "Kiefer" are the same person / a typo** → ✅ **Reality: TWO distinct surnames, both real.** **Jack Kiefer** (founder's son, SERP dev) and **Jason Kiefer** (Founder/CEO, Jack's father) are spelled **Kiefer**. **Anna Kifer** (Director of Software Dev & QA) is spelled **Kifer** — no second "e". Keep them distinct.
- ❌ **AI assumes there is one "Madison"** → ✅ **Reality: two different Madisons.** **Madison Parks** is a SWAC/WishDesk _developer_; **Madison Meilinger** is a _CS/ops management_ lead. Never merge them.
- ❌ **AI assumes "Serpy" is a typo for "SERP", or that SERPY Slack messages are written by a person** → ✅ **Reality: Serpy is an AI ops agent (Slack bot `U096P936NQ7` / "SERPY Dev"), distinct from the SERP platform.** Reading SERPY messages as human-authored is wrong.
- ❌ **AI assumes offshore devs are interchangeable** → ✅ **Reality: they are specialized** (see Offshore/Prixite table). Manish ≠ Subash ≠ Parish ≠ Aashish in skill, track, and risk tolerance.

### Approval & Escalation Authority

#### Discount / Enterprise Pricing (`#enterprise`)

- Gated human-judgment process: account managers request approval **before any non-standard discount**. Approval authority = enterprise/sales leadership (**Deb / Jen Connelly** historically); **Jason Kiefer (CEO)** is final escalation on strategy/exceptions. Approvers reason explicitly quantity-vs-margin (e.g. "I'd rather not do 10%, big drop in quantity") and instruct AMs to "publish" a higher price with room to discount down.
- Standing volume tiers: ~5% ($10k–$20k), ~8% (push-room concession), 10% (above ~$20k), 15% (very large, ~$250k+), ~20% (single-location shipping). **HHS/PPS** prepay programs ≈ 18% effective discount.

#### Serpy Operations Approval

- **Carolyn Pardee = primary Serpy approver** (ops manager). PO ops tag **@James** only (Neal was dropped by SERP PR #1138 when he left). Per-op-type rules are enforced in `config.py`: **`po_receiving` and `po_creation` are approvable only by James Emeric** (admins `base.group_system` can also approve rules-listed ops), and **inventory adjustments + scraps that net to more than ±500 units need Matthew Patrick** (only he can approve; no admin bypass). Real Serpy submitters: **James Emeric, Jose Miranda, Carolyn Pardee, Jack Kiefer**. (The n8n wine-alert workflow still tagged Neal as of 2026-09-11.)
- ❌ AI assumes `serp_approval_thresholds` ($5K ops / $50K finance) gates approvals → ✅ Reality: the table exists only in Retool (not in `serp_app`) and is **NOT referenced or enforced in SERP backend code**; actual gating is `SERPY_APPROVAL_RULES` (per-op-type approvers) and `SERPY_THRESHOLD_APPROVAL_RULES` (±500-unit net rule) in `config.py`, applied in `serpy/pipeline/approvals.py`. Other op types fall back to any ops/admin user; the Slack path checks `stock.group_stock_user`/`base.group_system`. The web `/{draft_id}/approve` endpoint and the Slack path share the same gate (`check_draft_approval_permission` via `serpy/router/approvals.py`).

#### PO / Bill Approval (SERP design)

- SERP PO approval gates: `ready_for_approval_at` → `ops_approved_at` → `finance_approved_at` → `ordered_at`. Thresholds **$5K (ops)**, **$50K (finance)** — design intent only: no such columns exist in `serp_app` and no backend code references them.
- **PO-receiving approval** was requested restricted to **James Emeric + Neal Hustava**; implemented as James-only for `po_receiving`/`po_creation` Serpy ops (SERP user id 49), and Neal has since left (2026-09-10). The live PO email sender is NOT yet enabled on live SERP; James is using normal email for Q4 POs (9/28).
- **Vendor-bill creators** (manual, in Odoo before the cutover, after goods arrive; bill creation is now in SERP — `serp_account_move`, Serpy create-bill; as of 2026-09-29 only one SERP-native vendor bill exists (created by SERP Bot), so the post-cutover owner isn't established; Nora and Neal have left): Nora Stein (556), James Emeric (436), Mike Fraser (112), Neal Hustava (94). Payment reconciliation happens in **QuickBooks**, not Odoo/SERP.

#### Glitch → Bug → Fix Process

- Non-dev staff file **glitches**; devs **only replicate glitches, never fix directly** — a separate **BUG ticket** must be Product-prioritized. Fixing a glitch directly violates process. **WishBot/Anna require an archive REASON for unreproducible bugs**, escalating to Critical if recurring for weeks or blocking redemption.

## The Systems & How They Connect

SugarWish runs on a constellation of systems that overlap and feed each other. The single most common AI failure is treating any one of them as the source of truth, or assuming clean one-system-one-database boundaries. They do not exist. This section maps every system, its host, its repo, its branches, its deploy model, and exactly how data flows between them.

**Since the 2026-09-26 cutover SERP has replaced Odoo.** `serp_app` (MySQL, Hetzner `5.161.233.240`) is the production ERP source of truth; Odoo is FROZEN read-only history (last `purchase_order` write 2026-09-25 21:12; stale for anything after 9/26); darklaunch (the dual-write validation system) is RETIRED. Sections below describe the post-cutover system; pre-cutover mechanics are kept only where marked history.

### SERP — The ERP (production since 2026-09-26)

- Live at `serp.sugarwish.com`. **Cutover executed 2026-09-26**: SERP replaced Odoo, and every read and write for inventory, costing/valuation, MOs, POs, BOMs/kits, moves and quants goes to `serp_app`. (History: the launch target slipped repeatedly — 2025 → Jan/Feb 2026 → ~early Aug → Sept 15 → executed 9/26; Anna Kifer mandated a launch ahead of the Q4/Halloween peak. The planned ~6–8 weeks of production testing, the "no do-nothing option" Odoo v15→v17 upgrade fallback, and the Popcorn/EW → cupped → all product-line rollout order are superseded history.)
- **NOT** a dashboard/replica — SERP **re-computes** Odoo's logic locally on MySQL, and is a deliberate from-scratch clone of **Odoo 15's ORM** held to line-by-line parity.
- Original 4 build phases (history): (1) Purchase Orders, (2) Bills, (3) Raw Materials/Components/Kits/BOMs, (4) Manufacturing Orders & Inventory. It became viable only after Seth reframed it into sequential pieces with Odoo dual-running.
- Two frontend apps: **red sidebar** = ERP (`app/(serp)/`: `/dashboard`, `/purchase-orders`, `/suppliers`, `/inventory`, `/receipts`, `/locations`, `/bill-of-materials`, `/manufacture-orders`, `/bills`); **teal sidebar** = Forecast (`app/forecast/`, isolated subsystem — Volume & Supplier Forecasting at `serp.sugarwish.com/forecast/live-products`, replacing the Retool live-products & ecard-inventory reports).
- **Frontend architecture:** React (NOT Odoo's OWL), custom TypeScript hooks (`createRpcHooks` factory, `useRpcAction`), `callKw()` for RPC. All model CRUD goes through a single **`POST /api/call_kw`** (JSON-RPC 2.0, mimicking Odoo) dispatching to `@api.callable` ORM methods; built-in CRUD (search, search_read, read, create, write, unlink) allowed without the decorator; private `_`-prefixed methods always blocked (403). **Direction (as of 2026-03):** editing/mutation is being REMOVED from the SERP frontend — create/edit/delete moves to Serpy.
- **Route gotchas:** FastAPI sub-routers REQUIRE the right trailing-slash convention. Root routes defined as `/` issue 307 redirects, browsers drop the `Authorization` header on redirect → 401; use `""` (`@router.get("")` not `("/")`). SERP routes to `/external-access` by checking `base.group_user`, but groups load AFTER the user object, so a post-login race briefly mis-routes a valid internal user ("Why does it think I'm external?" = groups-not-loaded race, not permissions).

**Schema & code**

- `serp_*` MySQL tables = 1:1 mirror of the Odoo PG schema (50+ models, ~1018 fields) using **Odoo dot-names**; every table is prefixed `serp_` and named after the Odoo model with dots→underscores (`stock.picking`→`serp_stock_picking`, `purchase.order`→`serp_purchase_order`, `mrp.production`→`serp_mrp_production`; also `stock.move`, `mrp.bom`, `sale.order`, `account.move`, `stock.valuation.layer`, `stock.quant`, `res.partner`…). `serp_app` holds ~163 tables (~113 `serp_*`, 8 `serpy_*`); live counts (2026-09-29): ~2.29M `serp_sale_order`, ~2,198 `serp_purchase_order`, ~1,913 `serp_mrp_bom`, ~29.6K `serp_mrp_production`, 55 `serp_res_users`.
- Costing is **FIFO** via `stock.valuation.layer` (SVL). Chain: `button_validate → _action_done → _consume_fifo → _create_out_svl → create_valuation_journal_entry` → Stock Journal (STJ, `journal_id=6`).
- **`serp_orm` was SEVERED from the Laravel half on 2026-07-30** (SERP PR #706 `15c5a5eb9`). SERP historically ALSO reached SugarWish-domain Laravel tables (`buyer_products`, `receiver_products`, `components`, `kits`, `component_kits`, `ec_order`, `preselect_orders`, `items`, `component_orders`, `locations`) through the ORM — no longer true. PR #706 deleted the `component` and `receiver.product` models and removed Laravel SQL from eight more ORM files; `serp_orm/` has zero references to `components`, `receiver_products`, `buyer_products`, `component_kits`, `kits`, or `locations`. Identity resolves through bridge FKs on the variant — `serp_product_product.component_id` / `.receiver_product_id` → `default_code` (SKU) + `serp_product_template.name` — and those columns are **`BigInteger`, NOT `Many2one`** (opaque external Laravel keys, same shape as `buyer_product_id`). Behavior deliberately removed where SERP has no source: kit membership, finished-good `vendor_cost`, the Laravel `location_id` filter, `get_shipping_address`, and `create_sku`'s INSERT into Laravel tables. Jack: "serp won't have tables like components anymore — those will have to be accessed in laravel now"; inserts that still must happen (create-product-everywhere) go through Laravel **outside** the ORM. **A serp_orm join reaching into a Laravel source table is categorically wrong.**
- `serp_product` bridge links to `components` (raw materials) and `receiver_products` (finished goods) via `component_id` / `receiver_product_id`.
- New logic → fat ORM models in `serp_orm/models/`. **Never** add per-resource REST routers for ORM-backed resources. REST routers (`backend/routers/`, ~88 files/39+ modules incl. chatter, locations, `inventory_counts`, `inventory_variance`, `sku_costs`, `shipping_cost_dashboard`, `reports`, `admin`, forecast, `retool_suppliers`, the `erp_*` family; the old `odoo` router is gone) are for non-ORM concerns (auth, forecast, PDFs, summaries, email, validation, sync-queue admin); the old "only for auth, forecast, Odoo sync, inventory_sync, receipts" list was never exhaustive, but the rule stands.
- **Approved divergences from Odoo** (`docs/ODOO_APPROVED_DIFFERENCES.md`, `docs/KNOWN_BUGS.md`) — **any OTHER divergence = bug; fix against Odoo 15 source** (`raw.githubusercontent.com/odoo/odoo/15.0`; slash commands `/check-odoo-alignment`, `/odoo-fix-divergence`). SERP deliberately does NOT replicate:
  - `button_confirm` allows an empty PO (test-pinned); byproducts via `serp_mrp_bom_byproduct`; sale-order names `f"S{ec_order_id}"` — **true of sale orders ONLY** (`ir.sequence` itself is fully implemented — `ir_sequence.py`, `next_by_code` — and IS used for purchase.order, mrp.production, mrp.unbuild, stock.picking, stock.scrap, stock.landed.cost, account.reconcile); SERP uses **CONTINENTAL** accounting (Odoo = Anglo-Saxon).
  - The invoicing flow (the order worker creates the SO with default `invoice_status='to invoice'`, never advances; `commitment_date`/`effective_date` left at worker values); `res.partner._increase_rank` supplier_rank bumps on PO confirm; PO receipt/vendor-bill recompute fields — only `qty_received`/`qty_invoiced` are plain fields (`purchase_order_line.py:38-39`); `qty_to_invoice` IS a faithful Odoo-parity compute (branching on `purchase_method`) and PO-level `invoice_status` is computed off it (`purchase_order_actions.py`, "Bug #12"); Odoo crons that move stock_move/quant states post-create — **except the prepick step, which IS replicated** (`_action_assign` fills `qty_done = product_uom_qty` on reserved lines; §2.7 of the approved-differences doc; it mirrors **SugarWish prod**, not vanilla Odoo 15).
  - No `procurement.group` model (`group_id` is a raw BigInteger), no lot/serial tracking, no package/owner tracking, chatter only at document level (never on stock.move/move_line/quant). `ir.model.data`/`env.ref()` ARE implemented (`serp_orm/models/ir_model_data.py` on `serp_ir_model_data`; `serp_orm/environment.py`, shipped 2026-07-24 with XML IDs for seven models); residual gap: `mail.template` lookup keys off the `template_ref` Char, not `env.ref()` (`docs/FUTURE_MIGRATIONS.md:20` and `mail_template.py:52` still claim it is unbuilt — stale in-repo).
  - **`sale.order.action_confirm` is deliberately INERT** — it does NOT launch procurement or create a delivery picking. The **SERP order-queue worker is the SINGLE creator of delivery pickings** via `stock.picking.action_process_new_order`; a second picking-creator would produce two pickings per order.
  - **Reconciliation:** SERP HAS an engine — `account_move_line_reconcile.py`, a 610-line Odoo-15 port (`reconcile()`, partials, `remove_move_reconcile()`, `account_full_reconcile`/`account_partial_reconcile`), shipped 2026-07-24 — but it does **NOT drive `payment_state`**: move-level `amount_residual` is a plain field the engine deliberately doesn't update ("lands in PR 2", never landed), so `action_reverse` still force-writes both, behind a stale "SERP has no reconciliation engine" comment at `account_move_actions.py:464`.
  - **Bin-level putaway/removal "approved divergence" is RETRACTED (2026-09-11).** SERP does not reliably write the parent location (move lines are stamped at the _gathered quant's bin_, `stock_move_actions.py:780-781`), and the behavior is not benign — it is the mechanism behind a parent→child self-cancel that inflates inventory (see "`_gather()` DOES expand child locations"). Whether a separate putaway-strategy gap exists is unverified; re-derive before re-adding anything.

**SERP auth (two unrelated layers)**

- **HTTP Basic Auth** — served by a dedicated **`serp-edge-auth` K8s Deployment** (`edge_auth/server.py`: the source of truth for the credential; nginx no longer consults `serp-basic-auth`; `k8s/edge-auth.yaml`, 2 replicas; `deploy-k8s.sh` upserts the `serp-edge-auth-cred` secret every deploy). Credential: `serp_admin` / `swserp12` — it gates the remote SERP hosts (`serp.sugarwish.com`, `serp-test.sugarwish.com`); browser automation without it hits `net::ERR_INVALID_AUTH_CREDENTIAL`. The old nginx `/etc/nginx/.htpasswd` / `deploy.sh` story is dead (`deploy.sh` no longer exists). The credential is browser-cached (Safari flushes aggressively), so a repeated "sign in" popup = Basic-Auth cache flush, **NOT** a JWT bug.
- **In-app JWT login** (`/login`) — 15-min HS256 access token (in memory); 7-day refresh token as HttpOnly cookie scoped to `/api/auth`; rotates on each refresh (old revoked).
- **Identity & roles** live in MySQL: `serp_res_users` (user auth moved to MySQL in the 2026-03 migration) + `serp_res_groups` (group-based RBAC). The `serp_users` id→`orm_user_id` bridge (formerly Retool PG, then hard-swapped to `serp_app`) was **dropped 2026-09-26** (SERP commit `feac4d1f4`): the users table in `serp_app` (`serp_res_users`, 55 rows) is now the ONLY user table, with Odoo/SERP/Laravel user info consolidated into it; Retool `serp_users` is legacy (last updated 2026-04-23; `_backup_serp_users` stale). **`serp_app` availability gates logins.**
- **Passwords** live in `serp_res_users.password` in `serp_app`, verified by `res.users.authenticate()` (`serp_orm/models/res_users.py`): bcrypt `$2b$` for Retool/SERP-origin users, PBKDF2-SHA512 for Odoo-only users (no `'synced-from-retool'` placeholders remain; a few users have no password). The old Retool-PG password home + `ensure_orm_user()` lazy sync is gone; `backend/auth/retool_connector.py` survives only as a thin facade over `pools.retool`. Historical Retool serial ids and ORM bigint ids were independent — **users were matched by EMAIL, not id**.
- `serp_refresh_tokens` and `serp_password_reset_tokens` are documented as Retool but actually live in MySQL (ORM, via `env.cr`).
- ❌ AI assumes a separate PostgreSQL auth DB (`.env` has `TEST_AUTH_DB_*` localhost:5432 vars) → ✅ those vars are dead code (`auth_database.py` no longer exists; auth reads `serp_res_users` through the ORM on the same MySQL). "Cannot connect to main database for auth on 127.0.0.1" = trying local MySQL (127.0.0.1:3307).
- ❌ `SUPERUSER_ID = 1` is hardcoded in `environment.py`; `get_env()` can return uid=1 — accidental production use = full privilege escalation.
- `is_internal` gates all internal API access (false → 403 `external_user_redirect`); internal-vs-external check is `user.groups?.some(g => g.full_name === 'base.group_user')`. External users = **suppliers** (Redstone, Blair Candy), portal-only.
- Local dev: backend `:8000`, frontend `:3002` (some sources 3000); login `jack@sugarwish.com` / `localdev123` (alt creds `jack@sugarwish.com` / `UZ(8!C5Q2Y1f`). E2E uses isolated ports (backend 8888, frontend 3333) with DB `serp_e2e`. Slack interactivity locally needs ngrok.

**SERP connection pools (standardized 2026-07-30, SERP PR #715 `37679e989`)**

Every pool asks `backend/pools/db_targets.py::resolve_target(pool)` for its target; **no pool branches on env vars itself**, and the resolver's `_TABLE` IS the pools spreadsheet. Pool files: `mysql_routed.py`, `mysql_serp_routed.py` (`serp_routed_pool`; `serp_app_routed_pool` was folded into it), `serp_live.py` (`serp_live_pool`), `mysql_laravel_live.py` (`laravel_live_pool` — **always live, read-only, intentionally NOT routed**, Jack: "I don't want laravel_live_pool routed"), `laravel_routed_pool` (in `mysql_routed.py`; **all Laravel WRITES** — prod→`laravel_live`, dev→`manage`, local→`laravel_local` — so a local edit can never hit production), `retool.py`, `redis.py`. Resolver rows: `laravel_live`, `serp_live` (fixed); `serp_routed` (prod→`serp_app`, dev→`serp_test`, local→twin; every SERP WRITE), `serp_routed_read` (same, plus the local `LOCAL_READ_LIVE_SERP_APP` read-only escape hatch), `serp_orm` (the app ORM; `DEV_ORM_USES_LIVE_SERP_APP` lets a dev stack point the ORM at live `serp_app` — it WRITES production and is rejected alongside `WORKERS_ENABLED=true`), `laravel_routed`. The Odoo pools (`odoo_routed_pool`, `odoo_live_pool`) are gone with the Odoo connection code. The environment axis is **`DETECTED_ENVIRONMENT`** (`local` | `dev` | `production`; `staging` also exists in the Literal) plus local-only `LOCAL_DB_TWIN` (`prod`|`staging`, which only picks the local Docker DB name); **`SERP_ORM_ENV`, `SERP_DB_ENV`, and `LIVE_DB_SOURCE` are DERIVED read-only properties, not settable env vars** — don't set them in `.env`. `DEV_DB_*`, `LOCAL_DB_NAME`, `USE_MANAGE_DB` and the old darklaunch pairing validator were deleted in PR #715 (`37679e989`, 2026-07-30); `LOCAL_DB_TWIN`/`LARAVEL_LOCAL_DB_NAME` were added. Pre-PR #715 pool names are dead in `backend/` — `live_mysql_pool`, `routed_mysql_pool`, `darklaunch_mysql_pool`, `serp_app_live_pool`, `serp_app_pool` (→ `serp_app_routed_pool` → folded into `serp_routed_pool`), and `get_darklaunch_pool()` / `serp_orm/darklaunch_pool.py` (former callers use `serp_routed_pool`). `ENVIRONMENT_SET`, `SERP_DARKLAUNCH_ENABLED`, `SERP_SHADOW_WRITES_ENABLED`, `USE_SERP_AS_LIVE`, `ODOO_ENV`, `DARKLAUNCH_DB_ENV` have zero hits in SERP `backend/` on `main` (only `deploy-k8s-staging.sh` still mentions `ENVIRONMENT_SET`). Credential blocks: LOCAL `TEST_DB_*` (local Docker MySQL `127.0.0.1:3307`), LIVE `LIVE_DB_*` (Laravel, via SSH tunnel), MANAGE `MANAGE_DB_*`, HETZNER `SERP_APP_DB_*` (`serp_test`/`serp_app`). Node env vars live in the node's `.env` (`/opt/SERP/.env` for prod), from which `deploy-k8s.sh` rebuilds the `serp-env` Secret every run — so a new var must be set there BEFORE the release merge (`/deploy` Step 1.5 checks); secrets are never committed. (The old committed `env-changes/*.yaml` convention is gone — no such dir on `main`.) The forecast connector's `execute_serp_app_query` reads live `serp_app` in EVERY environment via `serp_live_pool` (PR #1054), so a local `/forecast` mixes live data; the cost-tracker/inventory-variance SERP queries instead go through the env-routed pool (PR #1079, merged), which reads the local Docker twin unless `LOCAL_READ_LIVE_SERP_APP=true` (read-only — writes still hit the local twin).

Still-true capacity footguns: SERP runs **6+ independent connection pools**. Retool PG alone has THREE (asyncpg max=5 + psycopg2 sync max=15 + SQLAlchemy asyncpg 5+5). The SERP ORM pool (`serp_orm/db.py`, custom PyMySQL, size=5/overflow=8 = 13 max, `pool_recycle=300s`) is held idle for an entire 200–320s forecast request, so only ~13 concurrent requests exhaust it. The `ForecastDatabaseConnector` singleton holds pools for Live SERP/Laravel (SQLAlchemy MySQL via SSH tunnel), serp_app, and Retool asyncpg (min_size=2, max_size=15, timeout=90s). On the frozen AWS EC2 (`i-0a0de24d0845c6341`) this overwhelmed the box (504s); production is now Hetzner K3s but the pool/index footguns still apply.

**SERP local DBs:** `serp_local_staging` / `serp_local_prod` (+ `laravel_local`), one local Docker MySQL (`serp-mysql`, `127.0.0.1:3307`; SERP PR #46 consolidated from a 5-DB layout). Legacy `*_replica` / `*_darklaunch` names (and env values like `local-staging-darklaunch`) still normalise onto them via `LOCAL_ENV_ALIASES`. The deployed Hetzner DBs (`serp_app`, `serp_test`) can never be reseeded — every seed path refuses them. Never use a local Docker DB to reproduce what serp-dev/prod see: it is reseeded from scratch and lacks runtime-created rows.

**Migrations:** `serp_app` schema changes are **committed migrations** (`serp_app/migrations/*.sql`, run by `npm run migrate:serp-app` with its OWN `_migrations` table, independent of `seeding/migrations/`) applied ONLY by the runner — no manual DBA step, never a raw connection. Versions are timestamp-named `YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS_desc`; heads are independent per DB (last observed heads: see Databases) and a gap between them is the normal steady state (serp-dev migrates only its own `serp_test`; staging migrates nothing; prod `deploy-k8s.sh` [3/6] is the only writer of `serp_app`, so a migration promoted to staging is not on `serp_app` until the prod deploy). (Pre-cutover history: the AWS-era `deploy.sh` did not migrate the `manage` cluster and new `serp_*` tables needed a manual DBA apply by Manish before code shipped; `deploy.sh` and `seeding/migrations/*.sql` are gone.)

#### Hosting, Deploy & Environments

- **Production = Hetzner K3s cluster** — node `5.161.95.56` (k8s-serp-worker), namespace `serp`, live host `serp.sugarwish.com` (public DNS A record → `5.161.95.56`). Prod is a single dedicated node (`k8s-serp-worker`) with the order queue as its own process (9/29 touchbase: moved there for peak-load capacity); non-prod runs on a separate node (below). Deployments: `serp-backend` (HPA `backend-hpa.yaml`, **min 2 / max 5** replicas — multi-replica is safe since 2026-06-09 because live-path refresh tokens live in shared Redis — `backend/crud/crud_refresh_token.py`, keys `serp:refresh_token:<hash>`, redis pod in ns `serp`), `serp-frontend` (2), `serp-workers` (1, Recreate — email/serpy-sync/cron workers; pins `ORDER_QUEUE_WORKER_ENABLED=false` inline), **`serp-order-queue-worker`** (1, Recreate, sync-wave 11 — WW-3274: the order queue on its own pod, same image + `python -m workers_main` entrypoint with the other workers switched off inline; `ORDER_QUEUE_WORKER_ENABLED` comes only from the `serp-env` Secret, which is the single on/off lever; MUST stay 1 replica since `GET_LOCK` only fences detection), `serp-edge-auth` (2), `redis` (1), plus a promtail log-shipper DaemonSet. `WORKERS_ENABLED=false` MUST stay set on `serp-backend` — it comes from the **`serp-env` secret** via `envFrom`, invisible in inline `env:`; verify with `kubectl exec -- printenv WORKERS_ENABLED`. Redis is in-cluster (not the host install).
- **Manifests are owned by ArgoCD**, not the SERP repo: since 2026-07-29 prod Deployments/Services/Ingress are applied from `sugarwish-infrastructure` `SERP/serp-prod/` by the ArgoCD `serp-prod` Application (ArgoCD runs on `sugarwish-k8s-cp`, UI `deploy.sugarwish.com`; Munyr's prod-of-record repo — infra changes go by PR to Munyr). The repo's `k8s/*.yaml` (`backend.yaml` — `serp-backend` replicas 2; `workers.yaml` — `serp-workers`, workers gated by `WORKERS_ENABLED`; `ingress.yaml` — TLS secret `serp-tls-prod`, a Let's Encrypt cert renewed by host certbot over HTTP-01 via `certbot-webroot.yaml`, not cert-manager) is a stale pre-ArgoCD copy, dead for the cluster (a pre-commit hook blocks edits; override `ALLOW_PROD_MANIFEST_EDIT=1`). `serp-prod`/`serp-dev` use ArgoCD **automated sync with `selfHeal` and `prune` OFF** (policy lives only in ArgoCD, changed via `kubectl patch` on `sugarwish-k8s-cp`); a wave-15 `edge-auth-gate` Sync hook must pass before the wave-20 ingress (never use the "apply" sync strategy — it skips hooks). Never `kubectl apply/set image/edit` a prod, serp-dev or serp-staging object — the next sync overwrites it. Secrets (`serp-env`, `serp-edge-auth-cred`) stay node-side, upserted by `deploy-k8s.sh`.
- **Prod deploy is AUTOMATIC on every push to `main`.** A GitHub push webhook to `ciservice.sugarwish.com/github-webhook/` makes Jenkins (AWS `18.205.218.252`) SSH into `5.161.95.56` and run `deploy-k8s.sh main` ~1s after the push (every infra tag bump since 9/20 lines up with a Jenkins login). Jenkins **authenticates as root** (key `jenkins@ip-172-31-73-239` in `/root/.ssh/authorized_keys`) but the script **executes as `jack`**: `deploy-k8s.sh` exits 1 on uid 0 (guard since 2026-06-10), yet the Jenkins run on 2026-09-27 reached [4/6] (it raced a manual `/deploy` on `abea4312d` — the incident behind the flock), so the job's remote command must drop to `jack` before invoking it. That command lives only in the Jenkins job config at `ciservice.sugarwish.com` (no Jenkinsfile in SERP or `sugarwish-infrastructure`); docs that say it "runs as root" mean the SSH login, not the process. The script's 6 phases: [1/6] pull, [2/6] build + push images to ECR, [3/6] `migrate:serp-app` against `serp_app` (before the new pods start), [4/6] upsert node-local secrets + bump image tags in `sugarwish-infrastructure` `SERP/serp-prod/`, [5/6] health check, [6/6] prune. It does NOT `kubectl apply` anything (it only upserts the node-side `serp-env` — rebuilt from `/opt/SERP/.env` — and `serp-edge-auth-cred` Secrets); **ArgoCD performs the rollout** (async; `serp-prod` is on automated sync, so the [4/6] tag push rolls on its own — `/deploy` still stops at a "press Sync" handoff whose manual-sync prerequisite is a pending Munyr change), so a failed later phase is not a failed build — images and tags are already staged. After a merge to `main`, **wait for the Jenkins run** (`pgrep -af '[d]eploy-k8s'` on the node, then the `serp-deploy` infra commit) rather than starting a second one — run it by hand only if none appears within ~2 min; a whole-run flock (`/tmp/serp-prod-deploy.lock`, PR #1505) serializes overlap (the lock file must be writable by `jack` — a root-owned `/tmp/serp-prod-deploy.lock` fails the run). The script escalates via `sudo -n` only for the ECR login (root-only `[serp-ecr]` AWS profile) and the root-owned `/opt/sugarwish-infrastructure` push. ❌ The old AI-misconception entry ("AI assumes SERP auto-deploys from `main` via Jenkins → deploy is manual") is now **backwards**: a merge to `main` ships.
- **Branch flow:** `feature/*`, `bugfix/*`, `refactor/*`, `hotfix/*` → PRs target **`dev`** → `staging` (`/promote-to-staging`) → `main` (`/deploy` or `/merge-to-main`, `--merge`, never squash/rebase/`--delete-branch`); only `hotfix/*` may PR/merge straight to `main`, and must back-merge to `dev` in the same session. Never work directly on dev/main without permission; get explicit permission before pushing `main` (it deploys). **PR review/merge into `dev` (since 2026-09-28):** a launchd poller on Jack's Mac (`scripts/pr-review-poller.sh`, every 5 min, runs Codex on `/review-pr-jack --auto`) reviews each Slack-announced PR at its current head and posts the verdict in the PR's `#devgroup-serp` thread; when that review is YES and Jack reacts 🚀, `scripts/review/rocket-merge.sh` merges it (`--match-head-commit`) and starts the serp-dev deploy. `/create-pr` ends at "the poller will review it; merge with 🚀" — `/review-pr-jack` is the other sanctioned merge path. Any `backend/serp_orm/` change is flagged "ORM CHANGE — Jack review required" (P7).
- **Three stacks on two nodes** (WW-3307 moved serp-dev off the prod node and added serp-staging):

  | | Prod (`serp-*`) | serp-dev (`serp-dev-*`) | serp-staging (`serp-*-staging`) |
  | --- | --- | --- | --- |
  | Node | `5.161.95.56` | `5.161.51.208` (k8s-serp-stage-worker; ArgoCD cluster `serp-nonprod`) | `5.161.51.208` |
  | Namespace | `serp` | `serp-dev` | `serp-staging` |
  | Host | `serp.sugarwish.com` | `serp-test.sugarwish.com` (a web HOST — not the `serp_test` DB; public DNS → `5.161.51.208`) | `serp-staging.sugarwish.com` |
  | Branch / deploy | `main` — Jenkins auto-deploy (`deploy-k8s.sh main`) | `dev` — `deploy-k8s-dev.sh`, run as a post-merge step by whatever merged (the 🚀 poller or the merging Claude session: `ssh jack@5.161.51.208 'cd /opt/SERP-dev && flock -w 1800 /tmp/serp-dev-deploy.lock bash deploy-k8s-dev.sh'`; the old `deploy-dev.yml` GitHub auto-deploy is retired) | `staging` — `deploy-k8s-staging.sh` in `/opt/SERP-staging` (`/promote-to-staging`) |
  | Data | `serp_app` (prod) | `serp_test` (staging DB), Laravel reads via the **manage cluster** (`5.78.203.128`, `sugarwish_manage_new_2`) | reads **prod `serp_app`**, browse-only (serp mode) |
  | Workers | `serp-workers` (1) + `serp-order-queue-worker` (1) | `serp-dev-workers` (1), **manual-only** (serp_sync + cron "Run now" drain for `env='dev'`; auto-fire off) + `serp-dev-order-queue-worker` (1; `ORDER_QUEUE_WORKER_ENABLED` from the `serp-dev-env` Secret) | none — every worker/cron/email/Slack/Shopify/write flag off |
  | Migrations | `migrate:serp-app` on `serp_app` — the ONLY writer | own app DB (`serp_test`); phase hard-fails if it resolves to `serp_app` | none |
  | Images | ECR `serp-backend`/`serp-frontend` | ECR `serp-backend-dev`/`serp-frontend-dev` (stable `serp-dev-*` tags, `imagePullPolicy: Always`) | ECR `serp-*-staging` stable tags |

  serp-dev stack origin: PR #344, WW-1686 (2026-07-02). serp-dev specifics: clone `/opt/SERP-dev`, secret `serp-dev-env`, own redis + `serp-edge-auth`, backend mem 1Gi (live-products OOM, PR #363), TLS serves the pre-existing `serp-tls` cert (PR #349). It runs `ENVIRONMENT=dev` (a third env beside `local`/`production`): `IS_LOCAL` false so DB routes production-style via `LIVE_DB_*` → manage cluster, but `IS_PRODUCTION` false. **`ENVIRONMENT=dev` MUST be set explicitly** in `/opt/SERP-dev/.env` (auto-detection keys off `.rds.amazonaws.com`). Raw `ENVIRONMENT`-string checks (`== "local"` / `== "production"`) are a recurring bug class — `"dev"` matches neither and silently lands in the PROD branch (sent dev to prod tables and dev workers' XML-RPC to prod Odoo before PRs #352/#354, `TREAT_AS_LOCAL`). Serpy is ON in serp-dev: dev drafts write to `serp_test.serpy_drafts` (100+ in Sept 2026), not the prod queue (the old runbook line saying it is off via a blank `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is stale). serp-dev and prod hit DIFFERENT data because the env-routed `serp_routed_pool` targets a different DATABASE (prod → `serp_app`, dev → `serp_test`) with the **same table names** — no suffix.
- **ECR shared-repo outage (2026-08-24), the lesson:** serp-dev and prod once pushed to the SAME ECR repos under a "keep 5 newest images (any tag)" lifecycle, so frequent dev deploys aged the live prod tag out → next prod pod restart `ErrImagePull`/403 → `serp-backend 0/2 ready` → nginx 503 everywhere. Remediation (Munyr's repo): dev-only repos `serp-backend-dev`/`serp-frontend-dev`, lifecycle split (prod untagged-cleanup + keep 10; dev keep 5). Dev deploys must never share an ECR repo (or keep-N-any-tag lifecycle) with the live prod image.
- **Image accumulation:** un-pruned, each deploy leaks ~1.3GB into `/var/lib/containerd` (Docker uses the containerd store — `/var/lib/docker` looks tiny and misleads `du`); kubelet image GC force-kicks at 85% disk. `deploy-k8s.sh`'s `[6/6]` prune keeps current `$TAG` + `$PREV_TAG` + `:latest`, plus `docker builder prune --keep-storage=8G`.
- **Other Hetzner facts:** the `manage` cluster MySQL (AWS shut down Apr 29 2026), Desk2/Desk3 (WishDesk), and the SERP DBs are on Hetzner; `laravel_live` MySQL stays on AWS RDS (`database-1.cqqg1tfyyubp.us-east-1`, SSH tunnel). A fresh Hetzner MySQL needs `SET GLOBAL sql_mode='NO_ENGINE_SUBSTITUTION'`; Hetzner has no managed ALB/Redis/S3 (Hetzner Cloud LB + Let's Encrypt; self-host Redis; Object Storage via `S3_ENDPOINT_URL`, s3v4, virtual addressing, `payload_signing_enabled=False`, no dots in bucket names); attachments go to the bucket named by `S3_BUCKET` (prefix `attachments`; AWS S3 on prod, MinIO locally; `.env.example` says `serp-attachments`, the seeded Odoo chatter binaries sit under `s3://sw-serp/serp-attachments/`) — the prod value lives only in the node `.env`. Est. cost drop ~$128/mo → ~€9–18/mo. **Munyr owns the infra migration** (Seth/Anna sponsors); Jack is a consumer, not infra owner.
- **`serp_test` name collision (still true):** TWO databases are named `serp_test` on two Hetzner boxes — only the serp-db one (`5.161.233.240`, `@@hostname='serp-db'`, co-located with `serp_app`, hub MCP key `serp_test`, ~163 tables) is live. The **manage** box `5.78.203.128` carries a DEAD `serp_test` fossil (101 tables, last written 2026-06-30) — never use it (Jack, 2026-08-18). A `serp_test` query returning plausible-but-stale rows = you reached the dead twin. `serp_test` is STAGING SERP (serp-dev's DB) — real, but NOT production and NOT a throwaway/pytest DB.
- **Legacy AWS EC2** (`34.203.231.65`, `/opt/SERP`, PM2 + nginx, old `deploy.sh`) is **frozen legacy — never deploy to it**; workers disabled there. Its PM2 lore (history): PM2 caches env vars (`.env` changes need `pm2 delete serp-backend` then `pm2 start … --only`; plain restart/reload doesn't pick them up); over non-interactive SSH export `PATH=/home/ubuntu/.nvm/versions/node/v20.20.1/bin:$PATH; PM2_HOME=/home/ubuntu/.pm2`; Next.js build with a 1.5GB Node heap cap; PM2 apps were `serp-backend` (gunicorn 2 uvicorn workers, `WORKERS_ENABLED=false`), `serp-frontend`, `serp-workers` (single fork, `WORKERS_ENABLED=true`). `deploy.sh`, `ecosystem.config.js` and `deployment/` no longer exist in the repo.
- **Health probes (WW-3399–3403, Sept 2026):** `GET /api/health` stays static (it is the k8s readiness+liveness probe, so it must not flap on a DB blip); dependency checks live at `GET /api/health/<component>` (`routers/health.py` + `services/health_probes.py` registry — on `main`: serp-db, redis, laravel-db, retool-db, login-auth, order-queue, sync-queue, worker-email, worker-serp-sync, crons; the external serpy-ai/slack/gsheets/shipstation/shopify checks are merged to `dev` and ship with the next release), 200/500 status-only, gated by a probe key, scraped by the SRE Blackbox/Prometheus prober like WishDesk's routes. SERPY posts worker-failure / backlog alerts (e.g. "Worker failure: serp-order-queue (OrderProcessingTimeout)", "Backlog too large … threshold 2000") to **`#serp-alerts`** (`C0C0U9B7SNP`, the `SLACK_ERROR_CHANNEL`).
- **Odoo creds are off the app:** the nodes keep `ODOO_*` in a gitignored sibling `.env.odoo` (not in `.env`, so not in the `serp-env` Secret); only seed/parity tooling loads it (`load_odoo_env()`). The app has no Odoo pools at all, and the surviving `odoo_xmlrpc_service.py` raises on any write ("read-only post-cutover"). SERP also runs without Retool creds (the Retool pool is skipped when `RETOOL_DATABASE_URL` is unset).
- **Cluster introspection:** use `mcp__serp-prod__*` (read-only kubectl over SSH; `env` = `prod`/`dev`/`staging`) from a SERP session.

#### SERP workers & crons

- Background workers run `python -m workers_main`; the order queue has its own pod (`serp-order-queue-worker`) and everything else shares **one asyncio event loop** in `serp-workers`, supervised by an asyncio supervisor (`workers_main.py`/`supervisor.py`, added after a 2026-05-29 silent sync death; restarts individual wedged coroutines). No row-level locking — running workers in multi-worker gunicorn would double-fire Slack pings/emails/pickings. Two pollers on the **same** sync queue risk double-firing non-idempotent writes.
- **Workers can silently hang forever** on idle-dropped sockets (asyncpg `pool.acquire()`, `xmlrpc.client`, PyMySQL) — the pod looks fine while wedged. Fixes: PR #107 bounded `pool.acquire`, PR #108 per-poll `asyncio.wait_for` watchdog, XML-RPC socket timeouts. Recovery = restart the workers pod. A synchronous-blocking `_poll_cycle` (PyMySQL with no yield) starves other workers on the shared loop (contrast: use `await asyncio.to_thread(_poll_cycle)`; live 2026-05-29 the sync worker stalled ~95 pending while a blocking cycle ran).
- **Supervisor:** runs every 30s (`SUPERVISE_INTERVAL_SECONDS`), restarts dead or heartbeat-wedged workers, and now SKIPS config-disabled workers (e.g. `ORDER_QUEUE_WORKER_ENABLED=false`, `EMAIL_ENABLED=false`). History: before that fix it logged ERROR "Worker X task is not running — restarting" every 30s for them (the 2026-06-01 log spam) — not an outage. A "not running — restarting" line today means an ENABLED worker died.
- Workers are gated **individually** (`ENVIRONMENT=production` does not turn them off): `ODOO_SYNC_WORKER_ENABLED` (default False; the name is legacy — it gates the `serpy_sync_worker`), `EMAIL_ENABLED` (True), `ORDER_QUEUE_WORKER_ENABLED` (False by default, the WHOLE gate for the order-queue worker since 2026-09-11; on prod set true in the `serp-env` Secret and overridden false inline on `serp-workers`), `CRON_RUNNER_ENABLED` (True — fires `@cron` jobs). A test env carrying an old `.env` can accidentally run serp_sync and crons.
- **Crons** (`backend/workers/crons.py`, `serp_cron` rows one per env — `production`/`dev`/`local` — keyed by `cron_key` with an `active` flag; in `dev` only the two WCC crons are active): Refresh Move Line Costs (every 3 min; **`active=0` on production** as of 2026-09-29), Approval Reminder Digest, Pack-Tomorrow Daily Alert, Weekly Adjustment Summary, Core SKU Daily Report, Not-Reserved Daily Report (seeded paused), Add Retool Incremental Tables (legacy name — nightly rebuild of `sku_product_type_key`, `product_type_key_sizes`, `buyer_products_copy`, `bc_size_location_split` **in `serp_app`**), Update Projections, Data Assumptions Verifier, Check Component Orders, Auto-vacuum (2am MT), Kits Monitor (every 2h, posts one combined `#reserve-order` alert), Odoo Parity Daily Report (SERP's replacement for Odoo's not-reserved-products + failed-orders email digests → `#serp-alerts`), WCC Finale Snapshot, WCC VineBox Shipments, Google Sheet Export (hourly rebuild of the former Odoo `sw_excel_update` sheet from SERP tables; Google write gated on a service-account credential). **RETIRED (`active=0`):** reserve-order (superseded by Kits Monitor `recipe_coverage`), `darklaunch-drift`, `publish-serp-kits`, `reconcile-published-stock` (drift package deleted; the two publish rebuild bodies now live in the order-queue worker).

#### Order ingest & the Serpy sync queue (post-cutover)

Two live paths, plus a retired third:

**1. `serp_order_queue_worker.py` — the SERP order-ingest path (LIVE).** Ingests Laravel `ec_order` → `serp_sale_order` (~1,905 orders since 2026-09-27, all `odoo_id` NULL) and creates the delivery pickings, writing `serp_app`/`serp_test` ONLY (never Laravel, never Odoo). **NO queue table.** Detection reads Laravel through `laravel_routed_pool` and is scoped by per-order **SERP flags**, not timestamps or a cutover gate: new/cancelled → `ec_order.serp_sync = 0`; shipped → `serp_sync = 1 AND ship_date_serp_synchronized = 0`. Gap-safe (a late order is found whenever it flips to 0) and needs no id stamper. `ec_order.serp_sync` values (since 9/26: 1 synced ~1,919, 0 pending ~68, 4 ~90, 5 ~8; `2` = Odoo-era/backfill slot the worker never writes, still counted as unsynced demand) — it is **load-bearing for inventory correctness** (see the demand view under Laravel). Bypass-Odoo product types (`product_type.bypass_odoo = 1`: gift cards, vendor drop-ship) are excluded on every detector path. It also owns rebuilding `serp_published_kits`/`serp_published_stock` (`drain_kits_publish_requests` + a stock reconcile pass calling `rebuild_published_stock`; the runs write straight into `laravel_live`). Published tables are rebuilt inside this worker (stock reconcile every ~600s, kits every ~3600s, `serp_publish_kits_requests` queue); `serp_published_kits` covers 635 kits / 174 buyer products, computed from `serp_*` tables. **The order-queue pod is the SINGLE writer of `serp_published_stock`**: its new-order drain commits SERP reservations and flips `serp_sync=1` + the publish in one Laravel transaction under an in-process `PUBLISH_HANDOFF_LOCK`, so any other writer (e.g. a Serpy-draft stock republish in `serp-workers`) must enqueue a row in `serp_app.serp_publish_requests` (kits: `serp_publish_kits_requests`) for the queue to apply at a chunk boundary — a direct write elsewhere reopens the reserved-AND-unsynced double-count window (`workers/published_stock.py`). Queue observability tables in `serp_app`: `order_queue_activity`, `order_queue_backlog_snapshot`, `order_queue_failure`, `order_queue_quarantine` (read by the order-queue admin, `crud/crud_order_queue_admin.py`). The file's own docstring still says "darklaunch worker" — stale wording; only the darklaunch framing is retired, not the worker. Runs in its own Deployment (`serp-order-queue-worker`). Detectors: `detect_pending_orders` (one pass returning kinds `new` / `cold_cancel` / `hot_cancel`) and `detect_shipped_orders`; cancellations apply via `process_cancellation`/`process_cold_cancellation` in `workers/order_processing.py`. Detection is oldest-first (`ORDER BY ec.created_at ASC, ec.order_id ASC`; preselect `po.created_at ASC, po.id ASC`) with env-tunable caps: `ORDER_QUEUE_DETECTION_LIMIT` (default 5000, new + cold-cancel), `ORDER_QUEUE_SHIPMENT_LIMIT` (50), poll `ORDER_QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_SECONDS` (default 300); repeat failures quarantine an order (threshold 3, TTL 6h). (History: `ORDER BY … DESC LIMIT 50` starvation bugs, fixed.) **Cancellation policy (Jack, 2026-06-16):** cancel decisions must NOT depend on Odoo state; replacement rule "don't ship orders more than 1 year old". Receiver vs preselect routing splits on `serp_sale_order.ec_order_id` vs `preselect_order_id` (history: the Odoo mapping used an `sw_id` discriminator, `< 600,000,000` = receiver/ec_order, `>= 600,000,000` = preselect/wholesale).
  - Custom-branding **merchandise** orders are SERP-native (Jason's Feb 8 2026 decision: 100% SERP, never Odoo); the separate merchandise `order_queue_worker.py` was removed and `component_orders.inventory_source` is no longer read (the column, like `components.inventory_source`, is now a `(deleted)`-renamed column — WW-2994).

**2. Serpy sync queue** — table **`serpy_sync_queue`** in `serp_app` (env-routed; `odoo_sync_queue_live` was its old name; Retool-PG `odoo_sync_queue*` copies are FROZEN since 2026-06-26 — Retool is NOT the sync engine anymore). Worker `workers/serpy_sync_worker.py`, package `services/serpy_sync_queue/` (`odoo_sync_worker.py` / `services/odoo_sync_queue/` are gone). Triggered when a Serpy draft is approved. **Every op since the cutover routes to a `serp:<entity>` handler** and writes the SERP DB; `sync_target` values live since 9/26 are `serp`, `multi`, `laravel` (the last `odoo` target was created 2026-09-25). Columns include `entity_type, entity_id, operation, payload, status, attempts, max_attempts, next_attempt_at, error_message/details, odoo_id, odoo_response, priority, idempotency_key, sync_target`. Polls every **15s** (not ~30s), `BATCH_SIZE=1`, priority ASC then created_at ASC then id; **a draft's ops run strictly in order** (`_draft_order_gate`, 2026-09-26: a row can't be claimed while an earlier row of the same draft is pending/processing/failed — retry or cancel the failed row to release the rest; identical ops in one enqueue get `#n` idempotency-key suffixes); each cycle is bounded by `CYCLE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=180`. **No automatic retry**: a failed item goes to `failed` and needs a manual retry; only transient DB races (deadlock/serialization that rolled back) are re-queued `pending` after a flat 5s backoff until `max_attempts` (default 5). A persistent wedge (3 consecutive cycle timeouts) or a burst of consecutive errors makes the loop back off 300s and alert. **No DLQ state exists.** A **circuit breaker** (CLOSED/OPEN/HALF_OPEN, 5-failure threshold, 30s reset) halts processing on repeated failure. Statuses: `pending`/`processing`/`synced`/`partial`/`failed`/`cancelled` (no `dlq`). Handlers live in `backend/workers/handlers/` (`serp_orm/` — stock, purchasing, manufacturing, product, sales, vendors, landed_cost, chatter — plus `laravel.py`, `prelist_ledger.py`). **PO receipts flow through here**, not the order worker. Side effects that used to live only in the Odoo handlers were re-implemented SERP-side on 2026-09-26 — notably the **prelist drain** (`prelist_ledger.drain_prelist_qty`: after an MO completes or a positive inventory adjustment, decrement Laravel `receiver_products.prelist_qty` with a `GREATEST(…,0)` clamp and log to `serp_app.prelist_qty_log`; best-effort, never fails the sync). The Odoo XML-RPC handler arms and `/queue-test` were **deleted** 2026-09-26 (`90e712f39`); what remains is `services/odoo_xmlrpc_service.py` as a read-only client used only by the seeder's chatter-attachment fetch, the `ODOO_*` config, the legacy `ODOO_SYNC_WORKER_ENABLED` name, `workers/odoo_parity_reports/` and `serpy/ops/schemas/odoo/` (`ODOO_SYNC_DRY_RUN` is gone).
  - ❌ AI assumes items stuck 'processing' need manual reset → ✅ `recover_stuck_items()` runs at startup and every ~5 min (20 polls) for items `processing` > 5 min: orphans of a dead worker (transaction rolled back) are reset to `pending`; items whose cycle timed out or whose handler stamped `handler_committed` go to `failed` so a human verifies the outcome before retrying.
  - Admin view: `/api/admin/sync-queue/*` (legacy "Odoo" name).

**3. Retired:** the merchandise order-queue worker (removed), and the darklaunch shadow-write pipeline (below).

**Target state, NOT done (owner Manish; 9/29 SERP Touchbase):** drop the `serp_*` tables from `laravel_live` except `serp_published_kits`/`serp_published_stock`; remove inventory transactions + deprecated columns + related Laravel code; remove the Odoo inbound/outbound sidecars and SERP's Odoo connection code. Laravel `main` still carries the dead Odoo code (`app/Sidecar/SynchronizeOdooInventory.php`, `OdooInventoryService.php`, `Http/Controllers/Odoo/**`). Live check 2026-09-29: `laravel_live` still holds 70 `serp_*` BASE TABLES (near-empty: 19 `serp_sale_order`, 0 POs, 3 `serp_stock_quant`, 439 `serp_mrp_bom`) plus `serp_published_stock` (~4,865 rows) / `serp_published_kits` (~635 rows) as BASE TABLES.

### Serpy — The AI Ops Agent

- **AI inventory-ops agent** (Slack bot `SERPY` / `SERPY Dev`, user `U096P936NQ7`); code in `backend/serpy/`. NOT a dev experiment, NOT a typo for SERP, NOT a human. ❌ AI reads SERPY Slack messages as written by a human.
- Ops describe inventory changes in plain English (Slack `/serpy` or web UI `serp.sugarwish.com/serpy/<draft_id>`) → structured JSON ops → human approval (enforced by `config.py`) → applied to **SERP** (and Laravel where the op family says so) via the sync queue. Drafts post to **`#inventorymanagement`** (`C03G8LP36P6`) for approval — except drafts made up ONLY of PO-lifecycle ops, whose approval cards go to **`#po-updates`** (`C0C3DR7AW7Q`, `SLACK_PO_OPS_CHANNEL`, `resolve_approval_channel`, live since 2026-09-21); "don't change anything about who can approve serpy." **Approvals are enforced in `config.py`:** `SERPY_APPROVAL_RULES` (`po_receiving` + `po_creation` → James Emeric only) and `SERPY_THRESHOLD_APPROVAL_RULES` (inventory adjustments/scraps netting > ±500 units → Matthew Patrick, no admin bypass). There is no `serp_approval_thresholds` table in `serp_app` (no approval table at all), and PO gate columns (`ready_for_approval_at`, `ops_approved_at`, …) do not exist in `serp_app` — design intent only.
- Pipeline: user message → `classify_intent` → `find_products_with_locations` → `propose_operations` → **DRAFT** (`serpy_drafts`; was `serp_draft_operations`/`_live`) → `POST /save-raw-draft` (validates against `OpTypeRegistry` JSON schemas, `serpy/ops/types.py`) → `POST /ai-submit` (DRAFT→PENDING_APPROVAL, Slack "Operations Request", enqueue) → human approval → **`serpy_sync_queue`** (`serp_app`) → `serpy_sync_worker` (~15s poll). Image transcription happens in `serpy/agent/transcribe.py` via a vision pass BEFORE the agent loop (the agent never sees image bytes).
- **Not every SERP write is a Serpy draft:** the SERP UI cart's plain **Submit** (and chatter Log note / Send message) executes directly via `POST /api/inventory/moves/serp-execute` → `execute_ops_direct` through the SERP ORM — same validation as save-raw-draft + ai-submit, but **no draft, no queue row, no Slack**, with a `Direct-<hex>` reference instead of a draft id and an idempotency guard (PR #1361). Batches over the approval threshold are refused there (400) and must go through a Serpy draft. So `serpy_sync_queue` is NOT a complete ledger of SERP-UI edits. The `po_send_email` op was removed 2026-09-26 (PO email goes through the PO page's compose modal).
- Lifecycle: `DRAFT → PENDING_APPROVAL → APPROVED → EXECUTED`. Drafts are numbered ("Draft #860"). **Nothing hits the DB until approved.** Drafts are **per-user**, keyed by `serpy_drafts.user_id` = `serp_res_users.id` (e.g. 55 = jack@sugarwish.com; historically `serp_users.id`, a bridge dropped 2026-09-26), not Slack id.
- **Op routing is per-op-type, hardcoded.** **Op types were renamed 2026-09-26 (PR #1487):** the 38 `odoo_*` types dropped the prefix (`odoo_update_bom` → `update_bom`, `create_odoo_product` → `create_product`, `odoo_update_mo_date_finished` → `update_mo_date_finished`, entity `odoo_vendor_bill` → `vendor_bill`) and the kit types dropped `serp_` (`serp_create_kit`/`serp_update_kit`/`serp_archive_kit` → `create_kit`/`update_kit`/`archive_kit`); the old names stay registered as aliases (`serpy/ops/registry.py`), so old drafts/queue rows still resolve — expect BOTH spellings in history. All apply to **SERP** via `serp:` handlers; `laravel_*` ops (`laravel_update_component`, `laravel_update_receiver_product`) write Laravel. Also create_product, inventory adjustment, transfer, archive, swap; a single op can write multiple systems. `update_product` is archive/unarchive only (the handler writes just `active`). **`update_mo_date_finished`** edits a done MO's completion date; MO-**creation** requires a packed/MO date (`date_finished`, `YYYY-MM-DD` Mountain Time) which Serpy asks for via `request_user_choice` (last 3 calendar days as buttons + "Another date"). `sync_target` is a DRAFT-level column (`serp`/`multi`/`laravel` live; historically `odoo`/`serp`/`both`); per-operation routing exists in the backend but lacks a frontend dropdown.
- In `x/y synced`: `y` = total ops, `x` = applied to **SERP**; **a partial count = SERP-side validation rejection, NOT an infrastructure failure** (pre-cutover it meant Odoo-side rejection).
- "Replace SKU" = **kit component swap** (remove old + add new across every kit), NOT archive-old + activate-new.
- Guards fire on **structured facts** (`images_present`, classifier `op_types`, `has_replayed_image`), NOT semantic overlap with user text; embeddings may add long-tail examples but **never gate/drop** a guard rule.
- **Op rules live in the DB, not code (SOT flip 2026-07-21, PRs #595/#600):** `SERPY_OP_RULES_FROM_DB=true` on prod and serp-test — the loader reads active rule chunks from the DB (`serpy_op_rules` in `serp_app`, edit history in `serpy_op_rule_history`; **69 active** as of 2026-09-29; volatile; `MergedRegistry`; no fail-open). Built-in always-on guards (`frame.base`, `never_guess`, `phantom_bom_routing`, …) still come from code; op-scope rules come from the DB. **Editing the prompt/rules in code does nothing for op rules** — edit live via the `/serpy-rules` UI, durable via a `gen_rule_edit` migration; a drift guard keeps code↔DB aligned. Per-turn selection telemetry (`SERPY_PERSIST_CHUNK_SELECTION=true`) fills the "Rules used this turn" panel (records the used `chunk_selection`).
- **Provenance floors** (anything before its path's go-live can't be Serpy): `product_template` path **2026-03-24**, `create_product` **2026-04-13**, `create_receiver_product_everywhere` (SA- path) **2026-05-05**. `create_uid=55` (jack@sugarwish.com) is NOT a reliable Serpy signal (covers Serpy AND Jack's manual edits); the authoritative ledger for approved drafts is `serpy_sync_queue` (SERP-UI direct submits leave no queue row — check the document's chatter) (pre-2026-06-26 history: Retool `odoo_sync_queue_live`; pre-9/26 Odoo-side effects live in the frozen Odoo DB).
- **Serpy table namespace (renamed 2026-08-31; old `_live`/`_dev` suffix scheme is GONE):** `serpy_drafts`, `serpy_messages`, `serpy_turns`, `serpy_prompt_logs`, `serpy_sync_queue`, plus `serp_physical_counts`. Prod vs serp-dev separation is by the env-routed pool's target database, not by table name.

### Supplier Forecast (Teal Sidebar) — Isolated Multi-DB Subsystem

- **Out of scope for `serp_orm`.** Uses `asyncpg`/SQLAlchemy + raw SQL under `backend/services/forecast/`, REST routers `routers/forecast/`. NOT the `env.cr`/ORM-RPC pattern.
- Reads via `ForecastDatabaseConnector` (`backend/utils/database_connectors.py`): `laravel_live` MySQL (active SA SKUs + SA inventory, SSH tunnel) and **`serp_app`** for everything else — BOM SA→RM + RM inventory (the leg that formerly read Odoo PG) AND the forecast config/cache tables that used to be Retool PG (`size_projections_copy`, `sku_projections`, `week_settings`, `mix_predictions`, `projected_orders`, `product_type_key_sizes`, `sku_product_type_key`, `bc_size_location_split`, `redemption_distribution`, `sku_costs`, `sku_equivalency_ratios`, and the supplier config `supplier`/`supplier_buffer`/`sku_supplier`/`rm_sku_supplier` — repointed 2026-08-10, `baf0fb4af`). `execute_serp_app_query`/`execute_serp_app_write` are named-`:param` SQLAlchemy/asyncmy (not asyncpg positional); `execute_retool_query` still exists but has no non-test callers, and there is no Odoo leg. `services/forecast/repointable_queries.py` is now SERP-only (cost-tracker `sku_costs`, inventory-variance `sales`/`beginning_inventory` run on the env-routed SERP pool via `services.datasource_switch.run_serp_query`); the Odoo twins and `read_repointable` dispatch were removed at cutover.
- Two-tier cache (L1 in-process LRU + L2 Redis — in-cluster Redis on K3s, plain TCP via `REDIS_URL`; the pre-cutover `rediss://` ElastiCache is gone). Stale-while-revalidate at 80% TTL. Per-source circuit breakers. TTLs: static 600s, dynamic 300s, volatile 120s.
- **Consolidated:** orchestration lives entirely in `SupplierForecastPipeline` (`services/forecast/service.py`), which every forecast endpoint (`/forecast`, `/forecast/inventory`, `/forecast/suppliers`, `/forecast/export`) routes through; the old two-orchestration-path footgun is gone.
- Rule: add new forecast logic in `services/forecast/` (not ORM); add a **new pipeline+endpoint** rather than mutating the default. `globals.css` doesn't hot-reload — per-page CSS goes in a `<style>` block in the `.tsx`. Live PO email sending is NOT yet enabled on live SERP (James using normal email for Q4 POs, 9/28); PO-approval gates are not wired as columns (see Serpy approvals). Planned (9/28, James): fold supplier lead time / weeks-on-hand / order frequency from the forecast Supplier Settings page into the SERP vendor config and retire that page — not done.

### Odoo — FROZEN (read-only history since 2026-09-26)

- **Odoo 15 (v15.0.1.3)** on **Odoo.sh** under Seth's account — NOT self-hosted; prod host `sethfinley-sugarwish-odoo-main-5932805.dev.odoo.com:5432` (`sugarwish.odoo.com` UI; staging `sethfinley-sugarwish-odoo-staging-new-*.dev.odoo.com`, **URL/creds change on every rebuild** so cached strings go stale). **330s statement timeout** and **NO direct shell access** (Web UI, Server Actions/Python, XML-RPC/JSON-RPC, custom wizards).
- **Nothing writes to Odoo any more** (last write 2026-09-25 21:12). Its `ir.cron` jobs were turned off at cutover, so Odoo no longer drives orders. Treat the `odoo`/`odoo_staging` MCP DBs as **historical reference, stale for anything after 9/26**; investigate discrepancies in `serp_app`. Frozen snapshot: ~584 tables, ~5,800 active products, ~290 partners, ~2,600–2,700 locations, ~1,900 POs, ~2,078 BOMs, ~26,000+ MOs, ~13.4M `stock_valuation_layer` rows.
- **Pre-cutover mechanics (history):**
  - Odoo was a **puller, not a receiver**: `ir.cron` jobs pulled orders from Laravel's REST API (~6 min); Laravel never pushed. All imported SOs booked under one catch-all partner `sugarwish_customer` / id **94** "SW Customer" — real customer identity lived entirely in Laravel.
  - 27 active crons: "Sugarwish: Update Orders" (6 min), #26 "Update Failed Orders" (10 min), "Update Prepicks" (120 min), "Update Failed Prepicks" (15 min), "Re-Update Movelines Cost" (3 min), "Reset Reupdated Zerocost Movelines" (3 min), #57 "Send Failed Orders Email" (daily ~14:15, recipient Carolyn → routed to Nora Stein/Kellen Evans; dumps `failed_products_log` then unlinks), hourly Google Sheet exports (`sw_excel_update` → Sales→"Live", Purchase→"Purchase", Inventory→"Inventory" tabs of Sheet `1ESYinwhFztJfzo0Aec3BwafS9d7cHxWTUhh9vpVGGJA`; errors logged not raised = silent failures — SERP's Google Sheet Export cron now rebuilds the same sheet). The Laravel-side flag was the misspelled `oddo_synchronized` (now `(deleted)`-renamed).
  - Custom modules (`sugarwish-odoo`, Seth's repo; branch `staging_new` → `main`): main bridge **`sugarwish_integration`** (depends on `sale_stock_picking`, `mrp`, `sw_api_logs`), `purchase_features`, `sales_features`, `stock_features`, `mrp_features`, `pr_vendor_product_automation`, `sw_reports`/`sw_excel_update`, `odoo_logger`, `prixite_customization`. Built by Hamza Khan Niazi / Prixite's offshore team through mid-2025 (contacts Manish Chaudhary, Zain Arshad; relationship strained by May 2025 — a driver of SERP); 2026 SERP-era changes by Jack. Manish now owns the post-cutover cleanup. The Odoo v15→v17 upgrade (~Oct 2026 deadline) is **moot**.
- **Odoo rejects** (relevant when reading/querying the frozen DB or reproducing behavior): negated predicates (`NOT IN`/`!=`/`<>`) on indexed state columns of multi-million-row tables (use positive IN-lists); quant creation on consumable/service products; private `_`-prefixed methods over XML-RPC; UoM category mismatches; missing mandatory fields on remote create.
