### Laravel — E-Commerce & the Order Domain

**`sugarwish-laravel`** is the main e-commerce app and order domain (orders, products, giftcards). `laravel_live` is the production app DB and still co-hosts a thin, near-empty `serp_*` bridge.

- **`laravel_live`** (MySQL, AWS RDS `database-1.cqqg1tfyyubp.us-east-1`, read over SSH tunnel via user `replit-read-9173`): ~4.92M `giftcards_card`, ~4.01M `ec_order`, ~150K `company`; holds `receiver_products`, `components`, `kits`, `component_kits`, `ec_order`, `items`, `giftcards_card`. **CRITICAL: `laravel_live` is NOT "the SERP database."** Its 70 `serp_*` tables are near-empty (19 `serp_sale_order`, 0 POs) — NOT live SERP data (that is `serp_app`) — except two BASE TABLES that ARE live: `serp_published_kits` / `serp_published_stock`, which the order-queue worker publishes. **Kits/BOMs:** `laravel_live.serp_mrp_bom` + SerpBomService on-order kit explosion is the intended current SoT for kits (don't "fix"); a zero/small `serp_app` kit count is the wrong DB, not absence. Normal BOMs live in `serp_app.serp_mrp_bom`.
- **`manage`** (MySQL, Laravel **STAGING**, renamed 2026-05-11 from "laravel staging"): near-identical schema to `laravel_live` (~8% of prod: 396K giftcards vs 4.9M); already on Hetzner. It is serp-dev's "live" DB, so serp-dev's Laravel writes (Serpy `laravel_*` ops) land here — prod SERP's land in `laravel_live` (`laravel_routed_pool`: production→`laravel_live`, dev→`manage`, local→`laravel_local`).
- **Inventory views in `laravel_live`:** `receiver_product_inventory` (finished goods) and `component_product_inventory` (RM twin) are **VIEWs** (9 columns incl. `sellable_qty`; holds & prelist literal `0` for components so `sellable_qty == available_qty`), fed from `serp_published_stock`; readers are moving onto `receiver_product_inventory.sellable_qty`. **The demand leg tests `ec_order.serp_sync` (IN (0,2)), NEVER `serp_sale_order`** (a `NOT EXISTS` against that ~19-row stub matches every order, driving `sellable_qty` negative and auto-disabling the catalog). Demand window is 30 days. `manage` may lag/lead `laravel_live` on the sync-flag swap and perf indexes — check which env you are reading; staging-first is the normal steady state. `receiver_products.inventory_qty`, `odoo_inventory` ("West Coast Qty"), `mi_inventory_qty`, `inventory_updated_at`, `sw_inventory_updated_at`, `components.inventory_quantity`/`inventory_source`/`mi_inventory_qty` and `component_orders.inventory_source` are all `(deleted)`-renamed columns — the old names throw "Unknown column". Sellable = view `receiver_product_inventory` (sellable = available + prelist_qty; unsynced demand = `serp_sync IN (0,2)` over 30 days; `reservation_hold_qty` from `inventory_reservations`). The Odoo→Laravel `SynchronizeOdooInventory` sidecar (a Lambda via XML-RPC, sharding by `product_id % 10`) is dead with Odoo frozen; its removal is part of the planned cleanup.
- **Branch/deploy:** feature branches off `development`; `SUG-XXXXXXXXX:` ticket prefix (also `WW-*`). Flow: feature → `development` → `manage` (staging) → `blue` (production; "merge blue to main for release"). Devs: Subash (Laravel-track), Parish (SWAC-track), Manish (lead; Odoo-era, now post-cutover cleanup owner), Bilal (manage/blue pushes; did the Laravel 11 upgrade).
- **SERP↔Laravel DB access:** user **`SERP-readfull-writepartial-7161`** — FULL READ, PARTIAL WRITE (INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE only on `receiver_products`, `kits`, `component_kits`, `buyer_products`; table-by-table grants gated by Serpy approval). Intentionally NOT full-write.
- **Laravel→Odoo pull (HISTORY, replaced by `serp_order_queue_worker`):** Odoo's crons authenticated to Laravel `/api/odoo/*` endpoints (Bearer token, middleware `odoo.api`): `GET /api/odoo` (receiver orders), `GET /api/odoo/pre-pick`, `GET /api/odoo/components/ecard`, `POST /api/odoo/update-order-passed`, `POST /api/odoo/update-order-failed`, `PUT /api/odoo` (inventory). Laravel had no XML-RPC client. Now orders reach SERP via the order-queue worker above; the endpoints are slated for removal with the Odoo sidecars.

### WishDesk / SWAC — CS, CRM, Design, Billing

**SWAC** (GitHub `jasonbkiefer/SWAC`, description "SugarWish Activity Coordinator") **IS WishDesk** — same React + Express + TypeScript + Drizzle + MySQL codebase; one codebase serves both the admin/support console and customer-facing proposal/receiver flows. ❌ AI takes the description literally → ✅ it's the CS/CRM platform (live chat, tickets, knowledge base, AI workflows, proposal management). ❌ AI conflates the three "Wish" systems → ✅ **WishDesk** = CS product (internally "WishWorks"); **WishWorks** = in-house WW-#### ticket tracker (via WishBot; `desk.sugarwish.com/admin/wishworks/tickets`, introduced 2026-03-12, replaced glitch reports); **SWIRL** = company knowledge repo. All three are separate from Jack's sw-cortex.

| Subdomain             | Env        | Branch        | DB                                                              |
| --------------------- | ---------- | ------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `desk.sugarwish.com`  | production | `live`        | `sugarwish_wishdesk` (RDS)                                      |
| `desk3.sugarwish.com` | staging    | `staging`     | ⚠️ **points at LIVE DBs** — not isolated                        |
| `desk2.sugarwish.com` | dev        | `development` | `sugarwish_wishdesk_dev` + `manage` dev DB (auto-deploys)       |

- Branch flow: feature → `development` → `staging` → `live` (**not `main`**), branch naming `<username>/<desc>`, tickets `WD-*`/`WW-*`. CI/CD via Jenkins (`ciservice.sugarwish.com/job/sw-deploy-to-wishdesk/`). **Parish Shrestha** = release engineer/lead (merges nearly all SWAC PRs; confirmations in #devgroup_wishdesk); Madison Parks is a developer; Payton Castaneda and Jaypee contribute.
- Two DB pools: `server/db.ts` = WishDesk DB; `server/sugarwish-db.ts` = Sugarwish DB (the Mug Image Processor creates its OWN MySQL pools). **All timestamps are Mountain Time, NOT UTC.**
- Auth: **cookie-based sessions, NOT JWT** (Bearer only for curl/API); local dev needs `ENABLE_LOCAL_AUTH_BYPASS=true` + `APP_ENV=local`, or login `admin` / `swdev123`. Route auth: `isAgentOrAdmin` (most admin routes; **`agent` role ≠ admin**), `isAdmin`, `isAuthenticated`. Socket.IO needs sticky sessions with load balancers (except WebSocket-only transport).
- SWAC owns sleeve **resolution** (`server/services/sleeve-resolution.ts` writes `branding_records.physical_branding`); actual PDF imposition/printing is **livery**.
- **SWAC MCP server** (port ~3001, `/mcp`; ONE server, not per-env) must run as its own PM2 app — a recurring Oct–Nov 2025 bug: Jenkins deploy didn't restart it, so desk2 served stale code (23 tools vs 28 local); standing rule was to also ask Seth to restart it; fixed by moving to PM2.
- **WishDesk is downstream, not a master.** ❌ AI expects customer/order master data in WishDesk → ✅ truth is in SugarWish (Laravel) and now SERP; WishDesk stores IDs + cached snapshots. 3-tier flow: WishDesk → reads → Sugarwish DB ← syncs ← Laravel API. WishDesk does NOT integrate with Odoo/SERP directly; it sees order state via Sugarwish fields (`odoo_id`, `bypass_odoo` — legacy names; the Odoo flags `items.odoo_sync` and `ship_date_odoo_synchronized` are now `(deleted)`-renamed, replaced by `serp_sync`/`ship_date_serp_synchronized`).
- Cross-system links: `sw_user_id`/`users.sw_id`/`user_cache.user_id` = SugarWish (Laravel) CUSTOMER id; `sw_company_id`/`company_id` = corporate account id; `linked_order_id`/`order_ids`/`ecard_ids`/`wishlink_ids` (JSON) = SugarWish order/ecard/wishlink ids.
- **Integrations:** Gmail (`swcrm_z_gmail_*`, 20+ tables mirroring reps' mailboxes with AI reply drafts), RingCentral (`swcrm_ringcentral_*`), **Qdrant** collections (kb-v2, instructions, draft-instructions, agent-chats, kb-internal, discoveries_swirl, org-knowledge; OpenAI `text-embedding-3-small`). **SWIM** = WishDesk-embedded AI chatbot on kb-v2; live chat BOT→AGENT handoff (sessions start BOT/PENDING → "Request Agent" → AGENT/PENDING_AGENT → Slack #cs_wishdesk_chats → agent accepts). CRM backbone = polymorphic **`swcrm_links`** junction (bidirectional; `role` carries semantics). **Design config is full-overwrite synced from `sw-design`** — direct WishDesk edits to genies/boxes/sleeves are wiped.

### Retool — Not SERP-Owned, Not a Source of Truth

**Retool PostgreSQL** is an analytics/ops scratch + cache + config layer: a shared multi-app "Frankenstein" DB (~165 public tables; ~40 SERP-owned). Retool has been observed **overwriting `updated_at`** — don't trust it as a change timestamp. It NO LONGER hosts SERP's sync engine, auth bridge, or forecast data (all moved to `serp_app`), and SERP runs without Retool creds (2026-09-28). SERP's remaining Retool reads are narrow: fulfillment-throughput hours (`hours_wages_daily`), a few data-assumptions checks, and the `retool-db` health probe.

- **Frozen legacy copies:** `odoo_sync_queue`/`_dev`/`_live`, `odoo_sync_circuit_breaker*`, `odoo_sync_stats`, `serp_draft_operations`/`_live` (frozen since 2026-06-26).
- **Still in Retool but no longer SERP's copy:** the Retool versions of the forecast tables (`size_projections_copy`, `sku_projections`, `week_settings`, `product_type_key_sizes`, `sku_product_type_key`, `mix_predictions`, `projected_orders`, `bc_size_location_split`, `sku_costs`, `sku_equivalency_ratios`, `supplier`, `supplier_buffer`, `rm_sku_supplier`) — SERP reads and writes the `serp_app` copies, so a Retool row can be stale; `sa_projections_cache`, `rm_weekly_demand_cache`, `supplier_permissions`, `serp_approval_thresholds`, `serp_audit_logs` and the AI-observability tables (`serp_ai_messages_*`, `serp_ai_turns_*`, `serp_ai_prompt_logs`/`_live`, `serp_ai_prompts` — superseded by `serp_app` `serpy_*`) have no SERP code readers. Also mostly-stale mock tables (`quickbooks_dashboard`, `stripe_dashboard`, `shopifymonthlydata`, `mock_*` — all-text numerics, 2022 dates). ❌ AI treats QuickBooks/Stripe/Shopify tables as live transactional sources → ✅ reporting/reconciliation mirrors; sources are external.
- **Migrated to `serp_app` MySQL** (Hetzner, `SERP_APP_LIVE_DB_PARAMS['db']='serp_app'`, `backend/pools/serp_live.py` → `serp_live_pool` / `get_sync_serp_app_connection()` (the pool was `serp_app_live_pool` in `serp_app_live.py` originally); migrations `serp_app/migrations/*.sql` auto-run by `deploy-k8s.sh`): the auth bridge `serp_users` (hard-swapped with no fallback, so **`serp_app` availability gates logins**; later dropped 2026-09-26 together with `serp_user_counting_locations` — neither exists in `serp_app` now; Retool `serp_users` is legacy), `sku_supplier` (SA supplier metadata; `crud_forecast_suppliers.get_sa_supplier_metadata()`; `sku_supplier.supplier` column dropped in the move), `wine_category_goals` (`crud_wine_goals.py`) — both via `forecast_db_connector.execute_serp_app_query`; the counting tables are in `serp_app`: `serp_physical_counts` (successor of `serp_inventory_counts`, whose `_backup`/`_live_backup` copies remain), `serp_vendor_inventory_counts`, `serp_beginning_inventory_snapshots`. Check live code before assuming a `serp_*` app table is still in Retool. **serp_app SQL gotcha:** the live pool is **pymysql, not SQLAlchemy `text()`** — positional `%s` is correct and the `text()` landmines (bare `%`, `:word`) don't apply, but every migrated query was a hand-translated PG→MySQL rewrite (the forecast connector's `execute_serp_app_query` is the NAMED-param asyncmy path).
- The former **live/non-live suffix convention** (`_live` prod vs base/`_dev` local, centralized in `settings.SERP_APP_TABLES_ARE_LIVE` by PR #344 — true only for `ENVIRONMENT=production`; the env-suffixed sites `ai_table_name`, `_messages_table`/`_turns_table`, `_get_table_names` and `INVENTORY_COUNTS_TABLE` route through it) is superseded for Serpy tables by the `serpy_*` rename + DB-level routing; Retool's own suffixed tables still exist as frozen legacy.
- **Retool query timeout (June 2026):** 30s engine abort + JS wall-clock guard. MySQL: `SET SESSION max_execution_time = 30000`; PostgreSQL: `SET statement_timeout = 30000` (NOT via startup parameter — "unsupported startup parameter").

### n8n — Automation

**n8n** is self-hosted (`n8n.sugarwish.com`, v1.78.1); exports in `workflows/n8n/` are snapshots ("active" flag unreliable; ids differ from live — check the live instance). All post as bot user "n8n" (`U08QP0DL9L5`). Pattern: DB node(s) + Code node + IF gate + Slack alert. **n8n workflows intentionally write directly to production** (sanctioned, separate from read-only MCP DB access; via `sw_live_creds`/Retool credentials). **Alert fatigue is real** — most red-alert posts are auto-resolved noise (Seth: "No action on data/APIs should be necessary from alerts in #api-autofix").

- **Disabled at the cutover (live list 2026-09-29, all `active=false`, tagged "Disabled During SERP Cutover"):** Odoo Sync Error Handler, Odoo Order Sync Integrity Monitor (the successor of the old "Odoo Sync Monitor / Issue Detector `bR4rEQjFI3GuwkiY`", which is no longer in the instance), Sync Receiver Product Inventory with Odoo, Odoo - inventory in wrong location, Disable Unreserved Products V2, Core SKU Live Warnings (sellable_qty) WW-2451. Also inactive: Darklaunch Compare (`HpHN9Reme3L6bNBd`), Odoo - Import Missing Orders, Publish Serp Kits, Live Product Warnings, Daily Live Products Message, Core SKU Daily Report, Check Component Orders, Sheets Export, Update Projections (`IalsmpKBKbJM4LXg` — it was mislabeled as a second drift-monitor id), original Disable Unreserved Products.
- **Active cutover replacements:** `Disable Unreserved Products - Cutover` (the sanctioned prod write: `UPDATE receiver_products SET status='disabled'` for unreserved products, disable-only, never re-enables; the original ran every 1 min and was a known DEADLOCK source competing with Laravel/Retool writers), `Core SKU Live Warnings - Cutover`, `Inventory In Wrong Location - Cutover`, `Sheets Export - serp_app`, `Update Scorecards`, `Global Error Handler`. Other active fleet: Stripe reconciliation/watchdog, fraud alert, WW-3002 fixers, WW-2436/2539/2348 monitors, "Daily - FTP Ship Dates Not Synced", UptimeRobot-style health checks (desk2/Live WishDesk), etc.
- Cost-tracker workflows (`Cost_Tracker_Weekly_Average`, `Weekly_Cost_to_Scorecard`) consume SERP forecast `/api/forecast/cost-tracker/`; Daily Core SKU Report (~7am MDT) now also exists as a SERP cron; low-inventory warnings post to #live-product-warnings / #inventorymanagement at ~2x drop level; Order SKU/branding mismatch flags `-E`/`-A` mismatches (hourly).
- Slack routing: #live-product-warnings (`C084Z9EKDSL`), #ops-and-tech, #api-autofix, #jack-test (`C083M27KU8L` / `C07GY977AEM` for daily ops errors), #billing (`C4086K801`), #inventorymanagement, #serp-errors, #reserve-order (Kits Monitor); SERP's own (non-n8n) alerts: #serp-alerts (`C0C0U9B7SNP`, worker/backlog), #po-updates (`C0C3DR7AW7Q`, PO-op Serpy approvals).

### sw-design, swirl/SWIRL, and livery/SWOP

- **`sw-design`** (Jason-owned): source of truth for `design_images`, box+sleeve dims (`boxes/*/box.json` → `design_boxes`), merch recipes, Genie/router quiz configs (`genies/{key}.json`), CustomGenie quiz, builder-classic PDP, product-category sync, icon manifests. Builds → S3 (`s3://sugarwish-design/`) → WishDesk syncs in (**full-overwrite** — edits made directly in WishDesk are wiped). AI gen: Claude + Gemini + OpenAI. Clare McClaren (VP Creative & Merchandising) owns icons/design.
- **`swirl` / SWIRL** (`jasonbkiefer/SWIRL`, Jason-owned): (1) **SWIRL** = "Sugarwish Intelligence Reference Library", company-wide AI knowledge platform (markdown KB + Qdrant + discoveries + slash commands; SWIRL Bot Slack DM); (2) the **WishWorks datastore** (git-backed WW-#### tickets, auto-commits; `/ww` self-updates from this private repo + an n8n archival step; the ticket `track` field determines repo/team ownership). SEPARATE from Jack's `sw-cortex`.
- **`livery` / SWOP** ("Sugarwish Operations Platform", `csloan-sw/livery`, owner **Criston (Cris) Sloan**, Automation Engineer, Slack `U040UH4GVPX`, SERP user id 13, reports to COO Matthew Patrick): (1) warehouse **print-station backbone** (drives LogoJet printers; Mac Mini "fulfillment appliance") + MCP ops-tooling suite (`mcp-db-tool(-live)`, `mcp-slack`, `mcp-wishdesk`, `swim-kb`, `custom-shop-slip` PDF); (2) **sleeve imposition/printing** — `fulfillment/custom-shop-slip/lib/slip-data.js` `selectSleeveEntries` filters `branding_records.physical_branding.entries[]` by `ec_order.size` (actually a `buyer_products.id`), then `sleeve-pdf.js imposeBatch` maps `box_sku` → hardcoded `SKU_TRIM_TABLE` for print dims. Deploy: `feature/*`/`fix/*` → `dev` → `main` → `production` (pre-commit hook blocks direct prod commits). **CRITICAL: livery caches PDFs by orderId ONLY and NEVER invalidates on branding edits** — after a data fix click "Regenerate" (`POST /reset-status/:orderId`) then re-run generate-batch. A missing `SKU_TRIM_TABLE` key (e.g. `h_*` hot-sauce, `c_3`) throws and aborts the whole PDF (silent "no sleeve"). Debug mug/sleeve PDF issues in **livery**, not SWAC.

### Source-of-Truth Per Entity (the single biggest cross-system rule)

There is NO uniform sync direction. Ownership is split. **Post-cutover (2026-09-26):**

| Entity                                                | Owner / Source of truth                                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Normal BOMs, MOs, POs, product creation, moves        | **SERP** (`serp_app`; Serpy ops apply via `serpy_sync_queue` → `serp:` handlers). Odoo is frozen history                                       |
| Kits / phantom BOMs                                   | `laravel_live.serp_mrp_bom` + SerpBomService on-order explosion (intended SoT); Laravel `kits`/`component_kits` = Laravel-side recipe; edited via the Serpy `create_kit` / `update_kit` / `archive_kit` ops (formerly `serp_*_kit`) |
| `receiver_products` classification, `component_kits`  | **Laravel** (SERP writes it via `laravel_*` ops through `laravel_routed_pool` — `laravel_live` on prod, `manage` on serp-dev)                                                                               |
| Custom-branding merchandise (sleeves, mugs, apparel)  | **SERP only — never Odoo** (Jason, Feb 8 2026)                                                                                                |
| What's AVAILABLE to sell (SA)                         | **Laravel** — VIEW `receiver_product_inventory.sellable_qty`, fed from `serp_published_stock` (`receiver_products.inventory_qty` is a `(deleted)` column)   |
| Raw-material (RM) inventory + on-hand + valuation     | **SERP** `serp_stock_quant` / SVL in `serp_app` (Odoo only for frozen pre-cutover history)                                                    |
| Order ingest / order state                            | Laravel `ec_order` (with `serp_sync` flags) → SERP `serp_sale_order` via `serp_order_queue_worker`                                            |
| Payment status                                        | **QuickBooks** (external) — Odoo `payment_state` was 99.8% 'not_paid' and never reconciled; SERP's reconciliation engine doesn't drive `payment_state` |
| Forecast metric definitions                           | **Jason Kiefer**; canonical view is `serp.sugarwish.com/forecast/live-products`, not Retool                                                   |

Inventory has **no single source of truth**: sellable (SA) = Laravel; RM = SERP `serp_stock_quant`; accounting/valuation = `serp_app` quants/SVL.

When SERP-origin and Odoo-origin data collide on a natural key, **SERP usually wins** (users matched by email; exceptions exist).

**The `odoo_id` join rule (CRITICAL, still correct):** join cross-system/frozen-Odoo tables NEVER on `id=id` — join on **`odoo_id`**. The durable origin test is **`odoo_id IS NULL` = SERP-native, `IS NOT NULL` = Odoo-sourced** (NOT any `id >= 1B` range: Odoo-origin rows had `id=odoo_id`; SERP-runtime rows have small ids above MAX(odoo_id) with `odoo_id=NULL`; SERP-merge rows had `id>=1B`; the seeder bumped AUTO_INCREMENT to 1B during seed and reset to MAX(odoo_id)+1 — the old `id<1B` filter became wrong after finalize). Post-cutover orders in `serp_sale_order` are all `odoo_id` NULL. Child rows (stock_move, move_line, quant) got the parent stamped but left child `odoo_id=NULL`.

**Fabricated/encoded IDs (landmines):**

- `components.odoo_id` is SYNTHETIC `'800' + components.id` (varchar) — NOT a real Odoo product id; joining it to an Odoo PK matches wrong rows. The real bridge is `product_template.sugarwish_id`.
- `buyer_products.odoo_id` = `'500' + buyer_products.id` (id 42 → 50042); maps to Odoo `product_template.sugarwish_id`.
- `product_template.sugarwish_id` (THE external sync key, on the template not the variant) decodes by prefix: `'800'` → strip to get `components.id`; other int → `receiver_products.product_id` OR `buyer_products.id`; 0/NULL → no mapping.
- History: the legacy Laravel→Odoo sync flag on `ec_order` was the permanently misspelled **`oddo_synchronized`** (not `odoo_`; 0=pending, 1=synced ~3.9M, 2=in-flight, 5=vendor/special; value 3 never existed in prod). It — with `component_imported`, `ship_date_odoo_synchronized`, the `preselect_orders` odoo flags and `items.odoo_sync` — was renamed `(deleted)` at cutover; the old names now throw "Unknown column". The live ingest flag is **`ec_order.serp_sync`**, which drives SERP ingest and inventory demand.
- `ec_order.size` is misnamed — it holds a `buyer_products.id`, NOT a physical size.

**Two MCP `db` aliases to keep straight:** `laravel_live` (live SugarWish orders, with near-empty embedded `serp_*` tables + the two published tables) vs `manage` (Laravel staging, also stale). **Live SERP transactional data is in `serp_app`** (staging: `serp_test`); when Jack says "live" he means `laravel_live`.

### Darklaunch — RETIRED 2026-09-26 (history)

> **Darklaunch is RETIRED.** It was SERP's dual-write / parallel-run validation system (NOT a feature-flag library) — SERP shadow-processed Odoo's writes into a separate MySQL mirror, gated on **"<1% drift, stable 2 weeks"** as the cutover-readiness signal. It was switched off at the 2026-09-26 cutover. `SERP_DARKLAUNCH_ENABLED`, `SERP_SHADOW_WRITES_ENABLED`, `USE_SERP_AS_LIVE`, `ENVIRONMENT_SET` have zero hits in `backend/`; the drift-monitor package is deleted; `darklaunch-drift`/`publish-serp-kits`/`reconcile-published-stock` crons are `active=0`; the `read_repointable` dispatch is removed. Nothing below is current architecture — it is kept so old logs, tickets and Slack threads make sense, and because the lessons transfer.

- **Naming traps:** "Darklaunch", "replica", "shadow", and `serp_test` were FOUR distinct things (and the "compare-replica" tooling actually compared darklaunch). The 5-DB local Docker matrix `{prod, staging} × {replica, darklaunch}` (`serp_staging_replica`, `serp_prod_replica`, `serp_staging_darklaunch`, `serp_prod_darklaunch`) is gone (SERP PR #46 → `serp_local_prod`/`serp_local_staging`/`laravel_local`). Replicas were clean row-for-row mirrors of `manage`/`laravel_live`; darklaunch DBs were replica + Odoo overlay (normal BOMs, MOs, SVL, POs) with `_migrations` + `serp_darklaunch_meta` fingerprints. A predecessor **`serp_shadow`** mechanism (Hetzner `serp_shadow` DB, `SERP_SHADOW_WRITES_ENABLED`, `shadow_cutover_at=2026-05-08`) preceded it.
- **How it worked:** gated by `SERP_DARKLAUNCH_ENABLED` (default False in prod; additive, safe to disable). When ON, Serpy appended `SYNC_TARGET_SERP_DARKLAUNCH='serp_darklaunch'` beside every Odoo target → two queue items per op, with `serp_orm` handlers on a separate pool (`get_darklaunch_pool`) and `serp:<entity>` vs `serp_darklaunch:<entity>` routing keys. The order path (`serp_order_queue_worker`, formerly the "darklaunch order worker", file renamed 2026-07-30 `67fa02e60`) replayed Odoo's order writes with NO queue, resolving Odoo `sale_order.id`/`sale_order_line.id` via `sale_order.sw_id = ec_order.id` BEFORE creating rows; orders that never reached Odoo got NULL odoo_ids. Its predecessor `staging_copier_worker` / `STAGING_COPIER_ENABLED` (PRs #181/#182/#183, #587; 5-min cycle, `RECENT_WINDOW_DAYS=7`, PII-stripped via an allow-list + `PII_COLUMNS` deny-map) was DELETED 2026-07-30 (SERP PR #707 `94deba129`); it had copied recent PII-stripped Laravel rows into local `serp_test` tables — including a duplicate-bloat bug (staging `ec_order.order_id` non-unique and `EC_ORDER_COLUMNS` omitted `id`, so ON DUPLICATE KEY never fired, ~950k rows for ~5.1k orders) — moot now that Laravel data is read on a Laravel pool. Cutover was recorded per-env in `serp_darklaunch_meta.darklaunch_cutover_at` (prod 2026-06-04 09:27:20, staging 2026-06-03 11:52:27, primary 2026-05-30 14:14 MT); **`serp_darklaunch_meta` was DROPPED 2026-08-03 (`809a4d8f9`)** and the cutover-timestamp gate replaced by per-order SERP flags. Reseed lesson: pre-PR #316, `seed-full-reseed.js` Phase 1 (`setOrderSyncing(false)`) flipped `SERP_DARKLAUNCH_ENABLED=false`, which also killed the dual-write mirror, so Odoo orders/MOs created during the pause (e.g. `EW/MO/22086`) were never mirrored; the reseed then paused via `ORDER_QUEUE_WORKER_ENABLED` instead. Event log was `serp_darklaunch_processed_events`; tools `/compare-darklaunch`, `/compare-orders`, `compare_odoo_replica.py`.
- **The ISOLATION rule (lesson):** the darklaunch worker queried Odoo ONLY to resolve ids to stamp into `odoo_id` — it NEVER read Odoo VALUES at runtime; everything else resolved against the local seeded DB, so many drift/cost bugs traced to the SEED. Fix in the seeder or reproduce Odoo's logic in SERP — never add an Odoo runtime read.
- **`odoo_id_stamper`** (`workers/odoo_id_stamper.py`) — a separate post-create step that opened its own pool and was the only place the worker touched Odoo PG (asyncpg via `odoo_pool.execute_query_via_loop`; psycopg2 could not connect to the prod Odoo host). RETIRED 2026-06-25 (order detection moved to per-order flags). Its failure modes were instructive: it ran inside the worker's uncommitted REPEATABLE READ txn (fell back to natural-key matching that mis-stamped a committed neighbor: ~84% NULL / ~16% mis-stamped); a dead idle Odoo PG connection silently left `odoo_id=NULL`; it had to be wired into EVERY write path (fixed 2026-06-03); `/compare-darklaunch` diffed only rows with `odoo_id IS NOT NULL`, so unstamped children were silently excluded.
- **Invariant lesson — a validation mode must NOT change what the operation does.** ~2026-05-28 → 2026-07-29 the MO-create handler (`workers/handlers/manufacturing.py`) branched on `SERP_DARKLAUNCH_ENABLED`: OFF used `payload.components[].quantity` verbatim (the counted slip figure = source of truth); ON re-read `mrp.bom.line.product_qty` from Odoo and wrote `quantity = bom_line.product_qty × (product_qty / bom_qty)` = **BOM ratio × cup count**, discarding the payload quantity — and BOTH branches did `xmlrpc.create("mrp.production", …)` against LIVE prod Odoo, so flipping darklaunch ON silently changed the production write (motive: make Odoo's MO raw-move rows match SERP's `bom.explode` so the stamper saw no mismatch — "made the measurement pass by moving the thing measured"). Impact: ~620 MOs across ~123–135 Serpy drafts (and non-Serpy MOs), **71 finished-good SKUs stopped recording real slip consumption** (worst: Pistachios back to 02-18; every affected MO matched BOM to `diff=0.0000`, the signature of a computed value); found + flag turned OFF 2026-08-04, fixed 2026-09-05 (`38dd6ed72`, CI-pinned by `test_mo_create_darklaunch_additive.py`). Turning the flag OFF self-corrected NOTHING — the ~620 `done` MOs needed manual reconciliation in Odoo. Any COGS/inventory reading off RM consumption in that window is off by the BOM-vs-actual gap.
- **Drift monitor** (`/compare-darklaunch` + n8n `HpHN9Reme3L6bNBd`, later in-repo `workers/darklaunch_drift/worker.py`): hourly (cron `0 6-18 * * 1-5`, MT), windowed the latest ~1000 ids per table from Odoo prod PG, joined darklaunch on `odoo_id` across ~42 tables, Slack-alerted rows 30m+ old and MISSING or drifted (GRACE_MINUTES=30, WINDOW=1000, SAMPLES=5). `scripts/build_darklaunch_drift_n8n.py` was deleted 2026-06-11 (`63b694219`). n8n lessons: 9 linear LOOP-FREE nodes (SplitInBatches stalled ~14/42) with a **Merge barrier** ("Wait For Both") else the report fires with only the first branch; Slack `messageType:text` mrkdwn (block-only `blocksUi` throws `no_text`); date-only vs datetime tolerance; NULL↔numeric-zero coercion; float ±0.01; boolean↔0/1.
- **Most flagged "drift" was NOT a bug:** (A) post-seed Odoo staleness (Odoo crons edited rows after the seed snapshot; cleared by reseed); (B) by-design worker-row divergence on create-time columns (`sale_order.name='S'+ec_order_id` vs Odoo `ir.sequence`; `sale_order_line.sequence=10` always vs Odoo 10/300/301; the worker skipped Odoo's NULL-product separators — all in `WORKER_ROW_DIVERGENT_COLUMNS`), plus suppressed `write_uid`/`create_uid`, picking-name drift, float noise; (C) ~6h Denver/UTC datetime offset. Real bugs were the minority: dropped order lines when `product.default_code` is NULL, the seeder collapsing `partially_available`→`assigned`, bin-level `location_id` divergence. The approved-differences list above remains useful as Odoo-parity notes; note the bin-level-putaway suppression may have hidden a real corruption signal.
- Pre-cutover seeding lore: seed came from Odoo (`INSERT … ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE`), high-volume tables seeded with only the latest ~5,000 ids (so row-count deltas vs Odoo were expected); chatter seeded 2026-07-22 (`--chatter-only`; attachment binaries in S3 `s3://sw-serp/serp-attachments/` keyed by checksum; AUTO_INCREMENT floors bumped to `odoo_max + 10,000,000`); before rebuilding the darklaunch DB you had to `pm2 stop serp-workers` or hit MySQL `1412 'table definition changed'`, which killed in-flight shipments.

## The Database Landscape

SugarWish runs **12 databases** reachable through the unified `mcp__db__*` tools (`mcp__db__list_databases`; keys wired in `src/services/databases.ts`, documented in `.claude/rules/databases.md`): **9 MySQL, 3 PostgreSQL**. Name tables and columns EXACTLY as written; enum values, ID conventions, and SQL gotchas are load-bearing. `serp_app` is queryable now (a newly-added key still needs an MCP restart before it appears). Several `serp_*` DBs are **disposable local rebuilds**, NOT peer remote servers. `retool` is a shared multi-app DB, NOT SERP-owned. Since the **2026-09-26 cutover**, **`serp_app` is the production ERP source of truth and `odoo` is FROZEN read-only history**.

| Database (MCP key)                      | Engine     | Role                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------------------------------- | ---------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `wishdesk`                              | MySQL      | WishDesk/SWAC/WishWorks CS+CRM+design+billing (179 tables, SSH tunnel)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `wishdesk_dev`                          | MySQL      | WishDesk dev/staging twin (~5K vs ~64K prod users; partial sandbox; has in-progress tables not yet in prod)                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `laravel_live`                          | MySQL      | SugarWish/Laravel **PROD** e-commerce/order domain — orders/customers (AWS RDS `database-1.cqqg1tfyyubp.us-east-1`, SSH tunnel). Also co-hosts **70 `serp_*` tables** (all BASE TABLEs): mostly a thin bridge (e.g. 19 `serp_sale_order`, 0 `serp_purchase_order`) plus the live-path tables `serp_mrp_bom` (kit/BOM SoT), `serp_published_stock` and `serp_published_kits` |
| `manage`                                | MySQL      | SugarWish/Laravel **STAGING** (~8% of prod; renamed 2026-05-11 from "laravel staging"); holds SERP's upstream ORM tables (70 `serp_*` tables) + `serp_res_users` auth                                                                                                                                                             |
| `serp_app`                              | MySQL      | **LIVE/PRODUCTION SERP DB and the ERP source of truth** — Hetzner `5.161.233.240:3306` (`LIVE_DARKLAUNCH_DB_*` connection params — legacy name), database `serp_app`. 163 tables (113 `serp_*` plus Serpy, forecast, order-queue and data-assumption tables)                                                                         |
| `serp_test`                             | MySQL      | **STAGING SERP DB** — same Hetzner host/params as `serp_app`, database `serp_test` (163 tables). A real staging DB (serp-dev), NOT a throwaway/pytest DB and NOT production; a staging data slice (~30.9k `serp_sale_order`). Old `live_darklaunch_db` key was removed — same DB                                                   |
| `serp_local_prod`                       | MySQL      | Local Docker (`serp-mysql`, `127.0.0.1:3307`, devuser) — local SERP prod schema                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `serp_local_staging`                    | MySQL      | Local Docker (same container) — local SERP staging schema                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `laravel_local`                         | MySQL      | Local Docker (same container) — the 13 Laravel catalog tables, schema-only; local target for the mixed Laravel pool                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `odoo`                                  | PostgreSQL | Odoo 15 ERP PROD — **FROZEN** read-only history (last PO write 2026-09-25 21:12; stale for anything after 2026-09-26). Cloud-hosted on Odoo.sh — no shell access. See the Odoo section                                                                                                                                            |
| `odoo_staging`                          | PostgreSQL | Odoo staging clone — frozen legacy (lagged prod ~2 weeks / ~110k orders when last refreshed; credentials rotated frequently — check `#odoo-prixite`)                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `retool`                                | PostgreSQL | Shared BI/ops + forecast caches + legacy auth bridge `serp_users` (dropped from SERP 2026-09-26) + AI observability (~165 tables). Legacy: `odoo_sync_queue*` copies are frozen — Retool is NOT the sync engine anymore. **NOT SERP-owned**                                                                                                                             |

### The SERP databases

- **`serp_app` = production, `serp_test` = staging** — same Hetzner host and `LIVE_DARKLAUNCH_DB_*` params (host/port/user/password), only the database name differs. Query `serp_app` for production ERP data (inventory, SVL, MOs, POs, moves/quants) and `serp_test` to reproduce what serp-dev/staging workers see. **Never** use a local Docker DB for that: local DBs are reseeded from scratch and lack the runtime-created rows (e.g. worker `stock_move` rows carrying a `sale_line_id`: 955 on `serp_test` vs 0 on local, measured while darklaunch ran).
- **Independent `_migrations` tracks.** Each DB has its own `_migrations` table; versions are timestamp-named `YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS_<desc>` (not the old numeric `NNN`, so "078" no longer exists as a version string). Heads are independent timestamps per DB and a gap between them is the normal staging-first steady state, never by itself "a failed migration" — check live before asserting either "same head" or "behind". Last observation (2026-09-29): both `serp_app` and `serp_test` at `20260928_192637_restore_unlink_fk_indexes` (this can diverge again between deploys). Schema changes are committed migrations applied by the deploy runner (`deploy-k8s.sh` phase [3/6] `migrate:serp-app`), no manual DBA step. **Same head ≠ same data:** `serp_app` carries the full Odoo history plus all post-cutover SERP-native rows (~2.29M `serp_sale_order`; ~2,300 `odoo_id IS NULL`), while `serp_test` is a staging slice (~30.9k; ~914 `odoo_id IS NULL`). Counts also differ for `serp_mrp_bom` (serp_app 1,913 total / 439 phantom; serp_test 1,683 / 228 phantom) and `serp_product_template` (~8,020 vs ~8,426).
- **Local Docker DBs** (`serp-mysql`, `127.0.0.1:3307`, user `devuser`): `serp_local_prod`, `serp_local_staging`, `laravel_local`. SERP PR #46 consolidated the local DBs 5 → 3; the old `serp_prod_replica` / `serp_staging_replica` / `serp_prod_darklaunch` / `serp_staging_darklaunch` names (and `serp_*_darklaunch` env values, which now normalise onto the new names) are legacy — don't use them for anything new. Rebuild the LOCAL copy via `npm run db:pull:prod-replica` (local-build-only); the local DB is a lagging snapshot, never canonical over `serp_test`/`serp_app`.
- **`serp_darklaunch_meta` was DROPPED 2026-08-03 (commit `809a4d8f9`)** and darklaunch itself is RETIRED (switched off at the cutover) — there is no "darklaunch DB" fingerprint anymore. Historical replica-vs-darklaunch concept is covered in the SERP section's legacy note.
- **Hetzner DB box disk vs binlogs (2026-07-02):** the box (`5.161.233.240`) filled its disk and broke prod's forecast API (MySQL errno 28 on temp-table spills) — binary logging with 30-day retention was journaling every reseed's ~35–40GB of `serp_test` rewrites, for a DB whose recovery story was "run the reseed again" (no replica, no PITR). Since PR #353, every session from `seeding/scripts/_db_helpers.py::get_live_darklaunch_connection` (reseed/compare/backfill) runs `SET SESSION sql_log_bin=0` — session-scoped, so worker/app pools keep journaling normally. Requires the `SESSION_VARIABLES_ADMIN` grant (on `jack-serptest-write-20260428`, added 2026-07-02); without it the connection still succeeds with a loud WARN and journaling intact. Emergency relief = Munyr running `PURGE BINARY LOGS` (79%→58% disk). Historical reseed tooling (history): the LIVE `serp_test` reseed was `npm run db:full-reseed` (`/reseed-live-darklaunch`, streams full Odoo-prod history straight to Hetzner, never lands on local disk; latest full-history reseed ~102M rows, 2026-07-05); the old `db:push:prod-darklaunch` build-local-then-restore flow was REMOVED 2026-07-02 — don't cite it (nor `pm2 stop serp-workers`; workers run on K3s).

### Fingerprinting which DB a table belongs to

- **`serp_*` prefix** → SERP's own MySQL tables (`serp_app`/`serp_test`), modelled on Odoo (dots→underscores: `stock.picking`→`serp_stock_picking`, `purchase.order`→`serp_purchase_order`, `mrp.bom`→`serp_mrp_bom`, `account.move`→`serp_account_move`, `res.partner`→`serp_res_partner`). The **same table name without `serp_`** (`stock_move`, `sale_order`, `stock_picking`, `mrp_bom`, `account_move`) = the frozen **Odoo PostgreSQL** copy. Serpy tables use the `serpy_*` namespace (`serpy_sync_queue`, `serpy_drafts`, …). The same `serp_*` name also exists in `laravel_live` (live order path / `/kits`) — **resolve WHICH database before querying a `serp_*` table** (kit/BOM data SoT = `laravel_live`).
- **`swcrm_*`, `swcrm_z_gmail_*`, `orders_tickets`, `live_chat_*`, `design_*`, `ds_*`, `orders_*`, `sw_billing_*`, `merchandise_*`, `quiz_*`, `crm_*`** → **WishDesk** MySQL.
- **`ec_order`, `giftcards_card`, `cart`, `preselect_orders`, `company`, `buyer_products`, `receiver_products`, `components`, `kits`, `items`, `branding_records`** → **Laravel** (`laravel_live`/`manage`).
- **`serp_users` (legacy bridge, dropped from SERP 2026-09-26; Retool copy last updated 2026-04-23 — `res_users` in `serp_app` is the only live user table), `odoo_sync_queue*` (frozen), `sa_projections_cache`, `size_projections*`, `sku_projections`, `quickbooks_dashboard`, `opportunities` (Insightly)** → **Retool** PostgreSQL. Retool suffix convention: BASE locally, `_dev` locally for AI tables, `_live` in prod; auth/user/forecast tables have NO suffix. The Serpy operational tables no longer live in Retool: `odoo_sync_queue_live` → **`serpy_sync_queue`** and `serp_draft_operations_live` → **`serpy_drafts`** are in `serp_app` (renamed 2026-08-31); `*_backup` copies of the old names sit in `serp_app` too.
- **Fingerprint columns:** `_migrations` marks a SERP DB (schema ledger). Origin of a row is `odoo_id IS NULL` (SERP-native) vs `IS NOT NULL` (Odoo-sourced) — NOT `id != odoo_id` and NOT an id threshold (see the id/odoo_id invariant below).

### Legacy flag vocabulary (history — all retired)

Do not conflate these; they only matter when reading old code, tickets or Slack: (1) `*_replica` DBs — clean verbatim Laravel/`manage` mirrors with no Odoo overlay; (2) `serp_shadow` (Hetzner) — handler-correctness validation under real prod traffic, gated by `SERP_SHADOW_WRITES_ENABLED`; (3) darklaunch proper — `*_darklaunch` locally / `serp_test` on Hetzner, gated by `SERP_DARKLAUNCH_ENABLED`; (4) `serp_shadow_meta` / `serp_shadow_processed_events` — the predecessor of darklaunch (`shadow_cutover_at = 2026-05-08`). `SERP_SHADOW_WRITES_ENABLED` ≠ `SERP_DARKLAUNCH_ENABLED`. `/compare-darklaunch` (renamed 2026-05-28 from `/compare-replica`) compared against the darklaunch DB. **Neither flag (nor `USE_SERP_AS_LIVE`) exists in SERP `backend/` on `origin/main` anymore** — darklaunch was switched off at the cutover.

### The `id` / `odoo_id` join invariant (the #1 footgun — still in force)

Every `serp_*` table holding both Odoo-sourced and SERP-native rows carries a nullable `odoo_id` (bigint) = the PK of the matching (frozen) Odoo record. Buckets:

| Bucket                 | `id` vs `odoo_id`                                                                       | Origin                                      |
| ---------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- |
| Odoo-seeded            | `id == odoo_id` (Odoo's own id, < 1B)                                                   | seeder                                      |
| Worker-created, linked | `id` = MySQL AUTO_INCREMENT past the Odoo range; `odoo_id` = Odoo id → `id != odoo_id` | darklaunch worker (history; ~zero rows now) |
| SERP-native            | `odoo_id IS NULL`                                                                       | SERP runtime, no Odoo counterpart           |

- **ALWAYS join `serp.odoo_id = odoo.id`, NEVER `id = id`** (IDs diverge independently). `NULL` = SERP-native (NOT invalid).
- **Durable origin test: `odoo_id IS NULL` = SERP-native, `IS NOT NULL` = Odoo-sourced** — never an id threshold. The earlier `SERP_ORIGIN_ID_FLOOR = 1_000_000_000` scheme was reversed the next day (0 rows ≥1B on both `serp_app` and `serp_test`), and the "+10,000,000 offset above `MAX(odoo_id)`" allocation seen 2026-09-11 is **also gone** after the reseeds: at 2026-09-29 `serp_app.serp_sale_order` has `MAX(odoo_id)` 2,431,779 and its `odoo_id IS NULL` rows are ids 2,431,780–2,434,093 (~2,300 rows, growing) — right above the Odoo range. Any `id < N` / "just above max(odoo_id)" filter returns wrong rows; test `odoo_id IS NULL`. The `id != odoo_id` "linked-but-renumbered" bucket is negligible (138 of 2.28M `serp_sale_order`, 1 `serp_stock_picking`, 0 on `serp_test` as of 2026-09-11) — a fingerprint on it does not work.
- Worker/ingested order rows are most reliably identified by `name = 'S' + ec_order_id`. **SERP data wins conflicts** — Jack explicitly rejected a `serp_id` surrogate.
- Child rows (`stock_move`, `stock_move_line`, quants) often have `odoo_id = NULL`. The old drift tooling (`/compare-darklaunch`) only diffed rows with `odoo_id IS NOT NULL` (history).
- Most `serp_*` tables have NO UNIQUE constraint on `odoo_id`; live exceptions (checked 2026-09-29 on `serp_app`): `serp_sale_order` (`uk_so_odoo_id`), `serp_sale_order_line` (`uk_sol_odoo_id`), `serp_account_tax` (`uq_account_tax_odoo_id`).

### Inventory has no single source of truth

Sellable (SA) = Laravel view `receiver_product_inventory` (`sellable_qty` = available + prelist_qty, fed from `serp_published_stock`; unsynced demand = `ec_order.serp_sync IN (0,2)` over 30d; `reservation_hold_qty` from `inventory_reservations`) — the old columns `receiver_products.inventory_qty`/`odoo_inventory` are `(deleted)`-renamed and throw "Unknown column"; raw material (RM) + on-hand + accounting/valuation = `serp_app` (`serp_stock_quant`, SVL). Odoo is frozen history only. See the inventory source-of-truth notes elsewhere in the dictionary.

❌ AI assumes `laravel_live` is SERP-only or pure orders → ✅ Reality: it is the live Sugarwish app DB that ALSO co-hosts a thin, near-empty `serp_*` bridge (19 `serp_sale_order`, 0 POs) plus the kit/BOM and published-stock tables — NOT live SERP ERP data. Live SERP data lives in **`serp_app`**. When Jack says "live" he means `laravel_live`.

❌ AI assumes `serp_test` is a throwaway test DB → ✅ Reality: it is the **STAGING SERP DB** on Hetzner, not a throwaway — but also NOT production (**prod is `serp_app`**).

❌ AI assumes a zero/small `serp_app` kit or `serp_mrp_bom` count means "no kits" → ✅ the kit/BOM SoT is `laravel_live` (see the `serp_mrp_bom` note in the SERP section); the wrong-DB query succeeds silently.
