## Odoo (PostgreSQL) — FROZEN Legacy ERP (read-only history since 2026-09-26)

**Odoo is FROZEN.** Since the 2026-09-26 cutover nothing writes to it (last `purchase_order` write 2026-09-25 21:12; last `sale_order` create 2026-09-26 12:16; nothing operational after). It is **stale for anything after 9/26** — SERP (`serp_app`) is the source of truth for products, BOMs, MOs, POs, quants, SVL and inventory, and neither SERP nor Serpy reads from Odoo anymore. Investigate current discrepancies in `serp_app`; use the notes below to read pre-cutover history and to understand where SERP's `odoo_id`-linked rows came from (SERP's `serp_*` tables were seeded from this data; join on `odoo_id`, never `id = id`).

Odoo 15 (v15.0.1.3), ~584-740+ tables, cloud-hosted on Odoo.sh (no shell access). Statement timeout **330s** on prod. Catch-all customer partner is **id=94 "SW Customer"** (all ~2.21-2.43M sale orders). `sale_order` history begins **2022-11-15** (id=1); older orders only in legacy Laravel. Frozen counts (2026-09-29): `sale_order` max id 2,431,779 (was ~2,352,430 on prod / ~2,342,312 on staging earlier in the year), `purchase_order` 2,197, `mrp_production` 29,576, `mrp_bom` 2,284, `product_template`/`product_product` 7,610 each, `res_partner` 298. `odoo_staging` is a near-identical, older clone (lagged prod ~2 weeks / ~110k orders when last refreshed).

History (Odoo-side automation, now stopped): the Odoo puller/crons that pulled Laravel orders and drove the Odoo order pipeline no longer run; the SERP/Serpy Odoo XML-RPC handler arms were deleted 2026-09-26 (`90e712f39`; only the read-only `odoo_xmlrpc_service.py` + `ODOO_*` config remain). Odoo → Laravel `SynchronizeOdooInventory` is dead with Odoo frozen.

### Sales

**`sale_order`** (~2.21M rows at the time of the earlier survey; max id now ~2.43M) — `name` = `S#######` (used as `stock_picking.origin`); `name = 'S'+digits` is **NOT** the same as `id`. `state` (quotation/sent/`sale`/`done`/`cancel`): `sale`=active (~2.14M), `cancel` (~66k), `done` (6, ignore). `partner_id` is **ALWAYS 94** (grouping collapses to one bucket). **`sw_id`** = Laravel order id (`ec_order.order_id`) = THE cross-system join key; also `sw_datetime`, `increment_id`. On confirm: spawned `stock_picking` + one `stock_move` per BOM component.

**`sale_order_line`** — `product_uom_qty`=ordered; `qty_delivered`=shipped (from `done` moves). `display_type`=`line_section`/`line_note` → header rows (no `product_id`); **exclude when summing**.

### Products

**`product_template`** (~6,961–7,610 rows; ~5,804–6,167 active) — product MASTER at template level. `name`, `default_code` (template SKU), `sku`, `categ_id`→`product_category` (drives FIFO costing + GL), `list_price` (often 1.00 placeholder), `uom_id`, `uom_po_id`, `active` (archive), `tracking` (`none`/`lot`/`serial` — all `none`, lot/serial NOT in use), `landed_cost_ok`, `split_method_landed_cost`. `detailed_type`: `product` (storable, ~6,167), `consu` (consumable, not inventory-valued, ~1,109), `service` (~2). SKU prefixes: see Glossary.
- **`sugarwish_id` = THE external sync key**, lives on the **template only** (NOT `product_product`). Decode: prefix `'800'` → strip to get `components.id` (e.g. 800439→component 439); prefix `'500'` → `'500'`+`buyer_products.id`; other int → `receiver_products.product_id` OR `buyer_products.id`; 0/NULL → no mapping (**~2,264 of 7,515** as of 2026-09-11; was ~1,371).

**`product_product`** (~4,211–7,610 variants) — `default_code` = SKU = THE cross-system join key (e.g. `SA-15-014-A`). **~482–483 rows have NULL `default_code`** (dupes/ghosts) — MUST exclude (`WHERE default_code IS NOT NULL`). `product_tmpl_id`, `active` (false=archived; archived variants STILL hold `stock_quant` and `assigned` reservations — filter `active=true` for LIVE inventory but NOT for history audit), `barcode`, `weight`, `volume`, `combination_indices`. **Join Odoo↔Laravel on `default_code` (SKU), never `product_id`** — raw `product_id` is NOT the SugarWish id; go through `product_template.sugarwish_id`.

**`product_category`** (~137) — organized by product TYPE (01-Candy, 02-Popcorn, 03-Cookies, 06-DogTreats, 08-Coffee&Tea, 10-WineTastings, 14-Wine, 15-Flowers, 16-Candles, L-Labels, C-Carton) then by role (`-Packed Product`=sellable, `-Raw Materials`), not by pack size. Columns: `name`, `complete_name`, `parent_id` (self-FK tree), `parent_path`, `removal_strategy_id` (NULL all → default FIFO), `sugarwish_id`. **`cost_method`/`valuation` are NOT columns** — stored as `ir_property` keyed `res_id='product.category,<id>'`; company-wide FIFO (all 115 `ir_property` rows `value_text='fifo'`; `property_cost_method='fifo'` universally). Tasting-kit pack sizes encoded by category id: **55=3-pack, 56=6-pack, 57=9-pack, 165=12-pack, 190=4-pack, 202/203/205/206=monthly 6-pack**.

**`product_supplierinfo`** — `name` = SUPPLIER as `res_partner.id` cast to INTEGER (not a text name), `price`, `min_qty`, `delay` (lead-time days), `date_start`/`date_end`, `sequence` (priority), `product_code`/`product_name` (vendor's). **NO `case_qty` column** (would require migration).

### Inventory

**`stock_picking`** (~650K–3.02M rows) — transfer header. `name` = `WAREHOUSE/OPERATION/number` e.g. `EW/OUT/3178644` (trailing number ≠ id ≠ SO number). `state`: draft→confirmed→`assigned` ("Ready" = reserved, **NOT shipped**)→`done` (~3.02M)/cancel. **Only `done` moves inventory**; stuck `assigned` pickings cause phantom reservations. `origin` = TEXT source-doc (PO name like `P01906`/`P02056`, MO, `S#######`, `RO-200#####` receiver-order, `PSO-6####` preselect) — join picking→PO via TEXT `origin`, NOT id. `move_type`: `one`=all-or-nothing (~99.7%, SW default) vs `direct`=partials allowed. Also `picking_type_id`, `location_id`/`location_dest_id`, `date_done`, `backorder_id`, `sale_id` (FK to sale_order, ~99.6% used), `date_deadline` (~99.7% used).

**`stock_picking_type`** (135 rows = 9 types × 15 warehouses) — `code`: `incoming` (Receipts AND Returns, 30), `outgoing` (Delivery, 15; Deliveries ~3.0M pickings), `internal` (Pick/Pack/Transfers, 75), `mrp_operation` (Manufacturing, 15). `sequence_code` prefixes: IN, OUT, INT, PICK, PACK, PC, SFP, MO. EW ids 1–9 (1=Receipts, 2=Delivery Order, 3=Pick, 5=Internal, 6=Returns); TY ids 10–18 (10=Receipts, 11=Delivery, 14=Internal); WCC: 46=Receipts. Always join for `code + warehouse_id` (each warehouse reuses different ids).

**`stock_move`** (~14.5–15.8M rows: ~14.4M done, ~1.45M cancel) — inventory transaction spine, one row per product movement. `state` enum: **draft, confirmed, waiting, partially_available, assigned, done, cancel** (7 states; `assigned`=reserved-not-shipped; **only `done` changes inventory**). Fields: `product_uom_qty` (planned), `product_qty` (reference UoM), `quantity_done`, `location_id` (source), `location_dest_id` (dest), `price_unit`/`cost`, `date`, `reservation_date`. Link FKs: `bom_line_id` (MO raw-material moves AND phantom-kit delivery moves; ~30–95% coverage), `sale_line_id` (delivery moves; ~90%), `purchase_line_id` (receipts; PRIMARY PO→inventory link), `production_id` (finished output), `raw_material_production_id` (component consumption), `picking_id`, `is_inventory` (true for count adjustments). Delivery = `location_id=2008` (EW/Stock/Fulfillment) → `location_dest_id=5` (Customers).
- **CRITICAL perf**: NEVER filter `state` with a negated predicate (`NOT IN`/`!=`) on this table — forces a seq scan and hits the 330s timeout. Use the **positive** list `state IN ('draft','confirmed','waiting','partially_available','assigned')`.
- **CRISIS (frozen legacy):** at the last audit ~48,909 moves were stuck in assigned/confirmed/waiting with active `sale_line_id` (oldest May 2023; ~4.4k `assigned` + ~1.3k `confirmed` on the earlier survey), permanently locking `stock_quant.reserved_quantity`. Top product `B-CCC-REG-22-MUG-XXX` with 2,404 stuck. ~1.3M rows have zero `product_qty`.

**`stock_move_line`** (~13.3–14.5M) — execution detail. `move_id`→`stock_move`, `product_uom_qty`=reserved/planned; `qty_done`=executed; `lot_id`, `location_id`/`location_dest_id`, `state`, `unit_cost`, `total_value`. ~413 rows zero `unit_factor` (division hazard). `do_unreserve()` can leave a line at qty=0 while the parent move stays `assigned` = **zombie reservation** (Odoo bug). Orphan-reservation diagnostic: `location_id=2008 AND product_uom_qty>0 AND state NOT IN ('done','cancel')`.

**`stock_valuation_layer`** (SVL, ~13.2–14.4M) — FIFO costing ledger, **company-wide FIFO**. `quantity`/`value` are **SIGNED** (positive=receipt/inbound, negative=delivery/COGS/outbound), `unit_cost`, `remaining_qty`/`remaining_value` = unconsumed inbound portion (~3,705–3,900 layers >0); SUM(`remaining_value`) per product = current inventory $. `description` (e.g. `EW/IN/02132 - <product>`), `categ_id`, `stock_move_id`, `account_move_id` (GL), `stock_landed_cost_id`, `stock_valuation_layer_id` (self-FK child revaluation; there is NO `parent_layer_id` column). **NO `sequence_number` column** — FIFO order = `id`/`create_date` ascending.

**`stock_quant`** (~14,729 records; 4,211 products × 424–502 locations) — on-hand snapshot (not a log). `quantity`, `reserved_quantity`; **AVAILABLE = `quantity` − `reserved_quantity`.** Also `inventory_quantity` (counted), `inventory_diff_quantity`, `inventory_date`, `inventory_quantity_set` (count-pending flag), `lot_id`, `in_date` (FIFO arrival), `accounting_date`, `user_id` (there is NO `unit_cost` column on `stock_quant`). Filter on-hand by `usage='internal'` (NOT `inventory_date IS NOT NULL`). Odoo rejects quants on consumables/services. **ANOMALY**: ~482–5,918 records with **negative quantities** (−37.8M to −189,619 units): Vendors (−25.2M), Production (−7.2M), Inventory adjustment (−5.2M), Stock (−81K internal), Fulfillment (−20K internal). Negatives in VIRTUAL locations are normal Odoo double-entry; only INTERNAL-location negatives are real problems. Cycle-count to zero does NOT clear reservations → `quantity=0` + `reserved_quantity>0` = negative available = **root cause of negative "West Coast Qty"** in Laravel (history).

**`stock_location`** (~2,384–2,686, ~20-level hierarchy) — `usage`: `internal` (real owned stock, ~2,654–2,661), `view` (grouping ~18), `supplier` (Vendors **id 4**), `customer` (Customers **id 5**/id 3), `production` (id 15), `transit` (id 6/13), `inventory` (adjustment, id 14), scrap (id 16). `name`, `complete_name` (full path e.g. `EW/Stock/ROW2-P4-UP`), `parent_path` (materialized ancestry `1/7/8/2962/`), `location_id` (parent self-FK). **`sugarwish_id` controlled the (now dead) Laravel sync**: `>0` (only `EW/Stock/Fulfillment` id 2008 has =1; TY=2 label) synced to Laravel; `=0` internal Odoo-only; `NULL` virtual/system. `sugarwish_code` mostly NULL. **Key IDs**: EW/Stock=8, EW/Stock/Fulfillment=**2008**, TY/Stock=20, TY/Stock/Fulfillment=**2006**, EW/Packing Zone=12, EW/Pre-Production=17, EW/Post-Production=18, Production=15, Wine/WCC=5. **ANOMALY**: 88 exact-duplicate active internal shelf locations (worst: `TY/Stock/R4-7-AR` exists 4× as ids 3391, 4437, 4438, 4439). Test ids 2001/2002 (`EW/Stock/Zone1/2`) are referenced in Retool but DO NOT exist here.

**`stock_warehouse`** (15 rows, 13 active) — EW=id 1, TY=id 2, plus partner/dropship. `lot_stock_id` = main internal Stock (EW=8, TY=20). All warehouses `reception_steps=one_step`, `delivery_steps=ship_only`, manufacture_steps=`one_step` — receipt/delivery is **one move, not a chain**.

**Other stock tables** — `stock_scrap` (~1,388–1,590): `scrap_qty`, `state` (`draft`/`done`; only `done` reduces inventory), `scrap_location_id`, `move_id`. `stock_landed_cost` (~97): freight/duty allocation; adds VALUE only — does **NOT** add `stock_quant.quantity`; `split_method` (`equal`/`by_quantity`/`by_current_cost_price`=54 most common/`by_weight`/`by_volume`), `state` (`draft`/`done`), `account_move_id`, `vendor_bill_id`. `stock_lot`/`stock_production_lot`, `stock_warehouse_orderpoint` (reorder rules, NOT in SERP plan), `stock_putaway_rule` (288 rows), `stock_location_route` (66 rows).

### Manufacturing (MRP)

**`mrp_production`** (~26,195–29,576 rows, mostly historical/done) — manufacturing orders. `name` e.g. `EW/MO/05584` / `WH/MO/00848`, `product_id` (finished good), `product_qty` (planned), `qty_producing`, `bom_id`. `state`: draft (~21–28), confirmed (~9–13), progress, to_close (~3–9), done (~25,581–27,300), cancel (~584–594). `reservation_state` (NULL done / confirmed waiting / assigned reserved), `location_src_id`, `location_dest_id`, `production_location_id`=15, `date_planned_start`/`_finished`, `date_start`/`_finished`, `consumption`, `extra_cost`, `move_raw_ids`, `move_finished_ids`, `is_locked`, `is_planned`. **Phantom/kit BOMs create NO MOs** — kits explode directly into component delivery moves. **Cross-warehouse pattern (NOT error)**: ~6,191 MOs have source TY/Stock(20) but dest EW/Stock/Fulfillment(2008).
- **`date_finished` footgun:** marking an MO done (`button_mark_done`) stamps `date_finished = server-now`. A slip entered the next day therefore lands on the **wrong production day**, undercounting SERP's throughput dashboard (and skewing the packing bonus). Ops (Will/Sophie) catch this against paper packing slips; Jack historically corrected it with a one-off Python XML-RPC script. Two SERPY ops were added for it: **`odoo_update_mo_date_finished`** (PR #175 — edits the completed date on an already-done MO via the `/manufacture-orders` cart) and a **required packed/MO date on MO _creation_** (PR #176, branch `feature/serpy-mo-create-date`). Both convert the Denver calendar day → **noon naive-UTC** via `_denver_day_to_odoo_utc` so the dashboard's `DATE(date_finished)` buckets to the right day (per the ~6h naive-MT-vs-UTC rule). Post-cutover, SERP's own MO handlers own this.

**`mrp_bom`** (~2,078–2,284 total: ~1,303–1,310 normal + ~775–813 phantom; ~207–265 active phantom) — `type`: `normal` (manufactured via MO) vs `phantom` (KIT, exploded at SO confirmation, NO MO). **All 813 phantom BOMs are `active=false`** (in the earlier survey). `product_tmpl_id`, `product_id` (variant override — **~96–99.8% NULL**, template-level only, linked via `product_tmpl_id`; matching on `product_id` alone gives "no BOM found" — a caution for **new** queries only: SERP's own `_bom_find` already falls back to `product_tmpl_id`, `mrp_bom.py:170-219`), `product_qty` (output, usually 1.0), `consumption` (`flexible`/`strict`/`warning`), `ready_to_produce` (`all_available`/`asap`), `code`, `picking_type_id`, `active`. Phantom BOMs create ZERO `mrp_production` records; a phantom kit mapped to Laravel via `product_template.sugarwish_id` = `buyer_products.odoo_id`. Component SKU prefixes in phantom lines: S- (sweets, 238–253), P- (packaging/paper, 253–525), I- (inserts, 101–244), E- (extras, 46), L- (labels, 34–94), B- (boxes, 25–67), T- (tape, 22–77, all qty=0), C- (cards, 13). BOM ownership moved to `serp_app` at cutover (kits' on-order source is `laravel_live.serp_mrp_bom`).

**`mrp_bom_line`** (~6,478–14,000 rows; ~6,845) — `bom_id`→`mrp_bom` (**MISSING INDEX on `bom_id`**, critical for JOINs), `product_id` (component), `product_tmpl_id`, `product_qty` (qty per parent unit), `product_uom_id`, `sequence`, `operation_id` (routing FK for per-step/conditional components). **CRITICAL ANOMALY**: ~30–48+ active SA→RM BOM lines (~437 lines overall) have **`product_qty=0`** (optional/conditional packaging) → division-by-zero in `inventory_variance.py`; always filter `AND product_qty > 0` before using as denominator. Affected: SA-01-012/024/029/030/033/053/060/077/078/091/098/110/119/132-A, SA-05-045/049/051/052/055/058-A, +more.

**`mrp_unbuild`** (~200), `mrp_bom_byproduct`, `mrp_routing_workcenter` — `mrp_unbuild`: reverse-MO; `product_qty`, `state` (`draft`/`done`), `mo_id`, `bom_id`; **unbuild ADDS component stock and REMOVES finished-good** (inverse of MO); only `done` moves inventory.

### Purchasing

**`purchase_order`** (~1,846–2,197 rows; ~1,696–1,833 'purchase') — `name`=`P#####` (e.g. `P02056`), `partner_id` (vendor), `state` (`draft`/`sent`/`to approve`/`purchase`/`done`/`cancel`): `purchase`=**APPROVED** (NOT "arrived"). **THREE distinct dates (CRITICAL)**: `date_order` = PO CREATED (NOT arrival); `date_planned` = expected delivery (user estimate); **`effective_date` = ACTUAL arrival** (NULL until receipt picking validated `state=done`) — **USE `effective_date` for "has PO arrived" / inventory variance**. `date_approve` (~94% used), `invoice_status` (INDEPENDENT of state: `no`=not received / `to invoice`=ready-to-bill / `invoiced`=fully billed for received-so-far — open backorder still possible, NOT whole-PO-settled), `amount_untaxed`/`tax`/`total`, `picking_type_id`, `group_id` (procurement, ~94%). `payment_term_id`/`incoterm_id`/`fiscal_position_id` = 0 rows used.

**`purchase_order_line`** (~14,549–17,391) — `order_id`, `product_id`, `product_qty` (ordered), `qty_received` (auto-updated from validated stock_move; method `stock_moves`/`manual`), `qty_invoiced`, `qty_to_invoice` (= received − invoiced), `price_unit` (often 0.00 at PO creation, ~7% zero), `date_planned`, `state` (mirrors parent), `display_type` (`section`/`note`). `sale_order_id`/`sale_line_id` exist for drop-ship but EMPTY (0 rows) — all POs are pure stock replenishment. **ANOMALIES**: ~266 lines partially received; **~20 lines OVER-received** (`qty_received > product_qty`: P01654 +30K, P00749 +28.8K, P00449 +24.5K; ~2,300 by the broader count); 10 cancelled POs (P00010/11/57/58/68/89/94/117/174/199) have 2–18 active stock moves.

### Accounting (GL)

**`account_move`** (~13.2M, mostly auto inventory entries) — `move_type`: `entry` (plain GL, ~13.22M auto inventory/COGS via STJ), `in_invoice` (VENDOR BILL, ~1,569–1,719: 1,201–1,344 posted + 358–362 draft), `in_refund` (vendor credit), `out_invoice` (customer, **1 row, unused**). `state` (`draft`/`posted`/`cancel`), `payment_state` (`not_paid`/`in_payment`/`paid`/`partial`/`reversed` — **99.8% `not_paid`**: payments are reconciled in **QuickBooks**, never back to Odoo; NOT a source of truth), `amount_total`/`untaxed`/`tax`/`residual`, `invoice_date`/`invoice_date_due`, `invoice_origin` (PO name e.g. `P00876`), `journal_id`, `partner_id`. Bills link to POs TWO ways: `invoice_origin='P#####'` AND junction **`account_move_purchase_order_rel`** (~1,722 rows; many-to-many — de-dupe on joins; one PO → many bills, e.g. P00876 has 7; one bill → multiple POs rare; **100% linkage**). Vendor billing was **100% manual** (posting a bill increments `qty_invoiced`; bills NOT auto-generated from POs).

**`account_move_line`** (~24.3M) — `move_id`, `debit`/`credit`/`balance`, `account_id`, `purchase_line_id` (direct bill→PO-line match, how `qty_invoiced` is attributed), `is_landed_costs_line`, `is_anglo_saxon_line` (COGS auto-entry — true=system offset, NOT human-entered; **0 rows** since Sugarwish is Continental not Anglo-Saxon), `tax_line_id`, `reconciled`/`full_reconcile_id` (exist but NOT populated for bills). `display_type`: `product` (real lines), `tax`, `payment_term`, `line_section`, `line_note`, `rounding`. **`product_id` NULL = tax/section/balancing line** (don't sum).

**`account_journal`** (8 journals, heavily skewed) — `type` (`sale`/`purchase`/`bank`/`cash`/`general`), `default_account_id`. **STJ "Inventory Valuation" (id 6)** received ~12.2–13.2M auto COGS/stock entries; **BILL "Vendor Bills" (id 2)** ~1,594–1,719; INV/BNK1/MISC barely used. **GL accounts**: 110100 Stock Valuation, 110200 Stock Interim Received, 110300 Stock Interim Delivered, 500000 COGS/Expense. Anglo-Saxon COGS chain: receipt DR 110100/CR 110200; delivery DR 110300/CR 110100; COGS DR 500000/CR 110300; vendor bill DR 110200/CR A/P.

**`account_account`** (~41) — `code`, `name`, `internal_group` (reliable P&L bucket: asset ~18, expense ~10, liability ~6, income ~4, equity ~3 — don't infer from code prefix), `internal_type`, `reconcile`, `deprecated`. **`account_tax`** (2 active: 15% sale/purchase placeholders) — `type_tax_use` (`sale`/`purchase`/`both`), `amount_type` (`percent`/`fixed`), `amount` (15.0000), `price_include`, `tax_exigibility`; **NOT the system of record for US sales tax — Avalara AvaTax is external.**

### Partners, Sales, Utilities

- **`res_partner`** (~294–298 rows: 170 vendors, 124 internal) — vendor/supplier directory, NOT a customer DB. `supplier_rank` (>0 = vendor; 1=preferred/2=alternative/3+=lower), `customer_rank` (**0 for EVERY row** — no individual customers), `is_company`, `type` (`contact`/`invoice`/`delivery`/`other`/`supplier`), `commercial_partner_id`, `email`/`phone`, address, `purchase_warn`/`invoice_warn`/`sale_warn`. (`weeks_on_hand` is NOT an Odoo column — it exists only on SERP's `serp_res_partner`.) id 94 = catch-all "SW Customer".
- **`res_company`**: single id=1 "Sugarwish Englewood", `currency_id`=2 (USD), `partner_id`=1. **`res_currency`**: id=2=USD ONLY active.
- **`res_users`**: internal logins/bots ONLY (NOT customers). `login`, `active`, `share`, `partner_id` (1:1; join to `res_partner` for name/email). `id=55` = jack@sugarwish.com = Serpy writes AND Jack's manual edits (so `create_uid=55` is NOT a reliable Serpy signal). Author map: 76=Neal, 87=Mike, 240=James, 262=Nicole; common bill creators Nora Stein, James Emeric.
- **`uom_uom`**: `category_id`, `factor` (relative to category ref; ~413 rows factor=0 = division hazard), `uom_type` (`reference`/`bigger`/`smaller`).
- **`ir_property`**: holds `standard_price` & cost_method keyed `res_id='product.product,<id>'`/`'product.category,<id>'`.
- **`ir_attachment`**: centralized file storage (`res_model`, `res_id`, `db_datas`, `store_fname`, `checksum`, `url`); product images live here, NOT on product tables. `mrp_production`/picking/MO carry NO chatter (PO-only ~5,607 messages). `failed_order_logs`/`failed_products_log` are EMPTY tables.

---

---

## SERP (MySQL `serp_*`) — the Production ERP

SERP (GitHub `Jack-Kiefer/SERP`, `serp.sugarwish.com`) is SugarWish's in-house ERP. It is a from-scratch clone of **Odoo 15's ORM** held to line-by-line parity (divergences are bugs against Odoo 15 source), and since the **2026-09-26 cutover it replaced Odoo**: the `serp_*` tables in **`serp_app`** (MySQL, Hetzner `5.161.233.240`) are the source of truth for products, BOMs, MOs, POs, quants, SVL and inventory. `serp_test` is the STAGING copy. The `serp_*` tables were seeded from Odoo (hence `odoo_id`), and ~113 `serp_*` tables plus Serpy/forecast/order-queue tables now live in `serp_app` (163 tables total). Table names mirror Odoo models with dots→underscores (`stock.picking`→`serp_stock_picking`); SERP also carries SugarWish-domain tables. `serp_orm` was severed from Laravel source tables on 2026-07-30 (PR #706: `component`/`receiver.product` models deleted; bridge FKs on the variant are opaque `BigInteger` keys) — a `serp_orm` join reaching into a Laravel source table is categorically wrong. See "The Database Landscape" for DB roles, migrations heads, and the `id`/`odoo_id` invariant (join on `odoo_id`, never `id = id`; `odoo_id IS NULL` = SERP-native).

- **Deploy & migrations:** every push to SERP `main` auto-deploys (GitHub webhook → Jenkins → K3s `deploy-k8s.sh main`, which runs `migrate:serp-app` at [3/6] against `serp_app`, then ArgoCD rolls tags). Schema changes are committed migrations applied by the runner — no manual DBA step. (The historical "hardcoded darklaunch DB / no migrations in deploy.sh / manage-DB needs a manual migration" problems belong to the frozen AWS EC2 `deploy.sh` path — never deploy there.)
- **Order flow now:** the live order-ingest path is `workers/serp_order_queue_worker.py` (Laravel `ec_order` → `serp_sale_order`; ~1,905 orders since 9/27, all `odoo_id` NULL), writing `serp_app`/`serp_test` only; it also rebuilds `serp_published_kits`/`serp_published_stock` in `laravel_live`. Serpy writes go through `serpy_sync_queue` (`serp:<entity>` handlers). Odoo XML-RPC handlers are dead code awaiting removal. Planned, NOT done (owner Manish, target state): drop `serp_*` tables from `laravel_live` except `serp_published_kits`/`serp_published_stock`, remove inventory transactions + deprecated columns + related Laravel code, and remove the Odoo inbound/outbound sidecars and SERP's Odoo connection code.
- **Legacy replica/darklaunch DB matrix (history).** The local Docker layout was once four DBs — `serp_prod_replica` (verbatim `laravel_live`, no Odoo overlay, ~19 sale_orders/0 POs/MOs, nearly empty), `serp_staging_replica` (verbatim `manage`, 0 rows in most tables), `serp_prod_darklaunch` (Odoo PROD + `laravel_live`; ~10.2k sale_orders, ~2k POs, ~5.5k pickings, ~34k moves) and `serp_staging_darklaunch` (Odoo STAGING + `manage`; ~36,144 moves, 1,971 POs) — written by the darklaunch dual-write worker. Darklaunch is retired; SERP PR #46 consolidated local Docker to 3 DBs: **`serp_local_prod`**, **`serp_local_staging`**, **`laravel_local`** on `serp-mysql` (`127.0.0.1:3307`, `devuser`). DB routing today derives from `DETECTED_ENVIRONMENT` (the old `SERP_ORM_ENV` / `ACTIVE_ODOO_DB_NAME` / `DARKLAUNCH_DB_ENV` / `ENVIRONMENT_SET` split is gone from `backend/` except `deploy-k8s-staging.sh` mentioning `ENVIRONMENT_SET`). If a SERP page shows empty entities (normal BOMs, MOs, inventory) locally, the ORM is pointed at a near-empty local DB — a config-routing issue, not a bug.

### `serp_*` Table Reference (live column names verified against `serp_app`, 2026-09-29)

- **`serp_sale_order`** (~2.29M rows on `serp_app`, ~2,236 with `odoo_id IS NULL`; ~30.9k on `serp_test`) — PRIMARY ORDER BRIDGE. `order_type` enum (`receiver-order`/`preselect-order`): exactly one of `ec_order_id`/`preselect_order_id` set; `sw_id`, `sw_datetime`. `name` = `'S'+sw_id`. `state='sale'` = CONFIRMED. Dates: `create_date`/`write_date` (NOT `created_at`). `uk_so_odoo_id` unique on `odoo_id`. In `laravel_live` only ~19 legacy rows exist (not live data).
- **`serp_stock_picking`** — `state` is a genuine 6-state enum `draft`,`waiting`,`confirmed`,`assigned` (reserved/ready, **NOT shipped**),`done` (shipped),`cancel` — no `partially_available` (real Odoo parity, don't "fix"). `sale_id` (bigint = `ec_order.increment_id`); picking_type_id 1=EW/IN, 10=TY/IN. The old SERP-extension `purchase_order_id` FK column is no longer present (link via `serp_purchase_order_stock_picking_rel` / `origin`).
- **`serp_stock_move`** — `state` enum has all 7 Odoo states: `enum('draft','waiting','confirmed','partially_available','assigned','done','cancel')` (the old seeder collapse of `partially_available` → `assigned` was removed; `seeding/scripts/seeders/_seed_stock.py`). `quantity_done` is a **computed, non-stored** field (`stock_move.py` `_compute_quantity_done`) **with a working inverse** (`_set_quantity_done`), not a column — editing `serp_stock_move_line.qty_done` is the safer explicit route. `product_qty` (decimal(16,4)) is a stored compute converting `product_uom_qty` into the product's native UoM (mirrors Odoo's `product_uom._compute_quantity`). `component_order_id` → `component_orders` ties a move to a SugarWish component pick. Also `purchase_line_id`, `sale_line_id`, `bom_line_id`, `production_id`, `raw_material_production_id`, `unbuild_id`, `is_inventory`, `reservation_date`. (`odoo_move_id` no longer exists — `odoo_id` only.) Positive-state filters only: the 5-state list `state IN ('draft','confirmed','waiting','partially_available','assigned')` is correct on the SERP MySQL tables too. No row-level locking → duplicates if run in parallel.
- **`serp_stock_move_line`** (~15.06M) — `qty_done`.
- **`serp_stock_quant`** (~15k) — columns: `product_id`, `location_id`, `quantity`/`reserved_quantity` (DECIMAL 16,4), `in_date`, `company_id`, `inventory_quantity`, `inventory_diff_quantity`, `inventory_quantity_set`, `inventory_date`, `accounting_date`, `user_id`, `odoo_id`. **Available = `quantity` − `reserved_quantity`** (computed, no `available_quantity` column). Products are tracked only via `product_id` → `serp_product_product` (the old dual `component_id`/`receiver_product_id` columns and `odoo_quant_id` are gone).
- **`serp_stock_valuation_layer`** — `quantity`/`value` negative = outbound/COGS; `account_move_id`, `stock_landed_cost_id`, `stock_valuation_layer_id` (self-FK; no `parent_layer_id`), `remaining_qty`/`remaining_value`; same cols as Odoo + `odoo_id`. NO `sequence_number`.
- **`serp_stock_picking_type`** — `code` = direction/purpose, NOT progress.
- **`serp_product_template`** (~8,020 on `serp_app`, ~8,426 on `serp_test`; 410 `odoo_id IS NULL` on `serp_app`) — **NO `default_code` column here.**
- **`serp_product_product`** (~7,693 rows) — `default_code` (SKU) lives here, not on template. Three mutually-exclusive bridge FKs: `component_id`→components (~2,409), `buyer_product_id`→buyer_products (~710), `receiver_product_id`→receiver_products (~2,456; Integer because `receiver_products` PK is `product_id` not `id`); ~2,118 rows have NONE. All three are opaque bigint/int keys (no ORM model behind them). Also `recipe_key`. **Unified product FK target for all `serp_*` tables.**
- **`serp_purchase_order`** (~2,198 rows; 1 with `odoo_id IS NULL` at check time) — `state='purchase'` = confirmed PO (Odoo term, not "a purchase happened"; enum `draft`,`sent`,`to approve`,`purchase`,`done`,`cancel`). `partner_id` = **VENDOR**. Has `odoo_id`, `date_approve`, `effective_date` (actual arrival), `invoice_status`, `receipt_status` (varchar(16)), `mail_reception_confirmed` / `_date`, `amount_*`. The old SERP-extension columns `po_type` (`inventoried`/`service`/`blanket`/`distribution`) and `parent_blanket_po_id` do not exist on `serp_app.serp_purchase_order` (confirmed via information_schema). `serp_purchase_order_line.order_id` FK now correctly references `serp_purchase_order` (`serp_pol_order_id_fk`; the old FK-to-`purchase_order` migration-008 bug is fixed). The two-tier PO approval gate columns (`ready_for_approval_at`, `ops_approved_at`, …) do NOT exist in `serp_app` (design intent only); Serpy approvals are enforced in code by `config.py` `SERPY_APPROVAL_RULES` (`po_receiving` + `po_creation` → James Emeric only) and `SERPY_THRESHOLD_APPROVAL_RULES` (inventory adjustments/scraps netting > ±500 units → Matthew Patrick, no admin bypass); there is no `serp_approval_thresholds` table in `serp_app`. The live PO email sender is NOT yet enabled on live SERP (James used normal email for Q4 POs, 9/28).
- **`serp_res_partner`** (~308; ~298 have `odoo_id`) — role via `supplier_rank`/`customer_rank`. `weeks_on_hand` = SugarWish-added, non-standard.
- **`serp_mrp_bom`** — `type` ENUM(`normal`,`phantom`) (no third value). `/boms` filters `type='normal'`, `/kits` filters `type='phantom'`. **Two different copies — check which DB:** (a) `serp_app.serp_mrp_bom` — the normal-BOM SoT (1,913 rows; 439 phantom; 442 `odoo_id IS NULL`); (b) `laravel_live.serp_mrp_bom` — **kit SoT**, phantom-only (439 rows, 253 active, last written 2026-09-22), read by the live order path (SerpBomService on-order kit explosion; intended, don't "fix"). `serp_test` (staging) has 1,683 / 228 phantom — a count near 228 means you queried the wrong DB. Excluded from comparisons (`KNOWN_FILTERED_TABLES` filters `mrp_bom` to `active=true AND type!='phantom'`).
- **`serp_mrp_production`** (~29.6k, latest create 2026-09-29) — live MO table; new MOs are created here. **`serp_mrp_bom_line`**, **`serp_mrp_unbuild`** as Odoo.
- **`serp_account_move`** (36 columns live) — `purchase_order_ids` is a **Many2many through the Odoo-style junction** `serp_account_move_purchase_order_rel` (~1,877 rows; `account_move.py:141-147`), not a direct FK. `serp_account_move_line` uses **`product_id`** (`component_id` `action_reverse` bug fixed 2026-03-05; no `component_id` in the accounting models); filter `display_type='product'` for real goods; `is_anglo_saxon_line` = system COGS (not manual). `debit`/`credit`/`balance` are **NOT lossy floats** — SERP's `fields.Float` is Decimal-backed and maps to `DECIMAL(precision, scale)` (`serp_orm/fields.py:326`, `:344`), `digits=(16,4)`. `payment_state` is a **stored compute** (`account_move.py:122-131`, `_compute_payment_state` `:265-302`); `move_type`/`state` enums as Odoo. Move-level `amount_residual` is a plain field (`account_move.py:119`) the reconciliation engine deliberately does not update — see the `payment_state` caveat.
- **`serp_res_company`** — exactly 1 row, id=1 (`odoo_id`=1); `company_id` always 1. **`serp_res_users`** (55 rows; group-based RBAC via **`serp_res_groups`**: group ids mirror Odoo's `res.groups` ids — check `full_name`: base.group_user=1, base.group_erp_manager=2, base.group_portal=9, stock.group_stock_manager=42), `serp_audit_logs`. User info from Odoo/SERP/Laravel was consolidated into one SERP user table (2026-09-28); the `serp_users` bridge was dropped 2026-09-26 (commit `feac4d1f4`), so the `res_users` family in `serp_app` is the ONLY user table (Retool `serp_users` is legacy, last updated 2026-04-23).
- **`serp_product_supplierinfo`** — dual local FKs + parallel Odoo FKs (`odoo_partner_id`, `odoo_product_id`); `delay`=lead-time days. `components.odoo_id` (Laravel) is **varchar** and synthetic. The per-component darklaunch switch `components.inventory_source` enum (`'odoo'`/`'serp'`) was renamed **`inventory_source(deleted)`** on both `components` and `component_orders` at cutover (WW-2994) — the old name throws "Unknown column"; dead, ignore. Likewise the Laravel-side Odoo flags/columns (`ec_order.oddo_synchronized`, `component_imported`, `ship_date_odoo_synchronized`, `items.odoo_sync`, `receiver_products.inventory_qty`/`odoo_inventory`/`mi_inventory_qty`, `components.inventory_quantity`) are `(deleted)`-renamed; the post-cutover ingest flag is `ec_order.serp_sync`, and sellable inventory is the view `receiver_product_inventory` (fed by `serp_published_stock`, 4,865 rows; `serp_published_kits` 635 rows / 174 buyer products — rebuilt inside `serp_order_queue_worker`: stock reconcile every 600s, kits every 3600s via the `serp_publish_kits_requests` queue).
- **SERP-native (non-`odoo_id`) tables in `serp_app` worth knowing (all live 2026-09-29):**
  - **WCC / VineBox inventory ledger** — `serp_wcc_ledger_baseline` (per-SKU baseline qty/date), `serp_wcc_ledger_finale_snapshot` (Finale on-hand/available snapshots), `serp_wcc_ledger_vb_shipment` (Vinebox ShipStation shipments), `serp_wcc_ledger_issue_status` (per-SKU issue status), `serp_wcc_kit_build` (kit-build entries); CRUD in `backend/crud/crud_wcc_ledger*.py` (refactored to SERP-only in PR #1454).
  - **Order-queue support** — `order_queue_activity`, `order_queue_backlog_snapshot` (pending orders/shipments/cancellations per run), `order_queue_quarantine`, `order_queue_failure`; **`serp_publish_requests`** (product-level publish queue, sibling of `serp_publish_kits_requests`).
  - **Serpy** — `serpy_op_rules` (DB-backed op-rule chunks, `chunk_id` PK; `SERPY_OP_RULES_FROM_DB` flag, fails open to in-code seeds) + `serpy_op_rule_history` (audit of rule edits); its `op_types` still hold pre-rename `odoo_*` names (normalized by `LEGACY_OP_TYPE_ALIASES`).
  - **Odoo-model tables not listed above** — `serp_stock_putaway_rule` (298), `serp_stock_scrap` (~1.6k), `serp_stock_valuation_adjustment_lines` (landed-cost adjustments), `serp_entity_field_change` (~229k chatter field diffs, hangs off `serp_mail_message`), `serp_ir_exports`/`serp_ir_exports_line`.
  - **Other** — `forecast_reserve_cohorts`, `serp_report_watermarks`, quality forms `serp_fmea_study` / `serp_a3_form` / `serp_aar_form` (created Sept 2026, mostly empty), `serp_cron` (57 rows).
- **Auto-increment / naming leftovers:** `serp_stock_location` and `serp_stock_picking_type` `id` columns carry `auto_increment`; **`serp_res_currency.id` still lacks it** (`extra` empty), so a bare INSERT there still 1048s. M2M rel tables are now `serp_`-prefixed (`serp_purchase_order_stock_picking_rel`, `serp_stock_move_line_consume_rel`; formerly unprefixed `purchase_order_stock_picking_rel` / `stock_move_line_consume_rel`).
- **Fingerprints/meta:** `_migrations` (schema ledger; timestamp versions). `serp_darklaunch_meta` was dropped 2026-08-03 and `serp_shadow_meta` was local-dev only (`shadow_cutover_at`=2026-05-08); the old `darklaunch_cutover_at` values (prod 2026-06-04 09:27:20, staging 2026-06-03 11:52:27, primary 2026-05-30 14:14:26 UTC) are history.

### Declared Data Assumptions registry — SERP's guard against SILENT data breaks

**`backend/data_assumptions/registry.yaml`** (checker: `backend/workers/data_assumptions/`) is a versioned list of invariants SERP's code RELIES ON — an id is never NULL, a join always resolves, a sort key is always set, a state list is exhaustive. A cron runs each entry's `check_sql` on a schedule, compares to `expect`, and posts any BROKEN assumption to **#jack-test**. **If you are planning or reviewing a SERP change, check whether it needs an entry** — this is a real, enforced gate (`creating-pr` step 4c blocks the PR; `pre-pr-review`/`review-pr`/`code-review` flag a missing entry as a CONCERN).

- **The trigger is the FAILURE MODE, not the file extension.** An entry is needed when a change makes correctness newly depend on a data shape that would fail **SILENTLY** — wrong numbers, wrong order, wrong grouping, rows quietly missing — rather than **LOUDLY** (an exception). A break that RAISES needs no entry: the exception is already the alarm. Silent breaks are what nothing else catches — no test, no typecheck, no error in the logs.
- **NOT `.sql`-only — this is the misconception the registry's own history proves.** It was created 2026-07-08 and took **exactly ONE** assumption from a feature PR in its first ~9 days, because `source_query` had to resolve under `backend/sql/` AND every prompt keyed on "does the diff touch `backend/sql/*.sql`". Most SERP data access is ORM/RPC or Python-built SQL, so the registry was structurally capped and the gate passed silently on the majority of PRs. **Fixed 2026-07-17 (SERP PR #531):** `source_query` now ALSO resolves **repo-root-relative** (`backend/serp_orm/models/sale_order.py`, `backend/services/order_management/sql.py`), and all four build-path prompts key on the silent-failure test instead. PR #534 extended the same test to the three review gates.
- **The tell to look for:** a new `ORDER BY` / `GROUP BY` / `CASE` / `JOIN` / filter over a column the code now trusts.
- **Canonical case — WW-1960** (`39c7b6bd`): edited `backend/services/order_management/sql.py` (**Python — no `.sql` anywhere**) to sort orders by gift tier via `size_names.sort_key`. A NULL/0 there doesn't raise; it silently mis-orders the Order Management grid AND the printed slips PDF, so the wrong gift goes in the wrong box. Entry: `size_names_gift_tiers_have_positive_sort_key` (db `laravel`).
- **Entry shape:** `name` (unique), `author` (required, non-empty), `db` (exactly ONE of `odoo` / `odoo_staging` / `laravel` / `wishdesk` / `retool` / `serp` — an assumption never spans DBs; these are POOL names: `serp` always routes to the live `serp_app` pool and `laravel` to live Laravel, in every environment; `wishdesk` has no pool wired yet), `check_sql` (or `check_sql_file`) returning ONE numeric column aliased **`n`**, `expect` (an int, or `{op, value}`), `source_query`, `description`. **Post-cutover twin rule:** every `odoo`/`odoo_staging` entry REQUIRES a `serp_check_sql` (or `serp_check_sql_file`) MySQL twin, and the cron always runs the twin against `serp` (live `serp_app`) instead of the Odoo check, since Odoo is no longer the source. Validate with `python scripts/validate_registry.py` (fast, no DB); `tests/unit/test_data_assumptions_registry.py` runs it in `npm run test`, so a dangling path fails CI.
- **Dedupe before adding:** two entries are duplicates if they'd catch the SAME break, even worded differently or on a different `source_query` — **extend** the existing entry, never clone it (a near-copy double-alerts and drifts out of sync when only one is fixed).
- **`check_sql` honors the heavy-query rule:** POSITIVE state list `state IN ('draft','confirmed','waiting','partially_available','assigned')`, never a negated `NOT IN`; add an indexed window + LIMIT. The **5-state list is correct on the SERP MySQL tables too** (`serp_stock_move.state` includes `partially_available`; the earlier 4-state advice was wrong and the seeder override is removed). `serp_stock_picking.state` is a genuine 6-state enum with no `partially_available` — real Odoo parity. `text()`/asyncmy landmines apply: `%`→`%%`, no `:word`, no reserved-word aliases.
