---

## Laravel (`laravel_live` / `manage`, MySQL) — E-Commerce & Order Domain

`laravel_live` = SugarWish PRODUCTION app DB (SSH tunnel): ~4.92M `giftcards_card`, ~4M `ec_order`, ~150K `company`. It holds the full Laravel schema AND **70 `serp_*` tables** (live-counted 2026-09-29) — mostly a legacy, sparse bridge copy; only `serp_published_kits` and `serp_published_stock` are live-fed (see Inventory below). **NOT SERP-only, and NOT "the SERP database"** — live SERP data is in `serp_app`. **`sku_type`/`is_core` real values exist ONLY here.**

`manage` = Laravel **staging** (~15% of prod by `ec_order` rows; staging twin + SERP ORM upstream). Staging-only tables: `gift_card_processing_progress`, `label_generation_logs`, `box_images`. Seed source for `serp_staging_*`. SERP auth in `manage.serp_res_users`. **In `manage`, ALL `receiver_products` default to `sku_type='legacy'`, `is_core=0` — NOT real classification.** Never infer prod state from `manage`. (Staging usually leads prod: schema/index differences between `manage` and `laravel_live` are the normal staging-first steady state, not a failed migration.)

**Post-cutover state (2026-09-26 SERP cutover; live-verified in `laravel_live` 2026-09-29):** the old Laravel→Odoo path is history. Odoo is frozen. New orders are ingested into SERP by **`serp_order_queue_worker.py`** (reads `ec_order`/`preselect_orders` rows where `serp_sync=0`, writes `serp_app`/`serp_test` only, sets `odoo_id` NULL). Every Odoo-facing Laravel column was renamed with a `(deleted)` suffix (listed under "Deprecated `(deleted)` columns"). **Planned, NOT done (owner Manish, 9/29 SERP Touchbase — target state):** drop the `serp_*` tables from `laravel_live` except `serp_published_kits`/`serp_published_stock`; remove inventory transactions + deprecated columns + related Laravel code; remove the Odoo inbound/outbound sidecars.

### Order Domain (the gifting flow)

Flow: buyer checks out (`buyer_orders`) → creates gifts (`giftcards_card`, one per recipient) → recipient redeems → shipment (`ec_order`). Pre-curated direct-ship uses `preselect_orders`.

#### `giftcards_card` (~4.9M)

CENTRAL hub = one gift to ONE recipient. **PK is `card_id` (NOT `id`; no `increment_id`).** Gift = **choose-your-own credit**, NOT a stored-value card or pre-selected box. Source of truth for sender/receiver email.

- `card_status` tinyint: **2=REDEEMED/active (~80%), 1=SENT/awaiting (~329K), 0=OPEN/UNREDEEMED/canceled (~669K), 4=cancelled-voided (~3K)**.
- `card_amount`, `card_balance`, `paid`, `card_type` enum(`print`/`email`/`offline`/`sms`), `delivery_method` enum(`email`/`message`/`print`/`wishlink`/`sms`; `'wishlink'` identifies a WishLink), `product_type` int (13='Customize a Sugarwish' ~3.2M), `product_sku`, `card_code` (unique redemption), `expiration_date`, `redeem_only`, `wishlink`, `reason` (bounce/blocked/dropped/spamreport), follow-ups, `autodonation`/`maw_autodonate_date`, `hold_until_date`.
- LINKS: `company_id`, `user_id`→users (sender), `order_id`→buyer checkout, `branding_record_id`, `proposal_id`, `product_id`/`product_configuration_id`.
- **`ec_order.giftcards_card_id = giftcards_card.card_id` (NOT `.id`)** — 1-to-MANY fan-out (reships; one card → up to 13,719 `ec_order` rows).

#### `ec_order` (~4M, ~585K in some snapshots)

Redeemed RECEIVER order — one row = one physical shipment to one recipient. **PK is `id`.** Real data only in `laravel_live`; structurally present but **EMPTY (0 rows)** in SERP/replica DBs (SERP's own copy is `serp_sale_order` in `serp_app`).

- `increment_id` (bigint public order number, range ~200000001..20019817045; cross-system key — was the bridge to Odoo, now `serp_stock_picking.sale_id`), `order_id` (parent checkout, the `component_orders` join key — NOT increment_id), `giftcards_card_id`→`giftcards_card.card_id`.
- **`size` is MISNAMED — it holds `buyer_products.id`** (NOT a size). Join: `ec_order.product_type=buyer_products.product_type AND ec_order.size=buyer_products.id`. **GC-order collision**: low `buyer_products.id` values (41, 61-65, 90-91, 116, 137, 353, 923) collide with old disabled `receiver_products` ids → wrong candy on custom-shop slip. **NO `recipient_email` column** (querying errors).
- `status` varchar (**`pending` ~99% = giftcard issued NOT redeemed, NOT "awaiting payment"**; `processing`, `complete`, `shipped`, `canceled`), `delivery_status` (NULL/`delivered`/`returned`), `product_type` (there is NO `product_sku` column on `ec_order`; SKUs live on `items`).
- **`sw_fulfill`** tinyint: 1=SugarWish in-house (~559K), 0=vendor/dropship (~584K), NULL=legacy (~2.8M). NOT a shipment-status flag.
- **`serp_sync`** tinyint (the CURRENT SERP ingest flag; twin `ship_date_serp_synchronized`; same pair on `preselect_orders`): **0=placed/not yet ingested, 1=ingested/reserved in SERP, 2=archived-SKU parity (mirrors old `oddo_synchronized=2`), 4=cancelled-and-synced (drops out of the `serp_sync=0` detection pool; arrived with WW-3013's backfill), 5=bypass (`bypass_odoo` product types, e.g. Gift Cards/Vinebox/Bouqs)**. Backfill mapping: `CASE WHEN oddo_synchronized=5 THEN 5 WHEN =2 THEN 2 WHEN =1 AND status='canceled' THEN 4 WHEN =1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END`. Live recent mix (2026-09-29, last ~200K `ec_order` ids): 1 ≈177K, 0 ≈5.9K, 4 ≈5.7K, 5 ≈2.2K, 2 = 212 (last 2026-09-03). **Load-bearing for inventory correctness**, not a reporting flag: the demand leg of the inventory views tests `ec_order.serp_sync` (unsynced = `IN (0,2)` over a 30-day window; NEVER `serp_sale_order`, a ~19-row stub in `laravel_live` last written 2026-05-27 — a `NOT EXISTS` against it matches every order and drives `sellable_qty` negative). Treating the flag as only `0/1/5` miscounts cancelled orders as pending.
- **`oddo_synchronized`** (history: the legacy Odoo-sync flag; intentional misspelling 'oddo' — match it exactly if you ever query archived/staging schemas). ⚠️ **In `laravel_live` the column is now named `oddo_synchronized(deleted)` — the old name throws `Unknown column`; don't treat it as live** (likewise `ship_date_odoo_synchronized(deleted)`, `component_imported(deleted)`); on `preselect_orders` too — Odoo was its only writer and it is frozen. Historical values (Odoo was the only writer, advancing 0→2→1 via `/api/odoo` callbacks; `IndexController` flipped a row `0→2` when it handed the batch to Odoo's pull — a claim/lease, not "partial", ~64–166 in-flight): **0=not started (~77–81K at freeze), 1=synced (~3.88–3.97M), 2=claimed/exposed (1 row at rest), 5=vendor/bypass (~17–19K)**. **Value `3` (stuck/errored/archived-SKU) is reported by some sources but does NOT exist in prod** — live 2026-09-11 had only 0/1/2/5; don't filter on `=3`. `manage` carries the same `(deleted)`-renamed columns (checked 2026-09-29).
- **`avatax_status`** enum: `not-processed`/`processed`/`sent` (~2.6M)/`skipped`/`adjusted`/`voided`/`cancelled`/`locked` — Avalara TAX state, NOT shipping state. Companions `sales_tax`, `taxable_total`, `co_ship_tax`.
- **Print state moved off `ec_order`:** `is_printed`, `production_slip_batch`, `hold_until_date`, `insert_id`, `seasonal_box`, `theme_id`, `is_pdf_generated`, `physical_branding_printed` live on **`receiver_production_slips`** (join via `order_id`; live-confirmed 2026-09-29 — `ec_order` has none of them). `is_printed` multi-state int: 0=not printed, 1=printed/in-fulfillment (auto-removes from print group), 2=Issue (missing boxcard / no high-res image), 3=address/label-blocked / on-hold → **reset** after the API fix (not terminal), 5=in print group. `preselect_orders` still carries its own `is_printed`/`production_slip_batch`/`insert_id`. The old `ec_order.is_printed` survives only in archived schemas (`sugarwish_live_cme_temp`, `sugarwish_manage_new_prepicks`).
- `ship_date` (DATE — returns as UTC timestamp via mysql2, off-by-1 in MT if Date-converted; use `formatDateOnly()`), `merchandise_selections` JSON (recipient choices; **NULL for all rows historically** — WW-158 blocking), `test_order`, tracking fields, `source`, `vendor_imported`.
- **`ec_order` has DB triggers with huge blast radius** — a broken trigger jeopardizes ALL insert/update/delete (WW-142); emergency lever: Munyr can disable triggers.
- `receiver_orders` is **NOT** the shipment table — `ec_order` is (70+ columns). `receiver_orders` only tracks notification/survey state.

#### `preselect_orders` (~210K)

Direct-ship: buyer pre-selects contents + recipient address up-front (order numbers 6000+, vs receiver 200+; Odoo-era `sale_order.sw_id >= 600M`).

- `type` enum(`preselect`/`sweet-shoppe`/`sweetificate`) = ORDER CHANNEL (not status), `status` enum(`pending`/`completed`/`canceled`/`deleted`/`shipped`/`on_hold`), `master_id` (groups batch; bridge to `sale_order.sw_id` via `preselect_order_id`), `reissue_id`, `production_slip_batch`, `is_printed`, `is_pdf_generated`, `avatax_status`, `serp_sync` + `ship_date_serp_synchronized` (current; legacy `oddo_synchronized(deleted)`/`ship_date_odoo_synchronized(deleted)`), receiver\_\* address, `seasonal_box`, `insert_id`, `theme_id`. **70,799 orphan `master_id` not in `preselect_master_ids`**; 387 NULL master_id. Recent `serp_sync` mix skews to 4 (cancelled-and-synced, WW-3013 backfill).
- ⚠️ **Preselects from the WishDesk proposal system no longer carry `insert_id`** (Anna, 2026-07-23) — NULL `insert_id` on new preselects is expected, so the WW-1610 "insert_id is empty" production-slip Slack alerts fire for them (esp. test orders; Bilal to suppress test-order alerts). Don't treat a NULL `insert_id` on a proposal-sourced preselect as data corruption.
- `preselect_master_ids`: ID allocator only (id PK + timestamps, ~27,951 rows, ids 700000001+).

#### `buyer_orders` (~57K-1.3M)

Checkout HEADER (one purchase → many gifts; one buyer_order → MANY giftcards_card). `status` varchar (`complete`/`queued`/`processing`/`closed`/`not-applicable`/payment*review/paypal*\*) and `preselect_status` (SEPARATE independent state machine, mostly NULL) — two independent state machines. `preselect_master_id`. `product_sku` is free-text cart text — always use `ec_order` for attribution.

#### `items` (~15M)

Order line items — one row per chosen flavor, belongs to `ec_order` (recipient's chosen flavors). `order_type` enum(`receiver-order`/`preselect-order`/`sweet-shoppe-order`), `product_sku`, `product_id`, `qty_ordered`, `parent_sku`/`parent_name` (kit bundling — items sharing parent_sku form a kit), `vendor_cost`. **`odoo_sync` is now `odoo_sync(deleted)`** (history: 0=not synced, 1=syncing, 2=synced, 5=unknown; rows with 0/1 = NOT-yet-in-Odoo, were deducted from available inventory). **`items` has no `serp_sync`** — item-level sync state is read from the PARENT order's `serp_sync`.

#### `component_orders` (~2.6M)

Order-time exploded BOM (NOT a static recipe) — box/packaging component lines, actively written (latest row 2026-09-29). `order_id`→`ec_order.order_id`, `order_type` (varchar; `receiver-order`/`preselect-order`/`merchandise` — NOT an order-table selector: `merchandise` (mug + mug-box, live since Apr 2026) splits across BOTH `ec_order` and `preselect_orders`, so each demand branch joins on the order TABLE and never filters `order_type`), `component_id`, `component_sku`/`component_name` (denormalized), `quantity`, `location_id`, `accessory_images_id`.

- ⚠️ **`inventory_source` (enum `odoo`/`serp`) is now `inventory_source(deleted)`** on both `component_orders` and `components` (renamed at cutover, WW-2994). History: **`inventory_source='serp'` was always dead data here** — measured in `serp_test` 2026-07-17: 73,342 rows, ALL `'odoo'`, ZERO `'serp'`, including all 188 `merchandise` rows. The only writer that ever produced `'serp'` lines was the merchandise `order_queue_worker.py`, default-disabled its whole life and DELETED in PR #316 (2026-07-01). Don't infer the merchandise pipeline is live from `order_type='merchandise'` rows; a `WHERE inventory_source` filter no longer works at all (column renamed).
- History: the 3 `'serp'` components (ids 2578/2579/2580, MiiR white drinkware) never had `component_orders` rows — the column on `components` was an independent switch that did not propagate to order lines.

### Inventory — sellable availability, prelist, reservations

- **Inventory has no single SoT.** Sellable (SA) availability is published from SERP into `laravel_live`; raw material (RM) + on-hand + valuation live in `serp_app` (`serp_stock_quant`, SVL). The Odoo→Laravel `SynchronizeOdooInventory` sync is dead (Odoo frozen).
- **`receiver_product_inventory` (VIEW in `laravel_live`, the sellable source)** — currently **v6** (migration `2026_09_16_021813`, keys unsynced demand on `serp_sync`; the component twin is v6 too), 9 columns (live `describe_table` 2026-09-29): `receiver_product_id`, `on_hand_qty`, `reserved_qty`, `unsynced_demand_qty`, `unreserved_outbound_qty`, `reservation_hold_qty`, `prelist_qty`, `available_qty`, `sellable_qty` (no `qty_available`). Fed from **`serp_published_stock`** (BASE TABLE in `laravel_live`; columns `product_id`, `receiver_product_id`, `component_id`, `on_hand_qty`, `reserved_qty`, `unreserved_outbound_qty`, `computed_at`; ~4,865 rows, `computed_at` refreshed 2026-09-29; twin `serp_published_kits` ~635 rows / 174 buyer products with columns `buyer_product_id`, `recipe_key`, `component_id`, `quantity`, `sequence`, `computed_at` — both rebuilt inside `serp_order_queue_worker`: stock reconcile every ~600s, kits every ~3600s via the `serp_publish_kits_requests` queue) plus a 30-day unsynced-demand window (`serp_sync IN (0,2)`); `sellable_qty` = `available_qty` + `prelist_qty`, `reservation_hold_qty` comes from `inventory_reservations`. It does NOT exist in `serp_test`/`serp_app`'s `information_schema`. **`product_id` = SugarWish id, NOT Odoo product_id.** Its twin **`component_product_inventory`** (VIEW, same 9-column contract keyed on `component_id`; holds & prelist stay literal 0 so `sellable_qty == available_qty`; available = sellable for components; demand from `component_orders`) is deployed in `laravel_live` (the older "not deployed anywhere" note is stale). Both view bodies are canonical in `EXTRA_VIEWS` in `seeding/scripts/export_manage_schema.py`; DDL also in repo `migrations/20260721_inventory_views_receiver_and_component_base.sql` (hand-run only, NOT auto-deployed). View v2 (2026-07-21, PR #588) is rebased onto a `receiver_products`/components BASE (not quant-driven): every product gets a row even with zero `serp_stock_quant` internal rows (fixed prelisted/Laravel-only SKUs reading 0 in Parallel Test). Every publish upserts **including zeros** and **preserves sign**. `manage` may have diverged view/index definitions (WW-2496 perf indexes `idx_co_order_component_qty` on `component_orders (order_id, component_id, quantity)` and `idx_preselect_created` on `preselect_orders (created_at)` were on `manage` only as of 2026-08-14; both now exist on `laravel_live` too).
- **`receiver_products.inventory_qty`, `mi_inventory_qty`, `odoo_inventory`, `location_2_status`, `inventory_updated_at`, `sw_inventory_updated_at` are now all `…(deleted)`-suffixed** (live `describe_table` 2026-09-29): the legacy per-location qty columns are gone from active use; `odoo_inventory` ("West Coast Qty", had no replacement feed) is deprecated. Same for `components` (`inventory_quantity(deleted)`, `status(deleted)`, `drop_level(deleted)`, `mi_inventory_qty(deleted)`, `location_2_status(deleted)`). Don't write queries against the un-suffixed names.
- Still-present legacy tables: **`odoo_inventory`** (BASE TABLE, 5,338 rows, last write 2026-09-26 10:16 — frozen), `component_inventory` (BASE TABLE — `id, component_id, location_id, inventory_qty, created_at, updated_at`; 0 rows, never updated), **`inventory_transactions`** (68,944 rows, still written as of 2026-09-29 10:45; removal is part of the planned Manish cleanup), `component_inventory_transactions`, **`inventory_reservations`** (~5,000 rows, latest 2026-09-29; feeds `v_inventory_reservations_automation` and the reservation `hold` leg), `v_inventory_reservations_automation` (VIEW).
- **Prelisting** (`receiver_products.prelist_qty`, int NOT NULL default 0; added 2026-07-01; 501 products have `prelist_qty>0`): qty Sophie wants sellable before an MO is packed. SERPY subtracts it (floored at 0) when it MOs the product, logging each drain to **`prelist_qty_log`** — that audit table is in **`serp_app`** (not `laravel_live`). Reporting must SUBTRACT `prelist_qty` to avoid double-counting; it never touched Odoo, so costing stays correct. (History: the Odoo→Laravel inventory sync cron used to ADD it into `inventory_qty`.)

### Product Catalog (two-sided)

| Table               | What it is                                             | Key facts                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `buyer_products`    | What the **sender buys** (gift size/box/credit SKU)    | ~1,216 (~697 in older snapshots); `odoo_id` populated on ALL rows; `type`: `ecard`(~906)/`sweetshoppe`/`sweetificate`; `default_kit`→`kits.id`. **Dual-homed**: Retool synced copy (legacy) + `laravel_live` table                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `receiver_products` | Individual candy/snack **flavors the recipient picks** | ~5,233 rows (live 2026-09-29); `product_id`/`sugarwish_id` = join to `serp_product_template.sugarwish_id` (Odoo `product_template.sugarwish_id` for frozen history). Sellable qty now comes from the `receiver_product_inventory` view (see Inventory). **`product_id` = SugarWish id, NOT Odoo product_id.** **Non-`id` PK = `product_id`**                                                                                                                                                                                   |

- `ecard` type ≠ a greeting-card image. `kits`/`component_kits` = Laravel-side BOM (source of truth for component recipes, as `laravel_live.serp_mrp_bom` + SerpBomService on-order kit explosion — the intended current kit SoT); distinct from and can diverge from `serp_app.serp_mrp_bom` (normal BOMs) and frozen Odoo `mrp_bom`. **A zero/small kit/BOM count from `serp_app` is the wrong DB, not absence.**
- **`users`** (sugarwish) — sender/buyer accounts. `account_type`: ''/Guest/Onboarding/Personal/Company/Both. Company relationship is **M:N via `company_users_pivot`** — **no single `company_id` on users**. Column `oddo_api_access_token` exists (legacy). User info from Odoo/SERP/Laravel was consolidated into one SERP table (2026-09-28).

#### `buyer_products` (~1,216 rows)

BUYER-FACING catalog = what SENDER buys (box/credit/SKU; e.g. `candy-mini`, `gift-cards-25-pcs2`).

- PK `id`. **`odoo_id` = `'500'`+`buyer_products.id`** (e.g. id=42→50042; ~12/1215 mismatch) = the historical SugarWish↔Odoo link via `product_template.sugarwish_id`.
- `sku`, `type` enum(`ecard`/`sweetshoppe`/`sweetificate`/custom-mug/cert), `category`, `price`, `number_of_candies` (picks granted: 2/4/6/8/12), `product_type`, **`default_kit`** (FK→kits.id, year-round) / **`seasonal_kit`** (seasonal override; if seasonal_box use seasonal_kit else default_kit), `size_name_id`, `item_multiplier` (**=1 for Grand SKUs**, NOT 3 — Grand ships 3 boxes via kit_id=917 component qty=2), `sw_fulfill`, `location_id`, `accessory_id`, `disabled`/`deleted_at`.

#### `receiver_products` (~5,233 rows)

Individual treats recipient chooses. **PK is `product_id` (NOT `id`)**.

- **`sku_type`** varchar(16) NOT NULL, exactly 3 values — live 2026-09-29: **`core` 398, `seasonal` 25, `legacy` 4,810 (default)**. **`is_core`** tinyint(1): rule `is_core <=> sku_type='core'` (enforced in APP CODE only, NO DB trigger; helper `core_flag_for_sku_type()`; historically 1 live exception product_id 4177 SA-46-033); all 398 core rows are `is_core=1`.
- `status` varchar (`enabled`/`disabled`), `archive` tinyint (PRIMARY archive flag), `deleted_at` (soft delete — 95 rows once had `status='enabled'` BUT `deleted_at IS NOT NULL`, contradictory), `drop_level` int nullable (floor below which auto-disabled; NULL/0-500, outliers 4987/5886; n8n falls back to `feature_attributes` id=1 default), `threshold` (~2× drop_level, alert trigger).
- `prelist_qty` (see Inventory), `inventory_link` (parent product_id for cascading disables — set NULL on combo-line children in Apr-2026 migration), `location_id` (FK locations; 2,468 of 5,233 populated; SERPY create paths write it and pin new `-E` parents to Englewood=1 via `_force_englewood_for_e_sku`; NOT the deduction source — that is `serp_stock_location`), `vendor_id`, `vendor_cost` (decimal 8,2), `product_id` (= `sugarwish_id`), `product_type`, `tango_utid`, `odoo_id`. Also `hs_code`, `country_of_origin`, `customs_description`, `exclude_international`, `show_on_flavors`. **Duplicate SKUs exist** (SA-01-026-A as product_ids 1706+4499; 13 component SKUs duplicated) — use `product_id`/`id` for joins, NOT SKU.

#### `components` (~2,625 rows)

Raw materials / packaging. PK `id` (bigint unsigned). `sku`, `prod_slip_sku`, `name`, **`odoo_id` = FABRICATED `'800'`+`id` varchar** (NOT real Odoo PK — joining to Odoo PK matches wrong rows; real bridge is `product_template.sugarwish_id`), `company_id`, `number_of_picks`, `start_date`/`end_date`, `hide` (0=visible/1=hidden, PRIMARY toggle — no `deleted_at`), `shelf_id`, `tax_code_id`, `image_url`, `location_override`. `inventory_source(deleted)` and the other `(deleted)`-suffixed columns: see Inventory. **58-97 rows have swapped sku/name fields** (B-AAA-HAL-22-\*, seasonal).

#### `kits` (~1,157 rows, ~1,092 active) / `component_kits` (junction)

- `kits`: `id`, `name`, `buyer_product_id` (FK, nullable; one buyer_product → 3-6+ kits), `company_id`, `deleted_at` (soft delete). 89% single-component (just the B-\* box).
- `component_kits`: `kit_id`→kits, `component_id`→components, `quantity` (no `hide`/`recipe_key` columns here; `hide` is on `components` — set `hide=1` old / `hide=0` new when swapping; `recipe_key` lives on `serp_published_kits` and in `branding_records.merchandise` recipe snapshots).

#### `bill_of_materials` — does NOT exist in `laravel_live` or `manage` (checked 2026-09-29; formerly documented as an empty designed-but-never-populated table). BOM data is `serp_mrp_bom` / `component_kits`.

### Branding / Custom (Laravel)

#### `branding_records` (~6.4K in manage / ~18.8K in prod)

Sleeve/mug/awning assets. THREE JSON fields:

- `physical_branding` JSON: `{entries:[{type:'sleeve', box_sku, box_id, template_id, logo_url, logo_override, logo_colors, render:{s3_url 50DPI, thumbnail_url, mockup_url, print_url 1200DPI per-order UUID NULL-until-cron, print_rendered_at}, buyer_product_ids[]}], _metadata:{proposal_id}}`. NO active/primary/status flag — all entries equal; fulfillment filters to entry whose `buyer_product_ids` CONTAINS `ec_order.size`. box_sku vocab: `a_mini/a_small/a_medium/a_large/a_xlarge/c_1/c_small/c_medium/h_small/c_3`.
- `digital_branding` JSON: `awning.canvas_width/height`, `note_card_logo`.
- `merchandise` JSON: `items[].designs[].print_image_url`, `recipe_snapshot` (`recipe_id`, `recipe_key`="{N}cube-bp{buyer_product_id}", `outer_box_sku`, `additional_components:{add:[],remove:[]}`).
- Approval columns `physical_branding_approval`/`digital_branding_approval`/`merchandise_approval` tinyint: **0=default/unapproved, 1=needs review, 2=pending CS, 3=approved**. `print_render_status` enum(`pending`/`not_required`/`rendered`/`failed`).

#### `design_boxes` (WishDesk DB, 17 rows — NOT in `laravel_live`/`manage`)

`size_name_id`, `sku` (a_mini/a_medium/etc.), `product_type_ids` JSON. **Hot Sauce 2-sleeve bug**: a_medium/a_mini/a_xlarge still list type 46 in product_type_ids while a_small/a_large cleaned to [6,49] → buyer_product (type 46) matches BOTH a_medium AND h_medium.

### Other Laravel

- `themes` (recipient-facing branding; lives in `laravel_live`), `ecard_designs` (`inserts`=FK to box-card SKU; NULL breaks PDF cron → is_printed=2; `sku`, `full_image`, `occasion_id`, `theme`, `type` enum legacy/standard/html), `ecard_uploads`, `accessory_images` (mug: `review_status` 0=Main/pending, 1=Creative/in-review, 2=CS-review; `needs_customer_review`, `priority` unused, `metadata.workflow_history`, no `reviewed_by`/`reviewed_at` cols — metadata is only audit; **no index** → full scan), `accessory_image_giftcards_card` junction, `proposals` (lives in the WishDesk DB, ~185K rows — not in `laravel_live`; `details_json` multi-writer; 8 required: sender_id, product_id, product_sku, product_configuration_id, quantity, delivery_method, price, product_selections-nonempty), `locations` (id 1=Englewood, 2=Taylor, 3=City Pop, 4=Simple Times, 5=WCC, 6=Vista, 7=Becket and Quill, 8=Poppin & Mixin — Taylor (id=2) is retired at cutover; the old per-location qty columns `inventory_qty`/`mi_inventory_qty`/`location_2_status` are `(deleted)`), `product_type` (incl. `bypass_odoo` flag — product types that skip the ERP → `serp_sync=5`)/`product_lines`/`size_names`, `domain` (~80K rows email→logo), `company` (test_account flag), `manage_audit_logs` (~7K rows, 14 audited tables; receiver_products/components/kits NOT audited), `prelist` audit lives in `serp_app`.
- `serp_*` in `laravel_live` (70 tables — `serp_account_*`, `serp_stock_*`, `serp_mrp_*`, `serp_purchase_order*`, `serp_sale_order*`, `serp_product_*`, `serp_res_*`, …): legacy bridge copy, sparse/stale (e.g. `serp_sale_order` 19 rows). Only `serp_published_kits` + `serp_published_stock` are live-fed; drop of the rest is planned (target state, above). Same-named `serp_*` tables also exist in `serp_app` with the real data — resolve WHICH database before querying.

- Laravel `origin/main` (release branches `main`/`blue`) recent load-bearing changes: WW-2994 (released 2026-09-25) stopped reading/writing the frozen Odoo inventory/sync columns and renamed them `(deleted)` (Laravel code, `Order` fillable); the `SynchronizeOdooInventory` lambda was disabled at the 9/26 Laravel release (Manish). WW-1636 (released) unified all checkout totals on `CalculateOrderTotal` (`PlaceOrder` refactored into services/placer strategies; PayPal + coupon math share it). WW-3018/WW-3291 (released 9/21) shipped personalized merchandise v1: WishDesk render client + personalize-preview endpoint, order-path commit + overlay, retry job on the `survey` queue, production-slip line; when two merchandise designs share an id the recipient's chosen design is recorded and personalized. WW-3330/3322/3426 rasterize WebP/CMYK-JPEG box cards and note-card logos before wkhtmltopdf/wkhtmltoimage (those formats broke PDF/slip rendering). WW-3422 (9/28): the WishDesk hand-off runs post-sign-in checks and clears the stale `users.logout` flag that looped staff out of WishDesk daily.
- Tables added to `laravel_live` since mid-Aug (live 2026-09-29, `information_schema` counts): `avatax_items` (~10.9M), `invoice_adjustments` (~1.1M), `transactions` (~1.02M), `sendgrid` (~1.0M), `company` (~150K), `cart_partials` (~27K) + `cart_partials_prepicks`, `live_audit_logs` (~25K), `canceled_labels` (~12.7K), `order_attribution` (~8.3K), `billing_card_attempts` (~3.6K), `product_type` (82), `split_payment_holds`/`consent_banners`/`animated_intros` (0 rows); `ww3005_reactivation_log` / `ww2939_backfill_snapshot` are one-off WW backfill snapshots.
- Laravel `origin/main` still contains dead Odoo code (`app/Sidecar/SynchronizeOdooInventory.php`, `OdooInventoryService.php`, `Http/Controllers/Odoo/**`) — removal planned (Manish), not done.

---

## WishDesk / SWAC (MySQL) — CS + CRM + Design + Billing

Not a support-only DB: a full CS + CRM + design + billing platform (internally "WishWorks"; SWAC = WishDesk). Times stored in **Mountain time, NOT UTC**. TWO app DB pools (`server/db.ts` WishDesk + `server/sugarwish-db.ts` Sugarwish) plus a CRM pool (`crmPool`/`crmDb`, `server/db.ts`). WishDesk is DOWNSTREAM — a replica that stores ids + cached snapshots; customer/company/order truth lives in SugarWish (Laravel) and, post-cutover, SERP (`serp_app`) — Odoo is frozen history.

### CRM database split (2026-09-23 cutover, WW-3329) — read this before querying any `swcrm_*` table

- Since **2026-09-23** the `swcrm_*` tables live in their OWN database: **`wishdesk_crm` on `sw-helpdesk-db`** (a separate AWS RDS instance from WishDesk's `database-1`) in production; on dev (desk2) `wishdesk_crm_dev` on the same Hetzner box as `sugarwish_wishdesk_dev`. **No statement may join the two databases** (`CrossDatabaseStatementError` when `CRM_DB_ENABLED` is on; `CRM_DB_ENABLED` is read only at process boot). Only the `swcrm_` **prefix** moved: `crm_teams` and `crm_team_members` (`routing_method` ROUND_ROBIN/LEAST_LOADED/SKILL_BASED/PRIORITY; `max_capacity`/`current_load`) stayed in WishDesk. Drizzle bypasses the router — use `crmDb`, never `db`, for a CRM table. CRM datetime columns are Mountain time (comparing to `UTC_TIMESTAMP()` looks exactly 360 minutes stale).
- **`wishdesk.swcrm_actions` (and every other `swcrm_*` table) does NOT exist in prod `wishdesk`** — live 2026-09-29: the old copies were frozen at cutover and renamed **`deleted_swcrm_*`** (WW-3281, 2026-09-25; e.g. `deleted_swcrm_actions`, `deleted_swcrm_leads`, `deleted_swcrm_z_gmail_messages`); querying a `swcrm_` name in WishDesk now fails with `ER_NO_SUCH_TABLE` (a missed call site to fix, never a table to recreate). The `deleted_swcrm_*` rows are stale frozen history, NOT live CRM data. The AWS DMS task that used to copy them into `wishdesk_crm` is disabled — truth now flows the other way. The cron once-per-period receipt table `swcrm_cron_periods` was renamed **`wishdesk_cron_periods`** (WW-3284; exists in prod wishdesk); `swcrm_cron_runs` writes land in `wishdesk_crm` while `wishdesk_cron_runs` stays in WishDesk. Only `swcrm_z_gmail_backfill_requests` (0 rows) still carries the prefix in prod `wishdesk`.
- **The hub `mcp__db__` `wishdesk` key reaches only the WishDesk DB (`sugarwish_wishdesk`); `wishdesk_crm` has no hub key.** Every CRM table/count below is therefore the last known pre-split value ("pre-split; verify in `wishdesk_crm`") — the CRM-side facts (columns, semantics) are unchanged by the move.
- Reported 2026-09-23 as WW-3319: `sw-helpdesk-db` (CRM RDS) is undersized for the CRM workload (1.5 GiB buffer pool vs ~17.5 GiB data, CRM queries far slower since the split) — expect CRM slowness there, not a query bug.
- Prod `wishdesk` now has **215 tables** (live 2026-09-29; was 179 → 197 → 215 as CRM tables were renamed and new ones added).

**WishDesk LIVE DB host — `sugarwish-manage` RDS is GONE (NXDOMAIN); live is on `database-1`.** The old `sugarwish-manage.cqqg1tfyyubp.us-east-1.rds.amazonaws.com` RDS instance was deleted/renamed — the hostname no longer resolves, so ANY connection attempt to it fails with `Unknown MySQL server host` (direct) or `Lost connection … reading initial communication packet` (through a tunnel — the bastion can't resolve it either). **Do not build tunnels to it; the host is dead, not firewalled.** The live `sugarwish_wishdesk` schema lives on **`database-1.cqqg1tfyyubp.us-east-1.rds.amazonaws.com`** (the same private RDS as `laravel_live`/`sugarwish` — bastion tunnel required, not directly reachable; migrated ~2026-05-04, actively written). The old read-only user `wishdesk-read-only-for-wd-9182-2` works on `database-1`. SWAC's local `.env` `DB_HOST_LIVE` was repointed 2026-07-23 to `localhost:13307` (SWAC's own `LIVE_SSH` tunnel → `database-1:3306`); if `mcp-db-tool-live` fails to load in a SWAC session, the usual cause is **no 13307 tunnel up yet** (start `npm run dev`, whose tunnel manager binds it) — not bad credentials. Jack's TablePlus "WishDesk Live" confirms the same target: `database-1` / `sugarwish_wishdesk` over the `forge@34.194.213.27` bastion — that IS production, no other host. ⚠️ **`branding_records` on live WishDesk is genuinely EMPTY (0 rows) — the real production state, not a bug or lost data** — proposal branding lives **inline on `proposals.physical_branding` / `proposals.digital_branding` (JSON columns)** (e.g. proposal 149378 carries ~4 KB of physical_branding, sleeve entries with S3 render/mockup URLs); `preorders` has the same inline pair. Don't hunt for a `branding_records` row by id (a "branding_record_id 7197"-style reference does not resolve to that table on live). (Do NOT confuse it with `sugarwish_manage_new_2.branding_records` — the **manage app's own, separate, actively-written** table.)

**Manage (Laravel admin) lives on Hetzner — `sw-manage-db` `5.78.203.128`, NOT AWS** (Jack, 2026-07-23). The hub's `manage` MCP connection points there (`sugarwish_manage_new_2`) — the correct, current manage DB (actively written; e.g. `branding_records` ids ≤ 3332 updated 2026-07-22). A sparse, new/in-progress `sugarwish_manage_new_3` schema exists on the same box (`new_2` is live). Manage's `branding_records` is unrelated to WishDesk's despite the identical name. Separate Hetzner box `sw-wishdesk-db` `5.78.187.176` carries `sugarwish_wishdesk_dev` — WishDesk DEV, which SWAC's confusingly-named `DB_*_MANAGE` env vars actually point at.

### Subsystems

| Prefix / table                            | Subsystem                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `swcrm_*` (now `wishdesk_crm`)            | Full sales CRM (modeled on Insightly)                                                                                                                                       |
| `swcrm_z_gmail_*` (now `wishdesk_crm`)    | Gmail mirror + AI-draft (SWIM) email assistant (20+ tables, **~525k** messages as of 2026-09-11 — the old "~155k" is 3.4x stale; pre-split)                                  |
| `orders_tickets` / `orders_ticket_events` | CS email ticketing (stays in WishDesk)                                                                                                                                      |
| `sw_billing_tickets`                      | Slack-driven billing/finance queue (stays in WishDesk)                                                                                                                      |
| `swcrm_actions` (CRM DB)                  | sales tasks/to-dos (NOT customer-facing tickets)                                                                                                                            |
| `design_*`/`ds_*`/`merchandise_*`         | ecard/custom-product design studio                                                                                                                                          |

**Three independent ticket systems:** `orders_tickets` (CS email), `sw_billing_tickets` (Slack billing), `swcrm_actions` (sales tasks, in `wishdesk_crm`). Plus `wishdesk.orders_monthly_orders` (50 rows) = managed-gifting recurring-order planner (year-grid). **"Orders" means three different things** — see Gotchas.

### WishDesk-DB tables (live-verified 2026-09-29)

- **`users`** (~76.2K) = BOTH internal staff AND synced customer mirror (split by `role`: `customer` ~74.7K, `guest` ~10.2K, `user` ~5.9K, `admin` 70, `agent` 25, `archived` 24, `system` 1). `id` = internal PK (FK target). `role_type` staff: GC/HS/super/MOD/Billing/Sidekick/test. `auth_provider`+`google_uid`, `sw_id` = FK to Laravel user id, `slackid` = staff Slack id, `brand`. DISTINCT from the CRM 'user' OBJECT (which resolves via `user_cache.user_id`).
- **`user_cache`** (~30.9K) = denormalized customer snapshot synced from Laravel; **`user_id` = SugarWish/Laravel CUSTOMER id (THE join key), NOT `users.id`**. CRM `'user'` object resolves via `user_cache.user_id`. Metrics are cached, not live. `full_name`/`email`/`phone`, `company_id`/`company_name`, `lifetime_sales`/`l90d_sales`, `credit_balance`, `gifts_per_year`, `profile_metadata` JSON, `last_synced_at`. Companions: `yoy_sales_cache` (stayed in WishDesk); `swcrm_client_profile_cache`/`swcrm_client_search_index` are CRM-DB.
- **`orders_tickets`** (**~748**; priority `HIGH` 11 / `MEDIUM` 514 / `LOW` 223) — email CS inbox, SEPARATE from live chat. `ticket_id` = VARCHAR display id `SKTKT-...` (not the int PK); children join on **`ticket_id`** (VARCHAR). Columns include `requester_name/email`, `subject`, `status`, `assignee_name/email`, `priority` varchar(20), `type`, `due_date`, `company_id` (= SugarWish corporate account)/`company_name`, `gift_concierge`, `cc` JSON, `proposal_id`, `is_read`, `merged_into_ticket_id`, and **Slack-thread columns added recently: `slack_thread_ts`, `slack_post_top` (JSON), `anyone_can_help`, `anyone_can_help_before_open`**. `orders_ticket_events.type`: filter `IN ('INBOUND_EMAIL','OUTBOUND_EMAIL','INTERNAL_NOTE')` for actual correspondence.
- **`orders_ticket_attachments`** (~2,350; 2,297 with `s3_key`, the 51 without are still exactly ids 1–51) — ticket file attachments (join on `ticket_id` VARCHAR; `event_id` links to the event, `stored_name` = disk/S3 filename). **Attachments moved to S3 (WW-2187, released 2026-08-05):** the table gained `s3_key`(varchar 700)/`s3_bucket`(varchar 255); new uploads + the backfill write to the **`sugarwish-chat`** bucket under key prefix **`order-ticket-files/`** (bucket resolved at runtime from `AWS_BUCKET_NAME` on the desk box, NOT the design/SERP buckets). A row with `s3_key` set lives in S3; `s3_key IS NULL` = local-disk-only. As of 2026-08-10 the backfill is done: **1,836 / 1,887 rows have an `s3_key`; the 51 residual are exactly ids 1–51** (oldest Feb-2026 history whose bytes are gone from every instance — permanently 404, NOT a backfill failure or an "on another instance" split). WishDesk prod runs the desk app on `/opt/desk.sugarwish.com` (the live EC2 desk box, app root holds `uploads/order-ticket-files/`), which held 1,826 of the files — the backfill must run **on the box that holds the local files** (`npx tsx server/scripts/backfill-ticket-attachments-to-s3.ts`; completion = `missing on disk: 0`). The legacy local-disk read fallback was removed (**WW-2396, released 2026-08-17**): `readAttachment()` in `server/orders-tickets/attachment-storage.ts` now serves 404 when `s3_key IS NULL`.
- **`sw_billing_tickets`** (~421) — billing/finance queue (Slack-driven); has `slack_*` columns tying each to its thread; `sw_billing_ticket_comments` ~384. **`proposals`** (~185K by `COUNT(*)` 2026-09-29; table-stats estimate ~118K is stale): config is JSON (`details_json`, `recipient_json`, `metadata`); `parent_proposal_id` = revision chain; locked version mirrors to `branding_records` (keyed `proposal_id`; empty on live — see above).
- **`live_chat_sessions`** (~98K by priority counts; table-stats estimate ~76K): `session_id` (UUID), `type` enum(CHAT/TICKET/EMAIL/CALL), `chat_mode` (BOT/AGENT), `status`, `user_id`/`agent_id`, email-threading (`thread_id`/`message_id`/`in_reply_to`/`thread_references`/`requester_email`/`cc_addresses` JSON), SLA (`first_response_at`/`hold_started_at`/`total_hold_seconds`), `priority` tinyint (**2=Corporate ~13.9K, 4=Registered ~3.6K, 6=Guest ~78.6K, 8=Unsubscribe ~1.8K**). **Email-thread bug**: matching by `thread_id` ALONE (not verifying `requester_email`) merges different customers' replies to the same bulk email into one ticket.
- `live_chat_messages` (~412K; was ~94K): `sender_type` (CLIENT/AGENT/BOT/SYSTEM), `message_content`, `message_html`, `content_format` (MARKDOWN/HTML), `source` (WIDGET/EMAIL/AGENT_UI), `draft_send_id`, `deleted_at`.
- **SLA:** `sla_settings` (key-value, only two keys: `exclude_hold_time`=true and `sla_enabled`=true — global toggles; the per-priority targets are in `sla_priority_settings`) and **`sla_priority_settings`** (per `brand` `sugarwish`/`vinebox` × priority: 2 Corporate Buyer 60/120 min, 4 Registered/Time-Sensitive 120/240, 6 Other/Post-Order 240/480, 8 Unsubscribe 480/1440 — first-response/resolution; per-priority targets ARE now differentiated). `sla_events` (~96.8K): `event_type` enum(session_created/first_response/first_response_breach/resolution/resolution_breach/hold_started/hold_ended/reopened/sla_recalculated), `manually_reclassified`. `business_hours` (Mon-Fri 09:00-17:00 / 08:00-17:00 America/Denver) + `business_hours_exceptions`.
- Prompts/instructions: `admin_instructions` (`is_active`, `qdrant_sync_status`, `version`), `system_prompts` (no versioning), `prompts` (user-created, no is_active), `live_chat_email_templates`, `draft_classifications` (~11.6K; the Gmail-draft analysis: of 7,184 analyzed earlier, 57% HTML formatting, 36% greeting changes, ~11% signature changes; ~1% accepted as-is)/`draft_human_reviews`. `swcrm_email_templates` is CRM-DB.
- Recent WishDesk additions (created Aug-Sep 2026): `gift_ideas` (~90 rows) / `gift_idea_favorites` (gift-idea pages, SWAC `jason/gift-idea*` work), `chatbot_configurations` (1 row), `chat_conversation_reviews` (WW-2894 chat-review tagging, 0 rows), `merchandise_products` (21) / `merchandise_personalization_renders` (~247, WW-3018 personalization renders), `account_gifting_preferences` (~461), `wishworks_activity_log` (~10.3K), `wishdesk_cron_periods` (renamed from `swcrm_cron_periods`, WW-3284, the once-per-period cron duplicate-run guard), `saved_list_views` (29), `preorders` (~2.9K).
- Routing: `crm_teams`/`crm_team_members` (stayed in WishDesk), `channel_configurations`, `channel_routing_log`.
- Design/content: `articles` (74 published; NO URL column — synthesized `https://desk.sugarwish.com/articles/{id}`), `design_templates` (~954, SVG+JSON), `design_company_logos` (~9.3K; company_id=24 = consumer fallback with ~1,500 unrelated uploads earlier), `design_colors` (17; Sugar-0 #55c5ce, Wish-0 #d2232a), `design_restrictions` (~271), `merchandise_packaging_recipes` (46, `recipe_key` UNIQUE "{N}cube-bp{id}", `outer_box_sku`). `themes` (recipient-facing branding, ~1,332 earlier) is NOT in prod `wishdesk`; it lives in `laravel_live`.
- Cross-system links: `sw_user_id`/`users.sw_id`/`user_cache.user_id` = SugarWish (Laravel) CUSTOMER id; `sw_company_id`/`company_id` = corporate account; `linked_order_id`/`order_ids`/`ecard_ids`/`wishlink_ids` (JSON) = SugarWish order/ecard/wishlink ids.
- **WishDesk sync** = ONE-WAY Laravel → WishDesk.
- **Every `dev_*` table is EMPTY** (`dev_leads`/`dev_opportunities`/`dev_actions`/`dev_activity`/`dev_links` all 0 rows, re-confirmed 2026-09-29) — the parallel prod CRM pipeline they held is gone; they were never dev copies of prod data.

**CRM slow-route perf gotcha (WishDesk `crm-api`):** `rev_by_company_by_date_detailed` is a **heavy revenue-by-company-by-date MySQL VIEW** (`laravel_live`) whose unbounded, un-date-windowed use is the root cause of the multi-second-to-minute CRM slow routes (e.g. `GET /client-profile/company/:companyId/domain-insights`, med ~49s / max ~78s — EXPLAIN shows it materializing the full ~512M-row derived set). Fix pattern: **decompose the view into scoped base-table queries** (`buildDateKeyedComponent` / date-keyed component), not `FROM rev_by_company_by_date_detailed`; most interactive `crm-api` routes were migrated off it as of 2026-07-15, with `clientProfileService.ts` domain-insights the last hot-path straggler and `holidayReportingService.ts` remaining as offline/report-only usage. A second slow pattern: the **`ecard_summary` uncorrelated aggregate** (`GROUP BY gc.order_id` with no `order_id` filter — e.g. `/orders/company/:companyId`); scope it with `WHERE gc.order_id IN (...)`. Anchor: the company table has **no index on `domain`, `name`, or `magento_name`**, so both the domain-insights scan and the CRM search-route `LIKE` full-scan.

### CRM tables (now in `wishdesk_crm`; counts pre-split, verify there)

- CRM object naming: lowercase (`user`, `opportunity`) = new/native; capitalized (`Opportunity`, `Contact`, `Organisation`) = legacy Insightly-imported.
- **`swcrm_links`** (~1.15M–2.3M; frozen copy ~2.5M) — polymorphic bidirectional M:N BACKBONE of all CRM relationships (NO direct FK join tables between CRM entities). `object_name`+`object_id` (source) / `link_object_name`+`link_object_id` (target). Rows stored **BIDIRECTIONALLY** (A→B and B→A) — query BOTH `(object_name,object_id)` AND `(link_object_name,link_object_id)`, dedupe by direction. `role`: `related` (~80%, generic), `assigned_to` (opportunity→user=owner), `owned_by`/`owner`, `assigned_by`, `follow_up`, NULL (legacy Insightly).
- **`swcrm_opportunities`** (~36K: ~24K LOST, ~12K WON, ~170-182 OPEN) — **`opportunity_state`** enum (AUTHORITATIVE, use this): `OPEN`/`WON`/`LOST`/`INVALID`/`UNTAPPED`. Legacy `state` + free-text `status` exist but do NOT use for reporting. `pipeline_id`+`stage_id`, `probability`, `forecast_close_date`, `opportunity_value`/`total_opportunity_value`/`true_opportunity_value`/`bid_amount`, `category` enum(`SMALL`/`MEDIUM`/`LARGE`/`MEGA`/`NA` = deal SIZE, NOT product category), `products`/`quantity`/`occasion`, `user_responsible`/`gc_responsible` (Gift Concierge), `sw_company_id`, `sw_user_id` (SugarWish customer), `linked_order_id`/`linked_proposal_id`, `archived_at`/`archive_reason`.
- **`swcrm_pipelines` / `swcrm_pipeline_stages`** — only ONE pipeline (`Default`), 3 stages: 1 Expressed Interest, 2 Active Discussion, 3 Order Paid (=WON). `closes_state` enum(OPEN/WON/LOST).
- **`swcrm_leads`** (~158K): `lead_status` varchar is messy free-text (~115K `Won - Setup Account` = bulk-imported converted customers; 'No Response' ~29K, 'Qualification Pending' ~10K); use `converted` (0/1) + `converted_user_id`/`converted_company_id`/`converted_opportunity_id`; `user_responsible`/`created_user_id`, `email_opted_out`.
- ⚠️ **Corrected 2026-09-11 — this was exactly backwards:** `swcrm_activity` (**2,693 rows**) and `swcrm_campaign` (**63**) are POPULATED, not empty; `swcrm_field_change_log` + `swcrm_opportunity_stage_history` remain the history tables.
- `swcrm_actions` — sales tasks/to-dos (NOT customer-facing tickets).
- Dynamic custom-field registry: `swcrm_field_sync_history`, `swcrm_field_change_log`, `swcrm_field_registry`, `swcrm_available_fields`, `swcrm_field_permissions`, `swcrm_custom_field_definitions`.
- RingCentral: `swcrm_ringcentral_calls` (~7.85K; ~7,855 as of 2026-09-11), `_sms`, `_contacts`, `_user_phones`, `_config`.

### Gmail Integration (`swcrm_z_gmail_*`, 20+ tables, 'z' sorts last; now `wishdesk_crm`)

- **`swcrm_z_gmail_messages`** (**~525k** as of 2026-09-11; the old ~155K is stale): full Gmail mirror. `gmail_message_id`, `gmail_thread_id` (groups conversation — thread by this NOT subject), `linked_sw_user_id`/`linked_swcrm_lead_id` (CRM linkage), `from_email`/`to_emails`/`cc_emails`/`bcc_emails`/`subject`/`message_body_text`/`message_body_html`, `received_at`, `labels` JSON, flags `is_read`/`is_starred`/`is_important`/`is_spam`/`in_trash`/`has_attachments`, `status` enum(`ACTIVE`/`ARCHIVED`/`DELETED`), `draft_generated` (0/1), `draft_id`, **`ai_draft_status`** enum(`GENERATED`/`EDITED`/`SENT_AS_IS`/`DISCARDED` — only SENT_AS_IS/EDITED-then-sent were actually sent).
- `swcrm_z_gmail_drafts`: `draft_content_html`/`draft_content_text`, `status` enum(GENERATED/EDITED/SENT_AS_IS/DISCARDED), `similarity_score`, `metadata`. Known leak bugs: greeting 'Hi the customer,' (`{{firstname}}` not replaced), missing signature (`{{agent_name}}` placeholder).
- `swcrm_z_gmail_labels`: `label_type` enum(`SYSTEM`/`USER`), `parent_label_id` (nesting) — mirror each rep's REAL Gmail labels (vary by user, include Google system labels).
- `swcrm_z_gmail_sync_status`: `last_history_id` (incremental cursor), `sync_status` enum(`IDLE`/`SYNCING`/`ERROR` — latest run's state, RESETS on next success, NOT permanent failure).
- `swcrm_z_gmail_oauth_credentials`, `swcrm_z_gmail_sync_failures`, `swcrm_z_gmail_attachments`, `_inline_images`, `_message_labels`.

## Retool (PostgreSQL) — Analytics/Ops + Auth Bridge

167 tables (28 are `_backup_*` snapshots); only ~12 non-backup `serp_*` tables remain. Shared multi-app "Frankenstein" DB. **NOT SERP-owned.** Retool experts: Munyr, Haseeb, Aima. **Retool is NOT the SERP sync engine anymore** — the Serpy sync queue, drafts, AI conversation tables, inventory counts, forecast/supplier config and the user table all moved to `serp_app` MySQL; what remains here is BI/ops tables, reporting mirrors, forecast caches and frozen legacy copies.

Environment separation used to be by table-name **suffix** (`_live` = prod, `_dev`/none = local) — that convention now only survives in frozen legacy tables:

| Domain                    | Retool (frozen legacy)                                                      | Now lives in                                                          |
| ------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| draft-ops                 | `serp_draft_operations`, `serp_draft_operations_live` (only `_backup_*`)    | `serpy_drafts` (`serp_app`)                                           |
| inventory-counts          | `serp_inventory_counts` (49 rows), `serp_inventory_counts_live` (4,012 rows, last row 2026-01-31) | `serp_physical_counts` (`serp_app`, 4,013 rows, last row 2026-07-09); Retool copies kept as `serp_inventory_counts*_backup` in `serp_app` |
| AI messages/turns         | `serp_ai_messages_{dev,live}`, `serp_ai_turns_{dev,live}` (only `_backup_*`) | `serpy_messages`, `serpy_turns`, `serpy_prompt_logs` (`serp_app`)     |
| Odoo sync queue + breaker | `odoo_sync_queue`/`_dev`/`_live`, `odoo_sync_circuit_breaker`/`_dev`/`odoo_sync_circuit_breaker_live`, `odoo_sync_stats` — **FROZEN** (last `_live` row 2026-06-26, 4,395 rows) | `serpy_sync_queue`, `serpy_sync_circuit_breaker` (`serp_app`)         |
| auth / user               | `serp_users` (31 rows, last update 2026-04-23) — **legacy bridge, no longer read by SERP** | `serp_res_users` (MySQL ORM) is the only user table                   |
| forecast                  | (no suffix) — see Forecast Caches                                           | config layer on `serp_app`                                            |

### SERP Auth / Sync / Audit (legacy Retool copies)

- **`serp_users`**: was a stripped 4-column bridge (`id`, `created_at`, `updated_at`, `orm_user_id`→`serp_res_users.id` in SERP MySQL) hit on EVERY auth request; old columns (`name`, `email`, `password_hash`, `is_admin`/`is_finance`/`is_ops`/`is_internal`, `supplier_name`) moved to `_backup_serp_users`. **The bridge was dropped from SERP code on 2026-09-26 (`feac4d1f4` "drop serp_users bridge; res_users is the only user table")** — identity and roles are both in MySQL ORM `serp_res_users` + `serp_res_groups` (group-based RBAC; `is_internal`=false → 403 on internal endpoints), and user info from Odoo/SERP/Laravel is consolidated into one SERP table (9/28). ❌ AI assumes `name`/`email`/`role` columns exist on `retool.serp_users`, or that passwords live in Retool (formerly `'synced-from-retool'` placeholder in MySQL, users matched by EMAIL not ID) → ✅ Reality: 4-column frozen table, not read by SERP.
- **`serp_refresh_tokens`** + **`serp_password_reset_tokens`**: never actually lived in Retool despite docs — they live in MySQL (SERP ORM via `env.cr`; `crud_refresh_token.py`/`crud_password_reset.py` use `cr.execute()`). SHA-256 token_hash, 7-day refresh, 15-min access. Retool holds only `_backup_*`.
- `serp_audit_logs`: `user_id`, `table_name`, `record_id`, `action` (INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE), `old_values`/`new_values` JSONB, `ip_address`, `created_at` (a `serp_audit_logs` also exists in `serp_app`).
- **Serpy drafts** (`serp_draft_operations`/`_live`, now `serpy_drafts` in `serp_app`): `data` JSONB `{summary, warnings, confidence, operations:[], sync_target}`, `status` DRAFT/PENDING_APPROVAL/APPROVED/REJECTED/EXECUTED, `sync_target` VARCHAR. Legacy prod rows were 100% `odoo`; since 9/26 live targets are `serp`/`multi`/`laravel` (last `odoo` target created 2026-09-25). Serpy drafts-as-conversations (Apr 2026): event stream in `serp_ai_messages_dev`/`serp_ai_messages_live` + `serp_ai_turns_dev`/`serp_ai_turns_live` (now `serpy_messages`/`serpy_turns`); legacy `serp_ai_prompt_logs`/`serp_ai_prompt_logs_live` read-only/older (now `serpy_prompt_logs`); `serp_ai_turn_seq`, `serp_ai_prompts` remain.
- `serp_approval_thresholds` (ops=$5K, finance=$50K — table exists in Retool but is NOT enforced or referenced in SERP backend code; real approval gating is `SERPY_APPROVAL_RULES` / `SERPY_THRESHOLD_APPROVAL_RULES` in `config.py`).
- **`odoo_sync_queue`/`_dev`/`_live`** (Retool PG, FROZEN since 2026-06-26; the live queue moved to `serpy_sync_queue` in `serp_app` on 2026-09-11 and no longer drives Odoo — since the cutover all ops route to `serp:<entity>` handlers). Retool columns: `entity_type`, `entity_id`, `operation`, `payload`, `status`, `attempts`/`max_attempts` (=5), `priority`, `odoo_id`, `odoo_response`, `error_message`/`error_details`, `next_attempt_at`, `idempotency_key`, `sync_target`, `synced_at`. Current `serpy_sync_queue` statuses: `pending`/`processing`/`synced`/`partial`/`failed`/`cancelled` (no `dlq`); worker `workers/serpy_sync_worker.py` polls every **15s** (not ~30s), BATCH_SIZE=1 prod, exponential backoff capped 300s; "synced"/"partial" now mean applied to SERP / SERP-side validation rejection. `odoo_sync_circuit_breaker` (`state` CLOSED/OPEN/HALF_OPEN, 5-failure threshold) → `serpy_sync_circuit_breaker`. `odoo_sync_stats` frozen.
- **Retool-side SERP pieces still present (`serp_ai_turn_seq` no longer exists as a table):** `serp_worker_run`, `serp_shipment_usps_zone`/`serp_usps_zone_matrix`/`usps_zones`, `serp_vendor_inventory_counts` (also on `serp_app`).

### Orders & BI mirrors

- **`orders`** — mirror of SugarWish/Magento orders. `increment_id = '300' + id`. `path_status` = CS/routing classification (NOT fulfillment): includes `Test Account` (**~109k of ~325k rows, about a third of the table** as of 2026-09-29 — **filter out**), size buckets.
- **`opportunities`** (Retool) = legacy Insightly import (`opportunity_state` 'LOST'/'WON'/'ABANDONED'/'Open'/'OPEN' casing dups — normalize — /'SUSPENDED'); distinct from `wishdesk.swcrm_opportunities`. `organizations` (~1 populated row, sparse), `insightly_contact_data`/`insightly_import_records` = Insightly snapshots (reporting mirrors, NOT transactional).
- **`receiver_product_status`** drives Slack alerts (`status` enabled/disabled, `became_inactive_at`, `bc3x_alerted_at`/`_slack_ts`) — the `*_slack_ts`/`*_channel`/`*_alerted_at` columns are **de-dup guards**, not message content. A `receiver_product_status` also exists on `serp_app` (~1.3K rows; SERP's core-SKU report reads that copy); which copy the Retool/n8n alerts read can't be checked from the hub (verify). Also `receiver_product_change_log`, `replacement_orders`, `unshipped_order_notes`, `hours_wages_daily`.
- **`buyer_products` is dual-homed** (Retool copy + separate `laravel_live` table; `serp_app` has `buyer_products_copy`). ⚠️ **The Retool copy is badly out of sync — 511 rows vs 1,216 in `laravel_live` (re-checked 2026-09-29)** — under half the catalog, so any SQL reading the Retool copy silently misses most buyer products.
- External reporting mirrors (NOT transactional sources): `quickbooks_dashboard`, `QuickbooksLastUpdates`, `stripe_dashboard`, `shopifymonthlydata`, `slack_integrations`, `insightly_*`. Real QB sync state = `QuickbooksLastUpdates`/`ManageToLiveLogs`. ❌ AI treats these as live transactional sources → ✅ Reality: reporting/reconciliation mirrors; source systems external; `quickbooks_dashboard`/`stripe_dashboard`/`mock_*`/`sample_users`/`Sheet1` etc. are STALE seeded demos (2022 dates).

### Forecast / supplier config (MIGRATED to `serp_app`)

🛑 **The Retool forecast/supplier config layer MIGRATED OFF Retool PG onto `serp_app` MySQL** (sweep verified 2026-09-11; `sku_supplier`/`wine_category_goals` first, config-table cutover 2026-07-20). These now exist in Retool **only as `_backup_*` snapshots**, so a query against the bare name ERRORS: `sku_projections`, `supplier`, `rm_sku_supplier`, `sku_supplier`, `wine_category_goals`, `sku_costs`, `size_groups`, `supplier_buffer`, `sku_equivalency_ratios`, `mix_predictions`, `projected_orders`, `redemption_distribution`, `season_week_ranges`, `week_settings`, `product_type_min_levels`, `size_projections_copy`. Read them from `serp_app` (e.g. `execute_serp_app_query` in `backend/crud/crud_forecast_suppliers.py` `get_sa_supplier_metadata()`; `backend/crud/crud_wine_goals.py`). Column notes (same on `serp_app`):

- **`sku_projections`** — per-SKU popularity at 3wk/8wk/1yr from **recipient gift-selection survey data** (REAL data, NOT seeded; new SKUs = zero). Columns `product_type` (INT), `product_sku`, `sku_name`, `product_name`, `popularity_3_weeks`/`_8_weeks`/`_1_year` (double, default 0), `projection_date`. No active/archive flag — join `product_product.active` on the SERP product table (formerly Odoo) to exclude discontinued. No index on `product_sku`/`projection_date`. (~10K–206K rows across snapshots.)
- **`size_projections_copy`** (~18.5K rows on `serp_app`): source-of-truth size %; columns `projection`, `size_name`, `product_type`, `year` (int), `week` (int), `created_at` (Retool's bare `size_projections` is a different, older shape: `sku`, `last_year_projection`/`this_year_projection`, `projection_date`). **2025-2026 only — ZERO 2027 data → zero-cliff at year boundary.** Missing index on `product_type` and `(year, week)`.
- **`sku_supplier`** — per-SKU forecast config. `lookback_period` ENUM per-SKU: `8 Week` (default), `3 Week` (fast-moving), `Year` (seasonal). **No `case_qty` column here**; the `supplier` column was dropped in the move.
- **`supplier`** — vendor replenishment config (`lead_time`, `weeks_on_hand`, `override_buffer`); **`default_case_qty` lives HERE**. Real suppliers: Jack's, Albanese, Blair Candy, Redstone, CJ Dannemiller, Nuts.com, Jerry's, Buckin' Nuts, Sew Many Tails, AG Alchemy, The Pound Bakery. Related: `supplier_buffer`, `supplier_permissions`.
- **`rm_sku_supplier`** — RM SKU → supplier for automated RM POs; own `case_qty`, `discontinued`.
- `sku_costs` (manual override fallback), `sku_equivalency_ratios` (candy=1, popcorn=0.5), `wine_category_goals`, `product_cost_goals`, `size_groups`, `mix_predictions` (`mix_percent` 0-1, key "YYYY-WW-ProductType"), `projected_orders`, `season_week_ranges` (14 rows; `applies_to` 0=replacement/1=overlay, `percentage`), `week_settings`, `redemption_distribution`, `product_type_min_levels`.

### Forecast Caches & Ops tables still on Retool

- **`rm_weekly_demand_cache`** — RM purchasing forecast (PG, not SERP MySQL); column **`product_type_keys`** is PLURAL (ARRAY) — no singular `product_type_key`; also `rm_sku`, `week`, `year`, `weekly_demand`, `popularity_pct`, `refreshed_at`. Last refresh 2026-04-13 (73,630 rows) and no reader in SERP backend (confirmed) — treat as stale unless a Retool app still refreshes it (can't be checked from the hub; verify).
- **`bom_components_cache`** = cached BOM (RM→SA: `sa_sku`, `rm_sku`, `qty_used`, `uom`, `supplier_name`, `bom_id`; 3,959 rows) mirrored from Odoo `mrp.bom` — a **pre-cutover snapshot**; use `serp_app.serp_mrp_bom` for current BOMs.
- **`operation_levels`** = canonical inventory thresholds + alert state (226 rows; also `critical_level`, `minimum`, `goal`). `previous_level`: **0**=critical/red, **1**=minimum/orange, **2**=below-goal/yellow, **3**=at-goal/green. Category goals keyed **"CategoryName|product_type_id"** (e.g. "Bold Reds|10"); wine uses CATEGORY-GOAL not per-SKU. Ops Slack workflow (every 20 min) **writes** `previous_level` + `time_turned_red/orange/yellow`; Daily Operations Message (8 AM) **reads** them for "days below threshold" (the related n8n Daily Live Products Message / Core SKU workflows are currently inactive or replaced by `... - Cutover` versions — verify what still writes).
- `sa_projections_cache` (+ `sa_projections_cache_meta`), `sku_product_type_key` (~1,379 in Retool; ~1,434 on `serp_app`: `sku`→`product_type`(TEXT)+`product_type_key`+`size_group_name`; maps BASE skus AND `<sku>-bakerycafe` variants — base maps WRONG type for Bakery & Cafe), `product_type_key_sizes` (~531: `product_type_key`→`size_sku`/`size_name`/`buyer_product_id`; only Candy + Gourmet Goods & Spa have entries — Candles/Gift Sets missing → 0% forecast), `product_type_adj`, `bc_size_location_split`, `allocation_master`, `component_forecast_tracking` (`is_active`, `quantity_per_order`), `vietnam_stock` (`remaining_qty`, double-count risk vs POs), `redemption_curve` (~82% day-0 → ~41% day-11). `sku_product_type_key`, `component_forecast_tracking`, `sku_inventory_status_log` also exist on `serp_app`, and SERP's forecast code reads its `serp_app` copies (`services/datasource_switch.py`); the Retool copies are legacy.

### Inventory Counts (Retool — frozen; live counting is `serp_physical_counts` on `serp_app`)

- **`serp_inventory_counts` / `_live`** (14 cols): `sku`, `item_name`, `location_id`→stock_location (Odoo-era id), `location_name`, `count_quantity`, `counted_by_user_id`/`_email`/`_name`, `notes`, `counted_at`, `fulfillment_entry_index` (groups independent count sessions / multi-user counts; **NULL for ~75%** = single count), `odoo_product_id` (~99.3% mapped). (`counting_location` — `englewood`/`taylor`/`both`/`unassigned` — is NOT a stored column on these tables or on `serp_physical_counts`; SERP derives it in `backend/crud/inventory_count/` from `serp_user_counting_locations`.) **VERIFIED = 2+ different users, same sku+location+entry_index (+counting_location), matching qty**; single count NOT verified. Conflict types: `unverified` / `quantity_mismatch`; `conflict_group_key` = "sku::location_id::entry_index::counting_location". Uncounted SKUs get **ZEROED on sync** by design. `location_id` 2001/2002 were TEST ids — **0 such rows remain** (as of 2026-09-11). The Taylor location was retired at cutover (Jul 2026 decision), so `taylor` counting locations are historical.
- `serp_vendor_inventory_counts` (supplier-submitted, do NOT satisfy 2-user verification), `serp_beginning_inventory_snapshots` (pre-computed beginning-of-period on-hand — avoids scanning the 8.8M-row `stock_move_line`; also on `serp_app`), `serp_user_counting_locations`, `sku_inventory_status_log` (`in_stock` bool, `quantity_available`, `last_status_change_at`, `notified_at`).

### Cross-Database Relationship & ID Cheat Sheet

| Bridge                                                | From             | To                                                                                                             |
| ----------------------------------------------------- | ---------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ec_order.giftcards_card_id`                          | Laravel ec_order | `giftcards_card.card_id` (NOT .id), 1→many                                                                     |
| `ec_order.size`                                       | Laravel          | **`buyer_products.id`** (misnamed)                                                                             |
| `ec_order.increment_id`                               | Laravel          | Odoo `sale_order` (via `sale_order.sw_id`=`ec_order.order_id`) — pre-cutover; post-cutover orders ingest into `serp_sale_order` (`odoo_id` NULL) |
| `buyer_products.odoo_id`                              | Laravel          | `'500'`+id → `product_template.sugarwish_id` (Odoo-era)                                                        |
| `components.odoo_id`                                  | Laravel          | `'800'`+id (FABRICATED) → decode via `product_template.sugarwish_id` (Odoo-era)                                |
| `receiver_products.product_id` (=sugarwish_id)        | Laravel          | `product_template.sugarwish_id`                                                                                |
| `serp_*.odoo_id`                                      | SERP             | Odoo PK (NULL = SERP-origin)                                                                                   |
| `preselect_orders.master_id`                          | Laravel          | bridge via `preselect_order_id` → `sale_order.sw_id` (sw_id ≥ 600M) (Odoo-era)                                 |
| `stock_location.sugarwish_id`                         | Odoo             | Laravel location sync gate (only 2008=1 syncs) — Odoo-era, inert with Odoo frozen                              |
| `serp_inventory_counts.location_id`/`odoo_product_id` | Retool (frozen)  | Odoo `stock_location`/`product_product` (the `serp_physical_counts` copy on `serp_app` keeps the same columns) |

❌ AI assumes one uniform sync direction / source of truth → ✅ Reality (post-cutover): split — **SERP (`serp_app`) owns BOMs/MOs/products/POs/on-hand and RM inventory** (Odoo frozen); Laravel owns `receiver_products` classification, `component_kits`, and serves what's AVAILABLE-to-sell via the `receiver_product_inventory` view fed by `serp_published_stock`; kits/BOMs for the live order path are `laravel_live.serp_mrp_bom` (intended SoT, don't "fix"). For SERP-overlay seed conflicts, SERP-origin generally wins (users matched by email).
