## Cross-System: Inventory Source-of-Truth & Concurrent Writers

**Inventory is split — no single source of truth. Since the 2026-09-26 cutover, SERP (`serp_app`) owns everything except the sellable number Laravel serves:**

- **RM (raw material) + on-hand + valuation:** **`serp_app`** (`serp_stock_quant`, SVL, active `serp_mrp_bom`). Odoo is FROZEN — its `stock_quant` is read-only history, stale for anything after 9/26.
- **SA (finished/sellable):** Laravel serves what's available to sell via the **VIEW `receiver_product_inventory`** (`laravel_live`), whose `sellable_qty` is fed from **`serp_published_stock`** (BASE TABLE in `laravel_live`, a condensed ~4,865-row aggregate of receiver products + components that SERP publishes (rebuilt by `serp_order_queue_worker`); `serp_published_kits` is its kit counterpart, also a BASE TABLE). View columns: `receiver_product_id`, `on_hand_qty`, `reserved_qty`, `unsynced_demand_qty`, `unreserved_outbound_qty`, `reservation_hold_qty`, `prelist_qty`, `available_qty`, `sellable_qty`. Prelists are set in SERP and managed there; Laravel reads the same view.
- **Legacy columns:** `receiver_products.inventory_qty`, `mi_inventory_qty` and `odoo_inventory` ("West Coast Qty", formerly a cached int of Odoo `stock_quant usage='internal'` that could be off-by-one) no longer exist under those names — they were renamed to `inventory_qty(deleted)`, `mi_inventory_qty(deleted)`, `odoo_inventory(deleted)` (plus `inventory_updated_at(deleted)`, `sw_inventory_updated_at(deleted)`, `location_2_status(deleted)`); don't read them.
- **Available formula (current):** the auto-disable workflow uses `final_available = sellable_qty + reservation_hold_qty`, compared to `COALESCE(receiver_products.drop_level, feature_attributes.default_drop_level)`. (History: the Dec-2024 canonical formula was Odoo AVAILABLE qty — not on-hand — minus orders Laravel still held, with Odoo syncing its number to Laravel via `SynchronizeOdooInventory`, superseding the Jan-2023 "Odoo is the source" statement. That Odoo→Laravel sync is dead with Odoo frozen; the recurring bug where Odoo inventory adjustments did NOT propagate to Laravel `inventory_qty` is moot.)

**Concurrent writers / deadlocks:** Laravel backend, Retool apps, and n8n all write the same MySQL rows (e.g. `receiver_products`) simultaneously → `ER_LOCK_DEADLOCK`. The n8n auto-disable workflow deadlocks against other writers → **partial application** (some rows silently not updated). Current auto-disable = n8n **"Disable Unreserved Products - Cutover"** (every 30 min, reads the `receiver_product_inventory` view on Laravel; a `0. Flag` node keeps it **notify-only** — Slack to `#jack-test`, no disable — until toggled true for live disables + `#ops-and-tech`; flag is `false` (live n8n 2026-09-29)). It disables the parent and its `inventory_link` child SKUs; disable-only, no auto re-enable. The old Odoo-reading versions (`Disable Unreserved Products`, `... V2`) are inactive.

---

---

## Integrations & Sync (SERP ↔ Laravel; Odoo frozen)

Since the **2026-09-26 cutover**, **SERP (`serp_app`) is the ERP**; **Odoo is FROZEN** (read-only history, stale for anything after 9/26) and nothing reads from or writes to it operationally. The live integration surface is **Laravel ↔ SERP**: Laravel owns orders/catalog, SERP ingests orders, applies Serpy ops, and **publishes** inventory/kit aggregates back into `laravel_live`. Everything Odoo-shaped (pull crons, XML-RPC writes, dual-write, darklaunch, stamper, drift monitor, Odoo→Laravel inventory sync) is in the **HISTORY** block at the end of this section. Source of truth is **per-entity** (see the table below).

### Current flows (post-cutover)

#### 1. Laravel order → SERP: `serp_order_queue_worker.py`

- **Path:** Laravel `ec_order` / `preselect_orders` (+ `component_orders`, `items`) → `backend/workers/serp_order_queue_worker.py` → `serp_sale_order` (+ pickings/moves/reservations) in `serp_app` (`serp_test` on staging). It is the **live SERP order-ingest path** — ~2,150 `serp_sale_order` rows since the 9/26 cutover (2026-09-29; ~1,930 `ec_order`-, ~220 `preselect_orders`-sourced), **all `odoo_id` NULL**; it writes `serp_app`/`serp_test` ONLY and never touches Odoo. Production runs it as an **independent process** on the single dedicated server (9/29 touchbase). (The module docstring still says "Darklaunch Order Worker"; the "darklaunch" framing is obsolete — the worker is the primary write path.)
- **NO queue table — polls Laravel directly** through `laravel_routed_pool`. Detection is scoped by the **SERP-written flags, not a timestamp window**: new = `serp_sync = 0` (not cancelled → `process_new_order`); cold-cancel = `status='canceled' AND serp_sync=0` → `process_cold_cancellation`; warm-cancel; shipped = `serp_sync = 1 AND ship_date_serp_synchronized = 0`. One unified-detector pass (PR #965) claims `serp_sync=1` as it goes, so it is gap-safe (a late/out-of-order order is found whenever it flips to 0) and needs no stamper. Explicit belt: every detector path excludes `product_type` rows with `bypass_odoo = 1` (gift cards, vendor drop-ship — formerly the `oddo_synchronized=5` orders).
- **Gate + cadence:** `ORDER_QUEUE_WORKER_ENABLED` (config default `False`; production sets it on via env/Secret, not an inline manifest override — the `SERP/serp-dev/workers-deployment.yaml` override was removed in a `sugarwish-infrastructure` PR). It is the run/pause lever; a full reseed pauses ONLY this flag. `POLL_INTERVAL_SECONDS=300` is the **idle/failed-cycle** sleep — a busy cycle does not wait, so it is not a throughput cap. `POLL_INTERVAL_SECONDS` is env-tunable (`ORDER_QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_SECONDS`, default 300). Per-cycle caps: new + cold-cancel detectors surface up to `DETECTION_LIMIT=5000` (env `ORDER_QUEUE_DETECTION_LIMIT`, oldest-first, all drained in the cycle); the shipped detector stays at `SHIPMENT_LIMIT=50`; flushes are chunked (`FLUSH_CHUNK_ORDERS=100`, `FLUSH_CHUNK_PRODUCTS=500`). Idle-publish poll `SERP_IDLE_PUBLISH_POLL_SECONDS=15`.
- **Zero Odoo queries.** Every phase runs on local logic; worker rows carry `odoo_id=NULL`; the worker is allowed to run AHEAD of Odoo. **Never read VALUES from another system at runtime to "fix" a discrepancy** — if SERP differs from Odoo-15 behavior, fix SERP (seeds were the only allowed copy step). If local `serp_product_product.default_code` is NULL the worker drops that order line (`stock_picking_delivery.py`, "if not product_id or qty<=0") — fix = backfill `default_code` / the `sugarwish_id→product` map, not suppression.
- **Single picking-creator:** SERP's `sale.order.action_confirm` deliberately does NOT trigger procurement or create a delivery picking (permanent divergence from Odoo); this worker is the SINGLE creator of delivery pickings via `stock.picking.action_process_new_order` (create + confirm + assign). A second creator would produce two pickings per order. SERP permanent omissions: NO `procurement.group` model (raw `group_id` BigInteger only), NO lot/serial tracking, NO package/owner tracking (`ir.model.data`/`env.ref()` ARE implemented), chatter only at document level (never on `stock.move`/`move_line`/`quant`).
- **`sw_id` discriminator:** `serp_sale_order.sw_id < 600,000,000` = receiver/`ec_order` (bridge via `ec_order_id`); `sw_id >= 600,000,000` = preselect/wholesale (bridge via `preselect_order_id`, NOT `ec_order_id`). `ec_order` ids are 13..~19.9M; `preselect_orders` sit at a 600,000,001 offset (live data still disjoint). **The detectors no longer route on id magnitude** — the real signal is which `serp_sale_order` column is set (`ec_order_id` vs `preselect_order_id`) and which Laravel source table surfaced the row; a receiver order with id >= 600M (e.g. the 9e9 loadtest sentinel band) must still take the receiver branch.
- **Bookkeeping tables** (renamed `darklaunch_*` → `order_queue_*`, PR #1128, 2026-09-10; no `serp_` prefix, deliberate): `order_queue_quarantine` (drives retry behavior), `order_queue_failure` + `order_queue_activity` (logs behind the Order Queue Stats page). Order-queue bookkeeping, NOT validation tooling. Followed by `crud_darklaunch_admin.py` → `crud_order_queue_admin.py`, `useDarklaunchAdmin.ts` → `useOrderQueueAdmin.ts`; index names (`idx_dl_*`) and exported `Darklaunch*` TS type names intentionally NOT renamed.
- **Success audit** = presence of the `serp_sale_order` row (with `ec_order_id`); failures go to the logger and are re-attempted every cycle (idempotency from picking-state checks).
- **Orders that never reach SERP's ingest** (bypass types) intentionally have no `serp_sale_order`. Merchandise: the SERP-native merchandise `order_queue_worker.py` was **removed 2026-06-30 (PR #316)**; `merchandise` orders that carry a receiver/preselect component line (mug + mug-box, live since Apr 2026) go through the same worker, and `component_orders.inventory_source` is no longer read (renamed `inventory_source(deleted)` at cutover, WW-2994).
- **Cold-cancel non-termination lesson (PR #978, 2026-08-26):** orders cold-cancelled *before* ever reserving claimed no flag, so the detector re-surfaced them every cycle (~315 duplicate cancelled pickings per order, ~37K junk pickings on `serp_test` for 94 orders, phantom reservations). Fix = claim `serp_sync=1` on the cold-cancel pass. Any new terminal path MUST leave the `serp_sync=0` pool.
- **Detector starvation lesson:** a newest-first `ORDER BY … DESC LIMIT 50` strands the oldest tail when in-flight candidates exceed 50 (883 observed pre-cutover). Detectors process **oldest-first** (by `MIN(create_date)`/picking `create_date`, not date-granular `ship_date`) plus a per-cycle processing cap.
- **Performance (intentionally serial):** `button_validate → _action_done` = ~440–650 serial statements per 8–12 move shipment. Measured prod ≈ **1.02s mean / 1.34s p95 per shipment** (`docs/changelog/2026-08-14-ww2472-peak-volume-loadtest.md`); the old "~35–72s/shipment, ≈72/hr ceiling" was a local bench artifact, ~40× wrong. **Do NOT batch** — it would break `account_move.sequence_number` ordering and `/compare-costing` parity. Fix directions: batch per-move loops into recordset ops, co-locate DB (~75–80ms Laravel-host RTT dominates).
- **STANDING RULE (carried from validation days):** neither LAG nor DRIFT is an acceptable end-state — a worker that is behind (frontier not advancing, backlog tail never clearing) is the finding, not "it'll catch up"; only a fresh per-row poll-gap that provably converges next cycle is not.

#### 2. Serpy drafts → `serpy_sync_queue` → SERP handlers

- **Tables:** `serpy_sync_queue` / `serpy_sync_circuit_breaker` in **MySQL `serp_app`** (env-routed: `serp_test` on dev/staging) — fixed env-agnostic names, environment separated by DB not suffix. Drafts live in `serpy_drafts`. Retool-PG `odoo_sync_queue_live`/`_dev` (and `serp_draft_operations_live`) are **frozen legacy** (last write 2026-06-26, all 4,395 rows terminal) — Retool is NOT the sync engine.
- **Code:** `backend/workers/serpy_sync_worker.py` (asyncio, **15s poll**, `POLL_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 15`) + `backend/services/serpy_sync_queue/{service,models,circuit_breaker,notifications}.py`; handlers in `backend/workers/handlers/` (`serp_orm/{manufacturing,purchasing,stock,product,sales,vendors,landed_cost,chatter}.py`, `laravel.py`, `prelist_ledger.py`) and `backend/workers/ops/`. Ordering `priority ASC, created_at ASC`; `BATCH_SIZE = 1`. `get_pending_items` selects `pending`; `mark_failed` leaves errors requiring manual retry via `/api/admin/sync-queue`; a safe transient-DB-race path returns rows to `pending` with backoff. Re-describe the live table before querying; `payload` is MySQL JSON and `origin` is a JSON property, not a column.
- **Routing:** `resolve_write_target` (`service.py`) reroutes legacy `'odoo'` (input only — nothing produces it since 9/26) and `'multi'` items to their registered **`serp:<entity_type>` handler**, setting `_skip_id_resolution=true` (payload ids are already SERP ids — do not translate through `odoo_id` again) and rewriting `sync_target='serp'`. A missing twin raises `ParallelTestOrphanError` (dead-letters) rather than falling through to an Odoo writer. Default new-row `sync_target` is `'serp'`. Live `sync_target` mix since 9/26 (2026-09-29): `serp` 53 synced + 13 cancelled, `multi` 12 synced, `laravel` 1 synced; the last `odoo`-target row was created 2026-09-25. The persisted label alone does not identify every DB a handler touches (`laravel` handlers write `manage`/Laravel).
- **Semantics:** "x/y synced" means applied to SERP; `partial` = SERP-side validation rejection (or a handler's own internal partial). Statuses: `pending → processing → synced/partial/failed/cancelled` — **no `done`, no `dlq`**. The queue's legacy **`odoo_id` column holds the resulting SERP id** (MO results = `serp_mrp_production.id`); querying frozen Odoo with it silently misses/compares unrelated records. `idempotency_key` = `'{sync_target}:{entity}:{id}:{op}:{hash}'`. Circuit breaker (`serpy_sync_circuit_breaker`) still guards the worker (CLOSED/OPEN/HALF_OPEN). Common `entity_type`s (recent volume order): `mrp_production`, `inventory_adjustment`, `create_product`, `bom_change`, `purchase_order_state`, `purchase_order`, `laravel_receiver_product`, `create_component_everywhere`, `stock_scrap`, `stock_picking`, `po_receipt`, `create_bom`, `mrp_unbuild`, `odoo_vendor_bill`, `create_kit`, `update_product`; terminal statuses seen: `synced`, `cancelled`, `partial`, `failed`.
- **History — Odoo XML-RPC handler idempotency (pre-cutover):** guards via `_check_existing_record` (removed) + natural keys: `stock_picking` (origin), `inventory_adjustment` (`move_name`), `mrp_production` (origin+product_id), `stock_scrap` (origin). UNGUARDED: `mrp_unbuild` (a replayed 2nd unbuild reversed inventory twice), `po_state` edit/add, `stock_picking_correction`, `bom_change remove_component`. Re-check the SERP handlers' own guards before assuming replay-safety.
- **Fidelity audits** compare the queued payload to `serp_app` records (not Odoo): resolve BOM + finished product to templates for identity, compare payload quantities/components to MO header and raw moves. A matching payload proves **transport** fidelity only — a wrong carton chosen by SERPY before enqueueing passes both checks.
- **Recovery/idempotency (still true of the queue):** items stuck `processing` by a worker crash are not auto-reset except `recover_stuck_items` (`processing` >5 min → `pending`); Gunicorn restart kills the asyncio task (no heartbeat/TTL/startup sweeper). `'failed'` rows are never re-picked. Replay landmines (unguarded handlers): `mrp_unbuild`, `purchase_order_state` `'edit'`/`'add'`, `stock_picking_correction`, `bom_change remove_component`. Guarded: `stock_picking`, `inventory_adjustment`, `mrp_production`, `stock_scrap`, `po_receipt` (`_already_received_result()`).
- **Product-write fan-out (Serpy op types):** `create_odoo_product` (name is legacy) / `create_component_everywhere` / `create_receiver_product_everywhere` write SERP (+ Laravel for the last two); `create_serp_tracked_component` = Laravel + SERP only. Entity types include purchase_order, stock_picking, inventory_adjustment, mrp_production, bom_change, stock_scrap, product_product/template, po_receipt, laravel_receiver_product / laravel_component / laravel_kit_composition, stock_transfer, stock_picking_correction, mrp_unbuild, purchase_order_state.
- **Provenance:** `create_uid=55` = Jack's shared login for BOTH Serpy AND manual edits — **not** a Serpy signal. The ledger of "was this Serpy?" is the queue (search by `odoo_id`/SERP id or `payload` sku; `origin` = Slack link or `'SERP Batch #<draft_id> op <n>'`); pre-cutover Serpy rows live in the frozen `odoo_sync_queue_live`. Go-lives (nothing earlier can be Serpy): `product_template` 2026-03-24, `create_product` 2026-04-13, `create_receiver_product_everywhere` 2026-05-05. Odoo-side `ir_model_data.module='__export__'` = manual UI import (frozen history).
- **`/queue-test`** (harness that pushed one entity through the sync queue) was **deleted 2026-09-26** (SERP `90e712f39`, "delete Odoo XML-RPC arms and /queue-test"); historical changelogs still mention it.
- **Dead code, removal planned:** the Odoo XML-RPC handler arms were deleted 2026-09-26 (`90e712f39`), but `backend/services/odoo_xmlrpc_service.py`, `ODOO_URL`/`ODOO_DB`/`ODOO_XMLRPC_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` config, `ODOO_SYNC_WORKER_ENABLED` (default `False`; legacy name), `workers/odoo_parity_reports/` and the `serpy/ops/schemas/odoo/` op schemas (Serpy op-schema package name, still live) remain on `origin/main` (2026-09-29). Jack is removing SERP's Odoo connection logic (see planned cleanup).

#### 3. SERP → Laravel: the publish pattern

Laravel cannot join across the DB boundary to `serp_*`, so **SERP owns the computation and publishes the answer into `laravel_live`**; Laravel reads it locally. All three are in `laravel_live` (`serp_published_*` are BASE TABLES; the inventory objects are VIEWS):

- **`serp_published_stock`** — one row per stocked product, **PK `product_id` = the raw `sugarwish_id`** (NOT `serp_product_product.id` — WW-2529, PR #903). The sugarwish_id is decoded into both bridge FKs (`receiver_product_id` INT for finished goods; for RM a `800`-prefix + len>3 strips to `component_id` BIGINT — exactly one set). Columns `on_hand_qty`/`reserved_qty`/`unreserved_outbound_qty` (`DECIMAL(16,4)`, **`on_hand_qty` is SIGNED** — negative is real) + `computed_at`. **No FKs.** ~4,865 rows, freshest `computed_at` within minutes (2026-09-29; count is volatile). **Writers today are both inside the order-queue worker, computed from `serp_*` tables** (`workers/published_stock.py`, `serp_published_stock_sql.py` `SERP_AGGREGATE_SQL`; stock_quant summed ONLY over `usage='internal'` — NULL/blank usage is silently dropped and under-reports): (1) the per-phase publish — after the whole new-order drain, before shipments, ONE multi-row `INSERT … ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE` with VALUES emitted in ASCENDING `product_id` (lock-order invariant so it never deadlocks with the reconcile pass), in the **same transaction** as the `serp_sync` flag UPDATEs (`publish_and_write_serp_sync`); (2) a full **stock reconcile pass every `STOCK_RECONCILE_INTERVAL_SECONDS=600`** at a chunk boundary (`rebuild_published_stock`). The `reconcile-published-stock` **cron is RETIRED** (`active=0`, last call 2026-09-26 22:50). (History: that cron ran every 10 minutes — migration `20260908_161649_reconcile_published_stock_10min_interval.sql`, `interval_minutes=10` — and computed the table from Odoo; PR #899's earlier `serp_*`-MySQL-sourced version was wrong because `manage` had zero `serp_stock_quant`, so every `on_hand` published 0, and PR #903 superseded it.) Publish rules: upsert INCLUDING zeros (a skipped drop-to-0 keeps its last non-zero value forever), PRESERVE sign, stamp `computed_at`, skip products with neither Laravel FK. **Ordering is load-bearing: publish first, flag last** — a durable `serp_sync=1` ahead of its publish stops demand counting the order while its reservation is unpublished → reads high → oversell, and the detector (gates on `serp_sync=0` alone) would never re-surface it.
- **`serp_published_kits`** — keyed on the **triple** `buyer_product_id` + `recipe_key` + `component_id` (`recipe_key` added by migration `2026_08_13_093633`). ~635 rows / 174 buyer products (2026-09-29; volatile; ~495 default-box `recipe_key=''` at 9/11). Flattened, deduped phantom-BOM explosion from `serp_mrp_bom`/`serp_mrp_bom_line`/`serp_product_product` filtered against Laravel `components`. **Owned by the order-queue worker:** kit/BOM changes enqueue a `serp_publish_kits_requests` row (migration `20260925_160000`), drained at a chunk boundary (`drain_kits_publish_requests`), plus a periodic full pass (`rebuild_published_kits`, every `KITS_RECONCILE_INTERVAL_SECONDS=3600`). Rebuild = clear + insert in one transaction; a zero-row source read is treated as a glitch (skip, keep last good copy); an ambiguous `recipe_key` (>1 active phantom-BOM product) makes the rebuild raise. The `publish-serp-kits` cron is RETIRED (`active=0`, last call 2026-09-26 22:00) and n8n "Publish Serp Kits" is inactive. Replaces Laravel's four `serp_*` Eloquent models + the cross-schema join in `SerpBomService`. Kit/BOM SoT stays `laravel_live.serp_mrp_bom` + `SerpBomService` on-order explosion (intended; don't "fix"); normal BOMs live in `serp_app.serp_mrp_bom`.
- **`serp_sync` on `ec_order` and `preselect_orders`** (each with a `ship_date_serp_synchronized` twin) — replaced the `serp_sale_order` existence test and is now the ONLY order-sync flag (the `oddo_*` columns are renamed `(deleted)`, see HISTORY). Values: `0` placed/not ingested · `1` ingested/reserved · `2` archived-SKU parity (mirrored `oddo_synchronized=2`; the views still count `serp_sync IN (0,2)` as unsynced demand) · `4` cancelled-and-synced · `5` bypass (`bypass_odoo` product types). Live `ec_order` since 9/26 (2026-09-29): `1`≈1,970, `0`≈70 (in-flight), `4`=90, `5`≈9, `2`=0. `4` arrived with WW-3013's backfill (`oddo_synchronized 5→5, 2→2, 1&canceled→4, 1→1, else 0`); treating the flag as only `0/1/5` miscounts cancelled orders as pending. **`items` has NEITHER** (item sync read its PARENT order's flag; `items.odoo_sync` is now `odoo_sync(deleted)`); `items.serp_sync` never built.
- **The TWO views** `receiver_product_inventory` (finished goods) and `component_product_inventory` (RM twin, keyed on `component_id`; holds & prelist stay literal `0` so `sellable_qty == available_qty`; demand real from `component_orders`) read `serp_published_stock` locally. `receiver_product_inventory` (live definition 2026-09-29): 9 columns `receiver_product_id, on_hand_qty, reserved_qty, unsynced_demand_qty, unreserved_outbound_qty, reservation_hold_qty (active, unexpired inventory_reservations), prelist_qty, available_qty, sellable_qty`; `available = on_hand − reserved − unsynced_demand − unreserved_outbound − hold`; `sellable = available + prelist_qty`; demand leg = `ec_order`/`preselect_orders` created in the last **30 days** with `serp_sync IN (0,2)` joined to `items` on `items.order_id = <table>.id` with the ITEM-side `items.order_type` literal for that table (`'receiver-order'` for `ec_order`, `'preselect-order'` for `preselect_orders`; `items.deleted_at IS NULL`) — NOT `ec_order.order_type` (`merchandise` splits across both tables, so an order-level `order_type` gate silently drops merch demand). ⚠️ **The demand leg tests `serp_sync`, NEVER `serp_sale_order`** (a NOT EXISTS against that stub matches every order → negative `sellable_qty` → whole catalog auto-disabled), so `serp_sync` is load-bearing for inventory correctness. View bodies are canonical in `EXTRA_VIEWS` in `seeding/scripts/export_manage_schema.py`. This VIEW (`sellable_qty`) is where readers move off the legacy `receiver_products.inventory_qty`.
- **Column-deprecation workflow:** no Laravel column is dropped at the flip — it **freezes**, is **renamed with a `(deleted)` suffix** and left live ~weeks-to-a-month so stray readers error loudly, then hard-dropped in cleanup (pattern on `components`; now live on `ec_order`, `preselect_orders`, `items`, `receiver_products`, `component_orders`). See [[inventory-qty-view-cutover-plan]] and [[taylor-location-retired-at-cutover]].

#### 4. Per-entity source of truth (current)

| Entity                                                                                  | Owner                                 | Write path                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Normal BOMs/MOs/POs/product creation/quants/SVL/RM + on-hand inventory                  | **SERP (`serp_app`)**                 | Serpy draft → `serpy_sync_queue` → `serp:<entity>` handler                                             |
| Phantom BOMs / kits (`serp_mrp_bom type='phantom'`)                                     | **SERP-owned**                        | local `serp_mrp_bom_line` via `update_kit` (legacy alias `serp_update_kit`); published to `serp_published_kits`; on-order explosion SoT `laravel_live.serp_mrp_bom` |
| Custom-branding merchandise (sleeves, hats, water bottles)                              | **SERP-only** (Jason, Feb 8 2026)     | `component_orders` + SERP `stock_quant`/`stock_move`                                                   |
| `receiver_products`, `buyer_products`, `kits`, `component_kits` ⚠️ **frozen**           | **Laravel-owned**                     | SERP → Laravel via `laravel_*` ops over `laravel_routed_pool` (`laravel_live` on prod, `manage` on serp-dev) — no longer covers `component_kits` (`laravel_update_kit` deleted 2026-09-09; read-only inputs) |
| Sellable quantity                                                                       | **Laravel view** `receiver_product_inventory.sellable_qty` fed by `serp_published_stock` | SERP publishes (above); Laravel deducts unsynced demand |
| Users (Odoo/SERP/Laravel)                                                               | consolidated into ONE SERP table (9/28) | —                                                                                                    |

- **Conflict resolution** (seed-era rule, still the tiebreak): when SERP-origin and Odoo-origin data collide on a natural key, SERP is usually source of truth (users: match on **email**, SERP usually wins, with exceptions).
- **SERP Laravel write access (least-privilege):** SERP connects as **`SERP-readfull-writepartial-7161`** — FULL READ, PARTIAL WRITE with table grants gated by the Serpy approval flow: INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE only on `receiver_products`, `kits`, `component_kits`, `buyer_products`. Serpy writes `receiver_products` to **`manage` (staging)** and must add `sku_type`/`is_core` to the `RECEIVER_PRODUCT_WRITABLE` whitelist (`is_core` MUST equal `sku_type=='core'`, via `core_flag_for_sku_type()`).
- **Prelisting (`receiver_products.prelist_qty`, added 2026-07-01):** lets Sophie make MOs sellable on the site before they're packed without the placeholder-MO/unbuild trick that corrupts costing. `prelist_qty` is ADDED to `sellable_qty` by the view (was: the Odoo→Laravel sync cron added it into `inventory_qty`); SERPY SUBTRACTS from `prelist_qty` (floored at 0) when it MOs the product (`qty_after = max(0, prelist_qty − produced_qty)`); LIVE and firing (drain rows confirmed 2026-07-11). Audit ledger **`prelist_qty_log`** (singular; `prelist_qty_logs` does NOT exist): per-row `product_id`, `source` (e.g. `serpy`), delta, `qty_after`, `changed_at`, draft id. Wrong-key decrement bug (#392/#430) drained the wrong product's row (~9 SKUs backfilled). The ledger can be **incomplete** (a live `prelist_qty` correctly 0 with no logged decrement — net-harmless); zero rows for a SKU = prelist never added → a produced MO correctly drains nothing (no-op, not a bug). `receiver_products.prelist_qty` is the truth; the log is the imperfect trail.

#### 5. Identity & join rules (still authoritative)

- **Never join SERP↔Odoo on `id = id`; join on `odoo_id`.** `odoo_id` (bigint, nullable) holds the matching Odoo PK on Odoo-sourced rows (`serp_stock_picking` → `stock.picking`, `serp_product_product` → `product.product`, `serp_purchase_order` → `purchase.order`, …). **`NULL` = SERP-native** (created locally after seed; `NULL ≠ invalid`) — the durable origin test, NOT any `id >= 1B` range. `id = odoo_id` holds only for Odoo-seeded rows; finalize reset `AUTO_INCREMENT` to `MAX(odoo_id)+1`. No UNIQUE constraint/index exists on `odoo_id`. Post-seed population was uneven (`serp_purchase_order` full; `serp_product_*` ~6130/7387; `serp_res_partner` 290/295; `serp_stock_picking`/`serp_mrp_production`/`serp_mrp_bom` NULL despite seeding) — never assume `odoo_id` is populated; child rows created by the SERP ORM (stock_move/move_line/quant) leave `odoo_id` NULL.
- **SERP Bot** = the superuser's own partner (login `__system__`, partner id=2) renamed "SERP Bot" (PR #1052, `53f24679e`); there is no separate `__serp__` user/partner and no `2_000_000_001` id (that id and its PR #453 AUTO_INCREMENT-floor side effect are gone — do not reason from them).
- **Fabricated ids:** `components.odoo_id` = string `'800' + components.id` (component 439 → `'800439'`), NOT an Odoo `product_product.id` — joining it to Odoo PKs matches wrong rows (~597/598 set; ~6 real in some envs); 13 component SKUs are duplicated across ids — link by `component.id`, not SKU. `buyer_products.odoo_id` = `'500' + buyer_products.id` (1215/1215; ~12 outliers). Real Odoo `product_product.id` is ~1789–29210 — match by SKU (`components.sku = product_product.default_code`); de-reference `800611 → components.id 611 → product_product.id 28006`.
- **Bridge key `product_template.sugarwish_id`** (on `product_template`, NOT `product_product`; frozen Odoo history — and the key SERP's published tables reuse): `'800'` prefix → strip to `components.id` (~2238 templates); kit products = `'500'+buyer_products.id`; other integer → `receiver_products.product_id` OR `buyer_products.id` (a `sugarwish_id` without `'800'` is not necessarily a receiver); `0`/`NULL` → no mapping (~2,264 of 7,515 at 2026-09-11). `sugarwish-odoo` `sale.py` filtered on `startswith('500')`/`'800'`.
- **Bridge tables:** `serp_sale_order` (ORDER; back-refs `sw_id`, `ec_order_id`); `serp_product_product` (PRODUCT; `sugarwish_id`, three mutually-exclusive FKs `component_id`/`receiver_product_id`/`buyer_product_id`; `inventory_source` enum `'odoo'`/`'serp'` is legacy); `serp_users` (Retool PG, legacy AUTH bridge; `orm_user_id` → `serp_res_users.id`, 4 columns, was hit on every authenticated request until SERP dropped it 2026-09-26); `serp_product` (SERP MySQL bridge, unified product id space, `type` `'component'`/`'receiver_product'`, `sku`/`name` STATIC denormalized copies from seed time); `account_move_purchase_order_rel` (Odoo, frozen; ~1,575 bills → ~1,384 POs).
- **Order chaining:** `giftcards_card` (PK `card_id` — join on `card_id`, NOT `id`) → `ec_order` (`giftcards_card_id`, **1-to-MANY** via reships; one card has 13,719 `ec_order` rows; `increment_id` = the order number) → `serp_sale_order` via `sw_id`/`ec_order_id`. Historically the Odoo `sale_order` joined by `increment_id`; `ec_order` never stored an `odoo_id`.

#### 6. Ops surfaces: sync ordering, health probes, alerting, PO email

- **Sync-queue ordering and timeouts:** a draft's ops run **strictly in order** — `get_pending_items` sorts `priority, created_at, id` and `_draft_order_gate` refuses to claim an item while an earlier row of the same draft is `pending`/`processing`/`failed` (retry or cancel the failed row to release the rest; rows without `draft_id` are ungated). A cycle that exceeds its timeout records its in-flight item as abandoned (`[cycle timeout]` marker) and recovery moves it to `failed` (notify + draft progress) rather than re-running a write that may still commit; unmarked items (crash/restart) still re-queue. Manufacturing-order items require a positive `bom_id` (a BOM-less MO is rejected at validation, before approval).
- **Health probes (WW-3399/3400/3401):** `GET /api/health/{component}` (probe key checked before the name; 200 healthy / 500 unhealthy, status-only body; 10 s per-pod result cache) for the external SRE prober. Checks read `serp_app`: `serp-db`, `redis`, login-auth, laravel-db, retool-db, `order-queue`, `worker-email`, `worker-serp-sync`, sync-queue circuit breaker — worker checks use the per-cycle heartbeat row in `serp_worker_run` (order-queue threshold = 2x its 1200 s window). The static `GET /api/health` is only the k8s readiness/liveness probe.
- **Slack monitors:** the `@cron` monitors in `workers/crons.py` (`kits-monitor`, `check-component-orders`, `odoo-parity-reports`, ...) post to `#serp-alerts` and reply in one open thread stored in `serp_cron.last_thread_ts`; a stale parent is rewritten into the full report and re-rooted. The `publish-serp-kits`, `reconcile-published-stock` and `darklaunch-drift` crons were removed/deactivated 2026-09-26 (order-queue worker owns publishing).
- **PO email:** sending is the PO page's Send by Email compose modal (`mail.compose.message.action_send_mail`); the Serpy `po_send_email` op was removed 2026-09-26 (queued `purchase_order_state` `send_email` rows still sync). Live sender is not enabled on live SERP yet (see open items).
- **Prelist drain (SERP-native since 2026-09-26, `315ff02be`):** side effects that only lived in the deleted Odoo handlers were re-implemented in SERP. `prelist_ledger.drain_prelist_qty` runs after an MO completes and after a positive inventory adjustment. It resolves the Laravel row via `serp_product_product.receiver_product_id` (SKU fallback; skips `RM-` and child SKUs) and decrements `receiver_products.prelist_qty` with a `GREATEST(…, 0)` clamp, writing a `prelist_qty_log` row. MO drains are batch-atomic per draft+product after the last sibling MO. Drains are best-effort: they log a warning and never fail the sync. The trigger was draft 3428, whose MOs drained no prelist after cutover.
- **Forecast SA inventory:** the forecast/Live Products serp source reads sellable (SA) inventory from the `receiver_product_inventory` view on both sources (not a quant rebuild); it supersedes the July "no Laravel/serp mixing" rule for receiver products.

### Planned, NOT done (target state; owner Manish; 9/29 SERP Touchbase)

- Drop `serp_*` tables from `laravel_live` **except `serp_published_kits` / `serp_published_stock`**; remove inventory transactions + deprecated columns + related Laravel code.
- Remove the Odoo inbound/outbound sidecars in Laravel (still present on `sugarwish-laravel` `origin/main` 2026-09-29: `app/Sidecar/SynchronizeOdooInventory.php`, `app/Services/OdooInventoryService.php`, the `app/Http/Controllers/Odoo/**` `/api/odoo` endpoints, and `Order.php` `ordersForOdoo` still referencing the now-`(deleted)` `oddo_synchronized`) and SERP's Odoo connection code (`odoo_xmlrpc_service.py`, Odoo config). Jack is removing Odoo connection logic in SERP.
- Other open items: live PO email sender NOT yet enabled on live SERP (James using normal email for Q4 POs, 9/28); PO-approval gates are design intent only (the gate columns such as `ready_for_approval_at`/`ops_approved_at` do not exist in `serp_app`; enforcement today is `SERPY_APPROVAL_RULES` in `config.py`); whether to SPLIT the SERP DB off `laravel_live` (deferred, 2026-07-28 "SERP Final Phase Discussion"); the `(deleted)` columns get hard-dropped later.

### External Services (unchanged by the cutover)

- **AvaTax (Avalara):** `ec_order.avatax_status`: `not-processed/processed/sent/skipped/adjusted/voided/cancelled/locked`. Manual retry: DELETE `avatax_items` rows + set `avatax_status='not-processed'`. Root cause usually a bad user-entered address (free-text state field). Alerts → `#avalara-alert`.
- **Smarty (SmartyStreets):** paid metered (5k validations, bumped by Seth); rate-limit stalls fulfillment via `is_printed=3`; recovery = renew + reset `is_printed`.
- **USPS:** token array index-swap between auth and label workflows is a recurring label-failure cause (not USPS outages). Use `apis.usps.com` (not `api.usps.com`).
- **Vendor drop-ship:** routing code **`550`** = Vinebox → Shopify → vendor ShipStation (WCC's vinebox.com account). Drop-shipped SKUs bypass the ERP (`serp_sync=5`; formerly `odoo_sync=5`). Wine needs ShipCompliant/address compliance.
- **Tango Card:** `tango_orders` maps `ec_order_id`→Tango; `receiver_products.tango_utid` = catalog link.

### Genies/Routers & Qdrant

- Genies source of truth = **sw-design** `genies/{setting_key}.json`, NOT WishDesk `system_settings`. Pipeline → `s3://…/genies-sync.json` → `POST /api/quiz-config/sync-genies` → upserts `system_settings`. **FULL-OVERWRITE per key** — direct edits to `system_settings` get wiped on next sync.
- **Qdrant collections:** `kb-v2` (Product KB → SWIM), `instructions`, `agent-chats`, `kb-internal`, `discoveries_swirl`, `org-knowledge`. Embeddings: OpenAI `text-embedding-3-small`.

### HISTORY (superseded — Odoo-era integration; kept for lessons, NOT current behavior)

> Everything below describes how Laravel, Odoo and SERP were wired **before the 2026-09-26 cutover**. Odoo is frozen; these paths no longer run. Read it to interpret old rows, old tickets and frozen data.

#### Legacy Laravel → Odoo PULL (Odoo crons, `sugarwish_integration`)

- Odoo **POLLED** Laravel REST (no webhook, no XML-RPC client in Laravel; sync state tracked Laravel-side) on crons: **`Sugarwish: Update Orders`** every 6 min, **`Update Failed Orders`** (id 26) every 10 min, **`Update Prepicks`** every 120 min, **`Update Failed Prepicks`** (id 43) every 15 min, **`Send Failed Orders Email`** (id 57) daily ~14:15 (Carolyn Pardee, routed to Nora Stein / Kellen Evans to fix a wrong/missing Odoo id or unarchive a product). Odoo found products by `sugarwish_id`, created `sale.order` keyed `sw_id=ec_order.id` (+ `sw_datetime`), confirmed (phantom-BOM explosion → inventory deduction), all booked to catch-all partner `'SW Customer'` (`res_partner.id=94`), then POSTed back to `/update-order-passed` / `/update-order-failed`. `UNRESOLVED_GRACE_SECONDS` = 15 min.
- Endpoints (Bearer token from `ir.config_parameter`, middleware `odoo.api`): `GET /api/odoo`, `/api/odoo/pre-pick`, `/api/odoo/failed-ecard-orders`, `/api/odoo/failed-prepick-orders`, `/api/odoo/components/ecard`, `/api/odoo/component/ecard-orders` (`oddo_synchronized=1 AND component_imported=0`), `/api/odoo/component/prepick-orders`; `POST /api/odoo/component/update-passed-orders` / `update-failed-orders`; `PUT /api/odoo` (inventory). The controllers still exist in Laravel (see planned cleanup) but nothing calls them.
- Receiver feed = `Order::ordersForOdoo($status, 500)`: `WHERE oddo_synchronized=$status AND store_id=2 AND created_at >= '2022-12-04 19:00:00' AND whereHas(items → receiver_product NOT soft-deleted)`, `simplePaginate(500)`, **no `ORDER BY`** → oldest `order_id` first; serving a batch flipped rows `0→2` (claim/lease). ~78,825 orders sat at `oddo_synchronized=0` (2026-06-17) NOT from a sync bug — every product they chose was later soft-deleted in `receiver_products`, so the `whereHas` filter excluded them forever (old gifts of discontinued SKUs; ~1 qualified).
- **Critical race:** state changes between polls (<6 min) could be missed entirely. n8n integrity monitor (id `6iiMjS1arjKpoWmz`, now "Disabled During SERP Cutover"; the older `bR4rEQjFI3GuwkiY` "Sync Issue Detector"/"Integrity Monitor" no longer appears in n8n) reset `oddo_synchronized=0`+`component_imported=0`+`items.odoo_sync=0` for orders missing from Odoo; **~8–9% miss rate** (~500 missing at any time); posted to `#api-autofix` (mostly auto-resolved noise — Seth: no action needed).
- **Failed Products email / bridge loop:** fired when `sugarwish_id` not found in active Odoo products (`product_product.active=false` or NULL `sugarwish_id`, or a Laravel endpoint missing the `inventory_source='odoo'` filter → looped every ~10 min); `inventory_source='serp'` items never existed in Odoo.

#### `oddo_synchronized` (legacy column — now `oddo_synchronized(deleted)`)

- ⚠️ **Live state (`laravel_live`, 2026-09-29): `oddo_synchronized`, `component_imported`, `ship_date_odoo_synchronized` on `ec_order` and `preselect_orders`, and `items.odoo_sync`, are RENAMED with a `(deleted)` suffix** (frozen; awaiting hard-drop). Querying the old names fails "Unknown column"; use `serp_sync` / `ship_date_serp_synchronized`. Laravel code (`Order.php`) still references the old name (dead). The spelling below still matters when reading old data, tickets, code and views.
- Spelled **`oddo_synchronized`** (double-d, one o) NOT `odoo_synchronized`; companion `ship_date_odoo_synchronized` (correct spelling) tracked ship-date push separately — the typo and two-flag design were legacy debt signals.
- Values (pre-cutover): `0` not synced (~77.5K) · `1` synced/pushed (~3.88M, 99%) · `2` partial/in-flight/claimed (~64–166; 1 row at 2026-09-11) · `3` stuck/failed on an archived/disabled SKU (RARE — **1 order total (19783360)**, contrary to WW-510 which assumed many; not seen in prod 2026-09-11) · `5` vendor/special bypass (~17.4K). Stuck orders typically sat at `2` or traced to the April 2026 combo-line migration that archived child SKUs ("product not found"). Companion: `component_imported` (0 not imported → 1 success → 2 exposed/in-progress → 3 failed → 5 bypass), `items.odoo_sync` (0 needs sync · 1 syncing · 2 synced ~14M+ · 5 bypass ~22K; `IN (0,1)` = "Orders Not Imported", to be deducted from available inventory when deciding to disable a product).
- ⚠️ **No transactional integrity (Dec 23 2024):** orders were marked `oddo_synchronized=1` BEFORE Odoo confirmed; **906 `ec_order` rows were "ghost" orders** marked `=1` but never sent. Fix = mark `=1` only after a success response; no distributed transaction. No `odoo_id` on `ec_order` — matching used `increment_id`/`order_id`.
- The per-flag values later mapped 1:1 into `serp_sync` (WW-3013 backfill above).

#### Odoo → Laravel inventory sync (dead — replaced by publish-to-view)

- Only `stock_location.sugarwish_id > 0` locations synced (only `EW/Stock/Fulfillment` id=2008, `sugarwish_id=1`); `0` = internal-only (RM/packaging), `NULL` = system/virtual. Negatives were floored to 0 (`sugarwish_integration/wizards/sugarwish_stock.py:75-77`), hiding backorder depth.
- `SynchronizeOdooInventory` Lambda read the `odoo_inventory` table, sharded by `product_id % 10`, wrote `receiver_products.inventory_qty`, and never re-enabled products (disable-only). Its trigger (n8n `6LYL1ThTQOCg1Fj8`) stopped Dec 31 2025 → stale inventory; n8n "Sync Receiver Product Inventory with Odoo" (`Waf2sOm2Aj0nJAx4`) is "Disabled During SERP Cutover". `receiver_products.inventory_qty` / `odoo_inventory` / `mi_inventory_qty` / `inventory_updated_at` / `sw_inventory_updated_at` are now `(deleted)` columns; `components.inventory_quantity`/`inventory_source`/`mi_inventory_qty` and `component_orders.inventory_source` likewise. Pre-cutover gap: a Serpy `odoo_stock_transfer` moved Odoo stock with no SERP write and no Odoo→Laravel reverse pull, so `odoo_inventory` went stale (proposed fix was adding `"serp"` to its `sync_targets`; moot now). The Laravel `SynchronizeOdooInventory` sidecar and `OdooInventoryService` (which also hosted the interim `publishStock`, WW-2496) still exist as dead code.
- `Disable_Unreserved_Products` V2 (n8n `VRdmXlm2XTeRbOyT`) ran every 1 min against Odoo, disable-only with no cooldown (re-disabled within seconds of a manual re-enable); a known DEADLOCK source, and Odoo forecast timeouts usually traced to it re-firing a 330s query with no backoff. Now "Disabled During SERP Cutover"; **"Disable Unreserved Products - Cutover"** (`CGHWrz8FzJYFHQH0`, active) is the replacement (reads `receiver_product_inventory` on `laravel_live` every 30 minutes; **notify-only** — flag node `0. Flag` is `false`, Slack `#jack-test`, no disabling — until toggled true for live disable + `#ops-and-tech`). Other cutover replacements active: "Core SKU Live Warnings - Cutover", "Inventory In Wrong Location - Cutover".
- Root causes of stale `odoo_inventory`: ~86% of records >30 days old; only ~190/day from 3 hooks; `put_product_qty` silently returned False; `stock_move_line.py` write() and `product._compute_quantities()` hooks commented out; sale-order deliveries (origin `^S\d+$`) skipped `put_product_qty`. `receiver_products` had TWO decoupled caches (`inventory_qty` truth vs `odoo_inventory` stale up to 180× off, e.g. SA-03-050-A 1,974 vs 11); n8n alerts wrongly read `odoo_inventory`.
- Chronic bugs: inventory added in Odoo before the SKU existed in Laravel; wrong Odoo location; SKUs "not connected"; receiver product whose `base_product_id` wasn't in Laravel; BOM `add_component` "No SERP product found for Odoo product_id"; archiving one side without the other; `stock_transfer` ops updated Odoo only (no SERP/Laravel quant write, no reverse pull → `odoo_inventory` stale forever).
- **Dual-write "Odoo Sync Partial"** causes (dead with Odoo): the `sugarwish_integration` override of `stock.picking.button_validate` → private `stock.move._action_done` blocked over XML-RPC; the `put_product_qty` wizard's empty-API JSONDecodeError; **UoM mismatch** (sync sent `uom_id=1` for an `lb` product → cross-category reject); quants can't be created on consumables/services; wrong `entity_type` (`stock_transfer` vs `stock_picking`).

#### Legacy `odoo_sync_queue` (Serpy → Odoo XML-RPC) internals

- Worker `odoo_sync_worker.py` (~1,685 lines): 15s poll, `BATCH_SIZE=1`; retry exponential backoff `5*2^attempts` capped at 300s, `max_attempts=5` (column effectively unused; no working DLQ); circuit breaker OPENS after 5 failures, resets after 2 successes, `reset_timeout_seconds=30`; `ODOO_SYNC_DRY_RUN=True` generated fake ids `99990000 + entity_id`; `CYCLE_TIMEOUT=180s`/`MAX_CONSECUTIVE_TIMEOUTS=3` "Sync cycle wedged" was a self-recovering idle-socket-drop incident (marker `"[Recovered from stuck processing]"`, 8 events ever). Last `sync_target` mix (9/11): 6,302 `odoo`, 552 `multi`, 465 `laravel`, 421 `serp`.
- Real bugs fixed: `po_receipt` false "Sync Failed" after a successful receive (XML-RPC timeout retried into an already-`done` picking; `_already_received_result()` guard, 2026-06-01); `stamp_child_odoo_ids` wired only into the Serpy path, not the order path (2026-06-03).
- **Mirror-failure semantics (PR #1186, 2026-09-15):** a live-Odoo write that fully succeeded but whose darklaunch **mirror** failed became `synced` + `mirror_failed=True` warning (not `partial`), so the draft completed to `EXECUTED` instead of stranding in `APPROVED` with a false "0/1 synced" alert. Handlers' own internal `partial` still downgraded correctly.
- Historical `sync_target` values: `odoo`, `multi` (fan-out), `laravel`, `serp`, `serp_darklaunch` (constants still in `serpy/constants.py`, the expander strips `serp_darklaunch`). Historical PO receipts flowed through this queue, not the order worker.

#### Dual-write, darklaunch & cutover strategy (RETIRED 2026-09-26)

- **Darklaunch** = dual-write + shadow-validation: SERP and Odoo ran side-by-side (Serpy wrote BOTH, one-way Odoo→SERP seed/sync), gated on **<1% drift, stable 2 weeks**; retired at cutover (`SERP_DARKLAUNCH_ENABLED`, `SERP_SHADOW_WRITES_ENABLED`, `USE_SERP_AS_LIVE`, `ENVIRONMENT_SET` have zero hits in SERP `backend/` on `origin/main`; only `deploy-k8s-staging.sh` mentions `ENVIRONMENT_SET`). `serp_shadow` (`SERP_SHADOW_WRITES_ENABLED`, Hetzner `5.78.203.128`, provisioned by Manish 2026-04-28, `shadow_cutover_at=2026-05-08`) was a separate, predecessor feature. `serp_darklaunch_meta` (`darklaunch_cutover_at`; primary darklaunch cutover **2026-05-30 14:14 MT**; per-env reseeds prod 2026-06-04 09:27:20, staging 2026-06-03 11:52:27) was DROPPED 2026-08-03 (`809a4d8f9`).
- **Isolation rule (kept as a design principle, see order worker):** the worker queried Odoo only to resolve ids and never read Odoo VALUES at runtime; many drift bugs traced to the SEED, not runtime.
- **Local DB matrix (RETIRED names):** {prod, staging} × {replica, darklaunch} (`serp_prod_replica`/`serp_staging_replica` = clean Laravel mirror with no `_migrations`; `serp_prod_darklaunch`/`serp_staging_darklaunch` = replica + Odoo overlay). SERP PR #46 consolidated local Docker DBs 5→3: **`serp_local_prod` / `serp_local_staging` / `laravel_local`** (`serp-mysql`, `127.0.0.1:3307`). `serp_test` is Hetzner staging (TZ `America/Denver`); "compare-replica" tooling actually compared darklaunch. An "empty list" of Odoo entities was a routing-config issue (`SERP_ORM_ENV`/`ACTIVE_ODOO_DB_NAME`, `DARKLAUNCH_DB_ENV`).
- **Seed (build-time, allowed to copy Odoo values):** all tables from Odoo (`INSERT … ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE`); high-volume tables (sale_order(_line), stock_picking, stock_move*, SVL, account_move*, mrp_production) only the latest ~5,000 ids; chatter seeded 2026-07-22 (`--chatter-only`; `serp_mail_message`/tracking/`serp_ir_attachment` ~415k messages / 2.6k attachments; binaries to `s3://sw-serp/serp-attachments/` keyed by checksum; `sale.order` chatter windowed to the seeded order window — an old SO with "No messages yet" is by design); collision guard skips incoming rows colliding with runtime (`odoo_id IS NULL`) rows, AUTO_INCREMENT floors bumped to `odoo_max + 10,000,000` on shared chatter/catalog tables; `delete_orphan_quants` (full + recent phases only, SKIPs if Odoo returns <1000 ids). The seeder bumped `AUTO_INCREMENT` to 1B during seed; `id >= 1B` marked merge-phase rows (do not use as the origin test).
- **Cutover mechanics (executed):** `docs/plans/serp-odoo-cutover.md` (SERP PR #705, 2026-07-30) — a config flip with **no bulk data movement in the flip window** (the earlier "bulk-copy into `laravel_live`" plan, ~84.5 GB/~3 hr, was superseded): SERP tables stayed in their own DBs (`serp_app` prod / `serp_test` staging; migration `069` started it), Laravel could no longer join `serp_sale_order`/`serp_stock_quant`, so the surface flipped to "SERP publishes into Laravel" (the current design above). Final seed of Odoo history one-way into SERP's OWN DBs, then nothing wrote to Odoo again; the XML-RPC write service, sync-queue Odoo worker and drift monitor died AT cutover; new records `odoo_id=NULL`; the `/api/odoo` pull-and-callback endpoints were retired, replaced by the order-queue worker. Publish-then-flag ordering (publish stock FIRST, then flip `ENVIRONMENT_SET`) was the flip-day rule; the same ordering now lives per order in the transaction above. Full historical Odoo data seeding was mandatory for COGS accuracy. Related pre-cutover state: `manage` filtered the view demand leg on `oddo_synchronized = 0` while `laravel_live` already filtered `serp_sync` (2026-08-14), and the WW-2496 perf indexes (`idx_co_order_component_qty` on `component_orders`, `idx_preselect_created` on `preselect_orders(created_at)`) were on `manage` only (Manish, ~2026-08-14) — staging-first steady state; re-check live before asserting either now.
- **Strategy/timeline (superseded plan):** Jan 2026 pivot "SERP as workflow layer, Odoo as inventory SoT"; phasing (Seth) made it viable — Phase 1 POs, 2 Bills, 3 RM/components/kits (Matthew Patrick: no technical reason kits can't move; Manish Chaudhary = SERP secondary lead; Carolyn reluctant; Ric: kit/inventory problems resolve pre-cutover), 4 MOs/inventory dual-write (line-level control via `purchase_order_line.odoo_component_id`, NULL = SERP-only), 5/6 kits/BOMs + final cutover (Odoo freeze + physical count). Constraints then: Oct 2026 Odoo v15→v17 forced upgrade (moot now), Sept 15 release mandated by Anna Kifer with 6–8 weeks of prod testing before Q4 (slipped: 2025 → Jan/Feb 2026 → early Aug → Sept 15 → cutover 2026-09-26). Jack's milestones: repeatable sync script May 13 · dual-write wired Jun 3 · order queue expanded (`ec_order`+`preselect_orders`) Jun 24 · parallel test launched Jul 1 · complete Jul 15. Flagged risks: daily reconciliation too slow for silent XML-RPC failure, COGS divergence during dual-write, no rollback/point-of-no-return, Phase-6 freeze window (≤4h).
- **Pre-cutover Odoo cleanup blockers (frozen history):** 48,909 stock_moves stuck `assigned/confirmed/waiting` (oldest May 2023) locking `reserved_quantity` — unreserve only via `stock.move._do_unreserve()`/`do_unreserve()`, never SQL (Odoo bug: `qty_done>0` sets `product_uom_qty=0` but skips `_recompute_state()`; `scripts/odoo_cleanup_stuck_moves.py`; server action 376 = "Unreserve"); 482 negative `stock_quant` rows (only internal-location negatives real); 88 duplicate active `stock_location` `complete_name`s (worst 4× `TY/Stock/R4-7-AR`); 20 over-received PO lines, 10 cancelled POs with active moves; location ids 2001/2002 (`EW/Stock/Zone1/2`) in `retool.serp_inventory_counts` but not Odoo.
- **Old deploy footgun:** `deploy.sh` (AWS EC2, PM2/nginx) ran no schema migrations and hardcoded Docker `serp_staging_darklaunch` — new `serp_*` tables needed a manual manage-DB migration first. Now: committed migrations applied by the runner in `deploy-k8s.sh` (`migrate:serp-app`), no manual DBA step.
- **Event-loop lessons (pre-cutover, single process):** the darklaunch `_poll_cycle` did blocking PyMySQL in an `async def` on the shared loop (~35–72s/shipment × 50), starving odoo-sync (95 pending, 2026-05-29); fix = `asyncio.to_thread`; a supervisor coroutine in `workers_main.py` (added after the 5/29 silent odoo-sync death) restarts wedged coroutines. Order ingest is now an independent process. Pre-#316 full-reseed pauses flipped `SERP_DARKLAUNCH_ENABLED=false` and killed the dual-write mirror (e.g. `EW/MO/22086`, Odoo id 28439 never mirrored); reseeds pause via `ORDER_QUEUE_WORKER_ENABLED` only. PR #291 (2026-06-25) removed `_resolve_odoo_ids` (worker queries Odoo ZERO times; the cancel/ship gates lost their live-Odoo `sale_order.state` reads); `staging_copier_worker` (`STAGING_COPIER_ENABLED`; 2026-06-17 → RETIRED 2026-07-30, PR #707, `94deba129`) had staged a PII-stripped copy of Laravel rows into `serp_test`.

#### `odoo_id_stamper` (RETIRED 2026-06-25, PR #291)

- `workers/odoo_id_stamper.py` (gated by `ODOO_ID_STAMP_ENABLED`) resolved real Odoo ids into worker rows via natural key `(product_id, qty, location_id, location_dest_id)`; stamp NEW rows only; VARIANT id on `serp_product_product`, TEMPLATE id on `serp_product_template`. Retired when the drift monitor moved to stampless aggregates; worker rows are intentionally `odoo_id=NULL`.
- **Failure modes (why it was retired):** (1) REPEATABLE-READ race — `_stamp_sync` ran under the worker's uncommitted txn, `parent_replica_id` fell back to `parent_odoo_id`, and non-unique natural keys mis-stamped a committed **neighbor** picking (~84% NULL / ~16% mis-stamped on one 1,435-row batch); (2) a dead idle Odoo PG connection (SSL closed) silently froze it — **signature `MAX(odoo_id)` stuck while `MAX(id)` climbs**, fixed by a liveness-pinging pool (`_acquire_live_sync_conn`, `SELECT 1` on checkout, capped backoff, `max_conn_age_seconds=120`, `keepalives_idle=30`, `connect_timeout=10`; PRs #107/#108, 2026-06-03); (3) `/compare-darklaunch` silently EXCLUDED unstamped children (`odoo_id IS NULL`) → false "no-overlap". Better worker-row signal: `name='S'+ec_order_id`, NOT `id != odoo_id` (which misclassifies AUTO_INCREMENT-collision rows as seed rows).
- **Transport lesson (2026-06-03, 3 deploys):** prod Odoo SaaS (`*.dev.odoo.com`) sits behind an SSL proxy that refuses libpq's in-band SSLRequest handshake (psycopg2 fails "SSL connection has been closed unexpectedly" for every sslmode) while asyncpg works (`odoo_pool.execute_query_via_loop`, a `run_coroutine_threadsafe` bridge onto the pool's loop, `%s`→`$N`); the transaction-pooling proxy also rejects libpq startup `options` (use per-txn `SET LOCAL`). **When a connection error hits 100% of attempts including fresh connects, reproduce the raw connect first — the transport may be refused and no retry/pool logic helps.**

#### Drift monitor & `/compare-darklaunch` (RETIRED — package deleted)

- The `darklaunch-drift` cron is `active=0` in all envs (production last call 2026-07-16), the drift package is deleted, and n8n "Darklaunch Compare" (`HpHN9Reme3L6bNBd`) is inactive (`IalsmpKBKbJM4LXg`, once listed as a drift-monitor id, is now the inactive "Update Projections"); `scripts/build_darklaunch_drift_n8n.py` was deleted 2026-06-11 (`63b694219`). Jack, 2026-08-17: "dont use teh drift montior anymore". Design notes (kept for reading old reports):
- **Stampless aggregate compare (PR #291, 2026-06-25):** compared aggregate signatures (per-entity `COUNT`, `COUNT GROUP BY state`, `SUM(amount_untaxed)`, quant `SUM(quantity) GROUP BY (SKU, location_id)`) on the stampless `sw_id` bridge (`sale_order.sw_id = serp_sale_order.sw_id`, 100% populated), windowed on `sw_datetime` (`date_order`/`date_done` were replay-`now()`-derived); 15-min settle window (PR #296); cron `0 6-18 * * 1-5`, Slack `#jack-test` (`C083M27KU8L`); the earlier n8n form joined on `odoo_id` across ~42 tables with `GRACE_MINUTES=30`, `WINDOW=1000`, `SAMPLES=5`, loop-free Merge barrier and `_equiv` normalization (date-only vs datetime, NULL↔'0.0000', float ±0.01, bool↔0/1). The legacy row-join `compare_odoo_replica.py` was superseded (with stamping off every worker row is NULL → blind or false-MISSING flood). Odoo was the baseline (golden copy); `--target=live|local`.
- **Reading the report:** the only signals were `'X rows 30m+ old in Odoo MISSING'` and column-level `settled values: diverge` on Odoo-origin rows; `'K rows, C columns — clean'` = faithful mirror.
- **Expected noise (don't chase):** (A) post-seed Odoo staleness (crons/invoicing/PO receipt); (B) by-design create-time divergence in `WORKER_ROW_DIVERGENT_COLUMNS` (`sale_order.name` = `'S'+ec_order_id`, `date_order`/`sw_datetime`, `sale_order_line.sequence=10` always — worker skipped Odoo's NULL-product separator lines, `serp_sale_order.effective_date`); windowed-seed row-count deltas; `odoo_only` ids; SERP-origin `odoo_id IS NULL` rows; ~6h datetime drift (seeder stored naive-UTC as MT wall clock; darklaunch `America/Denver`); reservation timing (SERP immediately, Odoo ~6h later); `stock_quant` freshness gap; benign `invoice_status` (`invoiced` vs `to invoice`), tz format, `commitment_date`/`effective_date` nulls; suppressed `write_uid`/`create_uid`, picking name drift, float noise. Approved-diff notes: GL `j=2 ±5` = human-posted vendor bills; ":information_source: N Odoo-stale (SERP-correct)" cancel lines = Laravel-cancelled orders Odoo never applied (PR #341 classifier).
- **Real bugs the monitor found (useful Odoo-parity lessons):** worker dropping lines when `default_code` is NULL (e.g. missing product 27679 `SA-10-155-A`, 2 of 85 post-cutover orders); seeder collapsing `stock_move` `'partially_available'` → `'assigned'` (`_seed_stock.py:53-54`); worker mis-assigning `stock_picking.name` (EW/OUT/* reversed within a batch); bin-level `location_id` divergence — cause **unknown, re-derive** (the "no putaway/removal strategy" clause was retracted: `_gather` expands to child bins; removal strategy IS implemented at `stock_quant.py:79-90`/`:141-144`, resolver `:66-77` hardcodes `"fifo"` pending `removal_strategy_id`; putaway was then a stub, but is now ported from Odoo 15 `_get_putaway_strategy` as `get_putaway_strategy` in `stock_location.py`); do NOT add `location_id` to `WORKER_ROW_DIVERGENT_COLUMNS['stock_move_line']` (could mask the parent→child self-cancel signal).
- **2026-07-02 sweep (PR #361):** preselect GMV fix (redeemed preselects sum ALL lines incl. packaging; cold-cancels keep the bare-gift total; residual redeemed-THEN-cancelled under-count ~$0–4/day); orphan-quant cleanup; agreed-cancel picking noise excluded symmetrically (`sp.state='cancel' AND so.state='cancel'`); paused-worker annotation (during a multi-day full reseed a one-directional DL=0 bucket means "not yet backfilled", not drift).

---

## Business Rules & Workflows

> **Post-cutover reading guide.** SERP replaced Odoo at the **2026-09-26 cutover**; Odoo is frozen read-only history (nothing after 9/26). Rules below that are system-independent (formulas, packaging/recipe rules, costing rules, Laravel-side behavior) are unchanged. Workflows that were performed "in Odoo" are described the SERP way where verified against SERP `origin/main` code / live data, otherwise tagged **(pre-cutover, Odoo)** + **(verify SERP equivalent)**. Inventory/valuation truth for the present tense = `serp_app` (see the Inventory single-source-of-truth notes elsewhere in this file).

### Two Warehouses & Fulfillment Routing

- SKU site from `receiver_products.location_id`; **anything not location_id 2 defaults to Englewood**. BOMs deduct from the Englewood fulfillment bin (`serp_stock_location` id 2008 `EW/Stock/Fulfillment`, `is_fulfillment=1` — SERP kept the Odoo ids; TY is 2006). Production-slip printing hard-gated to EW/TY only (`validate_slip_rows`, `ALLOWED_LOCATIONS`).
- **Custom branding (sleeve) OVERRIDES default location/vendor:** Bakery & Cafe / Custom Mug & Treats default TY → **EW if sleeve/merch attached**; Mini Popcorn default external (Poppin & Mixin) → **CityPop if sleeve attached**.
- Custom mug: production=TY, but **forecast usage attribution=EW** (Jack explicit, twice-corrected). Forecast location = first item's `location_id`. Mug+treats can split EW+TY → doubled shipping + manual tracking emails.
- **Planned, not done:** the Taylor, MI location (`locations.id=2`) is slated for retirement as one bundled post-cutover cleanup (Jack decision 2026-07-17): the gate is unreachable today (all `vinebox_states` ship from Englewood id=1); `locations` row id=2, the `location_2_status` + `mi_inventory_qty` columns and their code branches drop together. Until then never point a vinebox state at `ship_from=2`. Future multi-location fulfillment comes from `serp_stock_location`, not these Laravel columns. (Not done as of 2026-09-29: the `locations` row id=2 `Taylor` still exists.)

### Seasonality & Capacity

- **Peak #1:** December holiday (~Dec 3–20). Supplier buffer locked by ~week 40 (early Oct). **Peak #2:** EAD (Employee Appreciation Day) = first Friday of March. Mid-summer slowest. Large summer bulk needs 2–3 wk lead + extra staff. BofA-scale clients need ~90 days notice.

### Forecasting & Redemption Model

- Redemption curve (`retool.redemption_curve`): ~**82.3%** day 0, ~71% day 1, ~48% day 7, ~41% day 11. Unredeemed = no inventory consumed. Discount tier uses **~70% redemption assumption**.
- **Purchasing/reorder rule (Ric):** keep 4 weeks on hand + coverage until next PO lands. No outstanding PO = 4 wks + lead time; outstanding PO = 4 wks + time-to-oldest-PO; PO outstanding > normal lead = 4 wks only.
- **Variance formula (Matthew):** Starting count + all purchases − all sales = Projected Inventory; compare vs physical count. Pre-cutover the inputs were Odoo's (starting Odoo count + all Odoo purchases − all Odoo sales); from 2026-09-26 the equivalent inputs are SERP's (`serp_app` quants/POs/sales) — the SERP-side report is the `/variance` router (`backend/routers/inventory_variance.py`).
- **SA vs RM layers:** `rm_quantity = rm_inventory / bom_quantity`; `total_inventory = rm_quantity + sa_inventory − unreserved_qty`; `total_days = total_inventory / daily_rate`. A SKU "requires packing" when `bom_quantity > 0`.
- **SA buildable runway limited by its OWN matching RM** (`SA-19-044-A` → `RM-19-044-A`, derived as `'RM'+sa_sku[2:]`). Summing all shared RMs/cartons **wildly inflates** inventory. **A carton does NOT count as an RM** for SA runout. Negative inventories are **acceptable/expected** — do NOT guard against them. Forecast at **parent-SKU granularity** (`_PREFIX_LEN=11`).

### SKU Naming, Suffixes & Classification

- **`-A` suffix** = standard/non-branded, Taylor (location_id 2). **`-E` suffix** = branded Englewood (location_id 1). Orders with a sleeve **must use `-E`**; non-branded **must use `-A`** (n8n hourly "SKU/branding mismatch" flag). `-A` and `-E` are the **same product** — roll up as ONE in forecasts. **"Move qty from `-A` to `-E`" (same location) = a PAIR of inventory adjustments, never a `stock_transfer`.** **ROUTING RULE:** attribute new products / historical sales to **`-E` rows**. When an `-E` lacks a BOM, the fix is a synthetic BOM pointing at `RM-XX-XXX-E` or a duplicated `-A` BOM with A→E swap.
- **Product-line source of truth = `laravel_live.product_type`** (via `receiver_products`) + SA-NN- prefix number. **Odoo categories are inconsistent and must NOT be used** (Odoo `product_category` is frozen history; the rule stands for SERP product categories too — verify before using any SERP category as a line key).

| product_type | Line             | product_type | Line                |
| ------------ | ---------------- | ------------ | ------------------- |
| 1/156        | Candy            | 25           | 12 Nights           |
| 2            | Popcorn          | 39           | Gourmet Pantry      |
| 3/45         | Cookies/Brownies | 51           | Gourmet Goods & Spa |
| 5            | Snacks           | 550          | Vinebox             |
| 6            | Dog Swag         | 10           | Wine Tastings       |
| 16/40        | Candles/Spa      | 14           | Wine                |
| 19/20        | Coffee/Tea       | 567          | Bakery & Cafe       |

- A `category` / `receiver_products.product_type` mismatch silently makes a product **non-orderable** despite stock + enabled (Retool alert + n8n daily ~7am MDT).
- **3-tier classification:** `sku_type` ∈ {`core`,`seasonal`,`legacy`}; `is_core` tinyint(1); `drop_level` int. `is_core` must equal `(sku_type=='core')`, enforced **only in app code** (`core_flag_for_sku_type`) — **NO DB trigger**, write both columns. Added Feb 2026 at Jason's request (~220 SA SKUs). SERP is canonical for classification; belongs on **parent SKUs only** (`parent_sku IS NULL`); propagates parent→child only in the **forecast READ pipeline**. Child SKU: `LEFT(sku,2) IN ('SA','FG')` AND >4 dash-segments.
- **Forecast simulation:** CORE = always replenished; SEASONAL/LEGACY run out and are NOT reordered — their demand redistributes onto surviving CORE SKUs in the same product_type. **Core 90% goal:** keep ≥90% of core SKUs live. `drop_level` = floor below which a SKU is disabled; `threshold` (~2× drop_level) = alert trigger. **Serpy writes `receiver_products` in `manage` DB** — must whitelist `sku_type`/`is_core` in `RECEIVER_PRODUCT_WRITABLE`.

### Live Availability & Auto-Disable

- **Auto-disable was THREE distinct mechanisms (mapped 2026-07-21) — do not conflate:**
  1. **Order-time PHP `ReceiverProduct::disableIfCanBe()`** (`app/Models/ReceiverProduct.php`, with `disableGateQty()`/`canBeDisabled()`) — **still live (Laravel, system-independent).** Fires synchronously on order placement right after the inventory decrement (receiver/preselect/sweet-shoppe/prepick controllers + `PrepickOrderJob`), disables when the view-backed gate qty `disableGateQty()` = `receiver_product_inventory.sellable_qty + reservation_hold_qty` (for the product or its `inventory_link` parent; a product with no view row never disables) is `<= drop_level` (formerly `inventory_qty <= drop_level`, pre-cutover; fallback = global `receiver-inventory-drop-level` 100), cascades parent↔child via `inventory_link`, is skipped entirely for non-warehoused products (`product_type.bypass_odoo`, via `canBeDisabled()`), reads NO prelist/reservations — the most important real-time disabler and the easiest to miss.
  2. **`SynchronizeOdooInventory` Python sidecar** (Odoo→Laravel sync, sharded `product_id % 10`) — **dead with Odoo frozen (pre-cutover)**; planned removal by Manish. It disabled after writing the synced qty when `inventory_qty <= 0 OR inventory_qty < drop_level` (strict `<`, NO cascade — subtly different from PHP's `<=`).
  3. **n8n auto-disable** — the old **Disable Unreserved Products V2** (every 1 min, queried Odoo directly; the deadlock source) is **disabled** ("Disabled During SERP Cutover"). Its replacement **"Disable Unreserved Products - Cutover"** runs every **30 min**, queries the `laravel_live` view `receiver_product_inventory` (`sellable_qty + reservation_hold_qty < COALESCE(drop_level, feature_attributes id=1 default)` with `unreserved_outbound_qty > 0`, parents only via `inventory_link IS NULL`, then cascades to child SKUs), and posts Slack. **As of 2026-09-29 it is in notify-only mode** (its `0. Flag: disable_actions_enabled` = false → alerts go to `#jack-test`, nothing disabled); flipping the flag to true makes it live-disable and post to `#ops-and-tech`.
- Pre-pick bypasses drop level. **"Live choice" rule (pre-cutover form):** `archive != 1` AND `status='enabled'` AND `deleted_at IS NULL` AND within date AND `(odoo_inventory − active reservations) > drop_level`. Post-cutover the inventory input is the published `sellable_qty` (view `receiver_product_inventory`: sellable = available + prelist_qty, unsynced demand = `serp_sync IN (0,2)` over 30 days, `reservation_hold_qty` from `inventory_reservations`; fed from `serp_published_stock`). `receiver_products.inventory_qty`, `odoo_inventory`, `mi_inventory_qty`, `inventory_updated_at`, `sw_inventory_updated_at` and `components.inventory_quantity` / `inventory_source` / `mi_inventory_qty` / `component_orders.inventory_source` are all `(deleted)` columns (old names throw "Unknown column"), so any rule below phrased in terms of those columns (PHP `disableIfCanBe` reading `inventory_qty`, `locations.column`) describes the pre-cutover implementation; Laravel `origin/main` now reads the view (`ReceiverProduct::disableGateQty()` → `receiver_product_inventory.sellable_qty + reservation_hold_qty`).
- `drop_level` fallback = `feature_attributes` id=1 (`receiver-inventory-drop-level`, value **100**). Parent SKUs = length 11; children = first 11 chars + suffix. Ops lever: lower `drop_level` (often to 5 or 1), re-enable parent+child.

### Kits & BOMs

**Post-cutover state.** SERP is the BOM system of record. Normal (manufacturing) BOMs and SERP-native phantom BOMs live in `serp_app.serp_mrp_bom` (live 2026-09-29: ~1,146 active `normal` — Odoo-sourced, `odoo_id` set except 3 — and ~253 active `phantom`, all `odoo_id` NULL, last write 2026-09-26; plus 328 inactive normal / 186 inactive phantom). Kits for the on-order path are read from `laravel_live.serp_mrp_bom` + `SerpBomService` on-order kit explosion, which is the **intended current SoT for kits (do not "fix")**; the published projection is `serp_published_kits` (BASE TABLE in `laravel_live`, rebuilt by `serp_order_queue_worker`). The old Odoo `mrp_bom` is frozen history. Odoo-side facts below are labeled history.

#### Parallel systems — a "replace X with Y" must touch each surviving one

| System                                                  | Owns                                              | SKU prefixes            | Key op                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Laravel `kits`/`component_kits` ⚠️ **FROZEN read-only** | Colored-box packaging                             | B-\*                    | ⚠️ **`laravel_update_kit` DELETED 2026-09-09** (SERP PR #1116) along with the `laravel_kit`/`laravel_kit_composition` sync handlers; `/kits` colored-box table is read-only and rows are frozen (last write 2026-08-20). **All kit editing now goes through `update_kit`** (Serpy op; the pre-rename name `serp_update_kit` still resolves through `LEGACY_OP_TYPE_ALIASES`). Still read at order time: `buyer_products.default_kit`→`kits.id`; `components.hide` |
| SERP `serp_mrp_bom` phantom BOMs                        | Shippers, inserts, paper, cups, merch recipe keys | S-_, I-_, P-_, E-_/C-\* | `update_kit`; `serp_find_kits_by_sku` (phantom only); SERP `/kits` page (`/kits/new`, `/kits/[id]`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| SERP `serp_mrp_bom` normal BOMs (formerly Odoo `mrp_bom`) | Manufacturing recipes                             | RM-\*                   | SERP `/boms` page (`/boms/new`, `/boms/[id]`); Odoo `mrp_bom` is frozen (pre-cutover, ~2,078 total: ~1,303 normal + ~775 phantom)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

- **CRITICAL id-namespace trap:** Laravel `kits.id` and SERP `serp_mrp_bom.id` collide numerically (e.g. 682 is a phantom-BOM id, NOT a `kits.id`). `serp_find_kits_by_sku` returns `serp_mrp_bom.id` wrongly passed as `kit_id` to `laravel_update_kit` → all ops fail (`component_kits WHERE kit_id=:kit_id` finds nothing → "removes fail not-found, adds fail already-exists"). **Real failure: draft #860 (Carolyn, 2026-05-12)** failed on `kit_id ∈ {682,683,684}`. The old fix (the `archive_impact_check` rule must call `find_laravel_kits` for the Laravel arm and `serp_find_kits_by_sku` only for the SERP arm) is moot now that `laravel_update_kit` is deleted, but the id collision still applies to any raw join.
- Swap = `remove_component(old)` + `add_component(new)` + `hide=1` old / `hide=0` new — ⚠️ **no longer "across all three"** (corrected 2026-09-11): the Laravel kit arm is retired, so a swap fans out to the remaining systems only and kit edits go through `update_kit`. **Discontinue = ARCHIVE** (`product_product.active=false` / `components.hide=1`), never delete. A component archived in one system while still referenced by a live kit = sync failure. Serpy "Replace SKU X with Y" SHOULD mean a kit COMPONENT SWAP (remove old + add new across every kit), NOT archive-old + activate-new — intent is only implicitly encoded in commit `34bd6da9` and Serpy sometimes mis-resolves it. New Serpy-created `receiver_products` default to `status='disabled'`, `archive=1` — must be manually enabled.
- **Phantom BOMs could not be created via the Odoo API (UI only)** — a pre-cutover Odoo constraint; in SERP phantom BOMs are created through the `/kits` page / `update_kit`.
- After Apr 2026: Laravel sends items+box, SERP adds shipper/insert/paper via phantom-BOM explosion (previously Odoo did this pre-cutover); both together define a complete kit. ✅ **The READ path is still live and additive** — `ReceiverOrderService.php` still loops `component_kits` into the component map, then **additively** merges SERP components via `SerpBomService::getBomComponents()`, with **`component_kits` taking precedence on conflict**. The Laravel rows are now **immutable inputs, not an editable system**. Note the SERP arm reads the published projection **`serp_published_kits`**, not `serp_mrp_bom` directly (`SerpBomService.php:74`).
- **Carolyn's confirmed split (ADDITIVE, not overlapping):** phantom BOMs (Odoo's, now SERP's) cover EVERYTHING EXCEPT the colored box (`S-*`, `P-*`, `I-*`, `E-*`, `L-*`, `T-*`, `C-*`); Laravel kits cover ONLY the colored box (`B-*`). An order needs BOTH sources for its full component list. 89% of Laravel kits have exactly ONE component (the box). Pure `B-*` SKUs live in Laravel kits (SERP phantom BOMs do not track boxes). **Documented double-count incident:** soap dish appeared in both a Laravel kit AND an Odoo BOM. `/boms` page filters `type='normal'`; `/kits` filters `type='phantom'`.
- Laravel `component_kits` lines carry a **`hide` flag** (1=archived, 0=active) + `recipe_key`; `buyer_products.default_kit`/`.seasonal_kit` FK → `kits.id`; the **cart record's `seasonal_kit_until`** decides seasonal vs default kit: if NOT NULL and today is on/before that date, Laravel uses `buyer_products.seasonal_kit` instead of `default_kit` (Anna, 2026-09-15). `ecard_designs.use_seasonal` only fires for Laravel-created cards, never for WishDesk orders (those land in `ecard_uploads`). The production slip visibly flags seasonal packaging — Ops relies on that indicator to put the right box on.
- **Seasonal packaging (WW-3237, in progress, release by 2026-10-02):** backend-only this year — burn down leftover older-year Halloween boxes. WishDesk stamps `cart.seasonal_kit_until` at add-to-cart from `occasion_tags.seasonal_kit_until` (new column, migration 0251; Halloween = **2026-11-07**), keyed by `proposals.details_json.occasion_tag_slug`; recipients with a custom sleeve are skipped (they keep normal packaging); prepick is out of scope (SWAC PR #4257 open, not merged). `buyer_products.seasonal_kit` must already point at the right old box. Jack is also building a general **seasonal replacement** entry in SERP (one replacement row instead of editing every kit) for any temporary packaging swap when a component runs out — planned, not shipped.
- **History — the April-2026 snapshot:** `serp_mrp_bom` phantom rows began as a one-time April-2026 snapshot (~224 rows, identical `create_date` 2026-04-03, no `odoo_id` links, `type='phantom'` only, ~29% coverage of Odoo's phantom BOMs), a known source of kit-sync divergence while Serpy routed edits between systems. Superseded: SERP phantom BOMs are now native (253 active, all `odoo_id` NULL).

#### Phantom vs Normal

- **Phantom BOM** = a kit that EXPLODES into component moves **at SALE/delivery time** (no separate MO). It creates ZERO manufacturing-order records. (Odoo pre-cutover: ~265-775 active phantom BOMs.) ~97.9% of Odoo BOMs have `product_id=NULL` (linked via `product_tmpl_id` only) → historically caused "no BOM found" in `serp_find_kits`, which matched by `product_id`. ⚠️ **Corrected 2026-09-11 — SERP handles this.** `_bom_find` (`backend/serp_orm/models/mrp_bom.py`) builds Odoo 15's combined domain (product-specific **OR** template-level `product_id IS NULL`) and resolves in two passes so the product-specific BOM wins regardless of MySQL's NULLs-first ordering. The old "only searches `product_id`, no fallback (docstring claims one; code doesn't implement it)" is exactly backwards and predates the 2026-05-28 `_bom_find` single-domain rewrite.
- **Normal BOM** = real manufacturing via a Manufacturing Order (MO), converting RM→SA (Odoo pre-cutover ~1,061-1,303 active; SERP now ~1,146 active). MOs are created/managed in SERP (`/manufacture-orders`, `/manufacture-orders/new`, cost-analysis + valuation sub-pages).
- Phantom-kit BOMs map to Laravel via `product_template.sugarwish_id = buyer_products.odoo_id` (where `odoo_id = '500' + buyer_products.id`, e.g. bp 60 → 50060) — pre-cutover Odoo mapping; 220/263 active phantom BOMs had `sugarwish_id` set; 43 had `sugarwish_id=0` (old duplicates). In SERP the product↔Laravel bridge is `serp_product_product.receiver_product_id` / `component_id` (`sugarwish_id` `800<id>` = component, else receiver_products.product_id — `_sugarwish_id_to_bridge_fk`).

#### Kit Explosion Scope (SERP delivery path)

- Phantom BOM explosion scoped to **`items`-origin lines ONLY** (gift selections). `component_orders` lines (wine/flower/candy-box/buyer-product phantom kits) do **NOT** explode — prevents double-booking. `items` = explode; `component_orders` = don't.
- Legacy (Odoo, pre-cutover): every `stock.move` has `bom_line_id=NULL` + `sale_line_id` (flat 1:1 `sale.order.line`→`stock.move`); legacy receiver/popcorn pickings were NOT built via BOM explosion at all — expansion into bulk-flavor+sticker+box+cups+inserts+production-paper happened at order-BUILD time in Odoo's Sugarwish import (`ec_order`→`sale.order`).
