Newer SERP-driven Laravel flow (`insertComponentOrdersFromRecipeBom → SerpBomService`, gated by `hasMerchandise()`) uses SERP phantom BOMs; legacy receiver orders use `component_kits` plus additive SERP phantom-BOM components.
- `ReceiverOrderService::updateInsertKit()` creates a `ComponentOrder` per `component_kit` automatically and deducts inventory by location — adding phantom-BOM components to a kit auto-generates component_orders, no code change needed.
- **BOM line gotcha:** ~30-48 `mrp_bom_line` rows have `product_qty=0` (optional/conditional packaging) → division-by-zero in RM→SA conversions and `inventory_variance.py`. Always filter `product_qty > 0`. BOM conversion rate = `mrp_production.product_qty ÷ SUM(mrp_bom_line.product_qty)` and MUST filter `stock_move.state='done'` (cancelled moves inflated SA-45-008-A to 27.17× vs the true 1.0).

#### April 2026 Combo-Line Migration (long-tail bug source)

Jack's SQL migrations CONSOLIDATED legacy combo-line `product_types`; Phase 2 ARCHIVED now-redundant child variant SKUs (`archive=1`, `status='disabled'`, `inventory_link=NULL`). Resulting bugs: (1) receiver/redemption flow kept resolving to disabled child SKUs → Odoo "product not found" → orders stuck at `oddo_synchronized=3` (pre-cutover symptom; the equivalent now is an order-ingest failure in the SERP worker); (2) ~14K giftcard `card_id`s had `product_id` pointing at the CORE product (type 51) instead of sweetshoppe (Jaypee corrected); (3) ~14K active ecards with old standalone product selections showed empty "no items" redemption screens. **Lesson: redemption resolves products at REDEMPTION time, so consolidation has a long tail of orphaned-SKU/stale-`product_id` bugs — historical cards must be backfilled.**

### Popcorn (special mechanism — read before touching)

- Popcorn flavors (`SA-02-*`) have **NO BOM**. Each flavor = a **$0 selection token** in Laravel only (ops-approved by Carolyn 2025-07-03), **archived in Odoo** (`sugarwish_id` removed) — a pre-cutover Odoo arrangement. Cost is on the **sticker SKU** (`L-B02-REG-23-0NN-COX`) + a 32oz cup, which carry the cost. **KEY TRICK:** the flavor's `receiver_products.product_id` IS the sticker's id, and the SAME `sugarwish_id` is placed on the sticker's `product_template.sugarwish_id` (e.g. flavor SA-02-017-A `product_id`/`sugarwish_id=2082`; sticker L-B02-REG-23-017-COX `sugarwish_id=2082`). Order lines keyed by `receiver_products.product_id` resolve (via `sugarwish_id`) onto the costed sticker, creating sticker SO lines, NOT bare popcorn (pre-cutover this was the `/api/odoo` payload; post-cutover the SERP order queue reads Laravel directly and `order_processing.py` resolves `receiver_product_id` lines through the `serp_product_product.receiver_product_id` bridge, with `sku` only as the last fallback). Sticker count caps sellable tubs. A multi-pick popcorn box carries, per flavor, BOTH a bulk-flavor line AND a separate per-flavor sticker line. Vendor-fulfilled (City Pop / Poppin & Mixin).
- **Do NOT fix COGS by adding a BOM or resolving by `product_sku`** — resolve by `product_id` (→ sticker). **COGS bug fix history:** the SERP worker initially resolved items by `product_sku` (the bare $0 bulk flavor) instead of `product_id` (the sticker), omitting flavor-sticker lines → ~$0.17-0.18 COGS undercount per flavor. Faithful fix = mirror Odoo's `sugarwish_id` line resolution (seed a `sugarwish_id→product` map), NOT SKU string-munging / row-merge / a real BOM.
- **Operational rule:** a popcorn order should **NOT** carry merch or a sleeve (recurring City Pop bug WW-964). Mini Popcorn moves vendor "Poppin & Mixin" → "CityPop" when a sleeve is attached. Popcorn forecast splits by vendor **CityPop vs Poppin** (no collapse). "popcorn-phantom-explode" also informally refers to mixed-direction shipment pickings; the 2026-05 "popcorn bombs" edge case had to be "added back into Odoo" (pre-cutover).
- ⚠️ **RETIRED 2026-09-01 (PR #1056, `4dd56cd1f`, on dev+main):** the SERP seeder's `make_popcorn_boms_phantom()` (the `feature/popcorn-phantom-explode` `normal→phantom` flip of `SA-02-*` in the LOCAL/seed data only, never in Odoo — an approved divergence, `ODOO_APPROVED_DIFFERENCES.md` §2.6) is DELETED; SA-02 popcorn BOMs stay `type='normal'`, matching Odoo, and the SA-02 carve-outs in `compare_odoo_replica.py` `KNOWN_FILTERED_TABLES` went with it (§2.6 marked RETIRED). Open consequence recorded as undecided: popcorn lines now ship as the uncosted flavor SKU instead of exploding into cup+sticker leaves, REOPENING the ~$0.17/flavor COGS gap `KNOWN_BUGS.md` once recorded as fixed 2026-06-02.
- **Popcorn costs** were hardcoded in `backend/schemas/cost_tracker.py` `POPCORN_COSTS`; that constant is deleted (commit `90e712f39`) and `get_sku_cost` (`backend/services/forecast/cost_tracker_base.py`) now reads DB-driven cached SKU costs (`get_cached_sku_cost`). Historical tiers (160 SA-02-\* SKUs): $3.00 premium / $2.40-2.44 standard / $2.37-2.24 sweet-cheese / $1.80 savory / $1.24 basic / $0.00 FL-variants. Synthetic BOMs map SA-02→RM-02 and SA-14→RM-14 because popcorn and wine have no real BOMs.
- **Popcorn on-hand lives on the STICKER product in the ERP, not on the flavor SKU.** Odoo held **zero internal-location quants** for the SA-02 flavor SKUs themselves (only net-zero bookkeeping pairs); pre-cutover the real on-hand (~250–2,600/SKU) lived solely in `receiver_products.inventory_qty` (since `(deleted)`). In `serp_app` the stock is booked on the costed sticker SKUs (`L-B02-REG-23-0NN-COX`; 80 sticker SKUs carry ~64K units in the Fulfillment bins 2006/2008) and reaches the flavor receiver through the sticker's `sugarwish_id` bridge — the seed gives the **receiver** the claim over a component bridge for these stickers (PR #1348, 2026-09-24) so `serp_published_stock` / view `receiver_product_inventory` publish the sticker's stock for the flavor (the 83-receiver zero-publish cutover gap, incl. 45 enabled popcorn flavors, was closed this way). The sticker's own component row (e.g. 1363) is unbound and publishes nothing.

### Sleeves, Branding & Custom Merch

- Sleeve chain: `proposals.details_json` → `branding_records` (`physical_branding`, `digital_branding`, approval flags, `print_render_status`) → `ec_order` (design via `giftcards_card` records, **NOT** a column on `ec_order`). To fulfill **without** sleeve: NULL `physical_branding`/`digital_branding`, approvals=0, `print_render_status='not_required'`, swap giftcards-card design, regenerate slip (removing branding alone is insufficient — the eCard/boxcard design on the giftcards card must ALSO be swapped and the slip regenerated, especially if already redeemed).
- Sleeves resolve by **`ec_order.size`** (= `buyer_products.id`, e.g. 62=Small Popcorn) → `physical_branding.entries[].buyer_product_ids[]` keyed by box family (`box_sku`: `a_mini`, `a_small`, `a_medium`, `a_large`, `a_xlarge`, `c_1`, `c_small`, `c_medium`, `h_small`, `h_medium`, etc.). Multiple products in the SAME box share ONE entry (e.g. `a_small` covers Dog S-Wag Small 42, Candy & Snacks Small 6978, Popcorn Small 62). **"Missing sleeve" bugs = `buyer_product_id` absent from the entry's array → fix = ADD the id to the existing entry** (extend, don't create a new entry/box).
- `branding_records` JSON layers: **`physical_branding`** (sleeves — currently the only `type` in prod), **`digital_branding`** (awning, note_card_logo), **`merchandise`** (mugs/items). Approval columns `physical_branding_approval` / `digital_branding_approval` / `merchandise_approval` (tinyint 0-3: 0=default/unapproved, 1=needs review, 2=pending CS, 3=approved). Entries have **NO active/primary/status flag** — all entries are equal; fulfillment must filter to the entry whose `buyer_product_ids` contains the order's `ec_order.size`. `print_render_status`: `pending | not_required | rendered | failed`. `branding_records.review_status`: 0=unset, 1=needs review, 2=CS review, 3=approved.
- `accessory_images.review_status` (mug images): only 0/1/2 written — **no real "approved"** value; approval = "Choose Variant" → S3 + `review_status=0` + `original_print_image_url`. Mug images normalized to **720×720 px**. SWAC mug-image-review endpoints must carry **`isAgentOrAdmin`** (`agent` ≠ admin).
- `recipe_snapshot` lives in `ec_order.merchandise_selections → $.items[0].recipe_snapshot` + `branding_records.merchandise`. Key = `CONCAT(cube_size,'cube-bp', ec_order.size)`. **Livery does NOT read `recipe_snapshot`** — it uses `branding_records.physical_branding.entries[].box_sku` matched by `ec_order.size`.
- **TWO fields, not one: `recipe_snapshot` (singular, legacy) vs `recipe_snapshots[]` (plural array) — at `branding_records.merchandise → $.items[N].recipe_snapshots`.** The scalar is ONE recipe frozen to whichever product was `product_selections[0]` at creation (arbitrary); the array carries one entry per offered option so redemption can pick the right one. By convention the scalar is kept equal to `recipe_snapshots[0]` for back-compat. **The array has TWO producers and ZERO consumers inside SWAC** — every SWAC reader (cart-check, mug-image-processor, previews, the `detect-merch-size-recipe-mismatch.sql` detection query) reads the scalar only. The **sole consumer anywhere** is Laravel `RecipeMerchComponentOrderService::resolveRecipeSnapshot()` at redemption: (1) array present → match entry on `buyer_product_id` → correct; (2) array present but no match → fall back to `largestBoxSnapshot()`; (3) **array absent → previously returned the frozen scalar regardless of what the recipient picked → wrong box + wrong inventory deducted**; now re-derived via `resolveFromScalarSnapshot()` where the derived key has kit components (that was WW-1991; Subash's fix, now on Laravel `origin/main`, adds `resolveFromScalarSnapshot()`, which re-derives the key `{cube}cube-bp{buyer product}` from the actual pick when that key has published kit components, else still returns the frozen scalar with a warning).
- ⚠️ **NEVER zip `recipe_snapshots[]` against `product_selections[]` — index _i_ is NOT index _i_.** SWAC silently drops selections whose recipe doesn't resolve (`console.warn` only), so the array can be _shorter_ than `product_selections` with no error. Always match on `buyer_product_id`, which is what Laravel does. Also: the PATCH-time rebuild uses raw `productSelections` while the builder uses de-duped `cleanedSelections`, so a duplicated bp yields duplicate entries from one producer and not the other.
- **Array presence tracks WHICH CODE PATH created the record, not how many products were offered** — the 2026-06-03 change was a code cutover, never a data cutover. Timeline: **2026-04-11** merch system starts, singular only (Apr–May = 100% scalar-only, correct for single-select); **2026-05-06** multi-select arrives → latent bug born (many products, still one frozen recipe); **2026-06-03** Jason's `94afd9c46` adds `recipe_snapshots[]` to the merch-first builder + `23e9a99b8` adds the PATCH-time rebuild; **2026-06-04** Laravel WW-1066 ships the array-matching consumer; **2026-06-26** Parish's WW-1509 size guard lands _inside_ the `!snapshotMatches` branch and inherits the same blind spot. The **other two writers were never fixed** — the merch-quiz path and the proposal-op widget (`ProposalBuyerMerchandiseSection.tsx` `saveMerchandise` ~`:316` fetches recipes for `buyerProductIds[0]` only and writes the scalar), and the widget additionally _destroys_ an existing array on edit. Result Jun–Jul: only 130 of 181 records carry the array (**June 61%, July 88%** — never 100%; 38–41 bad records, newest 6 days old as of 2026-07-28). **`recipe_snapshots` is untyped on the SWAC client** (`proposal.types.ts:198` — part of why `:360` drops it with no compile error). Filed as **WW-2120** (SWAC producer bug, Parish), bidirectionally linked to WW-1991 (Laravel consumer half). ⚠️ **WW-1991's root-cause section is STALE** — it claims "zero references to `recipe_snapshots`" and "nothing re-resolves the recipe"; both are false against `origin/blue`, WW-1066 shipped that in June.
- **Multi-size proposals** intentionally carry one sleeve entry per box-size variant (e.g. `a_small`, `c_1`, `c_small`). Print pipeline MUST filter to the entry matching the order's `ec_order.size`, or it sends all PDFs (over-print).
- **Custom mug print**: SWOP/Livery reads the image from `ec_order.merchandise_selections.items[N].design_selected.print_image_url` (matched by `item_id`), **NOT** `branding_records.merchandise.items[0].designs[0]`. Recurring bug: `design_selected=NULL` or `item_id` mismatch → PDF build silently renders nothing. Patching `branding_records` alone is NOT enough; `ec_order.design_selected` must be populated. Redemption can write a fresh `ec_order` with `design_selected=null`.
- **Render pipeline** (`branding-print-render-cron.ts`, every 5 min prod / 30s dev, `MAX_RECORDS_PER_RUN=5`, `MAX_RENDER_ATTEMPTS=3`): picks `WHERE print_render_status='pending' AND cart.purchased=1`. Logo precedence (`resolveRenderLogoInput`): `entry.logo_override` → `entry.logo_url` (pinned at checkout) → `user_id` fallback (re-resolve; risk of placeholder if company logo changed). After 3 failures an entry is permanently abandoned unless manually reset to 0.
- **Print-quality footguns:** render cron (`ENABLE_BRANDING_RENDER_CRON`) was never set in live `.env` → `print_url=null` → fallback to 50-DPI JPEG thumbnail (`s3_url`). Mug print is hard-capped at 800×800px at every stage (preview 225×225 → pixelated when scaled); sleeves got the high-DPI `render_tier:'print'` fix, mugs never did. Production-slip PDF is NOT regenerated when CS re-uploads a logo (only the `mugs-live/{orderId}.png` print file is) → slip shows stale low-res image.
- **Image dimensions:** mug print target 800×800, preview 512×409 with a 225×225 printable area (topPercent 24.45%, leftPercent 28.03%); white-bg removal threshold 200 (245 for template). Multi-save: on save, upload to print bucket, update `accessory_images.print_image_url`, preserve `original_print_image_url`, then copy to EVERY linked order's bucket and log `additional_saves` count in `metadata.workflow_history`.
- **Custom Mug & Treats (bp 6950 sender / bp 6961 receiver) is EXCLUDED from `physical_branding` generation** → every sleeve-eligible Custom-Mug order mismatches at redemption (fires `slip-data.js` warn 2× per render).
- **Hot Sauce "2 sleeves" bug**: `design_boxes` `a_medium`/`a_mini`/`a_xlarge` still list `product_type_ids=[6,46,49]` (include type 46/Hot Sauce) while `a_small`/`a_large` were cleaned to `[6,49]`. A Hot Sauce Medium buyer_product (type 46, size 3) matches BOTH `a_medium` AND `h_medium` → 2 entries with the same `buyer_product_id` → Livery over-prints 2 sleeves. **Fix:** `UPDATE design_boxes SET product_type_ids=JSON_ARRAY(6,49) WHERE sku IN('a_mini','a_medium','a_xlarge')`. (Same shape on Gourmet Goods Medium 7026 across `c_1.25`/`c_1.5`.)
- **Livery/SWOP caching footgun:** Livery caches PDFs by `orderId` ONLY and NEVER invalidates on branding edits. After a data fix, operator MUST click "Regenerate" (`POST /reset-status/:orderId`) then re-run generate-batch. A `box_sku` missing from `SKU_TRIM_TABLE` (e.g. `c_3`, `h_*` keys) makes `normalizeSkuKey` THROW → whole PDF aborts silently (no sleeve). `render-at-size` admin button copies the source entry's `buyer_product_ids` verbatim — wrong for a different target box (should call `filterProductsForBox`).
- **Sales-with-sleeves metric** = **% of total ecard value/revenue that includes a sleeve** (ecard value with sleeves ÷ total ecard value), NOT the dollar value of sleeves. Custom sleeves are described by Jason as the "biggest near-term revenue opportunity" (~$2-5 each, MOQ ~1000, 7-9 days after art approval).

### Reserve Recipes & Packaging (Jason/Jack 2026-09-25)

Aligned rules for reserve-order / gift-idea recipes:

- **Soft/flexible extras** occupy the signature-item bay or use available cube space; **fixed-cube extras** take a full cube space and dictate the overall box size.
- **Required recipe qualities:** merchandise type; product size (product ID, treat type, size); number of flexible extras; number of soft extras.
- **Exception:** Charles Schwab orders are restricted to the 6- and 9-box sizes and treat flexible extras as cube extras.
- **Existing-order data for recipe keys** comes from the pre-orders table and the merchandise record; Jack formats it into a recipe key and sends the definitions/formatting to Jason for exception review.
- **Component assignment for reserve orders:** inventory is drawn from the specific component **branded SKU** associated with the kit, NOT raw materials. Items like the pickleball paddle require a dust bag and can be linked directly to the merchandise kit.

### Billing, Cancellation & Discounts

- Default "undo" = **cancel-and-credit −10%** (credits 90% of gift value to corporate credit balance — NOT a card refund; within 1 year; not redeem-only). Now AUTOMATED for accounts with "auto cancel/credit" on; otherwise manual via the Retool "bounce request" list. True money-back refund to card = "Refunds - Without Cancelation" Slack shortcut (rarer, often large amounts, batched).
- **Redeem-only accounts:** pay 10% upfront, billed 90% on redemption; if cancelled before redemption, NOT eligible for 90% credit; **incompatible with HHS/PPS**; volume discount applies on the upfront portion only.
- Enterprise promo codes (`Enterprise5`/`8`/`15`) **do NOT work on pre-pick (preselect) orders** — billing applies the discount MANUALLY by deducting from the final invoice AFTER the order is entered. WishLinks: $2/link + redemptions; can now cancel-and-credit −10%.
- Revenue metric **"sales with sleeves"** — see Sleeves section.
- **Discount approval:** non-standard discounts require approval **before quoting** (`#enterprise`; escalate → Jason). Custom-box MOQ **1500 units** (8–10 wk lead); 1000–1500 units = +$250 setup. Annual price change coordinated by **Clare** via Slack List; **Caley** does bulk buyer-product updates by hand. Size names: Mini, Small, Medium, Large, X-large, Grand, Deluxe.
- **Custom Box / Merch pricing:** MOQ 1500 units (8-10 week lead). Per-box: Small $8.50 / Medium $11.50 / Large $12.00 / XL $12.50 (incl. proof+setup). 1000-1499 units: same pricing + $250 setup charge.
- **Standing volume tiers**: ~5% ($10k-$20k), ~8% (push-room concession), 10% (above ~$20k), 15% (very large, ~$250k+), ~20% (single-location shipping). **Redemption-based products**: estimate using **~70% redemption** to choose the discount tier.
- **Discount approval is a gated human-judgment process** (`#enterprise` channel): AMs request approval before any non-standard discount; approvers reason explicitly about quantity-vs-margin (e.g. "I'd rather not do 10%, big drop in quantity") and instruct AMs to "publish" a higher price with room to discount down. Authority = enterprise/sales leadership (historically deb/jen_connelly), CEO Jason Kiefer final escalation.

#### Prepay Programs (HHS / PPS)

**HHS (Holiday Head Start)** and **PPS (Pre-Pay)** are seasonal prepay-for-bonus-credit programs (alternatives to a flat % discount):

- Client pre-pays a lump sum → receives tiered BONUS credit (e.g. $5k+$2k tiers → stacked bonuses like $2200+$1000+$300+$100). Published in PDFs (e.g. `PPSWinter2025.pdf`).
- Each HHS/PPS purchase triggers/renews "Sugarwish Premium" status.
- Can be paid by PO/invoice (not immediate card); bonus granted on PO receipt. The "Purchase Credit" link is gated to active HHS/PPS windows; outside windows clients add credit via a pay-now invoice.
- Effective discount ≈ **18%** vs the standard 15% large-order discount.
- **Incompatible with redeem-only accounts.**

#### WishLink

- WishLinks were originally **NON-refundable**; CHANGED in **2024** to "cancel and credit −10%" like eCards.
- Pricing: ~$2/link + redemptions.
- Multi-use links can carry domain restrictions (clients often ask to remove them).
- Common cases: bought single-use but wanted multi-use (one-time exception waiving the 10% fee); expired link needing reactivation/credit (handled through billing).

### Costing, Accounting & Manufacturing

- Costing source of truth = `stock_valuation_layer` / `serp_stock_valuation_layer` (SVL; in `serp_app` since cutover), NOT `standard_price`. **One JE per `stock.move`** (not per picking) — SERP creates+posts per-move in a loop; consolidating to one-JE-per-picking is **wrong**. **Manufactured quantity rule:** SERP MO sync uses **actual manufactured quantity**, never back-fills from BOM standard; use `bom_qty_ratio_historical` for past batches. BOM conversion rate rules: see the BOM line gotcha above. Equivalency: candy=1, popcorn=0.5 (ratios are DB-driven now — `get_equivalency_ratio` in `backend/schemas/cost_tracker_constants.py`); cost-per-equivalent = dollars ÷ units (Ric corrected Jack 2026-04-03).
- **Equivalency ratio (cost normalization):** to compare costs across product lines (candy has many items, popcorn few), each product type gets a ratio: **Candy & Snacks = 1.0 (baseline), Popcorn = 0.5, Gourmet Pantry ≈ 0.75, Spa ≈ 0.75**, Wine/Gift Sets TBD. Cost-per-equivalent = dollars / units (NOT `dollars×ratio / units×ratio`, which cancels). Used for one normalized executive-scorecard number. The weekly cost automation (n8n, Sundays) computes avg cost/item shipped (~$1.75-$1.83) into the scorecard.

### Order Lifecycle

SugarWish's core model is **recipient-choice gifting**: an eCard is sent, the recipient redeems it and chooses their own gift, and it ships. This produces a chain of distinct lifecycle objects — they are NOT one "order" type. Reasoning about them as a single object is the most common source of error.

#### Object Chain

| Object                        | Table / Range                                                           | What it is                                                                                               |
| ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Buyer order**               | `buyer_orders` (~1.3M; `increment_id` pattern `200…`)                   | Sender's checkout HEADER. One buyer order fans out to MANY `giftcards_card` rows.                        |
| **Giftcard / eCard**          | `giftcards_card` (~4.9M; PK is `card_id`, NOT `id`)                     | One individual gift to ONE recipient. Pre-redemption record.                                             |
| **Receiver order (ec_order)** | `ec_order` (~4M)                                                        | The redeemed RECEIVER order — one physical shipment to one recipient. Buyer-chosen treats.               |
| **Items**                     | `items` (~5.3M)                                                         | Recipient's chosen gift-selection line items.                                                            |
| **Component orders**          | `component_orders` (~2.1M)                                              | Order-time exploded packaging/component lines (NOT a static recipe).                                     |
| **Preselect order**           | `preselect_orders` (~149K–212K; numbers `6000…`/`sw_id >= 600,000,000`) | Buyer pre-selects contents AND enters recipient shipping address up front. No recipient-redemption step. |
| **Sweet-shoppe order**        | `items.order_type='sweet-shoppe-order'`                                 | Self-purchase.                                                                                           |

#### Lifecycle Flow

1. **eCard sent** → `giftcards_card` row created (digital gift; `hold_until_date`, `delivery_method`, sender/receiver email). Status pre-redemption.
2. **Redemption** → recipient clicks through and CHOOSES treats/size → `ec_order` row created (one per shipment). `ec_order.giftcards_card_id` links them.
3. **Items / component_orders** populated for the chosen gift.
4. **Ingest into SERP** → the `serp_order_queue_worker` (SERP order-ingest path, live since cutover; ~1,905 orders ingested since 9/27, all `odoo_id` NULL) reads Laravel `ec_order` / `preselect_orders` rows where `serp_sync = 0` and mirrors each into a `serp_sale_order` + delivery picking (reservation), then advances `serp_sync`. A separate shipment arm mirrors shipping (`ship_date_serp_synchronized`, the SERP analog of Odoo's `ship_date_odoo_synchronized`) and a cancel arm mirrors Laravel cancellations. (History, pre-cutover: step 4 was "Sync to Odoo" — `ec_order` imported into an Odoo `sale_order` by 6 `ir.cron` jobs.) SERP's `sale.order.action_confirm` is deliberately INERT — the order worker is the single creator of delivery pickings via `stock.picking.action_process_new_order`; adding a second picking-creator would produce two pickings per order.

**Key relationship:** `giftcards_card` ↔ `ec_order` is **1-to-MANY** (fan-out via reships/replacements — one card was observed with **13,719** `ec_order` rows).

**JOIN KEY (critical):** `ec_order.giftcards_card_id = giftcards_card.card_id` — **NOT** `giftcards_card.id`. Chain to the ERP: `giftcards_card` → `ec_order` (via `increment_id` + `serp_sync`; `oddo_synchronized` for pre-cutover Odoo rows) → `serp_sale_order` matched by `increment_id`; pre-cutover Odoo `sale_order` matched by `increment_id`; join SERP↔Odoo on `odoo_id`, never `id = id`.

**Order processing model.** Pre-cutover, Odoo processed lifecycle **per-order, interleaved** — 6 `ir.cron` jobs (Orders/Prepicks/Component-orders/Component-prepicks/Failed-orders/Failed-prepicks, priorities 1–6, `numbercall=-1`) pulled `GET /api/odoo?perPage=500`, **no per-order webhooks** (Odoo crons no longer drive orders; the `/api/odoo` pull-and-callback endpoints are retired — SERP's order queue reads Laravel's DB directly). Odoo-era order-id ranges: `sw_id < 600,000,000` = receiver/`ec_order`; `sw_id >= 600,000,000` = **preselect/wholesale** (bridge via `preselect_order_id`; SERP splits on `serp_sale_order.ec_order_id` vs `preselect_order_id`). Order-number prefixes: `200`/`2000`=receiver, `6000`=preselect.

#### Key `ec_order` Columns & Enums

- **History — `oddo_synchronized`** (tinyint, **misspelled "oddo", NOT "odoo"** — the legacy Odoo-sync flag; **renamed to a `(deleted)` column at the cutover, so the old name now throws "Unknown column"** on live `laravel_live`, as do `component_imported`, `ship_date_odoo_synchronized`, the `preselect_orders` Odoo flags and `items.odoo_sync`): `0`=not synced (~77K), `1`=synced (~3.9M), `2`=partial/retry/in-flight (~64–166), `5`=vendor/special (~17K). **Value `3` did NOT appear in `ec_order` production data** (only 0/1/2/5); `3` was a _Laravel-side_ errored/retry / stuck-archived-SKU value referenced elsewhere — reconcile carefully.
- **`serp_sync`** (on `ec_order` AND `preselect_orders`; the SERP-side claim/detection watermark that replaced `oddo_synchronized` for new orders; the detector gates every arm on `serp_sync = 0`): `0`=placed, not yet ingested by SERP; `1`=ingested (SERP mirrored the order / holds a live reservation); `4`=cancellation mirrored; `5`=bypass-Odoo (`product_type.bypass_odoo`; never written by the worker, excluded from detection); `2` and `3` are Odoo-era parity slots the worker NEVER writes (the inventory view still counts `serp_sync IN (0,2)` as unsynced demand). Live distribution for orders since 2026-09-26: 1 ≈ 1,919, 0 ≈ 68, 4 ≈ 90, 5 ≈ 8. Load-bearing for inventory correctness: the `receiver_product_inventory` / `component_product_inventory` demand leg tests `ec_order.serp_sync = 0`, never `serp_sale_order`. `ship_date_serp_synchronized` = shipment-arm watermark (1 once SERP processed the shipment). A handler that never advances the flag re-processes its order every poll cycle (37,097 duplicate cancelled pickings on `serp_test`, 2026-08-25). `items` has NO `serp_sync` column — item-level sync reads its PARENT order's flag (`items.odoo_sync` was the old item flag, now `(deleted)`).
- **`size`** (MISNAMED): holds `buyer_products.id` (the box the recipient chose), **NOT** a physical size string and **NOT** `size_names.id`. Correct join: `ec_order.size → buyer_products.id` (matched with `buyer_products.product_type`). Real size lookup is `buyer_products.size_name_id`.
- **`status`** (varchar): `'pending'` (~99%/3.9M = giftcard issued, NOT redeemed — **NOT** "awaiting payment"; payment happens pre-giftcard on `buyer_orders`), `'processing'` (~25K), `'complete'`/`'completed'` (~17K), `'shipped'`, `'canceled'` (~32K).
- **`delivery_status`** (varchar): NULL (~2.4M, not delivered), `'delivered'` (~1.58M), `'returned'`.
- **`avatax_status`** (enum, tracks Avalara TAX state — NOT shipping): `not-processed | processed | sent | skipped | adjusted | voided | cancelled | locked`. `'sent'` dominant (~2.6M).
- **`sw_fulfill`** (tinyint): `1`=SugarWish fulfills in-house (~559K), `0`=vendor/3PL/dropship (~584K), `NULL`=legacy (~2.8M). It is the in-house-vs-vendor flag, **not** a shipment/label status. `sw_fulfill=0` skips label requirement.
- **`is_printed`** ⚠️ **(lives on `receiver_production_slips`, join via `order_id` — NOT a column of `ec_order`; confirmed 2026-09-11; an `ec_order.is_printed` reference errors on live `laravel_live`)** (multi-state, label/print queue, NOT boolean): `0`=not printed (Pending), `1`=printed (In Fulfillment; order auto-removed from its print group when set to 1), `2`=slip "Issue" from PDF-cron failure (missing boxcard/insert or no high-res image), `3`=address/label-blocked / On Hold queue (reset, not terminal), `5`=added to print group.
- **`merchandise_selections`** (JSON): what the recipient chose; currently **NULL for ~all/586K+ rows** (WW-158 blocking the merchandise/custom-anything flow).
- Two ID columns: **`order_id`** (short numeric; join key for `component_orders.order_id`) vs **`increment_id`** (public-facing, e.g. `20015627753`; the `sale_id` bridge to `serp_stock_picking`/`serp_sale_order`, and formerly Odoo `sale_order`). **Order-number prefixes:** RECEIVER orders start `200…`; PRESELECT orders start `6000…`.
- **No `recipient_email` column** exists — querying it errors. Sender/receiver email come from `giftcards_card`.

#### Preselect / Prepick

3-table hierarchy: **`preselect_master_ids`** (grouping/ID allocator — only `id` + timestamps; mints batch master IDs) → **`preselect_order_billing_details`** (aggregate payment) → **`preselect_orders`** (individual shipments). Payment methods: `stripe` (card), `checkmo` (check/invoice). One master can have 10K+ children (e.g. master `700017956` = 10,033 children).

- `preselect_orders.type` enum (CHANNEL, separate from status): `preselect | sweet-shoppe | sweetificate`.
- `preselect_orders.status` enum: `pending | completed | canceled | deleted | shipped | on_hold`.
- Odoo-era `sw_id >= 600,000,000` = preselect/wholesale; bridged to the ERP via `preselect_order_id` (NOT `ec_order`); `sw_id < 600,000,000` = receiver/ec_order. In SERP the order queue routes on `serp_sale_order.ec_order_id` vs `preselect_order_id`, not on `sw_id`.
- **Data-integrity gaps:** ~70,799 preselect orders have a `master_id` that doesn't exist in `preselect_master_ids`; ~387 orphaned (NULL `master_id`). `preselect_orders` has **NO `batch_date`** column.

#### Order Cancellation & Credits

- **Standard "undo" = cancel-and-credit minus 10%** (see Billing above).
- **Cancelled BEFORE/AFTER delivery** — mechanics were Odoo's (pre-cutover): before delivery `sale_order`/`stock_picking`/`stock_move` → `cancel`, no return needed; after delivery, if no return exists, `delivery.automate_picking_return()` created an `EW/RET/…` return picking, then `action_cancel()` (`sugarwish_integration/wizards/sugarwish_sales.py` ~lines 149-198, triggered when the Laravel API sent `order_status='canceled'`, `disable_cancel_warning=True`). **Now:** Laravel `status='canceled'` is mirrored by the SERP order-queue cancel arm (cold-cancel and hot-cancel arms for both `ec_order` and `preselect_orders`, flag → `serp_sync=4`); it cancels SERP's mirror (SO + picking) rather than creating one in 'cancel' state. **No post-delivery return-picking path exists in SERP:** `process_cancellation` cancels the SO via `sale_order.action_cancel()`, which skips done pickings, so an already-delivered order gets no auto-created return picking.

#### "Redeem-only" Accounts

Pay **10% upfront, 90% on redemption only**. **NOT eligible for the 90% cancel credit** if cancelled before redemption. **Incompatible with HHS/PPS** discounting. Volume discount applies on the upfront portion only.

### Fulfillment

#### Warehouses & Locations

- **EW = Englewood, CO** (`locations.id=1`, default; HQ 8450 Highfield Parkway Suite 100, Englewood CO 80112). **TY = Taylor, MI** (`locations.id=2`; retirement planned — see Two Warehouses).
- SKU suffix encodes warehouse: **`-A` = Taylor/standard** (location_id 2), **`-E` = branded Englewood** (location_id 1). Branded (sleeve/merch) orders MUST use `-E`; non-branded MUST use `-A` (an hourly n8n workflow flags mismatches). Forecast rolls `-A` and `-E` as the SAME product. A Serpy "move quantity -A to -E" request = a PAIR of inventory adjustments (never a stock_transfer). ⚠️ **The suffix is a CONSEQUENCE, not a decision — no code anywhere reads or writes `-A`/`-E` to route an order** (see Product-Line Routing).
- **8 fulfillment locations** total: 1=Englewood, 2=Taylor, 3=City Pop, 4=Simple Times, 5=WCC (Wine), 6=Vista, 7=Becket and Quill, 8=Poppin & Mixin. Gift cards (`product_type=43`) have `location_id=NULL`. Orders can contain items from multiple locations → grouping grain is "items shipped per location," not "orders per location." The throughput dashboard filters `locations.id IN (1,2)` (EW+TY only) — other locations and NULL are silently dropped.
- **Laravel `locations`** (physical warehouses) is separate from the ERP's `stock_location`/`serp_stock_location` (bins/zones); no FK between them. `locations.column` named the inventory column to read (`inventory_qty` for EW, `mi_inventory_qty` for TY — both columns are now `(deleted)`, though the `locations.column` values still name them); `locations.status_column` (`status` for EW, `location_2_status` for TY).
- **Odoo `stock_location.sugarwish_id` (pre-cutover mechanism)** controlled Laravel sync: **`>0` syncs to Laravel** (only `EW/Stock/Fulfillment` id=2008 had `sugarwish_id=1`), **`=0`** internal-only, **`NULL`** system/virtual. Post-cutover, stock reaches Laravel by SERP publishing (`serp_published_stock` → view `receiver_product_inventory.sellable_qty`); the receiver on-hand leg scopes to `serp_stock_location.is_fulfillment = 1` (the SERP mirror of `sugarwish_id > 0`; seeded and pinned to exactly ids 2006 TY and 2008 EW, guarded by the data-assumptions invariant `serp_stock_location_is_fulfillment_is_exactly_2006_2008`). **⚠️ `receiver_products.location_id` is NO LONGER all-NULL** (the old "unused" note is stale — live-verified 2026-08-11: ~2,468 rows carry a non-NULL `location_id`). It is populated by SERPY create paths, and new `-E` parent SKUs are pinned to Englewood (`location_id=1`) by `_force_englewood_for_e_sku` (`backend/workers/handlers/laravel.py`, shipped 2026-07-31 commit `5838a1e1e`; the `laravel_update_receiver_product` **update** op canNOT write `location_id` — it's not in `RECEIVER_PRODUCT_WRITABLE`, so unarchive/enable ops leave it untouched). A data-assumptions invariant (`active_e_parent_skus_are_englewood`, `backend/data_assumptions/registry.yaml`) asserts every **active** `-E` parent (`archive=0`, `deleted_at IS NULL`) sits on `location_id=1`; pre-guard legacy rows created before 2026-07-31 can violate it, and un-archiving one (e.g. product_id 5101 SA-03-045-E un-archived by draft #1861 on 2026-08-11) trips the alarm — a known/anticipated false-alert, corrected by an ops `UPDATE ... SET location_id=1`, not a create-path bug. **The physical inventory-deduction location was resolved via Odoo `stock_location.sugarwish_id`** (EW=2008, TY=2006) pre-cutover, NOT this column; in SERP the fulfillment bins are `serp_stock_location.is_fulfillment=1` (2006/2008).

#### Product-Line Routing (mid-2026)

Product line is determined by **`laravel_live.product_type` (SOURCE OF TRUTH), NOT Odoo `product_category`**. Routing:

- **Candy & Snacks, Gourmet Goods & Spa, Hot Sauce, Dog Treats & Swag** → Englewood (EW).
- **Bakery & Cafe** → normally TY, but **OVERRIDE to EW if a sleeve/custom merch is attached**. ⚠️ **The override is implemented as `laravel_live.buyer_products.custom_location_id` (nullable), NOT by any suffix logic** — `ReceiverProductService::resolveLocationForReceiverFilter()` (`app/Services/Receiver/ReceiverProductService.php`, prod branch `blue`) returns `custom_location_id` when the giftcard has a branding record with `physical_branding` or `merchandise`, else `location_id`; the caller then applies `if ($locationFilterId !== null) { $query->where('location_id', …) }`. **A NULL `custom_location_id` does NOT default to EW — it silently SKIPS the location filter entirely**, so the receiver is offered the unfiltered catalog (both `-A` and `-E`) and picks whichever. The column has no backfill in its migration (`2026_05_08_120000_add_custom_location_id_to_buyer_products_table.php` only adds it nullable) — it is hand-populated, so a missed row is a silent routing bug.
- **Mini Popcorn** → normally vendor "Poppin & Mixin," but **move to "CityPop" if a sleeve** is attached.
- **Custom Mug & Treats** → normally TY, but **override to EW if sleeve/merch**. This causes recurring pain: mug+treats orders split EW/TY → double shipping + manual tracking emails to recipient.
- **Forecast location attribution**: read the FIRST item in the order and its `location_id`. Physical mug production is in Taylor, but forecast usage **rolls up to ENGLEWOOD** ("custom mug usage should count as Englewood").
- **Bakery & Cafe EW/TY split**: `product_type 567` splits into `Bakery&Cafe@EW` / `Bakery&Cafe@TY` in the forecast (combined = "Bakery & Cafe"); mapping in `frontend/app/forecast/lib/formatters.ts`. Size projections split EW/TY by ratio of actual orders to each location over a rolling 30-day window, clamped to start no earlier than 2026-05-19. Low-stock alerts route by `receiver_product.location_id`; a "duo alert" @-mentions both location owners.

#### What Marks an Order Shipped / Finalizes Inventory

- **ShipStation LABEL generation** marks the order shipped and unreserves/finalizes inventory — **NOT** printing the production slip. Labels generate near-instantly on a recipient order, leaving little window to fix reserved-negatives before shipment.
- The ERP's "Reserved" should equal unshipped in ShipStation (Odoo pre-cutover; SERP's shipment arm now processes the shipped state into the mirrored picking — **verify the SERP reserved-vs-ShipStation check**). Pre-cutover, Odoo sent (on_hand − reserved) to Laravel as available; now SERP publishes `serp_published_stock` and Laravel reads `sellable_qty`.

#### Production Slips

- **Physical batching** via production-slip BATCHES: `preselect_orders.production_slip_batch`, `master_id` groups; `ec_order.component_imported`/`vendor_imported` flags (`component_imported` is now a `(deleted)` column; `vendor_imported` still exists); `production_slip_batch_orders` records per-order PDF generation/merge results (`pdf_fetch_success`, `pdf_merge_success`, `error_message`).
- Production slip batch number = `COALESCE(MAX(production_slip_batch)+1, 900000001)`. The slip UPDATE sets `receiver_production_slips.is_printed=1` (join via `order_id`) + batch + `batch_date`. Only `is_pdf_generated=1` rows participate.
- **Slip print is HARD-GATED to EW/TY only**: `validate_slip_rows` `ALLOWED_LOCATIONS` raises "Print blocked" for any non-Englewood/Taylor `location_name`.
- **GenerateReceiverOrderPdf** Artisan command sets `receiver_production_slips.is_printed` (0=queued, 1=printed, 2=issue, 3=on hold, 5=in print group).
- **Slip silently skips** (no row, invisible to generator) when: `ecard_design.inserts` is NULL, missing `ecard_designs` row, or `highResolutionImageFound()` is false (S3 check). Box-card / insert nullness → PDF cron sets `is_printed=2` and the order silently never prints/ships. Manual regen via the admin "Regen" button works.
- **Bug — `Item::mapForProductionSlip` non-deterministic `->first()`**: re-queries `receiver_products` by SKU with no `orderBy`; when duplicate SKUs exist (different `sort_key`), MySQL returns a non-deterministic row, overwriting `prod_slip_sku`. Fix: use stored `product_id` instead of re-querying by SKU.
- Provenance floors: Jack's local Claude transcripts go back only to **2026-05-04**; EW warehouse launch floor **2026-05-19**.

### Forecasting

#### Core SKU Availability Goal

Keep **≥90% of core SKUs live** (having inventory or ordered inventory) at all times.

- **3-tier classification** on `receiver_products`: `sku_type` VARCHAR(16) ∈ `core | seasonal | legacy` (default `legacy`; counts ≈ core 456, seasonal 8, legacy 4699) + `is_core` tinyint(1). **Business rule: `is_core` must equal (`sku_type=='core'`)** — enforced in **app code only** (helper `core_flag_for_sku_type`), **NO DB trigger**. One live exception: `product_id 4177` (SA-46-033) has `sku_type='core'` but `is_core=0`. ~220 SA SKUs flagged core. SERP is the canonical system (decided at Jack's L10, Matt confirmed); `kelleymax`/Kelley tags seasonal/legacy.
- Classification lives **only on PARENT SKUs** (`parent_sku IS NULL`) and propagates parent→child **only in the forecast READ pipeline** (display), never written to children.
- There is **NO `make_for` column** — "make for" = toggling `is_core`/`sku_type`.
- **`drop_level`** (int, nullable) = floor at/below which a SKU is disabled. **`threshold`** ≈ 2× `drop_level` = alert trigger. When cloning a SKU to a new location variant, `drop_levels` do NOT auto-copy correctly.
- **Serpy `update-receiver-product` whitelist** (`RECEIVER_PRODUCT_WRITABLE`) must include `sku_type` + `is_core` (writes to MANAGE MySQL, not prod `laravel_live`); when Serpy sets `sku_type` the handler must also set `is_core` to match.
- **Packed vs purchase goals:** the SA **packed goal is 2 weeks** of projected demand (first two forecast weeks; the current week is prorated by remaining share); the **purchase goal is 4 weeks + lead time**. The daily Core SKU report (`#live-product-warnings`) shows per product line **Pack** (10th-percentile SA days) / **Pack Cov** (% of core SKUs with 2 weeks packed) / **Buy** / **Buy Cov** (% with 4 weeks + lead time on hand), colored ✅ ≥90% / 🟡 ≥70% / 🔴 below; Pack Cov/Buy Cov match the supplier-forecast Packed / Purchase Coverage scorecard. Supplier forecast shows ~4 weeks on hand across the board while packed SA can run at 6 weeks of usage — "over-packing" is the gap to watch, not a forecast bug (Ric/Matthew, 2026-09-22).
- **Forecast inputs:** weekly volume is `serp_app.week_settings.order_qty`, a manually maintained sheet (Volume26JackExport "Total Orders" row) upserted hourly by the n8n "Sheets Export - serp_app" workflow — not computed by SERP. Reserve orders were not in peak projections as of Matthew's 2026-09-14 request to add them manually to the supplier forecast (FTE calculator handled outside the system); James also asked whether the packaging forecast counts reserve cubes and custom drinkware (2026-09-23, unanswered in Slack). A packaging PO can be confirmed then set back to draft to preview its forecast impact (James).

#### "Days of Inventory" — Three NON-Interchangeable Metrics

1. **`/forecast/live-products` SA/Total Days** = `inventory / daily_rate`, where `daily_rate` = last-7-day actual orders / 7. Per Jason: **SA Days = packing goal, Total Days = purchase goal**. Runout Days = MIN runout across ALL of an SA's materials (bulk RM + every packaging label/carton), not just the first component.
2. **`/forecast/dashboard` supplier `total_inventory`** = `rm_inventory + sa_inventory(BOM-converted) + rm_ordered(open PO qty) − drop_level`; demand = 25-week FORECASTED weekly demand from Retool `size_projections`.
3. **`/forecast/ecard-inventory`** = simulation-based days-until-90%-threshold.

Jason's metric definitions: **packing_goal** = min SA-days for core SKUs with RM available; **purchase_goal** = min total-days for core SKUs by product line; **Inventory Days** = current inventory / (L7D Use / 7).

#### Forecast Color Code (Volume & Supplier Forecasting App, `serp.sugarwish.com/forecast/live-products`)

GREEN = inventory available; ORANGE = manufactured (SA) runs out but raw material (RM) remains; YELLOW = on-hand runs out but incoming PO hasn't arrived; RED = all runs out. Predicts SKU/size popularity, weekly order quantities, runout week.

#### Product Type Key (PTK)

PTK = `'ProductType|Size'` string. **Dashboard and `sa_projections.sql` must produce IDENTICAL strings** or demand won't roll up. Popcorn sub-brands "City Pop"/"Poppin & Mixin" normalize to "Popcorn"; **explicitly NO Popcorn collapse** in the EW/TY-style split (CityPop vs Poppin split type-keys pass through). Custom Mug & Treats "mug" and "treats" are NOT separate SKUs (separate `size_name` rows under `CUSTOM_MUG_PRODUCT_TYPE`). `sku_product_type_key` has base SKUs AND `<sku>-bakerycafe` variants (base SKU maps to the _wrong_ type for Bakery & Cafe — a known data gap). Forecast HIDES an RM when ALL its SAs are archived.

#### Popularity Data

Popularity scores (`sku_projections`: `popularity_3_weeks`, `popularity_8_weeks`, `popularity_1_year`) come from **real recipient survey data, NOT code** — a brand-new SKU/product type has ZERO popularity until surveys arrive. `sku_supplier.lookback_period` selects which window per-SKU. `size_projections_copy` holds per-SA per-week `popularity_pct` (sums to 1.0 within a `product_type_key` — a market-share mechanism). `sa_projections_cache` feeds `rm_weekly_demand_cache` explosion (via `bom_components_cache` to explode SA→RM). `redemption_curve` (~82% day 0 → ~41% day 11) drives probabilistic forecasting (not all gifts redeemed).

#### Buyer Product = "Size"

"**Buyer product**" is a SYNONYM for "**size**" (a `size_projections_copy` row). It is NOT a separate buyer-level allocation. Forecasting applies `projection = order_qty × size_percentage × number_of_candies`. **`number_of_candies`** (integer 2/4/6/8/12 on `buyer_products`) = item count per package; a legacy cost-analysis scaling factor.

#### Outstanding eCard Quantity

In forecasts = items expected to redeem from already-placed ecard orders not-yet-shipped (the redemption backlog), computed from `volume25` demand.

#### Archived-SKU Forecast Policy (Neal/Mike/Jack decision)

Archived SKUs are **HIDDEN from forecasts EXCEPT when they have positive inventory** (to track for write-off/destruction), and must be marked "archived" when shown; no demand is forecasted for them. **Footgun:** in `backend/sql/forecast/serp/`, `sa_inventory.sql` and `rm_inventory.sql` filter `pp.active = 1`, but `packaging_component_inventory.sql` does NOT filter `pp.active` in its stock_quant join (only the `product_name` `MAX(CASE …)`) → archived packaging components can leak into the forecast. Also `sku_projections` has NO archive flag — must join the ERP's `product_product.active` (`serp_app` since cutover; was Odoo). Archived status does NOT sync Laravel→ERP (pre-cutover: RM stayed `active=true` in Odoo even when its SA was archived in Laravel; the forecast SQL now reads `serp_product_product.active`).

#### Forecast Footguns

- **Orchestration paths:** dashboard `analysis.py` and CSV `export.py` both use `SupplierForecastPipeline` on `origin/main` (the legacy `ForecastService.generate_forecast()` export path is gone; `forecast_service` remains only for health/legacy keys). The old 'bug fixes don't propagate between the two paths' footgun (W52 zero-cliff in CSV only) no longer applies.
- **Year-boundary zero-cliff**: `size_projections_copy`/`week_settings` only cover through 2026 → any 2027 forecast week INNER-JOINs to nothing → ZERO demand. Several PTKs broken for lack of `product_type_key_sizes` mapping (Wine|Small Wine; Gift Sets|Deluxe/Large/X-Large; Candles).
- **`sa_projections.sql`** (`backend/sql/forecast/serp_app/`) is a ~533-line, ~25-CTE query, ~7-10 min runtime (far over the 30s timeout), ~210K-492K rows from a CROSS JOIN of `week_settings` × SKUs; its `current_skus` parameter is IGNORED (it builds its own CTE → returns ALL projections). Timeout raised to 120s + indexes added.

#### Wine (special case)

- **Wine should be marked NOT core** (SKU churn from opportunistic buys; ~29 `product_type=10` + ~29 `type=14` were still core as of 2026-06-01, inflating the %; cleanup still needed). Wine uses a **CATEGORY-GOAL model** (target available-count per sub-category, NOT per-SKU), keyed `'Category|product_type_id'` (e.g. "Bold Reds|10") in `retool.operation_levels`. Wine = `receiver_products.product_type IN (10,14)` (exclude 25 '12 Nights', 550 'Vinebox'). Wine FC = `location_id 5` (WCC). Wine purchased as `SA-14-xxx` with **NO real BOM/RM** (self-maps SA-14→SA-14 → Bulk-vs-Packed double-count of the same `stock_quant`; fix = zero out packed inventory); `rm_ordered`=0 is **EXPECTED** (replenished via WCC gravity racks, ~1-week lead, outside the PO flow).
- Tasting-kit pack sizes map via category ids — SERP `backend/routers/wine_dashboard.py` `tasting_kit_categories` = {55=3-pack, 56=6-pack, 57=9-pack, 165=12-pack, 190=4-pack} (the earlier gap for 165/190 is fixed; ids are the Odoo-era category ids carried into SERP).

### Negative Inventory / Oversell

- **Negative quants are NORMAL in virtual locations** (Vendors id=4, Production id=15, Inventory-adjustment id=14 — Odoo ids; the double-entry pattern carries into `serp_stock_quant` seeded from Odoo) — these are double-entry accounting offsets, not physical stock. **Only negatives in INTERNAL locations (Stock/Fulfillment) are real problems.** As of early 2026 (Odoo): ~482-5,918 quants carried negatives totaling ~−37.8M units; breakdown ≈ Vendors −25.2M, Production −7.2M, Inventory-adjustment −5.2M, Stock −81K, Fulfillment −20K. Only the ~54-259 internal-location negatives needed cleanup.
- **Root cause of negative West Coast / internal Qty: cycle count does NOT drop reservations** — a quant with `quantity=0` but `reserved_quantity>0` yields negative available (`available = quantity − reserved_quantity`).
- **Oversell mechanism (pre-cutover, Odoo→Laravel sync):** the Odoo→Laravel inventory sync **floored negative values to 0** (`sugarwish_integration/wizards/sugarwish_stock.py` ~lines 75-77), HIDING backorder depth → products stayed enabled when oversold. Proposed fix then: send `(available − unreserved demand)` instead of raw inventory (temp workaround: nudge inventory ±1 to force re-sync). Post-cutover the view `receiver_product_inventory` computes `sellable_qty` (preserves sign; publishes upsert including zeros) and demand nets `serp_sync = 0` orders — **verify current oversell behavior**.
- **Truth is split**: the ERP (`serp_app`; Odoo pre-cutover) is canonical for on-hand/available and RM inventory; **Laravel is the source of truth for what's AVAILABLE TO SELL** (it deducts pending orders the ERP doesn't know about yet). Available-to-sell must deduct demand from orders placed but not yet ingested (pre-cutover `items.odoo_sync IN (0,1)`, now `(deleted)`; post-cutover the view deducts orders with `serp_sync IN (0,2)`). History: the daily inventory report had a bug — it read the STALE `receiver_products.odoo_inventory` column (could be 180× off, e.g. `inventory_qty=1,974` vs `odoo_inventory=11`) and only summed the "Fulfillment" location instead of all internal locations; both columns are now `(deleted)` — use the published `sellable_qty` (view `receiver_product_inventory`).
- **Auto-disable / drop-level:** see Live Availability & Auto-Disable. The legacy n8n `Disable_Unreserved_Products` was **disable-only with no auto re-enable and no cooldown** (a manual re-enable on a still-below-drop SKU was re-disabled within seconds) and was a known DEADLOCK source competing with Laravel/Retool writers on `receiver_products`; it is disabled now. Low-inventory warnings post to `#live-product-warnings`/`#inventorymanagement` at ~2× drop level, tagging the responsible owner (n8n "Core SKU Live Warnings - Cutover" is the active variant; older variants are disabled).
- **Stuck reservations block availability (Odoo-era, seeded history):** tens of thousands of `stock_move` rows were stranded in `assigned`/`confirmed`/`waiting` (counts ranged 600 → 3,915 → ~48,909 across snapshots; oldest from May 2023; top offender `B-CCC-REG-22-MUG-XXX`), mostly from cancelled orders whose moves were never transitioned to `cancel`/unreserved. They lock `reserved_quantity` permanently. **Must unreserve through the ORM workflow methods (`stock.move._do_unreserve()` / `stock.picking.do_unreserve()`; in SERP the equivalent `serp_orm` `_do_unreserve`), NEVER direct SQL** (direct SQL desyncs `stock_move_line.reserved_qty` ↔ `stock_quant.reserved_quantity` → "Cannot unreserve more than in stock"). **Odoo bug:** `do_unreserve()` on a move with `qty_done>0` sets `product_uom_qty=0` but skips `_recompute_state()`, leaving a "zombie" move stuck in `assigned` (SERP's `_do_unreserve` port does not reproduce it: it recomputes state via `_recompute_state()` after zeroing lines, `backend/serp_orm/models/stock_move_actions.py`).

### Receiving, POs, MOs & Inventory Adjustments (SERP)

- Since the 2026-09-26 cutover these are done in SERP, not Odoo: purchase orders and their receipts and vendor bills (`/purchase-orders`, `/purchase-orders/[id]/receipts`, `/purchase-orders/[id]/bills`), manufacturing orders (`/manufacture-orders`), inventory adjustments/counts (`/inventory-adjustments`, `/inventory-counts`, `/warehouse-ops/inventory-adjustment`), transfers (`/transfers`, `/warehouse-ops/location-transfer`), scraps (`/scraps`), landed costs (`/landed-costs`), vendors (`/vendors`), BOMs (`/boms`) and kits (`/kits`). SERP holds these to line-by-line Odoo 15 ORM parity, so the underlying accounting/stock rules (per-move JE, SVL costing, reservation via `_action_assign`, quant-backed on-hand) are unchanged.
- The **live PO email sender is NOT yet enabled on live SERP**; James is using normal email for Q4 POs (as of 9/28). **Serpy approvals** are enforced in code by `config.py` `SERPY_APPROVAL_RULES` (PO receiving + PO creation → James Emeric only) and `SERPY_THRESHOLD_APPROVAL_RULES` (inventory adjustments/scraps netting more than ±500 units → Matthew Patrick, no admin bypass). There is no `serp_approval_thresholds` table in `serp_app`, and the PO gate columns (`ready_for_approval_at`, `ops_approved_at`, …) do not exist in `serp_app` — they were design intent only.
- **Counts pages are deprecated:** the whole COUNTS sidebar section (`/inventory-counts`, All Counts, Conflicts, Roll Forward, Reconcile, Verified, Vendor Upload, Data Quality, Exports) is flagged "Deprecated" (2026-09-26) — pages still open but need changes; don't rely on them. Cycle counts are staged as inventory adjustments (a product with no quants can get a staged adjustment at a location from the product page Inventory tab).
- **Receiving mechanics:** receipts follow ported Odoo 15 putaway rules (2026-09-27; `stock.putaway.rule` had never been migrated, so before that stock landed in the header bin unless a location was passed) — incoming stock, internal transfers and MO finished moves auto-route to the rule's sub-bin; a Serpy transfer forces the destination the op asked for (a product's putaway rule pointing at its own source bin once netted a transfer to zero, draft 3289). Over-receipt/substitution of a real product not on the PO is allowed (Serpy `po_receiving`). Receiving a backorder after a partial receipt works. Adding a line or raising qty on a confirmed PO updates its receipt. Returns count against received/shipped qty, and the Receive button hides after a full return. `serp_purchase_order.effective_date` is the Odoo-15 stored compute (min `date_done` of done non-supplier-destination pickings) and feeds the PO Receipt Date / vendor on-time metrics.
- **Vendor bills:** a Serpy receipt posts "PXXXXX received — add a draft vendor bill?" in `#inventorymanagement`; the card total is the billable amount (received qty × price, credit note when negative), bills take the PO currency. PO-ops (create/edit/confirm) Serpy approvals post to `#po-updates` on live; receipts and everything else stay in `#inventorymanagement`. The Serpy `po_send_email` op is removed — email is sent from the PO page's compose modal.
- **MO rules:** every Serpy MO op MUST carry a positive `bom_id` and its `items.components` (validation rejects BOM-less or component-less MOs; the server does not expand a BOM from `bom_id` — draft 3213 synced 21 MOs with no component moves). A timed-out sync item is failed for verification instead of re-run (draft #3443 looped and stalled the order queue). A large-outlier MO quantity vs the SKU's 3-month history gets a warning before approval. Unbuilds count against the day the MO was built on the throughput dashboard, not the day of the unbuild. Duplicate same-day MOs double inventory — check for them when a finished-good count looks high.
- **PO email / RFQ:** the live email sender is not enabled (see above); the Odoo cron that mails PO responses was turned back on 2026-09-26 for Odoo-sent POs only, and replies do not route to the SERP sender. Supplier forecast on live reads SERP; lead times / weeks on hand / order frequency still live in the separate forecast-settings page — folding them into the SERP vendor config is proposed, not done (James wants one editable place; case-pack quantities for auto-rounding are also wanted).
- **Approvals:** see the Serpy approval rules above; Serpy draft outcomes post to `#inventorymanagement` as "Draft #NNNN: All N operation(s) synced".
- Odoo-only workflow descriptions elsewhere in the dictionary (Odoo UI receiving, Odoo MOs/BOMs, Odoo vendor bills) are pre-cutover history.

**Other live workflows (ACTIVE, not ops-inventory):** monitoring/alerts on the Laravel live DB — Buyer orders not completed within 10 minutes, Check Gift Cards Without Accessory Images, Child Products Disabled, Reset Company 143178 validated, Email for Giftcard Links Not Sent, Gift Cards w/ Phone Number in mail_to_email, Orphaned Transactions, Daily - Follow Ups Not Sent, Check Buyer Products Not in Size Group, Wine Redemptions Missing, Daily - IBU Mismatch, Disabled Selections in Favorites (12-hourly) / Recent Giftcards (hourly), Check Correct Product Used, Catch and post receiver product and category conflicts, Sugarwish Sender Name Monitor (30 min), Daily - FTP Ship Dates Not Synced, WW-2273 Legacy Reserve quotes missing company-discount stamp, WW-2526 Cancel-credited eCard reactivated or redeemed, WW-3002 mismatched-selection / wrong-size cancel+replace / pre-pick pick-count fixers (the detector `yNssgnlq71JeLCru` is SUPERSEDED, inactive), WW-2751 Pre-Pick slip flagged generated with no PDF; fraud/Stripe (Hourly Fraud Alert Detection, Stripe Reconciliation Watchdog + Error Handler, Stripe Transactions not in DB - Updated (TEST)); WishDesk/CRM (desk2 + Live WishDesk health checks, Low NPS to WishDesk Ticket, WW-2436 Welcome Email Monitor + its error handler, WW-2539 Opportunity Pipeline Stage Monitor, WW-2348 Genie Opportunity/Task Monitor, Corrupted Proposals, Hours and Wages Report); plus Global Error Handler; Bouned SMS with Webp Type Image (sic; hourly `0 * * * *`, `sw_live_creds`: SMS gift cards in `card_status=4` updated in the last 2 h whose image extension is not jpg/jpeg/png/gif — Twilio MMS rejects WebP — deduped per `card_id`, posts to Slack channel `C07GY977AEM`); `My workflow 3` (unnamed but ACTIVE: an Error Trigger → Slack `C07GY977AEM` error formatter hard-wired to the Stripe Reconciliation workflow `JztgWVoOnPFkdoOD`). None of these depend on Odoo (verify individually before relying on one).

- **Disable Unreserved (Odoo-era, INACTIVE) — 3-factor formula (history):** `final_available = Actually Available − SW Reserved (inventory_reservations.status='active') − Orders Not Imported (items.odoo_sync IN (0,1))`; SKU scope `default_code LIKE 'FG-%'/'SA-%'/'L-B02%'`, `sugarwish_id` 1–800,000. `z3gsFEZ9Z0853Gj1` already contained the 3-factor formula (commit `d7bf45f`); the "2-factor older version" narrative is wrong. Entry gate: only products with `unreserved_demand > 0` (open customer moves Odoo hadn't reserved) were evaluated — an **oversell tripwire, not a general low-stock disabler**; only enabled, non-archived PARENT products (`inventory_link IS NULL`) judged, children disabled via cascade. Disable-only with NO cooldown (every 1 min), so a manual re-enable was reversed within ~60s; also the 330s "oversell" offender and a **known deadlock source**. ⚠️ **Prelist blind spot (found and FIXED 2026-07-06; workflow `VRdmXlm2XTeRbOyT` activated that day):** it originally ignored `prelist_qty` (prelisting since WW-1667, 2026-07-01, bumped only the Laravel column and touched no Odoo inventory), so prelisted SKUs (e.g. SA-01-099-A, 100 prelisted) were auto-disabled; Node 2 gained `COALESCE(rp.prelist_qty,0) AS prelist_qty` and Node 3 added `+ prelistQty`. **The Cutover replacement replaces all of this with the `receiver_product_inventory` view** (`unsynced_demand_qty`/`reservation_hold_qty`/`prelist_qty` are view columns; `items.odoo_sync` no longer exists — renamed `odoo_sync(deleted)`).
- Inventory thresholds + days-below-threshold lived in **Retool `operation_levels`**, computed from timestamps written by Ops Slack — NOT computed at report time (Retool copy frozen legacy; current Core-SKU alert state lives in `serp_app`, see above). Sentinel-object guard: Code nodes return `{no_alerts:true}`/`{no_changes:true}`/`{no_products:true}` to prevent empty alerts.
- **n8n sanctioned production writes are intentional** (not read-only violations): `UPDATE receiver_products SET status='disabled'` (via `sw_live_creds` on `laravel_live`); the Odoo-era workflows also UPDATEd sync flags on `items`/`ec_order`/`preselect_orders` — those columns are now `(deleted)` and the writers are disabled.
- **Kill-switch convention for gated disable workflows (WW-2450/WW-2452, set 2026-09-02):** the enable/disable flag lives **IN the workflow JSON as a Set node** (name `0. Flag: Disable Actions Enabled`, boolean field `disable_actions_enabled`, default `false` = notify-only), wired right after the trigger — **NOT an env var** (a redeploy-to-flip is wrong for a cutover kill-switch) and **NOT a DB row**. Every downstream IF gate and Slack expression reads it via `{{ $('0. Flag: Disable Actions Enabled').first().json.disable_actions_enabled }}`. Dry-run (flag OFF) redirects the Slack alert to `#jack-test` standalone (no threading) instead of the live ops channel, and the wording flips "would disable"/"disabled". Workflow naming: `<base name> WW-<ticket#>`. To flip: open the node in the n8n UI, toggle, save — no redeploy. As of 2026-09-29 both active disable workflows (`CGHWrz8FzJYFHQH0`, `T7JJMUCmEJYEdNss`) have the flag `false` (notify-only).
- Credentials: `sw_live_creds` (MySQL live Laravel), **`SERP App`** (MySQL `serp_app`), `Retool` (PG, legacy), `Odoo_read` (PG read replica — reads FROZEN Odoo data; only the disabled Odoo workflows used it, verify), `Slack account`, Google Sheets account. The old "Live Darklaunch DB" cred = `serp_test` MySQL (legacy name, verify it is still referenced).
