## How Jack Works (Tools & Preferences)

### Jack's Working Preferences (read first)

- **Minimal, additive change.** Extend existing pages/tabs/structures; never create new ones. Do exactly what's asked.
- **Advisory by default for anything risky or data-related.** Surface SQL / diffs / full new-file contents as TEXT; let Jack apply. "ok I updated the projections" = he already applied it (NOT a request).
- **Never edit `.env`/secrets directly.** State the diff. **Never execute production DB writes** — surface SQL only.
- **Fix in the seeder, not via direct DB edits.** Local seed data (`serp_local_staging` / `serp_local_prod`; formerly the `*_replica`/`*_darklaunch` DBs) is **disposable** — reseed, never `UPDATE` to fix drift.
- **Read-only diff/compare tools must be strictly read-only against live.** (The darklaunch drift monitor these rules were written for is retired and its package deleted; the principle applies to any compare/parity tooling.)
- **Verify before claiming done** — confirm the data path actually changed (right pipeline? page refreshed? table populated?).
- **Root-cause only, TDD.** "NO WORKAROUNDS — FIX THE ROOT CAUSE"; write a failing test first.
- **Strictly-scoped commits.** Never `git add -A`. **`git stash` is FORBIDDEN** in all repos (caused a silent 4-hunk drop). Never commit/push without explicit per-action permission. Never work directly on the integration branch.
- **Stop immediately when Jack takes over / says "nevermind."**

### Git & Deploy (per-repo — NOT uniform)

| Repo                  | Branch off    | Promotion                                             | Deploy                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------------- | ------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SERP**              | `dev`         | feature → `dev` → `staging` → `main` (PRs target `dev`; `hotfix/*` may go straight to `main` and MUST back-merge to `dev` in the same session) | **AUTO-DEPLOY on every push to `main`** (Hetzner K3s): GitHub webhook → Jenkins (`ciservice.sugarwish.com`) SSHes to the K3s node `5.161.95.56` and runs `bash deploy-k8s.sh main` (steps [1/6]..[6/6], includes `migrate:serp-app` against `serp_app`) → ArgoCD rolls the new tags. **Never deploy by hand / never start a second deploy.** The old AWS EC2 `34.203.231.65` (`deploy.sh`, PM2+nginx) is frozen legacy — never deploy to it. Schema changes = committed migrations applied by the runner, no manual DBA step. Staging: `deploy-k8s-staging.sh` |
| **SWAC/WishDesk**     | `development` | `development` → `staging` → `live`                    | Parish runs promotions                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| **sugarwish-laravel** | `development` | feature (`SUG-*`/`WW-*`) → `manage` → `blue` → `main` | Jenkins jobs for `manage`/`blue`/`live`; live runs manually                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| **sugarwish-odoo**    | —             | `staging_new` → `main`                                | Frozen with Odoo (read-only history since the 2026-09-26 cutover; not cloned locally)                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

- `blue` = **integration branch**, NOT production (`main` is). `WW-*` tickets are NOT SWAC-exclusive; `SUG-*` is NOT Laravel-only — both span repos. ⚠️ **Corrected 2026-09-11 — both command names here were wrong.** The laravel promotion helper is **`/merge-to-blue`** (`sugarwish-laravel/.claude/commands/merge-to-blue.md`, exists); there is **no `/pr-to-blue`**, and **no `sw-*` command family exists in any repo** (the claimed June-2026 `ww-*`→`sw-*` rename did not leave any such files). The WW-\* **ticket** prefix is unchanged.
- **SWAC and Laravel intentionally bundle multiple unrelated features per branch/PR — do NOT suggest splitting.**
- **SERP PR auto-review poller + 🚀 merge (Jack's Mac only).** A launchd agent (`scripts/install-pr-review-poller.sh`; `npm run poller:on|off|status`; doc `docs/tools/pr-review-poller.md` on SERP `origin/main`) reviews open non-draft PRs into `dev` that were **announced in #devgroup-serp** (`C091G8ZEG78`, by the dev Slack bot via `scripts/dev-slack.sh`) and not yet reviewed at their current head — 🚀-reacted PRs first, then newest — with Codex reviewers (`--sandbox read-only`, default model `gpt-reserve`), up to 3 at once in the shared worktrees `.worktrees/_review`, `_review-2`, `_review-3` (that is where those worktree names come from; `/tmp/serp-review-*` are per-run pinned worktrees). It posts YES/NO as a PR comment + thread reply and **never merges on its own**. Merging is either `/review-pr-jack <n>` in a session or Jack's own `:rocket:` reaction (`DEV_SLACK_MERGER_ID`, Jack only) on the announcement/thread: within a minute `scripts/review/rocket-merge.sh` merges the PR's current commit (`gh pr merge --match-head-commit`, in `.worktrees/_merge`) **regardless of what the reviews said**, reacts ✅, replies, and starts the serp-dev deploy; on refusal it replies ❌ once and retries. Unannounced PRs are never polled (use `/review-pr-jack`). `/create-pr` ends at the PR link — it no longer runs a review or asks about merging. Runs only while the Mac is awake and has a memory-pressure guard.
- **SERP schema/migrations:** `_migrations` versions are timestamp-named `YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS_desc` (last observed heads: see Databases). The two heads are independent — a gap between staging and prod is the normal staging-first steady state, not a failed migration.

### SERP Env Flags (independent switches)

Config is derived from `DETECTED_ENVIRONMENT` (local/dev/production); the flip-day flags (`ENVIRONMENT_SET`, `SERP_DARKLAUNCH_ENABLED`, `SERP_SHADOW_WRITES_ENABLED`, `USE_SERP_AS_LIVE`) have zero hits in SERP `backend/` on `origin/main` (checked 2026-09-29) — the 2026-09-26 cutover executed the data-source flip and the darklaunch dual-write is retired. Flags that still exist in `backend/config.py` / worker code:

| Flag                                                         | Gates                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ORDER_MANAGEMENT_WRITES_ENABLED`                            | Prod writes from Order Management page (default False in `config.py:415`; the production value is set via the `serp-env` secret — verify)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ORDER_QUEUE_WORKER_ENABLED`                                 | Run/pause lever for the `serp-order-queue` worker (`serp_order_queue_worker.py`, the live SERP order-ingest path). Originally gated the (removed) merchandise order-queue worker; reused (PR #316, 2026-07-01) as the order worker's lever so a full reseed could pause ONLY order-processing. Default False in `config.py:409`; prod is `true` from the `serp-env` Secret and the worker runs in its own `serp-order-queue-worker` Deployment (WW-3274, 2026-09-23; `SERP/serp-prod/workers-deployment.yaml` in sugarwish-infrastructure), while the shared `serp-workers` pod pins it `false` inline                                                                                                 |
| `ORDER_QUEUE_SHIPMENT_LIMIT` / `ORDER_QUEUE_DETECTION_LIMIT` / `ORDER_QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_SECONDS` | Order-queue pacing, read via `os.getenv` (not `config.py`): `ORDER_QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL_SECONDS` (default 300) and `ORDER_QUEUE_SHIPMENT_LIMIT` in `workers/order_processing.py`, `ORDER_QUEUE_DETECTION_LIMIT` in `workers/serp_order_queue_worker.py`. `ORDER_QUEUE_SHIPMENT_LIMIT` (default 50; PR #1142, merged 2026-09-11) **replaced** `ORDER_QUEUE_BATCH_LIMIT` — the fairness dial capping the slow shipment arm per cycle; `ORDER_QUEUE_DETECTION_LIMIT` (default 5000) was a hardcoded detection cap now made a flag. ⚠️ **`ORDER_QUEUE_BATCH_LIMIT` is GONE** (0 hits) — the stale prod `=300` (set 7/15 for an obsolete meaning) was reset/removed. Prod overrides the poll interval (120s per an admin-page comment in `crud_order_queue_admin.py`, which calls the var `ORDER_QUEUE_POLL_INTERVAL`) and overrides the detection limit to 100 in the `serp-env` Secret (per comments in `crud_order_queue_admin.py`; the admin page reads the env var so it shows the effective cap) — runtime values, not source defaults |
| `ODOO_SYNC_WORKER_ENABLED`                                   | Legacy-named run gate for the Serpy sync worker (`workers/serpy_sync_worker.py` still reads `ODOO_SYNC_WORKER_ENABLED`, `config.py:95`, default **False**; the `odoo_sync_worker.py` file is gone). Name is a leftover — planned rename/removal along with the Odoo connection code (Jack is removing it; not done)                                                                                                                                                     |
| `WORKERS_ENABLED`                                            | Master worker switch (`config.py:587`, default True); set per-deployment via the `serp-env` secret. Startup fails if `DEV_ORM_USES_LIVE_SERP_APP=true` is combined with `WORKERS_ENABLED=true`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CRON_RUNNER_ENABLED`                                        | Master switch for the cron engine (the single `CronRunnerWorker`, `config.py:593`, default True). False stops ALL cron jobs (approval-reminder, pack-tomorrow, core-sku-report, …) while the continuous pollers keep running. Per-cron enable/disable is the **`serp_cron.active`** DB column (one row per env). Live `serp_app.serp_cron` (2026-09-29): production rows active = adjustment-summary, approval-reminder, autovacuum, check-component-orders, core-sku-report, data-assumptions, gsheet-export, incremental-tables, kits-monitor, odoo-parity-reports, pack-tomorrow (16:00), update-projections, wcc-finale-snapshot, wcc-vb-shipments; production `active=0` = **darklaunch-drift, publish-serp-kits, reconcile-published-stock (RETIRED — see Darklaunch)**, not-reserved-report, refresh-move-line-costs; `dev`/`local` env rows are inactive except the two `wcc-*` crons |
| `EMAIL_ENABLED`                                              | Outbound email (`config.py:548`, default True). The live PO email sender is NOT yet enabled on live SERP (James emails Q4 POs manually, as of 9/28)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `DEV_ORM_USES_LIVE_SERP_APP`                                 | Points a dev ORM at PRODUCTION `serp_app` (ORM writes land in prod; sync queue/order management/cron state still use `serp_test`); the TopBar shows a red "LIVE serp_app" pill. Requires `WORKERS_ENABLED=false`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ~~`PACK_TOMORROW_ENABLED`~~ ⚠️ **GONE**                      | **Does not exist** (0 hits in `backend/config.py`) — replaced by the DB cron row `pack-tomorrow` whose `active` column is the switch (`serp_app/migrations/006_seed_cron_pack_tomorrow.sql:7`: "`active` replaces the old PACK_TOMORROW_ENABLED env flag"; live prod `active=1, at_time 16:00`)                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ~~`ODOO_ID_STAMP_ENABLED`~~ ⚠️ **GONE**                      | **Does not exist** (0 hits in `backend/config.py`; only stale test comments) — consistent with `odoo_id_stamper` being retired 2026-06-25. Formerly: odoo_id stamping (off → no Odoo DB/XML-RPC during processing)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

- **Removed / history:** `USE_SERP_AS_LIVE`, `USE_MOCK_ODOO`, `SERP_SHADOW_WRITES_ENABLED` (the shadow/parallel-test mechanism was renamed toward darklaunch from 2026-05-08, commit `eadd890db`; treat `serp_shadow` entries as predecessor history), `SERP_DARKLAUNCH_ENABLED` (was the dual-write mirror + pool init gate; as of PR #316, 2026-07-01, no longer gated the order worker), and **`ENVIRONMENT_SET`**. `ODOO_SOURCED_ORDERS_ENABLED` was consolidated into `SERP_DARKLAUNCH_ENABLED` — treat standalone references as legacy. (Keep `LIVE_SSH_*`.)
- **`ENVIRONMENT_SET` history:** it was the boot-fixed data-source flag `odoo` | `serp` (→ `settings.source_is_serp` → `read_repointable` dispatched every paired `_odoo()/_darklaunch()` read; `GlobalSourceToggle` was DISPLAY-ONLY). **It was flipped to `serp` for production at the 2026-09-26 cutover** (publish stock first, then flip). Serp mode became writable 2026-07-22 (cart pages' read-only locks lifted; ops stamped `_skip_id_resolution=True` and routed to `serp:{entity}` handlers). It no longer exists in backend code; only `deploy-k8s-staging.sh` still mentions it. `read_repointable` dispatch is removed. The frontend badge is now `GlobalSourceToggle`, a display-only environment badge ("Local"/"Dev"/"Staging"/"Prod", plus the "LIVE serp_app" warning pill) driven by `GET /api/client-config`.
- The **darklaunch / dual-write validation system is RETIRED** (switched off at the cutover; the `<1%` drift gate served its purpose; the drift-monitor package is deleted, and the `darklaunch-drift`, `publish-serp-kits`, `reconcile-published-stock` crons are `active=0`). `serp_published_kits` / `serp_published_stock` are now rebuilt by the order-queue worker itself (`drain_kits_publish_requests` and the stock reconcile pass). Jack no longer owns darklaunch tooling.

### SERP Test & Local-DB Rules

- Tests may **write to exactly ONE DB: `serp_test`** (staging SERP, Hetzner) — NOT Retool, NOT `manage`, NOT `serp_app` (production). Odoo is frozen; Serpy/queue tests no longer update Odoo (the Odoo XML-RPC handlers are dead code pending removal — older notes that "Serpy/queue tests that update Odoo run against staging Odoo" are pre-cutover history).
- `mcp__python__run_python` is the **required** way to run Python validation — never `./venv/bin/python -c`. Always verify table structure with **live MCP queries**, not schema files.
- To align ORM with Odoo (parity is still the standard: SERP is a from-scratch clone of the Odoo 15 ORM, divergences are bugs): `curl https://raw.githubusercontent.com/odoo/odoo/15.0/addons/<module>/models/<file>.py`, quote the matching method, write a **failing test first**, then fix citing line numbers.
- **2** local DBs — `serp_local_staging` + `serp_local_prod` — rebuilt together via `npm run db:seed` (wipes + reseeds capped windows from the Odoo/manage sources; the dispatcher's `--target` is `local-staging | local-prod | serp-test | serp-app`, `--source` is `live` (Odoo prod + live auth) or `manage` (Odoo staging + manage auth), `--full` = uncapped history). The summary prints `N/2`. ⚠️ **Not 4** (corrected 2026-09-11): `laravel_local` is separate (`npm run db:seed:laravel-local`). ⚠️ **`npm run db:pull` no longer exists** (no `pull` script in `package.json`); the old "run it when `manage` schema changes" step is stale. Seeder **caps windowed tables** (`account_move`, `stock_move_line`) at latest 5–10k ids (90-day bulk window by default) — "nothing compared" in old drift reports was **by design**. **`APP_ENV` must be `local`** for safe seeding; local can never be `--full` (a full local reseed ate the disk, 2026-07-22). Seed order: Odoo first → APPEND SERP-native tables (phantom kits). Each table wiped between seeds. **The deployed targets (`serp-test`, `serp-app`) are refused permanently by `seed.js`** — both Hetzner DBs hold real post-cutover data; no flag overrides it. Schema is never the seeder's job (it comes from the `serp_app` migration track); seeds are data-only, `serp_*` tables only. (The `/reseed-serp-test` slash command still exists in SERP `.claude/commands` but is RETIRED: it tells the invoker to stop, and every reseed path — `seed.js`, `npm run db:full-reseed`, the Python seeders' `--target=hetzner` — refuses `serp-test`/`serp-app`.)

### The Three "Wish" Systems (do NOT conflate)

| System        | What it is                                                                                                          |
| ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **WishDesk**  | CS ticketing/CRM at `desk.sugarwish.com` (built on SWAC); has `swcrm_z_gmail_*` Gmail tables                        |
| **WishWorks** | Internal dev bug/feature tracker (`WW-####`) at `desk.sugarwish.com/admin/wishworks/tickets`; introduced 2026-03-12 |
| **SWIRL**     | Company-wide knowledge repo + "SWIRL Bot" Slack DM                                                                  |

**WishBot** (`U0AHZK4FDSA`): DM or tag in a thread (not main channel) to create a WW ticket. Ticket **TRACK** routes to the codebase/team (`laravel`/`wishdesk`/`retool`/`react-receiver`/`react/proposals`) — wrong track = wrong team. `/ww` slash command (exists in `~/.claude/commands`) **self-updates** from `jasonbkiefer/SWIRL` on each invocation (must re-read the freshly-downloaded file).

### sw-cortex (Jack's Personal Tooling — NOT production)

- Personal work-intelligence platform. MCP servers (**8**, re-verified 2026-09-29 from `~/.mcp.json`): db (read-only), slack-search (Qdrant, encrypted), knowledge (semantic search over `DICTIONARY.md` + `knowledge/COLUMN_MANIFEST.md`), jack-slack (Slack post/read), logs, github, **n8n** (live workflows, read-only), **sessions** (the Session Mesh — `mcp__sessions__*`). `sessions` in particular is policy-bearing (`~/CLAUDE.md` requires overlap checks through it), so an agent reading an old 6-item list would conclude it doesn't exist. `~/.mcp.json` runs via `npx tsx` — **no build step but requires Claude Code restart after editing `.ts`**.
- 30s query timeout (MySQL `max_execution_time=30000`; PG `statement_timeout=30000`; plus JS `Promise.race`). **`limit` param caps RESULT ROWS only** — does NOT stop a slow scan. `query_database_from_file` requires file under `~/Desktop/Projects` (override `MCP_DB_ALLOWED_DIRS`).
- `jack-slack` MCP posts as "jackbot" (uses `JACK_SLACK_BOT_TOKEN`, a Bot token not user token; bot must be invited to the channel).
- **`/remind` reminders run LOCALLY on Jack's Mac, NOT on Hetzner.** The Hetzner `sw-cortex-slack.service` / `sw-cortex-reminders.timer` systemd units exist but are **not** the maintained path — the live path is two local processes started/repaired each morning by **`/start-day` Step 0 check #9** (`scripts/reminders-up.sh`; a LaunchAgent installed by `scripts/launchd/install-reminders-agent.sh` also re-runs it): `scripts/slack-handler.ts` (Socket Mode button handler for Done/Snooze/Delete) and a 60s loop over `scripts/check-reminders.ts`. Both DM via **Jack Bot** (`A0AV5GDMHHV`) with **`JACK_SLACK_BOT_TOKEN`** (xoxb) + **`REMINDER_APP_TOKEN`** (xapp) — **NOT** SERPY's `SLACK_BOT_TOKEN`/`SLACK_APP_TOKEN` (PR #43 swapped to SERPY by mistake; PR #44 reverted it). Reminder rows live in local SQLite `tasks/tasks.db`. **`pgrep` finding the handler ≠ healthy:** a corrupt/duplicate `.env` key (dotenv takes the LAST duplicate — a bare team id `T…` in a `*_TOKEN` var is the corruption signature) leaves it running-but-dead, so every button click shows ⚠️ and the reminder re-fires forever as `🔁 Re-reminder`. Fix live with `bash scripts/reminders-up.sh` (auth.test-gated, kills+restarts an unhealthy handler). Re-firing rows are `status='sent' AND interacted=0` and do **not** appear in `manage-reminders.ts list` — delete by id.
- **The discoveries feature is removed.** There is no `add_discovery`/`mcp__discoveries__*` server and no `.claude/rules/db-discoveries.md` rule. Institutional knowledge now lives in this file (`DICTIONARY.md`) and is searched via the `knowledge` MCP (`mcp__knowledge__search_knowledge`); `/refresh-knowledge` (exists) distills new learnings into it.
- **Org-wide shared AI tooling (livery + SWIRL):** livery ships read-only MCP servers (`mcp-db-tool-live` with SQL keyword-blocklist + timeouts + SQLite audit log, `mcp-slack`, `mcp-wishdesk` stdio→HTTP proxy, `swim-kb` Qdrant). On the primary dev machine, live RDS / WishDesk require SSH tunnels first (live RDS → `localhost:13306`, WishDesk → `localhost:3001`). SWIRL is **symlinked** (not submoduled) into SWAC/Laravel/sw-design.
- **Hub model & session tooling (`~/CLAUDE.md`).** sw-cortex is the single Claude Code hub, normally a **Herdr** tab; `/go <task>` detects the repo and warm-swaps the tab into it (`/cd <repo>`, then `/serp-analyze` / `/swac-analyze` / `/research`), spawning a separate tab only for fire-and-forget or no-Herdr cases (`~/.claude/scripts/launch-repo-session.sh`, `herdr-switch-repo.sh`). Writable from the hub: SERP, SWAC, sw-cortex, `sugarwish-infrastructure` (Munyr's ArgoCD-managed prod-of-record — PR for review, never auto-merge); laravel/livery/sw-design/swirl are read-only. `repo-write-guard.sh` (PreToolUse) denies edits/commits outside those repos and tracked-file edits in a MAIN clone (work in `/tmp` worktrees; sw-cortex ships by PR to `main` from a throwaway worktree). Other guards in `~/.claude/scripts`: `memory-pressure-guard.sh` (16 GB Mac — one heavy typecheck/jest/browser run at a time; a 2026-09-02 crash came from stacked 7–8 GB Node heaps), `db-describe-first-guard.sh` (describe_table/KB before the first query on a table), `github-search-code-guard.sh`, `agent-route-guard.sh`. The **Session Mesh** (`mcp__sessions__list_sessions | check_overlap | read_session | message_session`) shows every Herdr session; call `check_overlap` when starting substantial work, and a peer's message is coordination, never Jack's approval. **Delegated-agent routing:** `agent-budget.sh --verdict` returns `claude | split | codex` from the Claude weekly % (cached from the statusline) and Codex quota; `split`/`codex` send read-only research fan-out to `codex exec --sandbox read-only` (its prompt must carry KB/repo instructions inline; never delegate writes to Codex). Terminal tab titles (`set-tab-title.sh`, emoji status) double as the mesh's coordination signal.
- **Slash commands that exist** (checked 2026-09-29): global (`~/.claude/commands`) — `/start-day`, `/go`, `/launch`, `/research`, `/ww`, `/remind`, `/db`, `/logs`, `/slack-search`, `/refresh-knowledge`, `/swac-analyze`, `/swac-ship-it`, `/shutdown`, `/blocked`, `/tab-title`, `/save-for-later`, `/resume-later`, `/close-later`, `/compact-global`, `/meeting`, `/sync-meetings`, `/draft-slack`, `/nerf`, `/paste-secret`, `/restore-sessions`, `/reseed-wishdesk-local`; SERP (`.claude/commands` on `origin/main`) — `/serp-analyze`, `/quick-analyze`, `/deploy`, `/hotfix`, `/implement`, `/create-pr`, `/browser-test`, `/reseed-serp-test`, `/odoo-compare`, `/odoo-fix-divergence`, `/serpy*`, and others; Laravel — `/merge-to-blue`. There is no `/pr-to-blue`, no `sw-*` family, no `/work`, and no `/analyze` in the global set (sw-cortex's own `.claude/commands` holds only `add-global`, `commit-push-pr`, `draft-slack`; its CLAUDE.md still lists `/analyze`/`/quick-analyze`, but no such files exist there).

## Gotchas, Footguns & AI Misconceptions

This is the highest-value section for an AI assistant working in SugarWish's stack. SugarWish runs five overlapping systems (**Odoo** (frozen since the 2026-09-26 cutover), **Laravel**, **SERP**, **WishDesk/SWAC**, **Retool**) wired together with fabricated IDs, typo'd column names, legacy flags, and intentional divergences. Almost every "obvious" assumption is wrong. Read this before touching data, writing SQL, debugging sync, or "fixing" a discrepancy. **Post-cutover orientation:** `serp_app` is the production ERP source of truth and Odoo is read-only history that is stale for anything after 9/26 — entries below that describe Odoo-era behavior say so; the **Pre-cutover gotchas (history)** subsection at the end covers darklaunch, drift monitoring, and the Laravel↔Odoo pull integration, which only matter for reading frozen data or old code.

---

### Terminology Collisions ("Orders", "Reserved", `move_type`)

#### "Orders" means three different things

| Table                            | Purpose                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `wishdesk.orders_tickets`        | CS support tickets **about** orders (**~681 rows as of 2026-09-11**, overwhelmingly `CLOSED` with a couple `IN_PROGRESS`; no `OPEN`/`WAITING_ON_CLIENT` rows currently exist — actively growing, not a static archive; was ~588 on 2026-08-10) — NOT the orders themselves |
| `retool.orders`                  | Actual e-commerce orders (~250k+), mirroring SugarWish/Laravel                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `wishdesk.orders_monthly_orders` | Managed-gifting / recurring-order planner (year-grid)                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

#### "Reserved" means two different things

⚠️ **Odoo/SERP `reserved`** = `stock_quant.reserved_quantity` (picking allocation). **Laravel `reserved`** = a large order sitting in the buyer's **CART** (plus `inventory_reservations` for preselect holds). Unrelated — distinguish which system before debugging a "reserved" discrepancy.

#### `move_type`, `order_type` and "order" all mean multiple things

⚠️ `stock_picking.move_type`: shipping **policy** (`one` ship-complete vs `direct` allow-backorders). `account_move.move_type`: accounting **document type** — `entry` (~13.2M auto inventory/COGS in frozen Odoo — NOT invoices), `in_invoice` (~1.7K vendor bills), `in_refund`, `out_invoice` (only 1, unused). Same column name, different meaning.

Distinct lifecycle objects, NOT one "order": `giftcards_card` (pre-redemption ecard), `ec_order` (redeemed receiver shipment, one per recipient, many per gift), `preselect_orders` (buyer-preselected, order numbers 6000+ vs receiver 200+), `items` (chosen gift selections), `component_orders` (exploded packaging lines). `items.order_type`: `receiver-order` / `preselect-order` / `sweet-shoppe-order`. `component_orders.order_type`: `receiver-order` / `preselect-order` / `merchandise` — and it is **NOT an order-table selector**: `merchandise` (mug + mug-box, live since Apr 2026) splits across BOTH `ec_order` and `preselect_orders`. `buyer_orders.status` and `buyer_orders.preselect_status` are **two independent state machines**. `stock_picking.state` is the picking lifecycle, NOT a payment/order status.

---

### Cross-System Architecture & Source of Truth

#### Source of truth is split per-entity, not uniform

❌ **AI assumes** there is one uniform sync direction and one source of truth (e.g. "Odoo always wins" — true until 2026-09-26, wrong now).
✅ **Reality (post-cutover):**

- **`serp_app`-owned:** products, normal BOMs (`serp_mrp_bom`, type `normal`), manufacturing orders, purchase orders, quants/SVL, RM and on-hand inventory, valuation. **Odoo is frozen** read-only history (last write on `odoo.purchase_order` was 2026-09-25 21:12).
- **Kits:** phantom BOMs in **`laravel_live.serp_mrp_bom`** (plus `SerpBomService` on-order kit explosion) are the intended current source of truth for kits (edited via the `update_kit` op; `serp_update_kit` is a legacy alias) — not a bug to "fix". Normal BOMs live in `serp_app.serp_mrp_bom` (live: **1,913 rows = 1,474 `normal` + 439 `phantom`; 1,429 of the normal rows have `product_id` NULL** — template-level BOMs; the 439 phantoms mirror the `laravel_live` kit set).
- **Laravel/SugarWish-owned:** `receiver_products` classification, `component_kits` — Serpy writes them through `laravel_routed_pool`, which targets **live sugarwish (`laravel_live`) in prod** and **manage** on serp-test/dev.
- On natural-key conflict during the SERP overlay seed, **SERP-origin data usually wins** (users matched by **email**), but exceptions exist.

❌ **AI assumes** either Laravel or Odoo is the single inventory source of truth (or relies on the old Jan-2023 "Odoo is source" statement).
✅ **Reality:** Truth is split: **`serp_app`** (`serp_stock_quant`, SVL) is canonical for raw-material, on-hand and valuation inventory; **Laravel** is source of truth for what is **available to sell** (it deducts pending orders the ERP doesn't know about) — readers are moving to the laravel_live VIEW `receiver_product_inventory.sellable_qty`, fed from `serp_published_stock` (a BASE TABLE in `laravel_live`, as is `serp_published_kits`). `receiver_products.inventory_qty`, `odoo_inventory`, `mi_inventory_qty`, `inventory_updated_at`, `sw_inventory_updated_at` and `components.inventory_quantity`, `inventory_source`, `mi_inventory_qty`, `component_orders.inventory_source` are all `(deleted)`-renamed on `laravel_live` — the old names throw "Unknown column"; sellable comes from the view. `serp_published_stock` (~4,865 rows) / `serp_published_kits` (~635 rows, 174 buyer products) are computed from `serp_*` tables and rebuilt inside `serp_order_queue_worker` (stock reconcile every 600s, kits every 3600s; `serp_publish_kits_requests` queue). **RM inventory used to live ONLY in Odoo — it now lives in `serp_app`.**

⚠️ **The two inventory views** are `receiver_product_inventory` (finished goods) and `component_product_inventory` (the RM twin — same 9-column contract keyed on `component_id`; holds & prelist stay literal `0`). Their demand leg tests **`ec_order.serp_sync = 0`, NEVER `serp_sale_order`** — a `NOT EXISTS` against the small stub matches _every_ order, driving `sellable_qty` negative and auto-disabling the whole catalog. `serp_sync` is load-bearing for inventory correctness, not a reporting flag. Publishing order: publish stock FIRST, then flip the environment flag — every publish upserts **including zeros** and **preserves sign**. (The `ENVIRONMENT_SET` flip was executed 2026-09-26; the name no longer appears in SERP `backend/` — config derives from `DETECTED_ENVIRONMENT`.)

#### WishDesk is downstream, not a source

❌ **AI assumes** customer/order master data lives in WishDesk, or that WishDesk integrates directly with the ERP.
✅ **Reality:** WishDesk is **downstream**. Truth lives in SugarWish (Laravel) and the ERP. WishDesk stores only **ids + cached snapshots**. WishDesk reads the Sugarwish DB; it touches ERP data only via Sugarwish fields — legacy Odoo-named columns: `odoo_id` (`components`/`buyer_products`) and `product_type.bypass_odoo` still exist, but `items.odoo_sync` and `ec_order.ship_date_odoo_synchronized` are now `(deleted)`-renamed (use `ec_order.serp_sync` / `ship_date_serp_synchronized`).

#### Retool is NOT SERP-owned and mixes mirror data with live config

❌ **AI assumes** Retool is SERP's database, the SERP sync engine, or that QuickBooks/Stripe/Shopify tables in Retool are live transactional sources.
✅ **Reality:** Retool is a shared multi-app PostgreSQL DB (~123–165 tables; SERP touches ~40). Those finance tables are **reporting/reconciliation mirrors** — source systems are external. Several are **stale mock/demo tables** (all-text numerics, 2022 dates): `quickbooks_dashboard`, `stripe_dashboard`, `mock_*`, `sample_users`, `test_table`, `jasonTest`, `Sheet1/Sheet3`, `workflow_test`. Real QB sync state is `QuickbooksLastUpdates` / `QuickbooksMismatchReportLastUpdates` / `ManageToLiveLogs`. The Retool `odoo_sync_queue*` tables are **frozen legacy copies** (frozen since 2026-06-26) — the live sync queue is `serpy_sync_queue` in `serp_app`.

#### Retool table suffixes were the environment switch (no separate DBs)

⚠️ In Retool, environment separation is by **table-name SUFFIX**, not separate databases (legacy layout; the Serpy queue and drafts have since moved to `serp_app`):

- Draft-ops / inventory-counts / prompt-logs: **base** name locally, `_live` in prod (`serp_draft_operations` vs `serp_draft_operations_live`). Serpy drafts now live in `serpy_drafts` (was `serp_draft_operations_live`).
- AI message/turn tables: `_dev` locally, `_live` in prod (`serp_ai_messages_dev` / `_live`).
- Sync queue: was plain / `_dev` / `_live` (`odoo_sync_queue`, `odoo_sync_queue_dev`, `odoo_sync_queue_live`) — the live queue is now **`serpy_sync_queue`** in `serp_app` (renamed 2026-08-31).
- Auth/user/forecast tables: **NO suffix**.
- `_live` tables hold real prod data with **far higher row counts**.

❌ **AI assumes** `serp_refresh_tokens` and `serp_password_reset_tokens` live in Retool (they are documented there).
✅ **Reality:** They actually live in **MySQL** (serp ORM, via `env.cr` / `cr.execute()`), not Retool. `crud_refresh_token.py` / `crud_password_reset.py` use the MySQL cursor.

#### Auth is single-table: `serp_res_users` (the Retool bridge is history)

❌ **AI assumes** Retool `serp_users` is still SERP's auth table, or that Retool user IDs equal ORM user IDs.
✅ **Reality:** Since 2026-09-26 SERP's ORM `serp_res_users` in `serp_app` IS the only auth/user table; passwords are stored there (bcrypt/PBKDF2) and checked by `res_users.authenticate`. History: auth identity lived in **Retool PostgreSQL `serp_users`**; roles/RBAC live in **MySQL ORM** (`serp_res_users` + `serp_res_groups`). They're bridged lazily on each login via `ensure_orm_user()`, **matched by EMAIL not ID** (Retool serial ID ≠ ORM bigint ID). Passwords lived in Retool only; MySQL has placeholder `'synced-from-retool'`. The `retool.serp_users` bridge (4 cols: id, created_at, updated_at, orm_user_id) was hit on **every authenticated request**; old columns (name/email/password/roles) moved to `_backup_serp_users`. **Post-cutover: SERP commit `feac4d1f4` (2026-09-26) dropped the `serp_users` bridge — `res_users` in `serp_app` is the ONLY user table (Odoo/SERP/Laravel user info consolidated). Retool `serp_users` is legacy (last update 2026-04-23).** The `ensure_orm_user()` email-matching bridge above is history.

❌ **AI queries** `retool.serp_users` expecting `name`/`email`/`role` columns.
✅ **Reality:** Removed — it's a 4-column bridge (frozen). Old schema is in `_backup_serp_users`.

❌ **AI assumes** SERP's `.env` `TEST_AUTH_DB_*` (PostgreSQL localhost:5432) is the auth DB.
✅ **Reality:** **Dead config, never used.** `auth_database.py` no longer exists; auth reads `serp_res_users` through the ORM on the same MySQL. Startup error "Cannot connect to main database for auth on 127.0.0.1" means it's hitting local MySQL (127.0.0.1:3307), not a separate PG.
